### [1] [Agent-Callable Feature Coverage: Measuring Software Readiness for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35789)
*Zedong Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Proposes GUI-API parity and two operational tools—ACFC and ARC—to measure how well software supports agent use; empirical evaluation shows significant gaps in discoverability and safety governance despite moderate API coverage, reducing agent effectiveness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As agents increasingly operate graphical software via screenshots, product design should provide structured, callable interfaces for agents equivalent to GUI features; current API coverage metrics miss agent-oriented discoverability and controllability, leading to poor agent usability and safety.

Method: Define GUI-API parity principle; propose ACFC (product-level readiness metric) and ARC (per-capability 0–9 score from three 0–3 axes); apply ARC across 30 systems in five categories to compute ACFC; conduct controlled task-based evaluation on ten deployed systems with API-only agents and a documentation ablation; run tests across three agent models including local Gemma4.

Result: ARC-based ACFC scores are substantially lower than Accessibility-only metrics; API-only agents completed 56/58 tasks when capabilities were exposed, but fell to 50/58 after documentation ablation, showing discoverability matters; inaccessible capabilities were not solved; findings consistent across three agent models including Gemma4.

Conclusion: The paper argues that GUI and agent APIs should be parity-enabled so agents can access all human-facing features via structured interfaces with safety metadata; introduces ACFC metric and ARC scoring framework (Accessibility, Discoverability, Controllability, 0–3 each) to quantify agent readiness, and demonstrates via empirical study that agent readiness lags behind API-only coverage, with discoverability and governance gaps reducing agent task completion.

Abstract: AI agents already operate graphical software through screenshot-based computer use, so the pressing question is not whether agents can operate software, but how well software supports them through structured, controllable channels. We formalize this need as the GUI-API parity principle: every capability available to human users through a graphical interface should also be accessible to agents through a structured, callable interface with appropriate safety metadata. To operationalize this principle, we introduce two contributions. First, Agent-Callable Feature Coverage (ACFC), a product-level readiness metric quantifying how much of a system's human-facing functionality agents can access and use. Second, Agent Readiness Conformance (ARC), a framework that scores each capability on three axes: Accessibility (can an agent call it), Discoverability (can an agent find and understand it), and Controllability (can an agent use it safely), each on a 0-3 scale, yielding a composite 0-9 readiness score. Drawing on the Richardson Maturity Model, ARC provides finer-grained assessment than binary coverage. In an empirical study of 30 software systems across five categories, ARC-based ACFC scores are substantially lower than Accessibility-only metrics would suggest: most systems achieve moderate API coverage but lag on agent-oriented documentation and safety governance. In a controlled testbed of ten deployed systems, API-only agents completed 56 of 58 tasks whose target capabilities were exposed through structured interfaces; a documentation ablation reduced this to 50 of 58, showing that Discoverability affects execution even among accessible capabilities, while inaccessible capabilities, included as negative controls, were not solved. These findings hold across three agent models, including Google's open-weights Gemma4 run locally.

</details>


### [2] [A Leakage-Safe, Cost-Aware Regression Testing Methodology for the Quantum Transpiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35834)
*Furqan Nasir,Muhammad Arif Shah,Ifitkhar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在严格的、预注册且避免数据泄露的评估下，针对 Qiskit transpiler 的复合 risk_score 策略未超越基于多样性的简单优先级方法；多样性/新颖性是有效信号，建议采用“多样性优先”并保证评估的泄露安全与预算绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件（如 Qiskit）频繁修改，单次 transpiler-pass 变更即可引入回归；因 CI 预算有限，必须在成本高度异质的测试套件中选择并优先执行测试。为避免测试设计中信息泄露与评估偏差，需要一个预注册且预算绑定的评估框架来衡量回归测试选择策略。

Method: 提出一种泄露安全（leakage-safe）、成本感知的回归测试选择流程并进行预注册评估。使用：1) 一个成本异质的测试语料库（116 单元，单元成本变化高达 9,945X，T_full=69.17s），预算在读任何测试标签前基于成本测量固定；2) 一个透明的risk_score选择器及若干基线（多样性、成本/历史/变更阶段、随机）；3) 组件消融分析；4) 独立预注册的第二轮评估（19 次变异测试事件）和两个真实的前向回归事件复核。所有代码与数据开源。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 透明的risk_score选择器平均检测-预算 AUC=0.721（95% CI [0.44,0.94]），落后于多样性仅策略 AUC=0.874（Cliff's δ=-0.64，效应量大）；成本/历史/变更阶段基线 AUC=0.840，随机 AUC=0.821；2) 消融显示多样性与新颖性是真正有效的信号；3) 在独立预注册的 19 次变异事件评估中，分解后的“多样性优先”选择器将性能提升至与最强基线持平（AUC=0.880 vs 0.880，δ≈-0.08），并显著优于原始复合方法（0.880 vs 0.768，δ=+0.57）；4) 两个真实前向回归事件进一步支持该结论；5) 结果为负面但诚实的发现，且所有工件公开以便复现。

Conclusion: 在预注册、避免数据泄露的严格评估下，作者提出的复合“risk_score”回归测试选择方法并未优于基于多样性（diversity-only）或简单基线；实际有效的信号是多样性和新颖性，而非严重性或成本。通过对组件消融与两个独立评估，作者展示将选择策略分解为“先多样性”可显著改进性能并匹配最强基线，且在真实回归事件上得到证实。

Abstract: Quantum SDKs such as Qiskit are revised continually, and a single transpiler-pass change can introduce a software regression, so continuous integration (CI) must select and prioritize tests from a large, cost-heterogeneous suite under a fixed budget. We present a leakage-safe, cost-aware regression-test-selection methodology for the Qiskit quantum transpiler, with a pre-registered, budget binding evaluation that avoids data leakage. The evaluation uses a cost-heterogeneous corpus (116 units, per-unit cost spread 9,945X, T_full = 69.17 s) whose budgets were fixed from measured cost before any test-oracle label was read. A transparent risk_score selector does not beat simple test case-prioritization baselines: mean detection-vs-budget AUC is 0.721 [95% CI 0.44, 0.94] versus 0.874 [0.65, 1.00] for diversity-only (Cliff's δ = -0.64, large), with cost/history/change-stage baselines at 0.840 and random at 0.821. A component ablation shows the composite underperforms its own best signal: diversity and novelty, not severity or cost, are the effective signal. A second, independently pre-registered evaluation (19 mutation-testing events: nine original plus ten new, verified operators) tests the decomposed, diversity-first selector this mechanism implies: it closes the gap to the strongest baseline to a negligible effect size (0.880 vs 0.880, δ = -0.08) while decisively beating the original composite (0.880 vs 0.768, δ = +0.57). Two verified forward-regression events from real Qiskit CI history corroborate it, reported per event, not pooled, under a pre-declared claim-scope rule. We report the negative result as an honest, pre-registered finding with its mechanism. All code, data, and artifacts from this empirical software engineering study are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond Rule-Based Mutation Testing: Test-Aware Mutant Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35841)
*Nils Kiele,Zainab Saad,Zirui Wang,Steve Drew,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将单元测试暴露给LLM，生成通过基准测试但会被扩展测试捕获的变异体，可高效发现测试套件缺陷，显著优于test-blind提示与传统工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规则化变异测试产生大量平凡、冗余或等价变异体，限制其实用性；已有LLM方法生成更现实的缺陷但通常‘test-blind’，无法针对现有测试用例的覆盖盲区进行有目的的变异生成。

Method: 在Prompt中同时提供问题描述、标准解法与基准测试，要求LLM生成能通过基准测试但会被扩展测试检测到的非平凡变异体；在HumanEval和MBPP上用五款LLM（Gemini 3.1 Pro/3 Flash、GPT 5.1/GPT 4.1 Mini、Qwen3-32B）评估，使用扩展的EvalPlus测试套件作为自动判别Oracle。

Result: test-aware提示在HumanEval/MBPP上分别达成87.7%和79.1%的经验证故障率，远超test-blind（12.2%/23.0%）和mutmut（4.4%/5.7%）；变异体失败的细微程度相当，但test-aware在每个经验证故障上的计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 将单元测试作为提示信息（test-aware prompting）显著提升了基于LLM的变异体生成在发现测试集缺陷上的有效性和效率，相较于test-blind提示与传统规则工具，其经验证的故障率和每故障成本均大幅更优。

Abstract: Mutation testing evaluates test-suite adequacy by injecting synthetic faults into program code. However, traditional rule-based tools often generate large numbers of trivial, redundant, or equivalent mutants that limit their practical use for identifying gaps in a test suite. While recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches generate more realistic faults, most remain test-blind: The model sees only the source code and cannot reason about what existing tests already cover. We propose test-aware mutant generation, in which an LLM receives the problem statement, canonical solution and base tests in a single prompt, and must generate a nontrivial mutant that passes the base unit tests. We evaluate this approach across a set of five LLMs -- Gemini 3.1 Pro, Gemini 3 Flash, GPT 5.1 Codex Mini, GPT 4.1 Mini, Qwen3-32B -- on the HumanEval and MBPP benchmarks. The extended EvalPlus test suites serve as an automated oracle to verify whether surviving mutants represent genuine bugs. Test-aware prompting yields verified fault rates of 87.7% (HumanEval) and 79.1% (MBPP), meaning these mutants pass all base tests but are caught by the oracle. This vastly outperforms the matched test-blind prompting (which yields only 12.2% and 23.0%, respectively) and the traditional rule-based tool mutmut (4.4% and 5.7%). While fault subtlety (the fraction of extended tests a mutant fails) remains comparable across all three methods, test-awareness minimizes the computational cost per verified fault, compared to test-blind prompting. Exposing an LLM to existing unit tests shifts mutant generation from untargeted bug injection toward effective discovery of weaknesses in an existing test suite. Our work establishes a concrete foundation for future research to scale test-aware mutant generation to production-level environments.

</details>


### [4] [PoliVEM: a Python-driven virtual element framework for computational solid mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35878)
*Paulo Akira F. Enabe,Rodrigo Provasi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PoliVEM 是一个以 C++/Python 实现的模块化 VEM 框架，验证了多种力学问题的一致实现，但在高阶与内存开销上存在性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少 VEM 各变体实现重复工作，提供统一、可复用且便于扩展与验证的平台，加速方法开发、比较与工程应用。

Method: 基于 C++17 的计算核心与 Python 接口；将顶点/边/面/单元自由度统一层次化存储；以公共多项式数据构建能量、应变与 L^2 投影；保留元素级一致性—稳定化分裂；通过组合将网格、材料、单元、组装器与求解器职责分离；新公式只需提供投影、离散形式与稳定化即可重用其余基础设施。数值基础设施包括多边形/多面体网格输入、高阶实体编号、缓存投影算子、静态缩并、彩色稀疏组装、基于矩阵结构的线性解算器选择、带线搜索与正则化的增量牛顿、显式/隐式时间积分。

Result: 通过多组数值试验（多项式重现、静态缩并后的恢复、高阶自由度处理、多边形/多面体网格、补丁测试、刚度谱、与独立有限元比较）验证了框架的正确性；识别出高阶多项式基在各向异性单元上的标度问题及稀疏直接分解的内存消耗为当前实现的主要限制。

Conclusion: PoliVEM 提供了一个模块化、可扩展的 VEM 软件框架，在多物理维度（1D梁、2D/3D弹性、轴对称、瞬态扩散、有限应变超弹性）中实现共享核心，验证了正确性并揭示了性能与可扩展性的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: This work presents PoliVEM, a software framework for the Virtual Element Method (VEM) in computational solid and structural mechanics. A C++17 computational core and a Python interface place one-dimensional beams, two- and three-dimensional elasticity, axisymmetric elasticity, transient diffusion, and finite-strain hyperelasticity in a common implementation. The framework stores vertex, edge, face, and cell degrees of freedom in one hierarchy, constructs the energy, strain, and $L^2$ projections from common polynomial data, and retains the consistency--stabilization split at the element level. The core separates the mesh, material, element, assembler, and solver responsibilities and combines them by composition. A new formulation supplies its projection, discrete form, and stabilization while reusing the mesh representation, degree-of-freedom numbering, boundary-condition treatment, sparse assembly, algebraic solvers, and Python binding pattern. The numerical infrastructure includes polygonal and polyhedral mesh input, higher-order entity numbering, cached projection operators, static condensation, coloured sparse assembly, linear solver selection based on the matrix structure, an incremental Newton method with line search and regularization, and explicit and implicit time integration. Three numerical experiments examine the shared implementation through polynomial reproduction, recovery after static condensation, higher-order degree-of-freedom handling, polygonal and polyhedral meshes, patch tests, stiffness spectra, and comparisons with independent finite element calculations. The results verify the common core for the beam and two- and three-dimensional elasticity formulations. They also identify the scaling of high-order polynomial bases on anisotropic cells and the memory required by sparse direct factorizations as the main limitations of the current implementation.

</details>


### [5] [The Artifact Promotion Control Model: An Implementation Case Study. Build on Target Machines vs. Build Once and Promote Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35891)
*Vasili Gavrilov,Enkli Ylli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Paper assembles known DevOps and supply-chain controls into a formal artifact-promotion model and validates feasibility and gaps via an AWS case study with measured rollout metrics and a controls checklist.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To argue for artifact promotion as a superior control model for cloud deployments, showing how it aligns with regulatory requirements and reduces attack surface compared with building on target.

Method: Part I: Formal restatement of existing practices (build-once, build/release/run split, binary authorization, separation/supply-chain properties, NIST SP 800-204D) into a single model and analysis of regulatory controls. Part II: Anonymized AWS case study measuring deployment timings and mapping implemented controls against the model (31 rollouts, timing data, control table).

Result: Part I synthesizes literature into a stricter model and derives security properties (e.g., artifact identity, secret-injection implications). Part II provides operational evidence: fast autonomous release path (one command), measured rollout times (36 s/server), frequency (22 releases in 4 weeks), and a gap analysis showing missing controls (no signature, no scan, no approval record, no verification at deploy) and two release units outside the pipeline.

Conclusion: Artifact promotion (build-once and move the same binary through environments) is a coherent control model that better satisfies several regulatory integrity and change-control requirements than build-on-target; secrets injected at build-time break artifact identity; downloading libraries at deploy time increases attack surface; release role need not hold production passwords.

Abstract: Artifact promotion means building the software once and moving that same built copy through the test and production environments, instead of building it again on each server. A previous treatment by the first author presented it as a control model for cloud deployment in accessible engineering prose. Part I of this paper restates the model in stricter form: the release object, the control domains a deployment crosses, the artifact-versus-environment identity distinction, source-control compromise as a control-domain question, and the regulatory controls (FedRAMP/SI-7, SOX 404, FFIEC, DO-178C, FDA 21 CFR Part 11, HIPAA, DoD IL) whose integrity and change-control requirements the model's properties meet more directly than build-on-target does. The parts of the model are each in the literature already: the build-once principle, the build/release/run split, binary authorization, separation as a supply-chain property, NIST SP 800-204D. The paper assembles them as one model, whatever form the built copy takes; states that a secret injected at build time breaks artifact identity; restates why downloading libraries at deployment time is an attack surface that grows with their number; and derives that the releasing role needs no production password. Part II is an anonymized case study of a production web application on AWS in which a release is one command and the artifact's path from upload to running fleet is autonomous. It reports 31 production rollouts timed stage by stage from the platform's records (36 s per server inside a six-minute rollout), 22 releases in the first four weeks, the application's service levels, a control table stating which controls the implementation has and which it lacks (no signature, no scan, no approval record, no verification at deploy), and the two release units the pipeline did not cover. A closing table checks each claim of Part I against that record.

</details>


### [6] [UNBIND: UNlearning By INference-time Directional Steering for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35913)
*Zhengyang Shan,Jiayun Xin,Yanjun Lin,Xu Qian,Zhiang Liu,Minghui Xu,Yue Zhang,Qin Hu,Kun Li,Xiuzhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UNBIND selectively suppresses memorized target code in code LLMs at inference time by manipulating hidden-state directions, achieving high forgetting rates with small impact on programming utility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large code LLMs can memorize and reproduce copyrighted or sensitive implementations; there is a need to remove specific implementations while retaining general programming ability, which is challenging because target and retained code share computational patterns.

Method: Construct separate directional components in hidden-state space: one identifying hidden-state signatures of target code and another that suppresses reproduction; apply these directions at inference time without modifying model weights to selectively reduce target code generation.

Result: Outperforms fourteen baselines on two code models and two corpora; reduces target reproduction by 97.3%–99.1% (F-BLEU); at most 2 fewer HumanEval+ and 6 fewer MBPP+ problems solved; near elimination of long exact-span extractions under repeated tests; mean best recovery ratio 0.43%–6.45%; effective across multilingual and related-code evaluations.

Conclusion: UNBIND enables selective, inference-time unlearning of targeted code from pre-trained code LLMs by identifying and suppressing hidden-state directions associated with the target, achieving strong forgetting with minimal utility loss.

Abstract: Code large language models acquire programming capabilities from large code corpora, but can also memorize implementations that later require removal. Code unlearning is needed to control their continued reproduction when copyright or security concerns arise. However, targeted and retained code share computational patterns, creating a tension between forgetting specific implementations and preserving general programming ability. We propose \textbf{UNBIND}, a code unlearning framework that separately considers which hidden states correspond to the target code and how to suppress its reproduction. By constructing separate directions for these objectives, UNBIND achieves selective unlearning at inference time while keeping model weights fixed. Our evaluation covers fourteen baselines across two code models and two corpora. UNBIND achieves the highest joint forgetting and utility score in every setting. It reduces target code reproduction by 97.3\% to 99.1\% as measured by F-BLEU, with at most two fewer HumanEval+ and six fewer MBPP+ problems solved than the original models. In repeated extraction tests under a fixed budget, the number of targets yielding exact spans of at least 50 tokens falls from 188--262 to 0--2 out of 300 per setting. No extracted span reaches 100 tokens, and the mean best recovery ratio ranges from 0.43\% to 6.45\%. Multilingual and related-code evaluations further show effective forgetting with limited impact on useful programming capabilities, supporting UNBIND as a practical approach to selective code unlearning.

</details>


### [7] [Evaluating Name-Only Directory Routing for One-Shot Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35918)
*Manoj Bajaj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于目录/文件名的路由能明显补充词法检索以提高文件召回与注释覆盖，但代价是更多模型调用和延迟；证据不足以表明收益来自目录层级本身或会提高实际问题修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位相关源文件是自动化编码代理的早期困难，固定词法查询容易漏掉相关文件；研究旨在检验是否能仅靠目录/文件名线索让语言模型补充或超越传统词法检索。

Method: 让语言模型仅根据目录与文件名对仓库进行路由（name-only directory routing），并将其与FTS5全文检索与固定的完整issue rg查询在82个审计问题（来自11个仓库、在修复前的提交）上比较。评估指标包括前8个候选中文件的金标准召回率、在16K-token上下文预算下的注释行覆盖率；使用仓库簇自助（bootstrap）估计置信区间；还测试了将路由与FTS5结合以及一个平坦路径控制的表现和成本（模型调用次数与延迟）。

Result: 在82个问题上，name-only路由在前8个候选中召回0.465的金文件，FTS5为0.352，固定rg为0.245；路由对FTS5的配对增益为0.113（95%仓库簇自助区间0.053–0.168）。在55个注释完全对齐的案例、共享16K-token上下文预算下，路由覆盖0.443注释行 vs FTS5的0.246。将路由与FTS5结合，在8文件限额下达0.491召回，但相对路由的增益不确定。探索性平坦路径控制达到0.572召回但使用24.6次模型调用/问题（路由为8.9次）。时间成本：路由平均8.9秒/问题；FTS5查询为7毫秒/次（构建0.9秒）。总体结论是路由能为一次性词法搜索补充相关候选，但不能断言层级信息是原因，也未证明能改善问题解决。

Conclusion: 目录路由（仅名称）在有限候选数量下显著提高了找到目标代码文件的召回率，但无法证明改进源于目录层级或能促进实际问题的最终解决。

Abstract: Finding the right files is an early challenge for coding agents. We test whether a language model can follow directory and file names to find annotated code files missed by fixed lexical queries. Across 82 audited issues from 11 repositories at pinned pre-fix commits, name-only directory routing recovered 0.465 of gold files within eight candidates, compared with 0.352 for FTS5 and 0.245 for a fixed full-issue rg query. The paired gain over FTS5 was 0.113 (95% repository-cluster bootstrap interval, 0.053 to 0.168). Under a shared 16K-token context budget, routing delivered 0.443 of annotated lines versus 0.246 for FTS5 on 55 cases with fully aligned annotations. At the same eight-file limit, combining routing with FTS5 reached 0.491 file recall, but its gain over routing alone was uncertain. An exploratory flat path control reached 0.572 recall while using 24.6 model calls per issue, compared with 8.9 for routing. Routing averaged 8.9 seconds per issue; FTS5 took 7 milliseconds per query after a 0.9-second build. On this cohort, directory routing added relevant file candidates to one-shot lexical search, but the study cannot attribute the gain to hierarchy or show that it improves issue resolution.

</details>


### [8] [The Uneven Decline of Collective Knowledge Production: Evidence from Stack Overflow After Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36069)
*Myokyung Han,Taegyoon Kim,Jinhyuk Yun,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Study treats ChatGPT-3.5 as a natural shock to Stack Overflow (2020–2025); finds Gen AI substitutes away easy, data-rich Q&A, leaving harder and rarer knowledge relatively preserved; effects validated by code-complexity measures and cross-language checks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how Gen AI affects the composition of collective knowledge produced by online communities, beyond aggregate participation declines—specifically which kinds of knowledge are lost first.

Method: Natural experiment using ChatGPT-3.5 release as shock; analysis of >2M Stack Overflow questions (2020–2025); operationalizing two dimensions (difficulty and data availability); longitudinal tracking of tag/topic shares; supporting analyses including code complexity measures and robustness checks across languages and methods.

Result: Easy questions and those in data-rich topics decline sharply after Gen AI release; difficult questions increase in share and code complexity rises; data-scarce topics gain share; interactions show decline in easy questions concentrated in data-rich domains; patterns stronger in more prevalent programming languages.

Conclusion: Generative AI's release (ChatGPT-3.5) correlates with an uneven erosion of collective knowledge on Stack Overflow: easy, data-rich questions decline while difficult and data-scarce questions rise, with consistent patterns across languages and corroborating evidence (increased code complexity).

Abstract: Generative AI (Gen AI) is reshaping how individuals learn and work, but its consequences for collective knowledge, the shared body of knowledge that online communities produce together, remain poorly understood. Prior work has documented an aggregate decline in participation on knowledge-sharing platforms, but it remains unclear which specific kinds of knowledge are being lost first. We study this question using Stack Overflow, one of the largest online communities for software engineering, treating the release of ChatGPT-3.5 as a natural shock. Analyzing over two million questions posted between 2020 and 2025, we track how two dimensions of collective knowledge, difficulty and data availability, change following Gen AI's release. Using diverse methods and robust checks, we find consistent patterns. Easy questions decline sharply while difficult questions become more common, a pattern corroborated by rising code complexity. Data-rich topics and tags lose share of questions, while data-scarce ones gain ground. The two dimensions also interact: the decline in easy questions is concentrated specifically within data-rich domains, while difficult questions increase regardless of data availability. This pattern extends beyond Python across programming languages, with more prevalent languages showing sharper shifts. Together, our findings reveal that Gen AI's impact on collective knowledge is uneven, eroding easy, accessible knowledge first while more complex, less common knowledge persists.

</details>


### [9] [The Invisible Scheduler: Dragging a Sprite Can Change What a Scratch Program Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36147)
*Xinyue Feng,Hanyuan Shi,Yuan Si*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StackSwap 系统枚举并执行不同精灵堆叠顺序来检测 Scratch 程序因调度导致的行为差异，能定位导致差异的相邻交换并给出原因线索；在现实数据中该问题并不少见，并提出对学习者、评分者与平台的改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scratch 项目的并发语义由精灵堆叠顺序决定，但该顺序对作者、教师或评判者可能不同，导致作业在不同环境下表现不一致，影响教学与评测的可重复性与可靠性。

Method: 提出 StackSwap：对固定项目文件，枚举和运行精灵初始堆叠顺序的置换（对最多五个起始脚本的精灵全枚举；对更多精灵按冲突图分类抽样），在四种观察角度下比较执行，将差异归结为相邻精灵交换并报告共享资源作为可能原因；输出两次运行的见证、稳健性证明或未决。

Result: 在三组真实样本（课程、在线评测、公开随机）上的实证：具有调度选择的程序中分别有 26.4%（课程）和 18.2%（公开）在某些堆叠顺序下表现不同；已保存顺序的结果在所有序列中重复出现的中位频率为 17%（课程）和 50%（公开）；在脚本化测试下 5/427 提交出现需求谓词在不同顺序下结果不同。还发现随机流是半数敏感程序中竞态对共享的最高优先资源。

Conclusion: 程序在 Scratch 中因精灵的前后叠放顺序导致调度非确定性，StackSwap 能发现并定位因两个相邻精灵交换顺序而改变行为的竞态，或返回全面的稳健性证明或未决结论。

Abstract: Tens of millions of children program in Scratch, whose programs are concurrent: when the green flag is clicked, every sprite's scripts start together and share the project's state. Which script starts first is decided by the sprites' front-to-back stacking order, which no block reads. Dragging a sprite brings it to the front, and the order is saved with the project. Hence, a program can work on the author's screen and fail on the teacher's, with the same blocks. Our key observation is that, with the rest of the file fixed, rearranging the sprites that start scripts among their positions produces exactly the permutations of their initial stacking order. Under a fixed input and seed the unmodified virtual machine reproduces every one of them. StackSwap runs a project under these orders, all of them for up to five sprites, one per class of a conflict graph beyond, and a sample where neither is feasible, and compares the runs under four observation lenses. It returns a two-run witness naming an adjacent pair of sprites whose swap changes the outcome, with the resources they share as candidate causes; otherwise an exhaustive or graph-conditional robustness certificate, or an unresolved verdict. On 767 real programs from a course, an online judge, and a random public sample, 26.4% (course) and 18.2% (public) of those with a scheduling choice behave differently under some stacking order. Among the sensitive programs whose every order ran, the saved order's outcome recurs under a median 17% (course) and 50% (public) of the orders. Under scripted play, a predicate written from a stated requirement holds under one order and fails under another for 5 of 427 student submissions with a scheduling choice. For half of the sensitive programs the random stream is the racing pair's highest-priority shared resource. We close with recommendations for learners, graders, and the Scratch platform.

</details>


### [10] [Live Architecture Models for Cloud-Native Architecture-as-Code: Early Results from Kubernetes Conformance Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36148)
*Denis Mamatin,Andrey Salov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将可编辑的架构模型与运行时事实通过显式对应和周期性一致性检查连接（使用 KDL 与 Archer 原型），在小规模示例上证明了可行性，但通用性、可扩展性与若干模型覆盖限制尚未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时与文档化架构意图之间会发生基础设施漂移（infrastructure drift）；需要一种可编辑的、与运行时事实挂钩并定期做一致性校验的架构模型来检测并暴露漂移，而不必强制变更部署。

Method: 使用 Archer 专用的 Kubernetes Deployment Language (KDL) 子集，并在 VS Code 中实现 Archer 原型，提供文本/图形同步视图；通过快照恢复将选定的 Kubernetes 运行时事实提取到 KDL；周期性与按需的只读一致性检查通过显式对应关系对模型与集群做比对，报告不一致但不执行修复。评估在三个特性选择的示例应用上进行，按作者定义的协议测量快照恢复和选定不一致检测的精确率与召回率；恢复可从保存的地面真值与恢复模型重现；检测基于手工扰动协议。

Result: 实验结果在所评估的 KDL 子集与示例应用上支持该方法的可行性：快照恢复与不一致检测得到可复现的测量结果。但结果没有证明方法在实际生产场景下的普适性或优越性；可扩展性、自动检测任意类型漂移与对开发者价值的实证均未解决；存储对象覆盖和 ingress 主机表示/默认处理是明确限制。

Conclusion: 在所定义的 KDL 范围内，基于“活体架构模型”的方法是可行的：通过 Archer 原型实现的快照恢复和不一致检测能够以可度量的精确率/召回率工作，但本文并不宣称在通用性、可扩展性或开发者收益上有优势，并指出存储覆盖与 ingress-host 表示/默认处理为当前限制。

Abstract: Infrastructure drift can separate a running Kubernetes system from its documented architectural intent. This paper investigates live architecture models: editable architectural representations connected to selected runtime facts through explicit correspondences and recurring conformance checks. The approach is instantiated through an Archer-specific subset of Kubernetes Deployment Language (KDL) and Archer, a VS Code prototype with synchronized textual and graphical views. Snapshot recovery extracts selected Kubernetes facts into KDL; periodic and on-demand read-only checks report model-cluster inconsistencies without enforcing or repairing deployment state.
  We assess feasibility on three feature-selected Kubernetes example applications under an author-defined protocol, reporting precision and recall for snapshot recovery and selected inconsistency detection. Recovery scores can be reproduced from saved ground-truth and recovered models; detection uses a documented manual perturbation protocol. The results support feasibility within the evaluated KDL scope. They do not establish comparative superiority, detection of arbitrary production drift, scalability, or developer benefit. Storage coverage and ingress-host representation/default handling remain limitations.

</details>


### [11] [The Invisible Throttle: Running on Borrowed Time in Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36152)
*Xinyue Feng,Hanyuan Shi,Yuan Si*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Scratch 中循环速率受“重绘闸门”与运行预算控制，很多项目因此对渲染与环境敏感；Thrott leCheck 发现高发生率并建议在平台/评分器中明确并模拟该语义，教孩子：一个循环的速度取决于它是否被迫让出给重绘，而不是简单的“每帧一次”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量儿童使用 Scratch，教育中通常把循环速度简化为“每帧一次”，但 VM 的真实调度规则不同且文档缺失，这导致程序在编辑器、自动评测器或不同机器上不可预测地改变行为、错误评分和教学误导。

Method: 通过观察 VM 中的单一“重绘闸门”标志，设计并实现 ThrottleCheck：在不修改虚拟机的情况下对运行时施加“每帧轮次预算”（重现重绘闸门行为）的语义，针对不同开关（隐藏绘图精灵、静音绘图请求、无渲染运行、改变预算）运行项目并产生“速率敏感”项目的证据。对 500 个流行公开游戏和 Whisker / 教程测试套件进行大规模实验并统计影响。

Result: 在 500 个流行游戏中，59% 含有既不绘制也不等待的循环；隐藏绘图精灵会极大加速其他循环（示例在笔记本上提升 77,000 倍）；在有绘图精灵的游戏中，静音绘图会在 10 秒内改变 24% 的游戏状态，隐藏绘图改变 18%，无渲染且无闸门则改变 61%；在 Whisker 与教程的例子集中，有 17% 与 19% 的断言会在解除节流后由通过变为失败或反之。若评分器无渲染且不模拟闸门，会给编辑器永远不会以该速率运行的程序打分。论文建议平台与评分器模拟闸门并公开预算，并给出可以教给儿童的一句话说明。

Conclusion: Scratch 的实际帧语义（由“重绘闸门”与运行时预算共同决定）使得“一个循环每帧迭代一次”这个教条通常不成立：循环速度可以在不改代码的情况下被渲染是否发生或运行环境的预算所改变，导致程序在不同环境下行为显著不同。平台与评测器应该显式模拟重绘闸门并声明预算，否则会误判与破坏可重复性。

Abstract: Scratch has 135 million registered users, most of them children, and 164 million shared projects. What they are taught about the speed of a script fits in one sentence: a loop iterates once per frame. Unfortunately, that sentence describes the exception. In the public virtual machine a frame repeats the scripts until something visible asks the screen to redraw, no script is left running, or three quarters of the frame's wall-clock time are spent. Hence a loop that does not draw is paced by whatever else is visible and by the machine. Hide the moving sprite of a two-sprite project, and the other's counting loop runs 77,000 times faster on a laptop. No documentation states the rule. Our key observation is that the redraw gate is one flag for the whole runtime, so a loop's speed can be changed without touching its code: hide the sprite that draws, change the machine's budget, or run the program in a tool with no renderer. ThrottleCheck implements a budgeted semantics (a stated budget of rounds per frame, the redraw gate emulated) on the unmodified virtual machine, runs a project under each knob, and reports a rate-sensitive project with a witness. On 500 popular public games, 59% contain a loop that never draws and never waits. Muting the requests of the sprites that draw changes the state of a played game after ten seconds in 24% of the games that have one; hiding them changes 18%; running the game without a renderer and without the gate changes 61%. Rules that read a position, a score or a clock after a fixed time reverse between pass and fail once the throttle is released, in 17% of Whisker's own example tests and 19% of a tutorial's checks on 224 of its remixes. A grader without a renderer grades a program the editor never runs, unless it emulates the gate and states a budget; we say what graders and the platform should do, and close with the sentence a child could be taught.

</details>


### [12] [From Dead Code and Static Requirements to Working Engines: Software Revival with Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36161)
*Tianyu Liu,Dingyuan Dai,Yufan Du,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReviveBench是一个用于评估编码代理恢复与重建大型软件能力的基准，结果显示高通过率的同时暴露出可执行验证本身的显著测量误差并提出对应缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验现代编码代理是否能在保留原有方法论的前提下恢复失效代码并从规范重构复杂工业级软件，同时评估现有可执行验证方法的可靠性。

Method: 提出并公开ReviveBench：包含两类任务（revival和reconstruction），使用针对原生运行环境、工程工具或参考实现校准的隐藏验证器进行评估；包含污染控制和审计流程来检测验证器错误。

Result: revival家族10项任务中最强模型在至少一次运行中通过全部任务；标识符混淆显著降低源码相似度但不影响通过率；重建家族13项任务中两模型满足通过标准；审计发现28处验证器缺陷（24假阴性、2假阳性），并指出CFD任务无法证明数值求解器能力。提出三项实用核查建议来降低验证误差。

Conclusion: 论文表明，通过精心设计的基准（ReviveBench）可以测评编码代理在恢复不可运行软件与根据开放规范重建工业软件引擎方面的能力，但同时揭示了以可执行验证为中心的测量管线会引入大量误差（验证器缺陷）。

Abstract: Can coding agents restore software that no longer runs while preserving its underlying methods, and reconstruct industrial software engines from open specifications? Here we introduce ReviveBench, a benchmark with two task families evaluated by hidden verifiers calibrated against native execution environments, established engineering tools, or purpose-built reference implementations. The revival family comprises ten tasks involving dependency incompatibilities, deleted core modules, legacy builds, and a GPU-based foundation model. Every starting workspace fails verification, and the strongest evaluated model passes all ten tasks in at least one run each. In contamination-control experiments, identifier obfuscation reduces line similarity to the original implementations from 0.51--0.96 to 0.03--0.44 without reducing the observed pass rate of any evaluated model. On repositories created after the stated knowledge cutoffs, the strongest model passes eight of nine runs. The reconstruction family comprises thirteen tasks spanning numerical, geometric, hardware, and transactional systems (e.g. CAD and CRM). Two models meet the benchmark's pass criteria on all thirteen, although our audit shows that the CFD task cannot establish numerical-solver capability. Benchmark construction and auditing uncover 28 verifier defects, including 24 false negatives and two false positives. These findings show that executable verification can itself introduce substantial measurement error. We present three practical checks: test whether prescribed methods can reach the grading thresholds, investigate agreement among independently generated candidates, and recompute diagnostics from submitted artifacts. ReviveBench thus provides both an evaluation of software revival and engine reconstruction, and cases in validating the verifiers used to measure coding agents for software design.

</details>


### [13] [Assay: Claims That Decay With the Code. Content-Addressed Evidence Graphs for Accountable AI-Assisted Software Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36170)
*Om Shankar Tiwari,Tangi Vass,Gagan Deep Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Assay cryptographically ties agent claims to dependency cones (Merkle hashes), enabling exact staleness tracking, minimal re-verification, and a model-free merge gate with evidence checks; implemented as a dependency-free Python tool and MCP server, it yields large efficiency gains and robust defense against adversarial agent behaviours in experiments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI coding agents often waste context rediscovering code locations and overclaim success; need for mechanisms that provide cheap context and accountable verification so that claims are precise, attributable, and automatically invalidated when dependencies change.

Method: Define claims tied to Merkle hashes of dependency cones; prove soundness and minimality of binding; implement Assay (Python + MCP server); evaluate with experiments on five public repositories measuring brief costs, rebuild times, re-verification rates, and adversarial resistance.

Result: Assay reduces exploration cost by 14x–114x for 600-token briefs, speeds warm rebuilds up to 5x, ensures cone binding re-verifies the correct subset of claims (7.9%–81.9%) versus other binding granularities, and enforces a merge gate that blocks adversarial behaviours while allowing honest ones; all results reproducible via released scripts.

Conclusion: Assay provides a principled, cryptographic approach to binding agent claims about code to the exact dependency cone they cover, enabling precise staleness detection and minimal re-verification; combined with risk-proportional review and a model-free merge gate, it improves efficiency and robustness in automated code curation.

Abstract: AI coding agents fail in two coupled ways. They spend most of their context window rediscovering where things live, and they assert success without evidence when the work gets hard. Repository indexes address the first with cheap context, and orchestration frameworks with adversarial review address the second with accountability. Both describe the same object, the structure of the codebase, at two timescales: what is true of the code now, and what was verified to be true, at which revision, by whom. Assay makes that observation operational. Every claim an agent makes (tests pass, no secrets, behavior preserved) is bound to the Merkle hash of the dependency cone of the code it covers, so the claim is stale exactly when that code or anything it depends on changes. We show the binding is sound and minimal, and that the blast radius of a change is precisely the set of claims it invalidates. On the graph we place a risk-proportional evidence obligation, a bounded review protocol with separation of duties, and a merge gate that consults no model: coverage, freshness, signatures, exit codes, plausibility, evidence monotonicity (the mechanical form of "do not delete the failing test"), and review status. Assay is a dependency-free Python tool with an MCP server. On five public repositories a 600-token brief costs 14x to 114x less than an exploration proxy, warm rebuilds are up to 5x faster than cold ones, cone binding re-verifies 7.9% to 81.9% of claims where repository binding re-verifies all of them while per-module binding misses 23% to 68% of required invalidations, and the gate blocks 9 of 9 scripted adversarial behaviours while admitting the honest ones. Every number in this paper is generated by the released scripts.

</details>


### [14] [How Much Prompt Is Enough? A Blackbox Minimization of Few-Shots in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36289)
*Ali Alfageeh,Rahul Gopinath,Amin Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 黑盒最小化框架能显著压缩 few-shot 提示并揭示提示中哪些成分对输出是必需的，同时揭示模型间在提示表达/解读上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的提示构成与因果层级不明确，细微提示变化会悄然改变关键系统输出，工程师缺乏可靠性和鲁棒性分析工具。

Method: 在 few-shot 学习系统上应用黑盒逐步最小化方法（无需访问模型内部），通过删去或保留示例片段并测量命题输出保真度来判断必要性；进行了跨模型对比以观察保留元素与模型间的通用性。

Result: 在实验中 few-shot 示例在字符数上平均被压缩 65.3% ± 15.8% 同时保持命题输出不变。模型倾向保留逻辑标识符和约束声明，舍弃自然语言叙述和跨提示关系注释；并发现有的模型更善于生成最小化提示（通用编码器），有的则更善于解读他模型的最小化提示（通用解码器）。

Conclusion: 提出了一个黑盒的提示最小化框架（\framework），能将 few-shot 提示压缩为必要的最小子集，保持输出语义一致并提供关于提示结构的可解释性见解。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for directing the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Yet the internal structure and causal hierarchy of prompts remain poorly understood: which parts are causally necessary and which are redundant is an open question. This opacity can have severe consequences. Subtle prompt variations can silently shift model outputs in critical software systems, and engineers lack techniques to reason about prompt reliability.
  We present \framework, a blackbox prompt-minimization framework that reduces few-shot prompts to their necessary minimal subset. We use a case study to apply \framework to a few-shot learning system and demonstrate the insights that this framework can provide.
  Our experiments show that few-shot exemplars can be reduced by a mean of 65.3\%~$\pm$~15.8\% in character count while fully preserving propositional output fidelity. The models preferentially retain logical identifiers and constraint declarations while discarding natural language prose and cross-prompt relational annotations.
  Our analysis also shows that some models are universal encoders, able to produce highly legible yet minimized prompts, while others are universal decoders, able to interpret minimized prompts from most other models.
  By identifying which components are indispensable, \framework provides a principled basis for prompt compression and structural analysis of few-shot exemplars.

</details>


### [15] [Strategies for Deploying AI Agents in Production at Scientific User Facilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36362)
*Ming Du,Xiangyu Yin,Michael Prince,Yi Jiang,Rajat Sainju,Tekin Bicer,Yanqi Luo,Eric Codrea,Peco Myint,Nina Andrejevic,Juanjuan Huang,Trupti Mohanty,Pawan Tripathi,Dishant Beniwal,Hemant Sharma,Doga Gursoy,Aileen Luo,Tao Zhou,Chenran Xu,Jan Ilavsky,Matthew T. Dearing,Ryan Chard,Hoon Seo,Dariusz Jarosz,Elaine Chandler,Thomas Fors,Hairong Shang,Pete Jemian,Xuli Wu,Madeline Miller,Ryan Aydelott,Nicholas Schwarz,Francesco de Carlo,Byeongdu Lee,Yine Sun,Moises Smart,Philippe Piot,Joe Sullivan,Mathew J. Cherukara,Alec Sandy,Stefan Vogt,Jonathan Lang,Laurent C. Chapon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM-driven agents can automate routine scientific facility tasks; paper provides practical, reusable architecture and governance recommendations for production deployments, with operational guidance and caveat that guidance is time-bound.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI is transitioning from demos to production at scientific facilities. There is need to codify practical, reusable strategies to safely and effectively harness LLM agents for routine scientific tasks beyond mere throughput gains.

Method: Analyzing deployments of LLM-driven agents at the APS, distilling practical strategies and design principles independent of specific implementations. The approach emphasizes modular architectures (agent harnesses), integration points (beamline control, facility knowledge retrieval, data analysis), and operational considerations (network/Linux ops, governed shared memory, deterministic orchestration).

Result: A set of reusable, implementation-agnostic recommendations and architectural patterns for integrating agentic AI into scientific facilities, with emphasis on governance, lifecycle learning, and operational infrastructure; recognized as a snapshot due to quickly evolving LLM capabilities.

Conclusion: Deploying LLM-driven agentic AI at scientific user facilities can automate repeatable tasks (calibration, measurement, QC, initial analyses), enabling scientists to focus on higher-level scientific work. Design principles and architecture patterns (inference endpoints, tool-server architectures, handling non-text data, heavy compute services, reusable skills, governed learning) are generalizable across instruments and facilities. Network, OS-level, shared memory, and deterministic orchestration techniques further extend these patterns. Recommendations are time-sensitive due to evolving LLM capabilities.

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) is moving beyond research demonstrations toward production use at scientific user facilities, including light sources, neutron sources, nanoscience centers, and autonomous laboratories. Its scientific value extends beyond increasing throughput. Agents can perform repeatable tasks in calibration, measurement execution, and quality control, as well as initial analyses that turn data into reviewable evidence, allowing scientists to focus on hypotheses, unexpected observations, and interpretation. Drawing on deployments of LLM-driven agents at the APS, this perspective distills practical strategies with an emphasis on elements that can be reused across instruments and facilities. We discuss agent harnesses for beamline control, facility knowledge retrieval, and data analysis while keeping the underlying design principles independent of any specific implementation. These principles cover inference endpoints, tool-server architectures, non-text data, computationally intensive services, reusable skills, and governed learning throughout an instrument's lifecycle. We also consider how network and Linux operations, governed shared memory, and deterministic orchestration can extend these patterns across facility services. Because LLM capabilities continue to evolve, these recommendations represent a snapshot of the technology as of the date on the cover.

</details>


### [16] [LatentSift: Policy-State Filtering for Token-Efficient Verification of Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36371)
*Yuning Han,Yangchenchen Jin,Tyler Jandreau,Jingwei Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 用策略隐藏态替代传统EF验证器可在不额外占用token的情况下高效筛选候选，显著减少验证开销并维持或提升最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合工作流先用基于LLM的执行无关验证器过一遍每条候选轨迹以筛选，然而这本身就需要对每条轨迹再做一次模型前向，造成与生成token相当的验证开销。目标是用策略已存在的内部表示实现“零token”的快速筛选。

Method: 在候选轨迹生成过程中提取策略的隐藏状态（推理、观测和函数调用三类）；用训练期间从成功与失败轨迹中收集的正负状态库对每个候选的隐藏态计算距离得分；将这些距离得分通过一个学习得到的线性权重融合，按得分保留有潜力的候选进入后续执行型验证阶段（无额外token开销）。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，对三种代理和两种策略规模、K=16时，LatentSift将EF-验证器的token消耗减少66.6%--81.0%，将包括测试生成在内的总验证token减少49.1%--62.1%；同时以混合Best@16工作流与基线持平或略有提升，例如DeepSWE-Preview从59.26%提升到60.06%。

Conclusion: LatentSift有效用策略在生成时产生的隐藏态来替代第一阶段的执行无关（EF）验证器，从而显著减少了验证所需的token消耗并在检索候选轨迹的同时保持或提升最终性能。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves software engineering agents by generating multiple candidate trajectories and selecting the best one. Verifying and selecting among these long interactions can consume as many tokens as generation itself. Existing hybrid workflows first apply an LLM-based execution-free (EF) verifier to filter candidates before running tests, which adds another model pass over every trajectory. We introduce LatentSift, a token-free and execution-free filter that replaces this first stage with hidden states the policy already produces while generating the candidates. It represents each candidate through its reasoning, observation, and function-call states, compares them with positive and negative banks of such states collected from successful and unsuccessful trajectories during policy training, and fuses the resulting distance scores with a learned linear score to retain promising candidates for the execution-based stages. On SWE-bench Verified, across three agents and two policy sizes, LatentSift cuts EF-verifier tokens by 66.6--81.0% and total verification tokens, which include test generation, by 49.1--62.1% at K=16, while hybrid Best@16 matches or improves on each agent's reference workflow, rising from 59.26% to 60.06% on DeepSWE-Preview.

</details>


### [17] [WitnessGym: Benchmarking Coding Agents on the Construction of Bug Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36635)
*Haomin Qi,Xiangzhe Xu,Yiming Huang,Jingbo Shang,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WitnessGym: an automated, extensible bug-injection framework that builds realistic, executable bug-validation benchmarks (≈1,300 Java cases) and shows current coding agents struggle to construct witnesses even when patterns are known.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benchmarking bug validation is hard: public historical bugs/witnesses are often reused and manual construction is costly and unscalable. The paper aims to automatically produce realistic, diverse, and executable benchmark cases to fairly evaluate coding agents on bug validation tasks.

Method: Witness injection into test-reached paths of real-world Java projects; rebuild projects and retain cases where the injected bug is exposed by a generated witness (input + harness). Extensible via bug specifications and execution adapters; bug-preserving transformations vary surrounding code while preserving witness behavior. Empirical evaluation of four coding-agent frameworks across six framework/model pairings.

Result: Automatically constructed ~1,300 benchmark cases from real Java projects, injected patches resemble historical bug fixes and are hard for evaluated models to detect or reproduce. Evaluations across multiple models/frameworks and transformation depths show witness construction remains challenging, demonstrating the benchmark’s difficulty and utility. Artifacts and evaluation scripts are released.

Conclusion: The paper introduces WitnessGym, an automated framework that constructs bug-validation benchmarks by injecting bugs into test-reached paths of real projects, rebuilding them, and keeping cases that a construction-time witness exposes. The resulting benchmark (≈1,300 Java cases) is realistic, diverse, and challenging for current coding agents; witness construction remains difficult even when patterns are known.

Abstract: Bug validation asks a coding agent to produce an executable witness for a reported bug. The witness combines a concrete input with a testing harness and exposes faulty behavior during execution. Such evidence makes audit findings actionable, yet benchmark evaluation is difficult when cases reuse public historical bugs and witnesses or require manual construction. We present WitnessGym, an automated framework for constructing bug-validation benchmarks through bug injection. It injects bugs into test-reached paths of real projects, rebuilds each project, and retains cases exposed by a construction-time witness. Bug specifications and execution adapters allow extension to additional bug types and languages. Bug-preserving transformations vary the surrounding structure while preserving the witness behavior. Based on real-world Java projects with test suites, WitnessGym automatically constructs 1,300 benchmark cases. The injected patches resemble historical bug patches and are difficult for the two evaluated models to distinguish in blinded comparisons. We evaluate four coding agent frameworks in six framework/model pairings across bug types, execution contexts, and transformation depths. Witness construction remains difficult even when the bug pattern is known. Our framework, benchmark cases, and evaluation scripts are available.

</details>


### [18] [CTE-Bench: Counterfactual Trace Evaluation for Stateful Software Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36647)
*Xinran Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CTE-Bench衡量模型预测对有状态服务的代码/状态干预如何影响未来响应的能力。实验显示模型在有真实反馈时表现有限、在无或自我反馈时大幅退化，端到端完全正确极为罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么忽略持久化服务状态、要么把评估集中在代理选择动作或最终状态；需要一个基准直接测量模型是否能预测对持续性服务的代码或状态修改如何改变未来行为，而不是评估其行动选择能力。

Method: 提出CTE-Bench：每个场景包含Python服务源码、干预前的若干调用与响应、一次干预（源码编辑或状态覆盖）以及40个固定的未来调用；模型需预测每个未来响应，答案通过执行服务验证。设计三种记忆协议（可见真实响应/不可见/可见自预测）控制模型能否看到早期真实反馈。核心集合为255场景、6个确定性Python服务、共10200次预测，主指标为effect-step VM（干预确实改变的2476次调用上的精确响应匹配率）。

Result: 在揭示真实早期响应时，四个API模型的effect-step VM在54.3%–61.5%间；隐藏真实响应时降到23.2%–28.9%；以自生成预测为条件时为24.8%–33.2%；至多1.2%的场景可端到端完全正确。结论是当前模型主要依赖外部正确反馈，且错误会随着时间滚动累积。

Conclusion: 模型只有在被提供正确的早期响应作为反馈时，才能较好地预测对有状态服务的干预对后续行为的影响；当这些响应被隐藏或替换为模型自生成的预测时，性能显著下降，且端到端完全正确的场景极少，说明错误会在展开过程中复合。

Abstract: Coding agents change running software: they patch a service's code or overwrite its stored state, and then act on their own expectation of how the service will respond afterwards. A wrong expectation may surface only several calls later. Function-level code-execution benchmarks omit persistent service state, and agent benchmarks score the actions an agent takes or the final state it reaches. We introduce CTE-Bench, which measures whether a model can predict how an intervention changes a stateful service's future behavior, without asking it to choose actions. Each scenario gives the model Python service code, the calls and responses observed before the intervention, the intervention itself (a source edit or a state overwrite), and 40 fixed future calls; the model predicts every future response, and predictions are checked by executing the service. Three memory protocols control whether the model sees the correct earlier responses, none of them, or its own earlier predictions. CTE-Bench-Core-v1 contains 255 scenarios over six deterministic Python services, giving 10,200 predictions per model. The main score is effect-step value match (VM): exact response equality on the 2,476 future calls whose response the intervention changes. With correct earlier responses revealed, four API-hosted models (DeepSeek V4-Flash, Kimi K2.5, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6) reach 54.3%-61.5% effect-step VM. Hiding those responses lowers effect-step VM to 23.2%-28.9%; conditioning on self-generated predictions gives 24.8%-33.2%, and at most 1.2% of scenarios are predicted exactly end to end. Current models thus track intervention effects mainly when correct feedback is supplied, and their errors compound over a rollout. We release CTE-Bench-Core-v1 with its executable oracle, evaluation scripts, and an evaluation card mapping each claim to its protocol.

</details>


### [19] [The Editor Has Read-Only Access: Correctness Signals in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36783)
*Angad Miglani,Samrath Singh Chadha,Kevin Li,Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 扩散模型的激活中含有可线性读取的正确性信号，但直接用线性探针的方向去干预生成并不能稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证扩散语言模型的内部激活是否编码代码正确性信息，以及是否能利用此信息来改进或引导代码生成。

Method: 在六个扩散语言模型上训练线性探针以区分通过/失败的代码生成尝试；在不同层级读取激活并比较强度；使用语义微扰作为对照以排除仅风格相关的判别；将探针估计的方向注入残差流以尝试操控生成；与模型自信度比较，并提供补充的实验细节、代码与结果归档。

Result: 线性探针能在多数模型的中后层区分通过与未通过样例；对照实验支持判别器检出的信息与正确性相关而非仅表面风格；探针点估计相较于模型自信度没有一致优势；将探针方向注入残差流未带来可靠改进，反向注入会降低性能；并强调这些结论与统计显著性或一般性可操控性主张的区别，提供方法与可复现性限制说明。

Conclusion: 内部表征确实包含关于代码正确性的信号，但该信号未必可被简单线性读取和可靠用于改善生成。注入基于线性探针的方向在所测设定下没有稳定提升，反方向会降低性能。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate code by repeatedly updating a partially masked sequence. We ask whether their internal activations encode code correctness and whether that information can improve generation. Across six diffusion models, linear probes distinguish passing from failing attempts, with the strongest reads generally appearing beyond the early layers. Controls using small semantic mutations support a connection to correctness rather than surface style alone. In comparisons with model confidence, probe point estimates offer no consistent advantage. Adding a probe-derived direction to the residual stream does not yield a dependable improvement in the tested steering settings, while the opposite direction degrades performance. We distinguish these observations from claims about statistical significance or a general inability to steer. Supplementary methods, archived results, and code document the tested interventions and the limits of their statistical calibration and reproducibility.

</details>


### [20] [XRepoSkill: Learning Transferable Skills for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36807)
*Yaoqi Guo,Haoyang Zhou,Jiayi Zhang,Yiran Zhang,Yang Liu,Qiuyuan Chen,Qiang Lin,Hande Dong,Jie M. Zhang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XRepoSkill通过对比成功/失败轨迹、用可执行谓词验证并跨仓库合并规则，学习到更可迁移的技能，从而在多种基准与大模型上稳健提升问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从经验中蒸馏的技能常难以跨仓库迁移，因成功轨迹中的行为不一定是真因（可能是偶然或模型习惯）；需要自动识别并验证真正可迁移的行为。

Method: 基于轨迹对比，找出成功与失败轨迹分歧位置，生成候选规则（动作与触发时机），为每条规则配备可执行谓词以在其它轨迹上系统评估；仅保留与成功强相关且在多仓库中复现的规则，并将仓库特定变体合并为可迁移规则；在新问题上选择相关规则引导代理。

Result: 在公开SWE-bench Verified轨迹上学习技能并在不重叠的评测仓库（SWE-bench Pro与DeepSWE）与三款不同供应商的大模型背书下测试，XRepoSkill在所有六个基准—LLM组合中取得最高的问题解决率；在DeepSWE上相较于无技能代理提升10.3个百分点，相较最强技能学习基线提升5.0个百分点。

Conclusion: XRepoSkill能从对比成功与失败的轨迹中提取并验证可跨仓库迁移的规则化技能，从而显著提升仓库级问题解决率，尤其在长航程任务上优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Software engineering agents increasingly use reusable skills distilled from prior experience to resolve repository-level issues, yet such skills often fail to transfer across repositories. A central challenge is that a behavior appearing in a successful trajectory is not necessarily responsible for the successful outcome: it may be genuinely useful, merely incidental, or simply a recurring habit of the model. We introduce XRepoSkill, a trajectory-based approach for learning transferable skills. We represent a skill as a collection of rules, each specifying what action to take and when to take it during issue resolution. XRepoSkill first contrasts successful and failed trajectories of the same agent on the same issue and derives candidate rules from where their execution paths diverge. Each rule is paired with an executable predicate that enables its prescribed behavior to be evaluated systematically on other trajectories. A rule is verified based on its association with successful issue resolution and retained only when its prescribed behavior recurs across multiple repositories; repository-specific variants of the same behavior are then consolidated into transferable rules. For a new issue, XRepoSkill selects relevant rules to guide the agent. We learn skills from publicly released trajectories on the official SWE-bench Verified leaderboard and evaluate them on SWE-bench Pro and DeepSWE using three backbone LLMs from different vendors; none of the evaluation repositories appears in the skill-learning trajectory pool. Against three recent skill learning methods, XRepoSkill achieves the highest issue resolution rate in all six benchmark--LLM combinations. In particular, on the challenging long-horizon DeepSWE benchmark, XRepoSkill improves issue resolution by 10.3 percentage points over the same agent without learned skills and by 5.0 points over the strongest skill-learning baseline.

</details>


### [21] [Cross-Organizational SysML Model Integration: A Survey of Challenges and AI-Supported Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37000)
*Zirui Li,Torsten Brix,Stephan Husung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 问卷显示：SysML跨组织模型集成是多维对齐问题，AI可在分析性任务上提供高价值，但必须保持人为主导与强制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SysML为跨组织MBSE协作提供承诺，但实践中模型交换与集成仍存在持续困难；同时大规模语言模型（LLM）带来AI辅助理解与集成的期望，但其可靠性与人工监督需求仍具挑战性。

Method: 对29名参与跨组织协作的MBSE利益相关者开展线上问卷调查，受访者对8类预定义的集成挑战和6类AI支持任务使用五点李克特量表进行评分，并按组织角色与参与频率分析差异。

Result: 受访者将模型集成理解为多维度对齐问题；不同组织角色与集成参与频次导致感知差异；AI被认为对语义结构分析与不一致性检测等分析性任务最有帮助；多数受访者偏好“人机协同、强制验证”的使用模式。

Conclusion: 利益相关者将模型集成视为跨语义、行为、可追溯性和交换互操作性等维度的对齐问题，偏好将AI作为增强工具而非替代者，要求人机协同并强制验证以保障工程责任。

Abstract: Cross-organizational collaboration is widely regarded as a key promise of SysML-based Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE), yet practitioners still face persistent challenges when exchanging and integrating system models. In parallel, Large Language Models (LLMs) raise expectations for AI-assisted model understanding and integration, while reliability and required human oversight continue to pose challenges. This paper reports the results of an online questionnaire survey with 29 MBSE stakeholders involved in cross-organizational collaboration. Respondents rated eight predefined integration challenge categories and six AI-supported task types on five-point Likert scales. The results indicate that stakeholders perceive model integration as a multi-dimensional alignment problem across semantics, behavior, traceability, and exchange interoperability. These perceptions vary by organizational role and frequency of integration involvement. AI is rated highly useful for analysis tasks such as semantic structure analysis and inconsistency detection, and respondents predominantly prefer human-in-the-loop use with mandatory verification. These findings motivate AI support that enhances, rather than replaces, engineering responsibility in SysML-based integration.

</details>


### [22] [GitCF: Reducing Incomplete Changes by Exploiting Multiple Similarities Among Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37045)
*Yuto Kitabayashi,Takashi Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GitCF：结合修改集合与提交文本相似性来基于历史变更推荐额外修改位置，在多个检索指标上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于协同变更规则的方法只依赖哪些元素共同被修改，无法覆盖罕见的共同修改对，且忽视提交所携带的文本信息；而仅使用源码或需求文本的技术又没有利用提交历史，也未以开发者当前变更作为输入。为弥补这些不足，需结合修改集合与提交文本并以当前变更为查询来生成推荐。

Method: 比较当前变更与历史变更的相似度，结合两类相似性来源（修改元素集合与提交文本——包括提交信息、代码差异与关联issue描述），对历史变更中出现的修改位置进行推荐。评估在543个不完整变更样本上与三种基于协同变更规则的基线方法比较。

Result: 在17个开源项目的543个不完整变更上，GitCF在五项指标（MAP、Recall@10、Recall@20、Hit@10、Hit@20）上均优于最强基线。其中Hit@10从0.322提升到0.403，平均精度的提升具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: GitCF通过同时利用历史修改的元素集合相似性与提交文本内容相似性，能更准确地推荐开发者当前修改应一并变更的位置，从而降低遗漏修改的风险。

Abstract: Software developers often struggle to identify all locations that their changes affect, and incomplete changes frequently result from these omissions. To mitigate this problem, several approaches have been proposed to recommend additional locations that should be modified together with a developer's current change. A large class of these techniques mines co-change rules from repository revision histories, but because they rely solely on which elements have been modified together, they cannot recommend elements that have rarely co-changed, and they disregard the textual information that accompanies commits. A second class of techniques does exploit textual information, but it derives it from the source code or from a change request rather than from commit history, and it does not use the developer's current change as its input. To address these limitations, we propose GitCF, a change recommendation method that compares a developer's current change with past changes in the revision history and recommends additional change locations by combining two sources of similarity: the set of modified elements and the textual content of commits, including commit messages, code diffs, and linked issue descriptions. We evaluate GitCF on 543 incomplete changes from 17 open-source projects, where an incomplete change denotes a commit in which an omitted modification is supplied in a later commit, comparing it against three co-change-rule-based techniques. GitCF outperforms the strongest baseline on all five metrics (MAP, Recall@10, Recall@20, Hit@10, and Hit@20); in particular, it raises Hit@10 from 0.322 to 0.403, and the improvement in average precision is statistically significant.

</details>


### [23] [LoLBench: Evaluating Coding Agents with Long-Horizon Proposals on Large Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37143)
*Yun Peng,Zihan Wu,Zeyang Zhuang,Xin Zhou,Rui Shu,Xu Han,Chun Yong Chong,Yuan Wang,Jiakun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoLBench是一个面向大型软件的端到端增强实现基准，显示当前编码代理在感知与实现两方面都仍远未成熟，代码定位尤为关键，提供额外的结构化上下文可显著改进表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重于从详尽规范实现代码，但现实模块化开发更常见的是从用户意图与高层设计推导出可执行规范，因此需要同时评估感知（归纳规范）与实现能力。

Method: 提出LoLBench基准：包含100个任务、29个软件系统、5个领域的多语言评估集。每个任务由人工撰写的增强提案（平均约5000字）和参考实现组成；评测涵盖28个编码代理，量化从提案到实现的端到端能力。

Result: 最佳代理仅解决14%的任务，Fail-to-Pass（F2P）通过率为52.7%。失败分析表明不完整的代码定位是主要瓶颈。若给出参考派生的文件树与API规范，可将解决率提高16–22个百分点（2.4–17×），最高达34%。基准已开源于指定地址。

Conclusion: 在大型软件系统的从提案到实现全流程中，感知（从高层意图和设计推导规范）与实现（实际代码修改）仍然是编码代理的主要挑战。仅靠现有模型难以可靠解决模块化开发任务。

Abstract: Modern coding agents can deliver increasingly large repository-level changes, and recent benchmarks reflect this by emphasizing long-horizon tasks with large reference implementations. Many benchmarks evaluate coding agents' implementation capability to produce correct code edits from detailed specifications. However, practical modular development tasks also require the perception capability of grounding user intent and high-level design to derive a specification. We introduce LoLBench to evaluate both capabilities through the entire proposal-to-implementation process on large software systems. It is a multilingual benchmark of 100 tasks across 29 software systems in five domains. Each task provides a human-written enhancement proposal with user intent and high-level design. On average, proposals contain about 5,000 words, software systems contain 2.4 million source lines of code (LoC), and implementation pull requests (PRs) change approximately 5,500 LoC. Across 28 agents we evaluated, the best agent resolves only 14% of tasks and achieves a 52.7% Fail-to-Pass (F2P) pass rate. Failure analysis identifies incomplete code localization as a major bottleneck, while providing reference-derived file trees alongside API specifications improves resolved rates by 16--22 percentage points (2.4--17$\times$), reaching at most 34%. These results show that both perception and implementation remain central challenges for coding agents in practical modular development on large software systems. LoLBench is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/lolbench26/LoLBench.

</details>


### [24] [CRJudgeBench: Can AI Detect Plausible but Invalid Code Reviews?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37216)
*Yue Pan,Jiawei Li,Ziyuan Zhang,Xiangxin Zhao,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CRJudgeBench并设计了仓库有证据检索的代理型判定器Sentinel，通过迭代动作级学习在识别不可信但貌似合理的评审评论上取得了明显改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型能生成看似合理的代码评审评论，但其中包含误导性的技术错误，缺乏评估单条评审评论技术可信性的基准与方法；希望填补对“技术可信性判断”这一任务的评估空白。

Method: 构建CRJudgeBench数据集（1199条实例，含真实PR与专家验证的扰动）；设计Sentinel——一个能主动检索仓库代码证据以验证评论的代理式判定器；以Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct为起点，采用带有特权教师的迭代动作级学习对Sentinel进行微调，并在测试集上评估性能。

Result: 在CRJudgeBench测试集（359例）上，Sentinel取得76.60%的准确率，较GLM-5.3高出6.13个百分点，较其基线模型提升19.78个百分点；表明迭代动作级学习与仓库证据检索能显著提升判定性能。

Conclusion: 文章提出了用于判断代码评审评论技术可信性的基准与方法，证明了现有通用大模型在识别“貌似合理但技术上不可信”的评论时存在明显不足，且基于仓库证据的“主动式”判定器并通过迭代动作级学习可显著提升准确率。

Abstract: Large language models can generate plausible code-review comments, but such comments may contain technically incorrect claims that mislead developers. We study technical trustworthiness judgment: determining whether a review comment's core technical claims are correct and applicable to the reviewed code in its repository context. Existing code-review benchmarks primarily evaluate review generation, issue discovery, or general comment quality, but do not directly assess whether an agent can determine the technical trustworthy of an individual review comment. To fill this gap, we introduce CRJudgeBench, a benchmark of 1199 instances constructed from real pull requests and expert-verified perturbations, covering both trustworthy and plausible but untrustworthy comments. We further present Sentinel, a repository-grounded agentic judge that actively gathers code evidence to verify review comments before making judgments. Starting from Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct, Sentinel is trained on the CRJudgeBench training split through iterative action-level learning from a privileged teacher. On the 359-instance CRJudgeBench test set, Sentinel achieves 76.60\% accuracy, outperforming GLM-5.3 by 6.13 percentage points and its base model by 19.78 points. These results show that even state-of-the-art general-purpose LLMs struggle to identify untrustworthy comments, while iterative action-level learning substantially improves the accuracy of repository-grounded trustworthiness judgments. Our dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/dcloud347/CRJudgeBenchmark

</details>


### [25] [SafeLLM4SE: Statistical Evaluation and Reporting for LLM-based Software Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37294)
*Francisco Ortin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SafeLLM4SE 用统计严谨的方法（自适应抽样、置信区间、分布感知比较、效应量）来评估基于 LLM 的软件工程任务，提升结果的透明性与可复现性，且提供开源实现和示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在软件工程任务中表现出随机性，常规的单次输出、平均分或 best-of-N、pass@k 等做法容易掩盖模型输出的变异性与估计不确定性，从而影响评估的有效性、可重复性与可比较性。

Method: 结合自适应抽样（带停止准则）以保证样本量满足置信区间精度要求；使用适合分布特性的统计检验（例如非参数或置换检验）与效应量指标；对指标构造分布感知的比较方法；并定义最小报告项（模型配置、可重复性细节、评估程序与资源消耗）。实现为一个可复现的开源软件包。

Result: 给出一套实用的评估与报告标准，减少因抽样误差与不透明实验设定导致的误导性结论；通过在 HumanEval 上的示例演示该方法如何揭示模型间在质量与稳定性上的差异；并提供用于实际复现实验的开源工具。

Conclusion: SafeLLM4SE 提出了一套以统计为依据的评估流程和报告标准，用于更可靠地评估基于大语言模型的软件工程系统。该方法将生成输出视为随机过程，区分质量、稳定性与估计不确定性，并通过自适应抽样、置信区间、分布感知的统计比较与效应量来量化不确定性。作者还提供了在 PyPI 上可用的开源实现，并在 HumanEval 基准上对两种 LLM 进行了比较示例。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software engineering tasks, yet their stochastic behavior challenges the validity, reproducibility, and comparability of their evaluations. Conventional practices such as reporting a single output, an average score, best-of-N, or pass@k performance can obscure variability and estimation uncertainty, potentially leading to misleading conclusions about system reliability. This article presents SafeLLM4SE, a practical methodology and reporting standard for statistically principled evaluation of LLM-based software engineering systems. Rather than treating generated outputs as deterministic artifacts, SafeLLM4SE treats them as realizations of a stochastic process and distinguishes quality, stability, and estimation uncertainty. It combines adaptive sampling with confidence intervals, distribution-aware statistical comparisons, effect sizes, and a minimum reporting standard covering model configuration, reproducibility, evaluation procedures, and resource usage. SafeLLM4SE is also provided as an open-source software package available on PyPI, enabling researchers and practitioners to reproduce and extend the methodology. We illustrate its application by comparing two LLMs on HumanEval, a benchmark of programming problems assessed through functional tests.

</details>


### [26] [Do Agent Benchmarks Do What They Say? An Executable-Contract Audit of Tool-Using Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37315)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Zichong Wang,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Benchmarks often trust tool-reported effects; treating interfaces as executable contracts and tracing score provenance uncovers implementation mismatches that corrupt evaluation; static contract checks are promising, dynamic probes are limited, and audit taxonomies should add this failure mode.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tool-using agents operate in real-cost domains but benchmarks grade based on tool-reported claims, assuming the tool did what its interface advertised; existing audit taxonomies lack a category for that assumption, leaving an unaddressed source of persistent defects.

Method: Treat tool interfaces as executable contracts; run a checker that (1) compares implementation to the advertised surface, (2) probes runtime behavior, and (3) traces score provenance from task files and evaluator code to the state the tool should have written. Audited 34 mutating tools across four benchmarks, injected defects for controls, and examined AgentDojo's 25-tool mutating surface.

Result: Across audited tools found 7 real tool defects and one evaluator property at pinned commits. On injected defects the checker produced 25 true positives with no false positives, flagged 2 of 5 negative controls, but missed many defects because probes did not reveal them; static analysis alone flagged 14 of 17 confirmed sites. In AgentDojo at least 5 of 25 mutating tools diverged from their advertised surface; concrete examples show evaluators rewarding reported messages rather than verifying state changes.

Conclusion: Benchmarks that trust tool-reported effects without verifying state are prone to systematic, reproducible defects; executable-interface contracts plus provenance tracing reveal many such defects and show that static checks are effective at locating implementation discrepancies while dynamic probes often fail to expose them.

Abstract: Tool-using agents are entering settings where a wrong action carries real cost, and the benchmarks certifying them grade what each simulated tool call reports having done, assuming the tool did what its interface advertises. The audit taxonomies we survey publish no category for that assumption, and a defect beneath a score is present on every rerun. We treat a tool's advertised surfaces as an executable contract, check the implementation against it, and trace each score's provenance through the task files and evaluator code to the verdicts that derive from state a defective tool should have written. Across 34 audited mutating tools in four benchmarks we confirm seven tool defects and one evaluator property at pinned commits. On injected defects the checker raised no false positive in 25 flags, flagged 2 of 5 negative controls, and missed most: in 29 of 33 scored misses a clause covered the defect but no probe revealed it. The checker's own static half, run alone, flags 14 of 17 confirmed sites, so on these findings the dynamic half confirms and traces rather than discovers. Twelve further AgentDojo tools, with six held-out tools and the seven audited first, complete its 25-tool mutating surface, on which at least 5 tools diverge from their advertised surface as our contracts read it, a rate for AgentDojo alone. No gold trajectory reaches either tau2-bench defect; on 1,120 paths built to isolate the telecom defect, a number fixed by construction, the evaluator rewards a refuel of a suspended line and fails the repaired tool. The clearest case is a clinical benchmark whose tool tells the agent each write executed under a documented no-write design its interface does not disclose; its grader takes that message as evidence, so its action success rate records whether a request carried the expected payload, not whether any record changed.

</details>


### [27] [Complexity-Aware Evaluation of LLM Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37405)
*Ali Mohammadi Esfahani,Nafiseh Kahani,Samuel A. Ajila*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 按结构复杂度分组评估两款LLM，发现从低到高复杂度准确率显著下降，提示应在评估LLM代码理解时纳入复杂度维度以获得更诊断性的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM代码理解能力的评价多依赖总体准确率，无法反映模型在结构更复杂代码上的稳健性与退化趋势，需一个复杂度感知的基准框架来提供更具诊断性的评估。

Method: 使用环形复杂度（cyclomatic complexity）、嵌套深度、分支因子和Halstead体积将300个Python函数分为低/中/高复杂度三组；对两个模型（DeepSeek-Coder-V2、Llama）进行自动输入-输出预测评估（300函数）并对平衡子集60个函数做人工语义理解判定；并用相关性分析和逻辑回归检验复杂度度量与正确性的关系。

Result: DeepSeek-Coder-V2自动准确率78.33%（低→高：93.52%→52.78%）；Llama 70.33%（低→高：87.04%→47.22%）。人工语义理解：DeepSeek 100%→75%，Llama 90%→60%。高复杂度时错误率显著上升，相关和逻辑回归显示复杂度指标与错误有负相关/负向关联。

Conclusion: 复杂度感知评估能揭示LLM在代码理解任务上的可靠性下降，单一的整体准确率会掩盖随结构复杂性增加而急剧降低的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for software engineering tasks that require understanding existing source code, including behavior prediction, function explanation, debugging, and code review. However, aggregate benchmark accuracy can conceal how model reliability changes as source code becomes structurally more complex. This paper presents a complexity-aware framework for evaluating LLM code comprehension using cyclomatic complexity, nesting depth, branching factor, and Halstead volume. We evaluate DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and Llama through two complementary tasks: automatic input-output prediction over 300 Python functions and manually assessed semantic comprehension over a balanced subset of 60 functions. The functions are grouped into Low-, Medium-, and High-complexity bands. DeepSeek-Coder-V2 achieves an overall automatic accuracy of 78.33%, compared with 70.33% for Llama. However, accuracy decreases substantially from Low to High complexity, from 93.52% to 52.78% for DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and from 87.04% to 47.22% for Llama. Incorrect predictions are consistently associated with higher values of all four complexity metrics, and correlation and logistic-regression analyses confirm broadly comparable negative associations between structural complexity and correctness. Manual semantic comprehension shows the same degradation pattern, with accuracy decreasing from 100.00% to 75.00% for DeepSeek-Coder-V2 and from 90.00% to 60.00% for Llama. These findings demonstrate that complexity-aware evaluation provides a more diagnostic assessment of LLM code-comprehension reliability than aggregate accuracy alone.

</details>


### [28] [Exploring Emotional Intelligence in Software Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37526)
*Cristina Martinez Montes,Pushpamalar Rajendran,Grace Karuri,Felix Dobslaw*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Interviews with 16 agile software testers reveal EI as a constant, role-critical skill: testers manage stress and interpersonal delivery of criticism, and convert frustration about changing requirements into practical triage, advocacy, or workarounds—especially important where formal, independent testing phases are absent.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior SE emotion research focused mainly on developers; testers have a distinct role—producing criticism under uncontrolled deadlines—so the study investigates how EI features in testers’ daily work, team communication/conflict, and responses to requirements change.

Method: Semi-structured interviews with 16 software testers in Sweden working in agile teams across multiple domains (aviation, automotive, healthcare, IT services, administration, banking, pharmaceuticals); data analysed with reflexive thematic analysis using Goleman’s EI framework as an interpretive lens.

Result: Three themes: (1) regulating stress under deadline pressure and deriving motivation from recognition, clarity and autonomy; (2) delivering critical findings in ways that preserve trust with colleagues; (3) responding to requirements volatility with frustration that becomes triage (what to leave untested), advocacy for process change, or tactical workarounds. Role-specific observations: the tester’s work is often critical feedback, success is invisible while failure is blamed, and testers’ effective window narrows with upstream delays.

Conclusion: For software testers, managing emotions is an ongoing, job-level requirement: emotional intelligence (EI) is central to coping with deadlines, delivering criticisms, and handling requirements volatility. EI matters especially when there is no independent testing phase. The findings have implications for team practices, organisational support, and future research.

Abstract: Background: Emotional Intelligence (EI) is the ability to recognise, understand, and manage one's own and others' emotions. Software testers deliver judgements about colleagues' work under deadlines they do not control, and prior work on emotion in software engineering has mostly studied developers.
  Aims: To explore how software testers describe the part EI plays in their day-to-day work, in communication and conflict within the team, and in responding to requirements volatility.
  Method: Semi-structured interviews with 16 software testers in Sweden working in teams that use agile practices, across aviation, automotive, healthcare, IT services, administration, banking and pharmaceuticals, analysed with reflexive thematic analysis informed by Goleman's EI framework.
  Results: Three themes. Testers described regulating stress under deadline pressure and drawing motivation from recognition, clarity and autonomy; managing the daily delivery of critical findings to colleagues so that trust survives; and responding to requirements change with frustration that turned into decisions about what to leave untested, into advocacy for process change, or into workarounds.
