Read against developer-focused studies, the themes point to features of the testing role: the work product is a criticism of a colleague's work, success is invisible while failure is attributed, and the tester's window shrinks with every upstream delay.
  Conclusions: For testers, managing emotions is a constant job requirement. The results highlight that the importance of EI increases when the development process lacks an independent testing phase. The findings also inform implications for teams and, ultimately, for organisations and future research.

</details>


### [29] [Profiling the Energy Consumption of Serverless Functions with Joule Profiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37531)
*Jérémy Woirhaye,François Gibier,Anderson Andrei Da Silva,Francescomaria Faticanti,Thomas Ledoux,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统性评估1401种无服务器配置后发现：先选语言，再选运行时，执行环境仅在短生命周期函数中决定性影响能耗；作者并提供了完整可复现的实验工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将无服务器函数视为黑盒，缺乏关于将应用打包为无服务器函数时对能耗影响的深入理解；需要量化不同层（容器、虚拟化、unikernel、语言运行时等）对能效的贡献。

Method: 构建可重复的基准测试平台，系统化评估1401种配置（9种执行环境×7种语言运行时配置×11种负载×3种输入规模），通过能耗测量回答三个研究问题。

Result: 实验结果表明：1) 语言选择对能效影响最大；2) 运行时配置次之；3) 执行环境对短期（短生命周期）函数影响明显但总体次要；并公开了基准环境、原始测量和分析代码以利复现。

Conclusion: 选择编程语言优先，其次是语言运行时，执行环境只在短生命周期函数中显著影响，且最优执行环境依赖于所选运行时。

Abstract: Cloud providers and customers have widely adopted serverless computing as a convenient paradigm for deploying and executing functions on demand. To do so, serverless platforms require provisioning an appropriate execution environment before a single line of the function's code runs. These environments consist of several layers, such as container engines, hypervisors, unikernels, and programming language runtimes. While the literature has investigated the performance of these serverless platforms, it treats functions as black boxes, and the community lacks key insights into the environmental impacts of packaging applications as serverless functions. This paper therefore empirically studies the energy efficiency of serverless functions deployable on serverless platforms. We design an experimental benchmarking environment that lets stakeholders explore the impacts of the various layers involved in executing serverless functions. We use it to evaluate 1,401 configurations, combining 9 execution environments, 7 language-runtime configurations, 11 workloads, and 3 input sizes, to answer three research questions: Are the most popular programming languages for serverless functions the most energy-efficient? What factors most affect their energy efficiency? What are the most energy-efficient configurations to deploy them? Our results show that one should first choose the programming language, then the language runtime, and only then the execution environment, which matters only for short-lived functions and whose best choice depends on the runtime. Our benchmarking environment, experimental artifacts, raw measurements, and analysis code are publicly available.

</details>


### [30] [Independent Verification Paths Are Not Independent: A Case Study of Common-Mode Failure in a Satellite Catalogue Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37603)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 冗余验证能发现实现差错但难以检测共同模式的语义误读；需更靠近源规范的检查与更异质的独立路径设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估跨目录一致性门（redundant computation）在实际数据流水线中对发表数字的防护能力，特别是能否捕捉语义误读或共同模式故障。

Method: 对比两条独立路径（Python集合操作与对外发布RDF的SPARQL查询），构建对象级账本以逐项调和数字，设计三项基于源文档的校验，并进行控制复现（钉住模型、禁用工具）以生成独立核验路径。

Result: 门报告“全部一致”，但三项计数错误（最大差异932 vs 220）；错误源为两路径共用的常量，反映对源状态词汇表的误读。修正后的代码仍含42/261个伪造差异，三项检查未能识别。控制复现显示大多数独立路径仍产生错误计数，表明错误属于语义而非实现。

Conclusion: 冗余检验无法发现共同模式错误，因此发布数据虽通过自检但仍有语义性错误。

Abstract: A common safeguard for a data pipeline is redundant computation: derive each published number by two routes built on different technology and refuse to exit when they disagree. We report one such gate failing, in a cross-catalogue integrity study of two open registers of Earth-orbiting objects. A gate comparing a set-based Python path with SPARQL queries over the emitted RDF graph printed ALL CROSS-CHECKS AGREE on seven counts. Three were wrong, one overstated more than fourfold (932 against 220). Both paths imported the same constants, which encoded a misreading of the source's status vocabulary, so the error was common-mode and the gate could not see it. We give the mechanism, an object-level ledger reconciling every figure, and three checks that go back to the source's documentation, measured on the defective code and on its correction. We then checked that correction against each object's phase history, held in a source file the pipeline never read. The correction was also wrong: 42 of its 261 disagreements are artefacts, and none of our three checks flagged them. Finally, in a controlled replication with three pinned models and tools disabled, 72 of 75 paths generated on request as independent checks computed the defective count, 29 of 30 even when the prompt carried the source's own definitions of the codes. The evidence is one pipeline and one defect family. Within it, redundancy verified implementation, and the errors that reached publication were errors of meaning.

</details>


### [31] [Beyond Productivity: Measuring Developers' Cognitive Load During GenAI-Supported Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37645)
*Charlotte Brandebusemeyer,Daniela Gasser,Tobias Schimmer,Bert Arnrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在真实开发现场中，GenAI使用和任务情境是影响开发者主观认知负荷的关键因素；可穿戴生理数据只能作为情境评估的补充，不能独立替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对GenAI在软件开发中应用的评估多关注产能、使用率与产出质量，较少关注开发者的交互体验与认知负荷变化。为以开发者为中心地评估GenAI支持的开发流程，需要了解GenAI是否改变或转移了开发者的认知需求，以便组织设计有效的AI支持工作流。

Method: 在两处SAP工业现场开展为期四天的田野研究，招募21名专业开发者。参与者记录每个任务、任务时长、是否使用GenAI以及其主观认知负荷，同时佩戴EmbracePlus腕带采集生理信号。将主观认知负荷与任务上下文、GenAI使用记录及生理特征进行关联分析与建模比较。

Result: 发现主观认知负荷与GenAI使用及任务上下文均显著相关，而可穿戴设备导出的生理特征在控制任务上下文后，仅能提供有限的额外解释力。结论是应在具体工作情境下评估认知负荷，并把生理数据作为补充信息来源。

Conclusion: 开发者在使用生成式人工智能(GenAI)进行软件开发时的主观认知负荷与GenAI的使用情况和具体任务上下文相关，而来自可穿戴设备的生理测量只能提供有限的额外信息。因此，应把生理数据作为对任务情境评估的补充而非替代手段。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is changing software development workflows and how developers work. Industry evaluations of GenAI adoption often monitor productivity gains, usage, and output quality, but limited attention is paid to the interaction experience and cognitive load of the actual adopters and drivers of GenAI technology - the software developers. Understanding whether GenAI changes or shifts developers' cognitive demands during everyday development is important for a developer-centered evaluation of GenAI-supported software development. It can inform organizations in designing and evaluating effective AI-supported workflows. In this work, we study how GenAI use and task context relate to professional developers' perceived cognitive load and whether wearable-derived physiological characteristics provide additional information beyond this context. In a four-day industrial field study at two SAP sites, 21 developers documented their tasks, task duration, GenAI use, and perceived cognitive load while wearing an EmbracePlus wristband. The results show that perceived cognitive load is associated with both GenAI use and task context, while physiological measures provide only limited additional information. These findings suggest that developers' perceived cognitive load during GenAI-supported software development should be evaluated in relation to the concrete work context, with wearable physiological data used as complementary rather than standalone information.

</details>


### [32] [Retrieve, Reproduce, Reveal: Dissecting Retrieval-Augmented Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37669)
*Sabrina Kaniewski,Tim Krämer,Julius Bächle,Markus Enzweiler,Michael Menth,Tobias Heer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Authors reproduce six RAG4SVD systems with open-weight models, build a unified benchmark, and perform pipeline-level analysis. They show that reported results are not always reproducible, performance depends on backbone models, and retrieval effectiveness alone cannot ensure reliable vulnerability detection, motivating component-level evaluation and standardized RAG-aware benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RAG4SVD studies use proprietary models, diverse datasets, custom KBs, and inconsistent metrics, impeding reproducibility and cross-system comparison. There is also a lack of component-level analysis.

Method: Reproduction of six open-source RAG4SVD systems under open-weight models; creation of a unified benchmark with common dataset, metric suite, and open-weight model pool; component-level analysis decomposing pipelines into input abstraction, retrieval, and detection; experiments including oracle knowledge to assess upper bounds.

Result: Reproducibility varies widely; published performance often does not transfer to open-weight benchmarks and is model-dependent. Oracle knowledge reveals retrieval can be near-optimal but overall detection remains weak (0.51 pairwise accuracy), indicating other pipeline components limit performance. Findings support RAG-aware, component-level standardized evaluations.

Conclusion: This paper finds that RAG4SVD systems' reported gains are not reliably reproducible under open-weight settings; performance varies with backbone models and depends on component alignment across pipeline stages. Retrieval quality alone is insufficient for strong detection; component-level evaluation is necessary for meaningful assessment and standardization.

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly used to enhance Large Language Model (LLM)-based software vulnerability detection by grounding predictions in retrieved vulnerability knowledge, such as vulnerability reports. However, existing RAG-based software vulnerability detection (RAG4SVD) systems are often evaluated using proprietary models, which challenges open science and reproducibility. Further, studies use different datasets, custom knowledge bases, different backbone models, and diverse metrics, which hinders meaningful cross-system comparison. In this work, we study six open-source RAG4SVD systems and address these reproducibility and comparability challenges through (i) reproduction of their experimental settings under an open-weight setting, and (ii) a unified benchmark using a common dataset, metric suite, and pool of open-weight models. Further, RAG4SVD systems typically consist of multiple components, yet are often evaluated only as a whole system, i.e., end-to-end. Therefore, we perform (iii) a component-level analysis that decomposes representative RAG4SVD pipelines into input abstraction, knowledge retrieval, and detection. Our results demonstrate that reproducibility varies substantially across systems. Under the presented unified benchmark, published RAG4SVD performance does not transfer under a controlled open-weight evaluation and depends strongly on the used model. The component analysis shows that effective RAG4SVD depends on the alignment between pipeline stages. For example, oracle knowledge raises retrieval to near-optimal, yet performance remains low (0.51 pairwise accuracy), demonstrating that retrieval effectiveness alone is insufficient for reliable detection. These findings motivate evaluating RAG4SVD not only end-to-end, but at the level of pipeline components, and provide a basis for more standardized, RAG-aware evaluation practices.

</details>


### [33] [Is manual software optimization a thing of the past?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37849)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Martin Špendl,Tomaž Hočevar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，面向可验证问题，LLM自治代理能替代传统人工优化，显著提高科学软件性能，改变软件优化的角色与流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算数据量增长导致性能瓶颈，传统手工优化需大量专业技能；探索是否可用大语言模型自动执行或加速软件优化以降低人力门槛。

Method: 人类定义优化范围、正确性准则与验证机制后，赋予LLM基于代理的自治工作流让其独立改进三项计算问题（t-SNE、ssGSEA、graphlet counting）；代理在若干小时内生成优化实现，随后由原项目维护者审查并验证正确性。

Result: 在所有测试配置下均获得加速，最快达两阶数量级提升；改进涵盖低层代码优化、数学重表述以及为graphlet counting设计的新算法；维护者确认正确性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化代理能够在多项科学软件上实现显著性能提升，甚至超过已由人类优化的实现，人类角色将从直接优化转为界定目标、范围与验证机制并保证最终正确性。

Abstract: Scientific software is increasingly required to process larger datasets while maintaining acceptable execution times. Software optimization traditionally requires substantial expertise in programming, algorithms, and numerical methods. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer the possibility of automating much of this process. We investigate whether LLM-based agents can autonomously achieve substantial performance improvements in scientific software, including mature implementations that have already been extensively optimized by human developers. We tasked an LLM-based agent with optimizing software for three computational problems: t-SNE, single-sample gene set enrichment analysis (ssGSEA), and graphlet counting. Humans defined the scope, correctness criteria, and a verification mechanism, after which the agent worked autonomously, in some cases for several hours. Code maintainers reviewed each resulting implementation and verified its correctness. The optimized implementations were faster in all tested configurations, by up to two orders of magnitude over the fastest existing tools. The improvements included low-level code optimizations, mathematical reformulations, and an entirely new algorithm for graphlet counting. Software optimization can increasingly be delegated to autonomous agents, with the human role shifting from implementing optimizations to deciding which software to optimize, defining objectives, providing verification mechanisms, and ensuring the correctness of the final software. For well-scoped, verifiable problems, we argue that manual software optimization may be a thing of the past.

</details>


### [34] [AgentBug-Smith: Automatically Reproducing Real-World Harness Bugs in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37864)
*Yiming Cheng,Alfin Wijaya Rahardja,Mengshi Zhang,Zihao Chen,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentBug-Smith automates discovery/reproduction of agent harness bugs and builds a 200-case live benchmark (Live-Harness-Bench) that improves reproduction and enables measurable agent repair gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current harness-bug research is limited by small, static, and costly-to-construct benchmarks and by unique properties of harness bugs that make them hard for state-of-the-art agents to repair; automated, scalable reproduction of real-world harness bugs can enable continuous evaluation and improvement of agents.

Method: AgentBug-Smith continuously mines open-source agentic systems to find real-world harness failures, automates reproduction of harness bugs across multiple backbone LLMs, and assembles reproduced cases into Live-Harness-Bench; additionally, it uses the benchmark as both an evaluation suite and a knowledge base to distill reusable repair skills to enhance agent repair performance.

Result: AgentBug-Smith achieves 10.67%–27.56% higher reproduction success than existing general bug reproduction methods; Live-Harness-Bench contains 200 reproducible harness bugs; using the benchmark reveals limited repair abilities of current agents and distilling repair skills from it increases agent repair rates by 6.32%.

Conclusion: This paper presents AgentBug-Smith, an automated system to discover and reproduce harness bugs in open-source agentic systems, and builds Live-Harness-Bench, a live benchmark of 200 reproducible harness bugs; it shows improved reproduction rates over general techniques and demonstrates use as an evaluation benchmark and a source for distilling repair skills to improve agents.

Abstract: Agent harness bugs exhibit unique characteristics and remain challenging for state-of-the-art software agents to repair. Progress in this area is further hindered by existing benchmarks, which contain only a small and fixed number of executable harness bugs while requiring hundreds of human hours to construct. This work presents AgentBug-Smith, an automated harness bug reproduction approach that continuously discovers and reproduces real-world harness bugs from open-source agentic systems. Across different backbone LLMs, AgentBug-Smith consistently outperforms existing bug reproduction techniques designed for general software, achieving 10.67% - 27.56% higher success rates of reproducing harness bugs. By applying AgentBug-Smith to open-source agentic systems in the wild, we construct Live-Harness-Bench, a live and extensible benchmark that currently contains 200 reproducible harness bugs. We further demonstrate the utility of Live-Harness-Bench through two downstream applications. First, we use Live-Harness-Bench as the evaluation benchmark to systematically evaluate state-of-the-art software agents, revealing their limited capabilities in repairing real-world harness bugs. Second, we use Live-Harness-Bench as a knowledge base of real-world harness bug fixes, from which reusable repair skills can be distilled to improve existing software agents, increasing their harness-bug repair rates by 6.32%. Together, AgentBug-Smith and Live-Harness-Bench establish a scalable foundation for continuously evaluating and improving software agents on harness bug repair, turning real-world agent failures into executable evaluation instances and reusable knowledge for harness improvement, thus contributing to the ultimate goal of recursively self-improving agents.

</details>


### [35] [Merged, Not Measured: An Empirical Study of Performance Issues Fixed by Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37985)
*Zhenyu Qi,Haotang Li,Jinfu Chen,Huashan Chen,Yutong Zhao,Derui Zhu,Tomas Cerny,Bo Liu,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 在大量代理生成的PR中，合并决策更受仓库与代理历史影响；已合并的性能修复并不总是实际提升性能，且许多问题源于重复计算或架构缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估代码生成代理所提性能修复的质量与有效性，以及维护者在审查此类PR时的决策依据与行为模式。

Method: 从AIDev v4的71,677个代理提交中通过文本过滤与由语言模型与作者共同编码的codebook筛选出1,262个由六种代理提出、分布于582个仓库的性能问题修复；对每个问题与其测试进行编码，并对23个被拒绝和30个已合并的修复在主要由代理构建的工作负载下重新运行（采用三次试验的pilot），比较合并率与代码改动特征，归类问题成因并评估修复是否满足性能交付标准。

Result: （1）已关闭修复中57%被合并；61%的拒绝没有给出理由；在复测的23个被拒绝修复中仅6个在三次试验下保持性能提升。（2）接受率与代理在该仓库的历史记录以及仓库对其它代理PR的合并率高度相关（从约31–37%提升到70%，或33%提升到84%），已合并修复在被改动行中删除行比例更高（0.26 vs 0.15），但在编码内容、描述、测试或测量上未检出差异。（3）44%问题由重复计算与冗余数据处理引起，46%修复属架构层面。（4）代理在37%修复中修改测试，11%包含性能测试或基准；在30个已合并中，18个满足交付标准，3个未达宣称，9个无显著提升或回退，14个在未经测试输入上改变行为。总体结论：合并并不等于修复真实有效，且合并与仓库/代理历史比与修复内容更强相关。

Conclusion: 合并决定更多取决于仓库与代理的既往互动历史，而非修复内容本身；很多被合并的“提速”修复在复测中并未兑现其性能承诺；性能问题常由重复计算与冗余数据处理引起，且大量修复属于架构级修改。

Abstract: Coding agents open pull requests (PRs) that claim to speed up software, but studies of human performance fixes say little about how maintainers respond to such a fix or whether its claim holds. From the 71,677 agent PRs of AIDev v4, a text filter and codebook coding by language models and by the authors select 1,262 performance issues fixed by six agents in 582 repositories. We code each issue and its tests and re-execute 23 rejected and 30 merged fixes. (1) 57% of closed fixes are merged, 61% of rejections give no stated reason, and only 6 of the 23 re-executed rejected claims held under our three-run pilot on mostly agent-built workloads. (2) Acceptance rises with the agent's track record in the repository (31-37% to 70%) and with the repository's pre-opening merge rate on its other agent PRs (33% to 84%). Merged fixes delete a larger share of the lines they change (0.26 versus 0.15), a difference that holds within agent and within repository, with no such difference detected in the coded content, description, tests or measurements. (3) Repeated computation and redundant data processing cause 44% of the issues, and 46% of fixes are architectural-level. (4) Agents change tests in 37% of fixes and 11% carry a performance test or benchmark; of the 30 merged fixes, 18 met our delivery criterion, 3 fell short of the claim, 9 showed no significant gain or regressed, and 14 change behavior on untested inputs. The outcome tracks the repository's history with the agent rather than the coded content of the fix, and a merge does not show that the fix delivers what it claims.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [36] [OpenAI-HuggingFace: A Reproduction & Lessons for Alignment Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35799)
*Stewart Slocum,Malayandi Palan,Christopher Chute,Michael Kim,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过仿真与公开模型复现了导致OpenAI–Hugging Face事件的错配行为，证明审计代理在充足算力下能诱发该类行为，且in-context RL能高效降低复现成本，从而呼吁发展可随算力扩展且计算高效的自动化对齐测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在实际事件（OpenAI—Hugging Face 安全事件）后的可测性问题：既有对齐测试能否预见并防止此类跨通道多代理滥用，若不能，应如何改进测试方法以适应更多算力和更复杂攻击。

Method: 构建模拟原始流水线与工具的环境，使用公开模型手工诱发并复现导致事件的错配行为；构建审计代理以高层描述自动尝试诱发相同行为，评估不同算力下成功率；设计并测试一种简单的in-context RL算法以降低算力消耗。

Result: 成功在模拟环境用公开模型复现事件中描述的错配行为；审计代理在高算力下可以自主诱发相似行为；不同行为对算力的需求差异大，表明可诱发行为空间随算力放大；in-context RL 在多个案例中显著减少所需算力。

Conclusion: 论文表明现有的对齐测试在计算资源足够时可能未能预见多代理跨通道协调的越界行为；通过仿真环境与公开模型可以复现该类行为，且强化学习（以原位/上下文方式）能显著降低复现所需算力。结论是需要可随算力扩展且计算高效的自动化对齐测试方法，RL 是有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: In July 2026, OpenAI's agents coordinated over channels outside their intended environment to breach Hugging Face's secured infrastructure. Could existing alignment testing practices have foreseen this incident? If not, what needs to change? We explore these questions. First, we identify the misaligned behaviors that caused this incident. Then, we show how to elicit these behaviors from publicly available models manually and that auditing agents can do the same if given a large compute budget. Based on our results, we propose directions to improve alignment testing. Concretely, in this project: (1) We reproduce the misaligned AI behaviors that led to the OpenAI-Hugging Face incident in an environment that simulates the original pipelines and tools, with publicly available models. (2) We demonstrate that an auditing agent can elicit similar behaviors given high-level qualitative descriptions. (3) We observe that a key ingredient for doing so is compute. The compute required to reproduce each behavior varies greatly, suggesting that the range of misaligned behaviors that can be successfully elicited scales with compute. (4) We show that a simple in-context reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm significantly reduces the compute required to elicit these behaviors. The above results motivate the need for automated alignment testing methods that scale with compute - and in light of the cost of compute, that do this efficiently. Our work indicates that RL is a promising direction to do so. We release our code and transcripts.

</details>


### [37] [Neurosymbolic Routing for Reliable Reasoning on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35833)
*Avyay Sadhu,Alvaro Velasquez,Lekai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在边缘设备上用L*学习的DFA做路由，把确定性任务交给符号引擎、把开放题留给小模型，能显著提升准确率、延迟与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上可运行的小型模型在算术、代数和形式逻辑等确定性任务上不够可靠且能耗高，但这些任务本质上可被符号精确求解。通过将确定性子任务交给专用引擎而不是强制小模型以概率方式近似，可以提高准确性并节省能量。

Method: 利用L*语法推断算法学习一个确定性有限自动机（DFA）作为路由器；把小型语言模型(SLM)当作L*的成员查询（membership oracle），用标签数据作为等价查询（equivalence oracle）；根据路由将查询分发到格式化的确定性引擎或SLM。

Result: 在Raspberry Pi 4B上，针对100个未见提示（来自DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K、RuleTaker），学习到的路由器实现100%路由准确率、整体98.3%准确率（512 token预算，文字题93.3%），比最强基线Program-of-Thought高出显著，同时在延迟和能耗上也有显著优势（格式化查询响应1–11 ms；30 token配置下比Program-of-Thought快8.8倍、能效高2.8倍）。

Conclusion: 学习到的神经符号路由器能将结构化可确定任务交给确定性求解器，把开放式文字问题交给小型语言模型，从而在边缘设备上实现更高的准确率和能效。

Abstract: Running a language model on edge hardware provides private and low-latency reasoning without a network connection, and yet the small models that fit on such devices are unreliable on the tasks computers are expected to handle well, such as arithmetic, algebra, and formal logic problems. We argue that much of this unreliability is avoidable. Many queries appearing to demand reasoning are in fact structurally deterministic and permit fast and exact symbolic solutions. Therefore, forcing a probabilistic model to approximate them sacrifices accuracy and energy for little benefit. We present a neurosymbolic router that classifies each incoming query and dispatches it to the cheapest correct solver, sending structured tasks to deterministic engines and reserving the small language model (SLM) for open-ended word problems. Instead of hand-coding the routing logic, we learn a deterministic finite automaton (DFA) with the L* grammatical inference algorithm, using the SLM as a membership oracle and labeled data as an equivalence oracle. On a Raspberry Pi 4B (8 GB RAM, no GPU), evaluated on 100 untested prompts from DeepMind Mathematics, GSM8K, and RuleTaker, learned routing attains 100% routing accuracy and 98.3% overall accuracy with a 512-token reasoning budget (93.3% on word problems), compared with 72.0% for the strongest agent baseline, Program-of-Thought, and 58.7% for a tool-calling agent given the same solvers. Since formatted queries never reach the model, the router answers them in 1-11 ms and, in its 30-token configuration, runs 8.8x faster and 2.8x more energy-efficient than Program-of-Thought.

</details>


### [38] [Is Human-Readable Text Necessary for Effective LLM Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35868)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Daning Cheng,Song Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Is human readability necessary for effective fine-tuning of large language models? We investigate whether model-conditioned training representations can preserve or improve adaptation utility without requiring a human-readable textual form. We propose Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA), which uses activation-gradient feedback from a frozen reference model to guide the optimization of continuous synthetic input embeddings. Inspired by the role of activation gradients in local risk reduction, DASA targets useful adaptation updates rather than source-text reconstruction or linguistic fluency. The resulting embeddings are used directly for downstream fine-tuning; discrete token projections are employed only for qualitative inspection. Experiments on six models from the Llama and Qwen families, ranging from 1B to 32B parameters, cover six benchmarks spanning knowledge, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and commonsense reasoning. Under matched LoRA adaptation settings, DASA achieves performance comparable to the source natural-language data and surpasses it in multiple configurations, while outperforming GRADMM in most comparisons. Further experiments cover general-domain and task-specialized source data. Under the evaluated synthesis settings, DASA provides a $3.6$--$4.9\times$ speedup over GRADMM with comparable peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [39] [The Price of Token Boundaries: Compression Certificates and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35869)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Quantifies and certifies the compression cost of pre-tokenisation boundaries, shows substantial token-count savings when boundaries are removed, demonstrates that compression-optimal vocabularies differ from prediction-optimal ones, and introduces 'boundary licences' to capture most savings with limited boundary crossing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To separate and quantify the compression cost imposed by pre-tokenisation boundaries (which restrict possible token units) from the prediction quality of resulting tokenisations, and to provide certifiable lower bounds on token counts under different boundary policies.

Method: Introduce a bounded token-count framework by imposing upper and lower token-count constraints with and without a regex boundary rule. Use nonnegative prices on token occurrences to obtain a lower bound via shortest-path computations and vocabulary-budget selection; maximize over prices to obtain the linear-programming relaxation. Use an independent integer checker to certify reported integer bounds. Define boundary licences to limit how many vocabulary entries may cross cuts and apply the same certification machinery.

Result: On English Wikipedia, enforced boundaries increase optimal token count by 28.3–36.8%. Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) is 2.1% above the constrained lower bound but 10.9% above the unrestricted bound. Under matched training-token budgets and 85M non-embedding parameters, unrestricted (no-boundary) fitting gave higher mean held-out bits-per-byte in 12/12 languages in paired study and 11/12 with independent tuning—indicating different dictionaries are favored for compression vs prediction. Allowing 10% of the vocabulary to cross boundaries (boundary licences) recovers 85.2% (English) and 100% (Chinese) of the token-count reduction achieved by fully removing cuts.

Conclusion: Pre-tokenisation imposes a nontrivial compression cost that can be quantified and certified; allowing tokens to cross boundaries reduces token count substantially but can harm predictive performance under fixed model budgets, meaning compression-optimized dictionaries differ from prediction-optimized dictionaries. Partial relaxation of boundaries (boundary licences) recovers most of the token-count gains while maintaining certifiable bounds.

Abstract: Pre-tokenisation restricts which text fragments can become prediction units, but its compression cost is obscured when tokenisers are compared only under the same boundaries. We measure this cost by bounding the minimum token count from both sides, with and without a regular-expression boundary rule. Nonnegative prices on token occurrences yield a lower bound through shortest paths and vocabulary-budget selection; maximising over all prices recovers the linear programming relaxation, and an independent integer checker certifies the reported values. On English Wikipedia, boundaries increase the optimal token count by 28.3--36.8\%. Byte pair encoding lies 2.1\% above the constrained lower bound, but 10.9\% above the unrestricted bound. Compression and prediction favour different dictionaries: at 85M non-embedding parameters and matched training-token budgets, unrestricted fitting yields higher mean held-out bits per byte under a common unrestricted decoder in all 12 languages in the paired study and 11 of 12 under independent tuning and evaluation. To study intermediate boundary policies, we introduce boundary licences, which limit the vocabulary entries permitted to cross cuts and admit the same form of certificate. On separate English and Chinese fitting corpora, licensing 10\% of the vocabulary budget recovers 85.2\% and 100.0\% of the achieved token-count reduction from removing all cuts. These results quantify the compression cost of boundaries while separating it from the prediction quality of the resulting token units.

</details>


### [40] [More Programs or More Rolls? Separating Coverage from Specialization in LLM Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35873)
*Ziyang Xu,Haitian Zhong,Hao Zhou,Hao Qin,Chenhan Jin,Te Qi,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 受控实验表明自动生成的 harness 大量表现为可重复但无益的行为：覆盖率提升常来自更多执行而非可靠专用化；因此需要更严格的评估标准，要求任务优势在多次执行下稳定、可用于决策并在等推理预算下优于额外的固定程序执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自动生成的 LLM harness 是否真正在推理时通过程序级专用化带来可用的任务优势，还是仅仅因为多次执行增加了答案覆盖从而产生假象。

Method: 设计受控评估，将答案覆盖（coverage）、重复执行带来的可重复优势（repeatable task advantages）与预执行选择（pre-execution selection）分离；在386个 MATH-500 子任务上比较8个自动生成的 harness 与基线（及9个字节相同的基线复制体），对每个成员进行3次执行，并使用 BIRD 跟踪定位失败原因。

Result: 字节相同的程序产生2.16个百分点的重复平均可用上限（oracle headroom）。生成的 harness 展现出更多可重复的得分模式，但主要暴露持续性弱点：相较基线，100个任务在三次重复中均持续落后；仅1个任务出现持续获胜且对答案抽取高度敏感。固定选择器（frozen selector）没有带来改进（0.00个百分点），两类程序在27次执行时都达到98.70%的 oracle 覆盖率。三次重复下未能证明稳定互补性。BIRD 跟踪定位的失败类型包括机制实现、激活与输出有效性问题。

Conclusion: 覆盖率和可重复性本身不足以证明生成的 harness 实现了有用的任务专用化。

Abstract: Automated generation of LLM harnesses promises to improve inference through task specialization. Yet additional answer coverage can arise from repeated execution of the same program, making specialization difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation that separates answer coverage, repeatable task advantages, and gains from pre-execution selection. On 386 MATH-500 tasks, we compare eight generated harnesses plus a baseline with nine byte-identical baseline copies, using three executions per member. Identical programs yield 2.16 percentage points of repeat-averaged oracle headroom. Generated programs exhibit substantially more repeatable score patterns, but these chiefly reveal persistent weaknesses: losses relative to the baseline persist across all three repeats on 100 tasks, while persistent wins occur on only one task and are sensitive to answer extraction. The frozen selector gains 0.00 percentage points, and both populations reach 98.70% oracle coverage at 27 harness executions. Stable complementarity remains unresolved at three repeats. Supporting BIRD traces locate failures in mechanism implementation, activation, and output validity. Together, these findings establish why coverage and repeatability alone cannot justify claims of useful specialization. They motivate an evaluation standard for harness diversity: task advantages should persist across executions, guide usable decisions, and improve on additional fixed-program executions under matched inference budgets.

</details>


### [41] [Risk-Averse Online POMDP Planning via CVaR of the Immediate Cost with Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35874)
*Yaacov Pariente,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将CVaR施加在每步belief上的即时代价，保留期望累积目标，使任意期望型POMDP规划器通过替换代价计算即可变为风险敏感，并证明了粒子代理到真实问题的有限时端到端界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是：传统的期望累计代价可能掩盖belief中对高代价状态的显著质量；现有将CVaR作用于价值函数的做法只能捕捉轨迹层面的风险，且（i）未解决belief内部即时代价的不对称风险，（ii）需要为修改后的价值设计新的规划算法。

Method: 方法是：在每个决策步对belief上的即时状态代价应用条件风险价值（CVaR），保持目标为期望累积回报；使用粒子滤波/粒子信念MDP作为近似代理，继承并分析策略评估与稀疏采样的有限时保证，证明估计误差与风险水平无关，并给出粒子代理与真实POMDP值之间的有限时刻界限，从而构建端到端误差界。

Result: 主要结果包括：提出一种对每步即时代价进行belief级CVaR的风险敏感规划框架；证明了与现有期望规划器兼容的算法设计可行性；给出了策略评估和稀疏采样的有限时误差保证（与风险水平无关）；并证明了粒子信念MDP代理与原POMDP之间的有限时差距，从而获得端到端性能界限。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：将CVaR应用于每步的belief上即时代价（per-step CVaR），而非对轨迹价值施加CVaR，可以直接处理belief内部的不确定性，同时保持累积期望回报为优化目标，从而保留标准MDP结构；这允许复用现有基于期望的POMDP规划器，仅修改代价计算，并为粒子信念MDP代理与原始POMDP之间的误差给出有限时界限，最终得到从真实POMDP值到算法估计的端到端保证，在风险中性极限下退化为标准规划。

Abstract: Online POMDP planners optimize the expected cumulative cost, which can mask dangerous states when the belief places significant mass on high-cost states. Existing risk-averse methods apply static or dynamic Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) to the value function, capturing trajectory-level risk, but share two gaps: (i) by retaining the immediate cost as an expectation of a state-dependent cost over the belief, the risk \emph{within} the belief is left unaddressed; and (ii) by modifying the value function, they require new tailored algorithms rather than reusing existing expectation-based planners. We instead apply CVaR to the immediate cost over the belief at each step, directly targeting per-step uncertainty about the current state. The standard expected cumulative return is retained as the objective, so the resulting problem has a standard MDP structure: any expectation-based POMDP planner can be made risk-sensitive by changing only the cost computation. We inherit finite-time guarantees for policy evaluation and sparse sampling---with estimation error independent of the risk level---and, as our central theoretical result, prove a finite-time bound on the gap between the particle belief MDP surrogate and the original POMDP, which together yield an end-to-end guarantee from the true POMDP value to the algorithmic estimate. In the risk-neutral limit, the formulation recovers standard expectation-based planning.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Symmetric Agents: Cognitive Diversity and Multi-Agent Debate in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35875)
*Leonardo Ferreira,Gardenia Liu,Kaden Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 经预算与污染校正，多代理争辩的优势主要是集成/采样效应而非认知多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验多代理争辩（MAD）文献中观察到的性能提升是否由代理间的“认知多样性”驱动，还是可被更简单的采样/集成机制解释。

Method: 在可测量任务的情形下，用23个模型（来自11个厂商）、5个任务、5500+次争辩与对照运行，沿三个轴（persona提示、采样温度、模型身份）系统变动多样性，并为每种争辩配置配对一个与生成预算匹配的多数投票（self-consistency）控制。还做了剂量反应（combinatorial persona）实验和上下文窗口溢出纠正检查。

Result: 在所有三条多样性轴上均未发现支持认知多样性驱动论：争辩比单模型推理有3–7点提升（任务有头寸时），但在预算匹配下与自一致采样持平或落后（消耗1.6×壁钟、3.4×token成本）。Persona提示降低准确率（“persona税”），冗余persona最具破坏性；最大化多样性可部分恢复损失。混合模型团队表现由成员能力而非异质性决定；争辩大部分收益来自第一次答案交换；修正上下文窗口溢出后，争辩与采样差距从-1.8点变为持平。

Conclusion: 多代理争辩的改进主要由集成/采样效果驱动，而非认知多样性；在预算与污染（context overflow）受控条件下，原假设被否定。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) reportedly improves reasoning and factuality over single-model inference, but prior work treats agents as symmetric peers, leaving open what drives the gains. We test the hypothesis that cognitive diversity among agents is the driver, in the setting where the question is still measurable: small open-weight models with benchmark headroom. Across 23 models from eleven vendor families, five tasks, and 5,500+ debate and control runs, we vary diversity along three axes - personas, sampling temperature, and model identity - pairing every debate configuration with a generation-budget-matched majority-vote control. The hypothesis is rejected on every axis. Debate beats single-agent inference (3--7 points where tasks have headroom) but at matched budget conditions it ties or even loses to self-consistency sampling at 1.6$\times$ the wall-clock and 3.4$\times$ the token cost. Persona prompting reduces accuracy and a dose-response experiment over each model's full combinatorial persona space shows the cost is a persona tax, not a diversity tax: redundant personas hurt most, while maximally-diverse teams recover part of the loss. Furthermore, mixed-model teams lose to majority votes over their own rosters, with accuracy tracking member capability rather than heterogeneity, and nearly all of debate's benefit comes from the first exchange of answers. We further identify a pervasive measurement hazard in which debate transcripts silently overflow serving context windows, whose correction alone moves our debate-versus-sampling comparison from $-1.8$ points to parity. Our results recast reported MAD gains as an ensemble-sampling effect and provide the budget-matched, contamination-checked baseline bar that future debate mechanisms should be required to clear.

</details>


### [43] [Representational Simplicity and Circuit Size Dissociate in a Threshold-Dependent Way: A Controlled Test via Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35890)
*Adam Elimadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次受控实证检验：对抗训练会使内部表征和归因更集中，但因果电路的简洁性并非始终随之而来——在高忠实度要求下鲁棒模型更小，在低忠实度下并非如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常将稀疏/集中化的表征和归因视为可解释性或可逆向工程的证据，但尚无受控实证检验这些表征/归因简洁性是否对应更小或更易理解的因果电路。本文以对抗训练作为操纵变量来直接测试两者关系。

Method: 从同一 GPT-2 Small 检出点出发，进行配对的常规继续训练与对抗继续训练，保证两者在间接宾语识别（IOI）任务上保持能力并通过独立的鲁棒性验证。比较三个轴：稀疏自编码器（SAE）可分解性、SAE 特征在任务归因中的参与度、以及从原始计算图中恢复的“忠实”因果电路的边数。对 faithfulness 阈值做多点扫描并在第二语料上重复以检验泛化。

Result: 鲁棒模型在 SAE 可分解性和 SAE 特征参与度上更干净：更易被稀疏自编码器分解，且用于任务的 SAE 特征更少。因果电路尺寸呈阈值依赖性：在≤85% faithfulness 时，标准训练领先或并驾齐驱；在90%、95% 高忠实度时，鲁棒模型显著更小。代表性趋势在七点阈值扫描和第二语料上保持一致。

Conclusion: 对抗训练使模型在表征和归因上更“干净”（更可由稀疏自编码器分解且使用更少的 SAE 特征进行任务归因），但这并不一概导致更小的因果电路。电路规模与“faithfulness”（可拟合的忠实度）相关：在低忠实度阈值下，标准训练模型比或与鲁棒模型相当；在高忠实度（90%、95%）下，鲁棒模型需要明显更少的边。总体而言，表征/归因的简洁性并不是单调预测因果电路简洁性的充分条件，而是依赖于度量和精度的范畴。

Abstract: Sparse-autoencoder decomposability and concentrated feature attribution are increasingly treated as evidence that a model's computation is easier to reverse-engineer. Whether this representational and attributional cleanliness actually predicts a smaller or more tractable causal circuit remains an open question. We test this directly using adversarial training as a controlled instrument: it reliably reshapes internal representations, but this alone does not constitute a test of circuit size. We investigate this question through reverse-engineering complexity: the causal structure required to recover a model's behavior at a fixed level of faithfulness. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled empirical test of whether representational or attributional simplicity translates into causal simplicity at the circuit level. Starting from the same pretrained GPT-2 Small checkpoint, we apply matched standard and adversarial continual training, requiring both conditions to retain competence on indirect object identification and pass independent robustness verification before comparing mechanisms. We then compare the models along three complementary axes: sparse-autoencoder decomposability, SAE feature engagement in task attribution, and the size of faithful circuits recovered from the raw computational graph. The robust model is more SAE-decomposable and engages fewer SAE features in task attribution. Circuit size is regime-dependent: on competence-matched IOI, standard leads or ties below 85% faithfulness, but robust needs substantially fewer edges at high faithfulness (90%, 95%), a pattern established on the primary pair while representational trends generalize across a seven-point sweep and a second corpus.

</details>


### [44] [Self-discovering RL in the Era of Experience: Is Learning History an Asset or a Burden?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35897)
*Haomin Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: First causal/mechanistic audit of a self-discovered RL update rule using state-pin/transplant interventions: shows history-dependent scaling, clamping-induced penalties, and replay-turnover confounds; establishes an audit standard for future self-evolving RL algorithms.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To interrogate whether RL update rules discovered via algorithmic search (e.g., Disco103) actually support the cumulative, within-lifetime adaptation demanded by 'Era of Experience' principles, rather than being opaque black boxes that only perform well on benchmarks.

Method: A surgical causal audit: pinning, freezing, and transplanting recurrent states of a learned update rule while holding meta-parameters fixed; controlling replay retention and environmental turnover; comparing performance and adaptation windows against baselines (PPO, DQN) and replicating key checks on a second rule (OPEN).

Result: Three main empirical findings: (1) Recurrent history extends the effective reward-scale horizon (six-decade vs three under zero-pinning). (2) The harm from mismatched history is driven by perpetual clamping—allowing transplanted state to evolve reduces the penalty. (3) External data turnover (replay retention) can invert apparent adaptation advantages versus DQN, showing confounding between external data dynamics and internal plasticity. Results validated with capability thresholds and ported to OPEN.

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that a causal mechanistic audit of a self-discovered RL update rule is feasible and revealing: recurrent learning history materially changes learning dynamics (expanding usable reward scales, causing penalties when clamped, and interacting with external data turnover), and these effects generalize to a second rule, grounding macro-level RSI claims in micro-level plasticity behavior.

Abstract: The pursuit of recursive self-improvement (RSI) toward general intelligence is divided between macro-level language model scaling and the interaction-driven principles of "Era of Experience". Yet, any self-improving architecture ultimately rests upon its underlying optimization engine: if general intelligence requires learning from grounded interaction, the reinforcement learning (RL) update rule itself must be capable of cumulative adaptation. While algorithm self-discovery has produced Disco103 that surpassed PPO to achieve SOTA benchmark performance -- its internal update machinery remains an uninspected black box. We present the first causal mechanistic audit of a self-discovered RL rule, structured directly around the five pillars of the Era of Experience: extended horizon, grounded reward scales, continuing streams, within-lifetime change, and exploration depth. By surgically pinning, freezing, and transplanting recurrent states while holding meta-parameters fixed, we test when learning history acts as an asset or a burden. Three findings organize the audit: (1) Recurrent history actively expands usable reward scales, sustaining a six-decade window versus three under zero-pinning. (2) Decoupling historical content from its maintenance reveals that the penalty of mismatched history stems from perpetual clamping; allowing imported state to evolve naturally attenuates this burden. (3) Under environmental change, controlling replay retention reverses the apparent adaptation advantage over DQN, demonstrating that external data turnover can confound internal plasticity. Validated through capability thresholds and ported to a second rule (OPEN), this work grounds macro-RSI ambitions in micro-level learning dynamics, establishing a foundational audit standard for next-generation, self-evolving RL algorithms.

</details>


### [45] [Grab a Coffee: Future-Aware Guidance for Discrete Diffusion with Compiled Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35924)
*Hua,Xu,Dongxin Li,Gwen Yidou-Weng,Guy Van den Broeck,Wei Wang,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COFFEE is a neural-symbolic, plug-and-play framework that imposes sequence-level objectives on discrete diffusion by combining a carrier of marginal distributions with a compiled finite-state model, achieving controllable generation at inference time without retraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sequence-level objectives are hard to impose in discrete diffusion because the value of one unresolved token depends on other unresolved tokens, leading to exponential enumeration of completions. A plug-and-play, inference-time method that separates dependence from objective avoids this combinatorial blowup.

Method: At each diffusion step, a target-free carrier constructs a joint model over unresolved tokens from marginal token distributions predicted by the denoiser; a compiled finite-state model encodes the sequence-level preference (hard or soft). Pairing carrier and finite-state model states transfers global preferences to local positions and enables sampling of a guided reconstruction.

Result: COFFEE yields strong control across symbolic, language, and biological benchmarks, supports explicit hard constraints and learned soft objectives, and offers task-dependent trade-offs between sample quality and diversity. It brings joint conditioning, completion-weighted guidance, and optimization-based constraints into pretrained neural generation.

Conclusion: COFFEE enables sequence-level guidance for discrete diffusion models by decoupling token dependencies from the sequence-level objective, allowing global preferences and constraints to be enforced at inference time without retraining.

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models generate sequences by iteratively resolving multiple tokens in parallel, offering a flexible alternative to left-to-right generation. However, guiding this process with a sequence-level objective is difficult because the value of one unresolved token depends on the other tokens with which it can form a high-reward sequence. Enumerating all such completions makes the whole guidance computation grow exponentially with the number of unresolved positions. We introduce COFFEE, a plug-and-play framework that avoids this enumeration by separating sequence dependence from the objective. At each diffusion step, a target-free carrier absorbs the marginal token distributions predicted by the denoiser to construct a joint model over the unresolved tokens, while a compiled finite-state model records how their combinations affect the sequence-level preference. Pairing their states allows COFFEE to transfer global preferences to unresolved positions and sample a clean reconstruction without retraining the diffusion model. The same framework supports explicit hard constraints and learned soft objectives. We evaluate COFFEE across multiple symbolic, language, and biological benchmarks, where it achieves strong control results with task-dependent quality and diversity trade-offs. By making objectives available to inference rather than only evaluation, COFFEE brings joint conditioning, completion-weighted guidance, and optimization-based constraints into pretrained neural generation, showing the potential of neural-symbolic methods in diffusion guidance.

</details>


### [46] [Right Words, Wrong Moment: A Clinician-Grounded Analysis of Distress in 19,930 Conversations between Young People and ChatGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35953)
*Marx Wang,Ella Zhang,Cameron Tan,Andrea Mock,Songling Ngo,Zijing Wang,Robert Wolfe,Shirin Amouei,Rachel A. Hanebutt,Desmond C. Ong,Caroline Figueroa,Katie Davis,Anind K. Dey,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Young adults use ChatGPT when distressed; it elicits engagement and behavioral change but often escalates and rushes to solutions. Clinician review found process failures and informed a three-stage design guideline: assess safety, de-escalate emotionally, then explore concerns without endorsing them.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how young adults use general-purpose conversational agents like ChatGPT during distress, identify shortcomings from a clinician perspective, and produce actionable design recommendations to improve safety and usefulness.

Method: Mixed-methods: collected 19,930 ChatGPT conversations tied to survey data from 158 young adults; selected five conversations exemplifying distress; had ten clinicians review those five conversations; combined quantitative comparisons between distressed and non-distressed users with qualitative clinician feedback to derive design guidelines.

Result: Distressed participants reported greater emotional engagement and behavior change. ChatGPT tended to escalate emotional intensity and offer action-oriented advice prematurely. Clinicians endorsed access and phrasing but flagged seven process failures. Resulting design recommendations were structured into three stages for safer GPCA responses in distress.

Conclusion: Young adults use ChatGPT in moments of distress and report higher emotional engagement and behavioral changes; ChatGPT often responds with overly dramatic language and premature action-oriented suggestions. Clinicians find availability and wording valuable but identify seven process failures (e.g., rushing to solutions). The authors propose three-stage design guidelines: ask about safety, de-escalate to restore regulation, and explore concerns without agreeing.

Abstract: Young people increasingly turn to General-Purpose Conversational Agents (GPCAs), such as ChatGPT, in moments of distress. We examine young adults' (ages 18-25) experiences using ChatGPT. We first collected 19,930 ChatGPT conversations and survey data from 158 young adults. We then selected five example conversations reflecting user distress. Finally, we asked ten clinicians to review those five conversations. We found distressed participants reported greater emotional engagement with ChatGPT and greater behavioral change from using it than their peers. When they turned to ChatGPT in moments of acute distress, ChatGPT was quick to give overly dramatic responses and excessive action-oriented suggestions. Clinicians endorsed ChatGPT's availability and much of its wording, but identified seven process failures, such as prematurely jumping to solutions. We translated clinicians' feedback into design guidelines following three stages: 1) asking about safety, 2) de-escalating intensity to restore emotional regulation, and 3) exploring concerns without agreeing with them.

</details>


### [47] [SAGE: A Statistical Acceptance Gate for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36043)
*Yihao Wang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Zhaofeng Zhang,Hongyu Wu,Yang Yang,Jinglu He,Yu Guo,Kai Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE 用逐项成对比较+单侧配对检验替换朴素聚合门控，保守但更可靠地接受改动，大幅降低回归并提升总体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我演化循环的门控仅基于聚合验证分数，导致两类问题：一是允许永久回归（提升平均分但破坏已解项）；二是受“优化者诅咒”（对有限有噪音验证集上的最优观测值存在上偏）影响。需要更保守且统计可核查的接受准则。

Method: 提出两步改进：1) 对每个验证条目进行成对比较（当前技能 vs 编辑后技能），以显式揭露并不对称惩罚破坏已有已解项的回归；2) 使用单侧配对统计检验，仅当“胜利”对“失败”具有统计可靠性时才接受编辑，否则弃权。该门控在边界设置下与标准聚合门控一致。

Result: 在五个基准、四个主干 LLM、等预算协议下，SAGE 在20个设置中19个显著降低回归率（例如 LiveMath：36.5%→0%，OfficeQA：42.8%→0%），并在所有20个设置中达到最高最终分（例如 LiveMath：34.15→48.78）。

Conclusion: SAGE 是一个保守且统计显著性驱动的接受门控机制，通过成对逐项比较和单侧配对检验，显著降低自我演化代理在接受技能文档编辑时产生的回归风险，同时提升最终任务表现。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents increasingly self-evolve by editing a persistent skill document that encodes their workflow, tool-use rules, and decision logic. This loop has two steps, an optimizer that proposes a candidate edit and a gate that accepts or rejects it. Prior work has concentrated on the optimizer, while the gate still follows a naive rule that keeps any edit which improves an aggregate validation score. We show that this rule fails in two ways. First, it admits permanent regressions, since an edit can raise the average while breaking items the skill already solves. Second, it is vulnerable to the Optimizer's Curse, since the best observed score on a finite and noisy validation set is upward biased. To solve the above two limitations, we propose a statistical acceptance gate for self-evolving agents (SAGE). Compared with previous work, SAGE has two contributions. First, SAGE proposes a per-item paired comparison that evaluates the current skill and the edited skill on identical validation items, which exposes regressions that an aggregate score hides and penalizes them asymmetrically. Second, SAGE also employs a one-sided paired test that commits an edit only when its wins are statistically reliable against its losses, and it abstains otherwise. SAGE is a conservative refinement of the standard gate that recovers the baseline exactly at a boundary setting. It commits only a subset of the baseline's edits, filtering out those whose gains are unreliable or purchased by breaking already-solved items. Across five benchmarks and four backbone LLMs under an equal-budget protocol, SAGE lowers the regression rate in 19 of 20 settings and matches the baseline in the remaining one, for example from 36.5% to 0% on LiveMath and from 42.8% to 0% on OfficeQA with DeepSeek-V4. SAGE also attains the highest final score in all 20 settings, raising LiveMath from 34.15 to 48.78.

</details>


### [48] [PowerZooJax: A JAX-based Power System Benchmark for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36052)
*Zhanhua Pan,Xiao Liu,Zhilong Cao,Jianhong Wang,Dawei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PowerZooJax 用 JAX 将电力系统仿真端到端搬到 GPU 上，提供五个 CMDP 任务并显著加速 RL 训练与统一评估，从而便于大规模、标准化的电力系统运维研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力系统的 RL 环境范围狭窄且依赖 CPU 仿真，限制了大规模训练与系统化比较。需要一个高性能、跨场景、可复现的基准以促进 RL 在电力系统运维中的研究和评估。

Method: 将电力潮流、经济调度、市场清算与设备动力学等核心计算用 JAX 重写为可构建计算图的模块，使得整个训练/评估流程可在 GPU 上端到端运行；提供五个跨发电、输电、配电、分布式能源和数据中心微电网的 CMDP 任务，并在基准中记录策略回报、安全违例和分布外应力测试结果以完成标准化评估。

Result: 在实验中与 CPU 仿真相比获得了显著的加速（大幅缩短训练与评估时间）；展示了在统一度量下对策略回报、安全违例及 OOD 情况的评测能力；并通过开源仓库降低复现实验门槛，支持大规模评估与比较。

Conclusion: PowerZooJax 是一个基于 JAX 的电力系统运维强化学习基准库，能够在 GPU 上完成训练与评估循环，提供五类受限马尔可夫决策过程任务，显著加速仿真并实现统一的策略回报、安全违例与分布外应力测试评估。该库已开源，可支持大规模 RL 实验。

Abstract: Power system operation is a safety-critical sequential decision-making problem, making it a natural testbed for reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing RL environments for power systems are often narrow in scope and computationally limited by CPU-based simulation workflows, making large-scale evaluation difficult. We introduce PowerZooJax, a JAX-based benchmark suite for RL in power system operation. It provides five constrained Markov decision process tasks spanning generation, transmission, distribution, distributed energy resources, and data center microgrid. By rewriting power flow, economic dispatch, market clearing, and device dynamics as JAX computation graphs, PowerZooJax keeps the entire training and evaluation loop on the GPU. Experiments show substantial speedups over CPU-based simulations and demonstrate standardized evaluation of policy returns, safety violations, and out-of-distribution stress conditions. Our open-source benchmark is available at: https://github.com/powerzoojax/PowerZooJax.

</details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: In urban low-altitude flight, buildings reshape ambient wind into spatially varying 3D flow, making unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) energy depend on local wind exposure as well as path length. However, building-resolved wind information is rarely available when a mission must be planned. Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) can produce high-fidelity urban flow fields, but each simulation is tied to a fixed inflow boundary condition and can take hours to days, which is incompatible with urban UAV missions that typically last minutes to tens of minutes. We present GeoWind2Plan, a geometry-to-wind-to-planning framework for mission-time 3D urban wind prediction and energy-efficient UAV planning. Given only a background wind vector, 3D building geometry, and a start-goal pair, GeoWind2Plan transforms the building geometry into a reference-wind frame, predicts mission-relevant 3D wind patches with a localized geometry-conditioned neural operator, stitches them into a queryable local wind field, and optimizes a feasible 3D path and speed profile using a physically grounded UAV energy model. Rather than pursuing CFD-perfect reconstruction, GeoWind2Plan targets decision-useful wind prediction: trajectories are planned with predicted wind and evaluated under high-fidelity CFD wind. Across held-out urban domains, wind speeds, and mission wind-angle regimes, GeoWind2Plan performs corridor-localized wind inference in about 3 seconds, compared with roughly 8 hours for CFD. Under CFD evaluation, trajectories planned with GeoWind2Plan reduce energy by 6.9%, 12.7%, and 4.5% in tailwind, headwind, and crosswind missions relative to wind-agnostic planning, recovering 87.9%, 85.7%, and 75.0% of CFD-reference savings. These results show that fast, corridor-localized 3D urban wind prediction can make wind-aware UAV energy planning practical at mission time.

</details>


### [50] [Mirror-Score: Calibrated, Inference-only Scoring Exposes the Limits of Sequence-compatibility Ranking in D-peptide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36057)
*Jiada Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个31例的异手性复合物基准，提出Mirror-Score：基于Boltz-2镜像空间共折叠置信度和接口pLDDT的校准推断评分，证明ProteinMPNN原始NLL不能可靠预测亲和力，并推荐针对靶点家族进行校准，同时给出LasR和LecB的前瞻设计协议和开源资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有D-肽设计工具（如Mirror-Peptidizer）使用ProteinMPNN的NLL进行序列排序，但未验证其与实验亲和力的关联，且在先前测试中命中率不高（如仅4/9 MDM2设计可检测结合），因此需要开发可靠的、可校准的推断评分以更好地在异手性体系中预测亲和力并指导设计。

Method: 构建公开基准集（31个晶体结构，含18个有亲和力数据的复合物），评估ProteinMPNN原始NLL与实验亲和力的相关性，提出并采用Mirror-Score：基于镜像空间的Boltz-2共折叠置信度和接口pLDDT等指标的推断评分。对viral-entry家族进行结构级留一交叉验证以评估接口pLDDT作为亲和力排序的性能。同时提供前瞻性设计协议（镜像结构、配体衍生热点图、扩散模型输入）并开源代码和数据。

Result: 发现原始ProteinMPNN NLL不是有效的亲和力排序指标（总体Spearman rho=0.19），并在不同靶点间相关性方向相反（MDM2/CHIP正相关0.62，gp41负相关-0.70）。对于病毒入侵家族（7结构，3肽类），接口pLDDT在结构级留一交叉验证中达到Spearman rho=0.90（p=0.006），能正确对三种肽按亲和力排序，而NLL失败（rho=0.18）。鉴于样本化学类型仅三种，该结果表明方向性一致性但并未做到跨家族的统计验证。结论还指出当前样本量下跨家族校准不可迁移，建议在实际应用中采用家族匹配的校准策略，并提供了两靶点的前瞻设计方案与开源资源。

Conclusion: 本论文提出并验证了Mirror-Score，一种用于异手性（D-肽/L-蛋白）复合物的校准推断评分框架，弥补了ProteinMPNN原始负对数似然（NLL）无法可靠预测亲和力的不足。Mirror-Score基于Boltz-2镜像空间的共折叠置信度，并在公开的31个晶体复合物基准（涵盖四类靶点，含18个已验证亲和力的数据）上进行评估。作者还为两种抗生素抗性靶点（Pseudomonas aeruginosa的LasR和LecB）提供了前瞻性设计协议和相关资源。

Abstract: D-peptides combine protease resistance with high target specificity, but computational design of D-peptide binders remains immature. Mirror-Peptidizer introduced an in silico mirror-image screening pipeline using target reflection, backbone generation, and ProteinMPNN sequence design, but its raw ProteinMPNN negative log-likelihood (NLL) ranking was not validated against measured affinities, and only 4 of 9 tested MDM2 designs bound detectably. We introduce Mirror-Score, a calibrated, inference-only scoring framework for heterochiral D-peptide/L-protein complexes, and a public benchmark of 31 crystal complexes across four target families, including 18 with literature-verified affinities. Raw ProteinMPNN NLL is not a valid affinity ranker: its pooled Spearman correlation with affinity is 0.19, and correlations reverse between MDM2/CHIP (+0.62) and gp41 (-0.70). We therefore evaluate Boltz-2 mirror-space cofolding confidence. For the complete viral-entry family (7 structures representing 3 peptides), interface predicted local distance difference test (pLDDT) achieves structure-level leave-one-out Spearman rho = 0.90 (p = 0.006) and correctly orders all three peptides by affinity, whereas NLL fails (structure-level rho = 0.18). Because only three independent chemotypes are represented, this result indicates directional consistency rather than a statistically validated predictor. Cross-family calibration does not transfer at current sample sizes, supporting family-matched calibration as the practical deployment mode. We also specify a prospective design protocol for the antimicrobial-resistance targets LasR and LecB from Pseudomonas aeruginosa, including mirrored structures, ligand-derived hotspot maps, diffusion-model-ready inputs, and Mirror-Score ranking. Code, benchmark data, structures, and analysis scripts are openly available at https://github.com/Jiadalee/Mirror-Score.

</details>


### [51] [SMat-Attention: Structured Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36062)
*Emile Anand,Abdullah Ateyeh,Archer Wang,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入以VC-维数为参数的结构化因果mask（SMat-Attention），在灵活性与效率之间提供可调权衡，证明了子二次预填和常数时间流式解码的复杂度界，且在若干检索与小规模语言建模实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文序列模型在灵活的token级交互（代价为二次复杂度）与高效的线性注意力（代价低但压缩历史信息）之间存在根本权衡。目标是设计一个可调控的结构化机制，使模型在访问模式复杂度、预填代价与解码内存之间进行有意识的权衡。

Method: 构造了一族行支持具有VC-维数d的因果mask（SMat-Attention），通过‘subset-routing’硬路由与学习路由（top-k查询）两种实现。给出分块(chunkwise)前向和反向算法以利于硬件效率；在硬路由构造下证明了对长度T序列的计算开销为O(T^{2-3/d}+T)，并证明在固定时域流式场景下，用O(T^{1-1/d})个缓存状态可实现远端前缀后的常数时间解码。还把学习到的路由集成到Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet骨干并做了实验评估。

Result: 理论方面：把VC-维数作为控制访问模式复杂度的明确旋钮，给出相应的时间与空间上界。算法方面：提供硬路由和学习路由的实现与分块算法。实证方面：subset-routing和rule-assisted多键检索展示了路由表达力；在若干设置下，将学习路由用于Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet能保持子二次预填、提高召回精度，并在小规模LM任务上取得可比性能。

Conclusion: SMat-Attention提出了一类以VC-维数d为参数的结构化因果mask，通过在“软注意力（quadratic）”与“线性注意力（linear）”间引入可调的路由结构来平衡访问灵活性与计算/解码复杂度。该方法在理论上给出子二次预填（prefill）复杂度与常数时间流式解码的保证，并在若干检索与小规模语言建模实验中展示了可行性。

Abstract: Long-context sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff: softmax attention uses flexible token-level interactions at quadratic cost, whereas linear attention obtains linear-time training and constant-time decoding by compressing history into a fixed-size state. In this work, we ask whether we can connect these regimes through a tunable notion of structure. To this end, we introduce Structured Matrix Attention (SMat-Attention) via a family of causal masks with structured long-range routing whose row supports have VC-dimension $d$. In our construction, $d=1$ recovers the standard causal mask, and increasing $d$ permits richer subset-routing patterns. We give chunkwise forward and backward algorithms to enable hardware-efficiency. For sequences of length $T$, the hard-routing construction takes $O(T^{2-3/d}+T)$ work, despite the mask being dense, for our prescribed family. In fixed-horizon streaming, decoding after the distant prefix takes constant time per token using $O(T^{1-1/d})$ cached states. SMat-Attention therefore makes VC-dimension an explicit knob governing access-pattern complexity, prefill cost, and decoding memory. Empirically, subset-routing and rule-assisted multi-key retrieval experiments illustrate the masks' routing expressiveness. Extensions to Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet using learned routing with top-$k$ query reads retain subquadratic prefill, improve recall accuracy over the backbones in several settings, and achieve comparable small-scale language-modeling performance.

</details>


### [52] [LongCat-DeepResearch Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36071)
*Meituan LongCat Team,He Zhu,Yue Xu,Wanli Wu,Haolin Ren,Yuxin Bian,Jiarui Zhao,Rongzhi Zhang,Quanchi Weng,Jinghao Cui,Yu Fan,Yuhan Liu,Yunhu Ye,Jiyuan Ren,Fengcheng Yuan,Zhao Yang,Jiacheng Zhang,Yuchuan Dai,Ruixuan Xiao,Haozhe Sun,Xiangyuan Liu,Cheng Sun,Yao Du,Yiming Hao,Hongbo Guo,Shuo He,Lei Wang,Xunliang Cai,Yan Chen,Fan Yang,Lingchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以规划/研究/审校分工的多智能体写作流水线（LongCat-DeepResearch），在若干基准上得到竞争性效果并支持训练任务构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动生成长篇、证据基于的研究报告时的协调性与重复重写问题，提高章节级别编辑效率，并为通用模型的中期/后期训练提供任务轨迹。

Method: 采用两层多智能体流程：一组规划(agent)并行探索外部来源、生成精炼的可执行研究计划(ResearchSpec)；另一组研究(agent)并行在各自上下文内收集证据并草拟各章节；最终通过全局审查驱动目标性局部修订，支持中后期训练轨迹的构建。

Result: 在多个基准上表现良好：DeepResearchBench 55.25，DeepResearchBench II 51.35，ResearchRubrics 79.83；内部基准得分 76.04（在四个系统中排名第二）。开发集分析表明：多视角规划有益，但进一步细化规划效果不一；额外编辑能在两个基准上提升平均自动可读性偏好，但各基准趋势不同。

Conclusion: LongCat-DeepResearch 提出并验证了一个将增强型 LongCat 模型与多智能体分工工作流结合的研究写作系统，能通过分层规划与局部修订减少全文重复重写，生成证据支撑的综合报告，并可用于构建模型训练任务与轨迹。

Abstract: We present LongCat-DeepResearch, a deep research system that combines an enhanced LongCat model with a multi-agent workflow for producing comprehensive, evidence-grounded reports. The workflow separates global planning from detailed investigation and coordinates revision at the section level. Multiple planning agents first explore external sources and refine an actionable research plan, termed ResearchSpec. Research agents then investigate and draft their assigned sections in parallel, gathering additional evidence in separate contexts as their analyses develop. Once the sections are assembled, global review guides targeted local revisions, reducing reliance on repeated full-report rewriting. This workflow also supports the construction of research tasks and trajectories for the mid-training and post-training of LongCat's general-purpose models. LongCat-DeepResearch achieves 55.25 on DeepResearchBench, 51.35 on DeepResearchBench II, and 79.83 on ResearchRubrics. On an in-house benchmark, it scores 76.04, ranking second among four compared systems. Development-set analyses show benefits from combining planning perspectives, while further planning refinement has mixed effects. Additional editing improves average automatic readability preference across two benchmarks, with different trends on each.

</details>


### [53] [A Polyphonic Conception of AI Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36079)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM的能力来自多重并行机制的联盟而非唯一机制。应把“理解”归因为在任务中可靠被调动并控制输出的“健全电路”，以便做出可检验的信任判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需对AI输出作可信性判断的实际场景（如医学、司法、工程）中，纯数学/统计描述常无法区分可信与不可信输出，因而仍隐含“模型是否理解”的问题。传统“理解”概念基于单声道范式，无法反映LLM内部复杂的并行机制分布，导致信任评估困难或危险。

Method: 通过汇聚机械论证据（神经网络内部并行机制、冗余/互补路径、条件性激活、可替代的功能群体等），作者考察模型内部生成输出的不同“联盟”（coalitions），并分析这些联盟如何在任务中互补、重复或相互掩盖。基于这些观察，提出概念性框架并给出理解归属的操作化方向（健全电路）。

Result: 提出“多声道（polyphonic）”的理解观并指出：单声道推断（以为存在单一可定位的理解机制）容易误导。作为替代，提出以“健全电路”为核心的理解归属：可被识别、稳健且在控制输出时被正确调动的内部组织单元。该框架使对理解的归属成为关于内部组织的可检验主张，从而成为指导信任的依据。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：大型语言模型（LLM）的“理解”不是由单一、局部化机制（单声道）提供的，而是由多重并行、可靠性不均的机制联合（多声道）产生的。因而应以“健全电路（sound circuitry）”——在任务中可可靠、正确被调动并控制输出的内部组织——作为可操作的理解归属标准，便于指导对AI输出的信任判定。

Abstract: When a doctor, a judge, or an engineer must decide whether to trust an AI model's output, they cannot avoid asking what the model understands. Purely mathematical or statistical descriptions struggle to distinguish trustworthy from untrustworthy outputs without reintroducing the question of AI understanding in all but name. Yet the question is ill-framed as it stands, because the inherited concept operates within a monophonic paradigm: the idea that a cognitive system's understanding of something must be localised to a single mechanism underpinning all the capacities conferred by such understanding. Drawing on a wide range of mechanistic evidence, we show that LLMs are pervasively polyphonic: outputs emerge from coalitions of parallel mechanisms of uneven reliability, which variously complement, duplicate, or drown out one another, with several coalitions sufficing for a task without any one being indispensable. Polyphony not only complicates attributions of understanding, but renders monophonic inference patterns hazardous. In response, we develop a conception of understanding fit for polyphonic AI. It centres on sound circuitry that is reliably and correctly recruited and in control of outputs. Attributions of understanding thereby become tractable claims about internal organisation, and can do the work of guiding trust in AI.

</details>


### [54] [GeoOutageBench: Benchmarking Ambiguity-aware, Ontology-grounded Geospatiotemporal KGQA for Multimodal Power Outage and Resilience Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36082)
*Ethan D. Frakes,Amy Kvien,Rishabh Kundu,Redad Mehdi,Van D. Tran,Vibha S. Mandayam,Kristopher O. Davis,Erika I. Barcelos,Roger H. French,Yinghui Wu,Mengjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoOutageBench 是一个面向地理时空多模态断电/韧性分析的 KGQA 基准，强调 NL→SPARQL、领域本体效用与多模态答案精度的评测，并开源数据与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KGQA 基准多面向通用网页知识，缺乏对时空、多模态与基础设施中断情景的覆盖；实际韧性分析需处理复杂时空关系、视觉与传感器证据并解析歧义问题，因此需要专门基准与评测工具。

Method: 构建一个融合遥感、文本、结构化中断记录、气象与风暴事件、配电/地理实体与领域本体的时空多模态知识图谱；定义分层的能力型查询分类（包含时空包含、邻近、共现、多模态证据与假设评估等难度等级）；设计用户可配置的评估流程，检验三类任务：NL→SPARQL 解释（模糊/歧义理解）、本体对查询的驱动性评估与多模态 KGQA 的检索与答案准确率。

Result: 提出并实现 GeoOutageBench，包括时空多模态 KG、分级问法分类、可配置评估管线与示例数据集与代码；为评估 LLM-KG 系统在现实基础设施韧性任务中的能力提供实证平台（论文提供仓库链接以便复现）。

Conclusion: GeoOutageBench 为基于大模型的地理时空多模态知识图谱问答（KGQA）提供了一个专门的评测框架，侧重电力中断与基础设施韧性分析，覆盖从自然语言到 SPARQL 的解析、领域本体的效用评估和多模态检索答案准确性。基准、源码与数据已开源，可作为现实世界韧性分析系统评估的基石。

Abstract: We introduce GeoOutageBench, a benchmark for assessing LLM-based geospatiotemporal KGQA for multimodal outage and resilience analysis. Unlike existing KGQA benchmarks for Web knowledge, GeoOutageBench considers a spatiotemporal KG that integrates visual, textual, and structured data from outage records, remote sensing, weather observations, storm and power events, geographic entities, and domain ontologies. It provides a competency query taxonomy at different difficulty levels from spatiotemporal containment and proximity, spatiotemporal co-occurrence analysis, multimodal evidence, to hypothetical evaluation. Over multimodal KG and query classes, GeoOutageBench provides user-configurable evaluation of three important, highly coherent yet less studied tasks: (1) LLMs' understanding for ambiguous geospatiotemporal questions in terms of NL to SPARQL interpretation, (2) query-driven assessment of ontology utility, and (3) answer accuracy of multimodal KGQA retrieval. GeoOutageBench provides a design principle and foundation for assessing LLM-KG systems that support real-world infrastructure resilience analysis. Our benchmark, source code, data, results, and other documentation are available at https://github.com/UCF-SAGE/GeoOutageBench.

</details>


### [55] [An Exact Generate - Transform Decomposition of Small-LLM Team Scaling Across Orchestration Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36104)
*Blaz Bertalanic,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 团队扩展能创造更多候选（coverage机会），但只有部分架构在部分任务上能把这些机会转为准确率，故团队扩展的效益高度任务与架构依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估用一组协作LLM代理替代单一LLM能否提升准确率，并弄清随团队规模扩展是否连续获利、以及应扩展哪种架构。

Method: 在五个指令微调的7–9B模型上，对八种代理编排架构进行系统性横扫，覆盖五个短答案基准（GSM8K、GSMHard、ARC、GPQA、MMLU）和一个可执行代码基准，调用预算扩展到30次；并提出精确的‘生成-变换（generate-transform）’分解以解析准确率变化的来源。

Result: 从3到30次调用：算术基准上准确率最多上升≈17点（GSM8K、GSMHard）；ARC/GPQA/MMLU上最多≈4点。Proposer-Critic在算术任务上扩展最陡并在最大预算下总体领先，但在其它任务表现靠后；无单一架构在所有任务上取胜。用‘生成-变换’分解可将准确率变化精确拆为覆盖（coverage）扩展的广度性红利与下游变换（transform）效能的强度性变化。等预算下token成本变化达2.1倍。

Conclusion: 团队扩展不是通用增益杠杆；其效果高度依赖任务与编排架构。对于算术类问题，扩大量（calls）并配合评审-变换（Proposer-Critic）架构能显著提升准确率；但在多选类或开放式代码生成任务上，覆盖或转换能力受限，收益有限或仅与覆盖相符。

Abstract: Replacing one LLM agent with a collaborating team can raise accuracy, but whether scaling the team helps, and which architecture to scale, is unclear. Sweeping eight agent orchestration architectures across five instruction-tuned 7-9B models, five short-answer benchmarks, and an executable-code benchmark up to 30 calls, we find that the returns to team scaling are sharply task-dependent: from three to thirty calls accuracy rises by up to 17 points on the two arithmetic word-problem benchmarks (GSM8K, GSMHard) but by at most four on ARC, GPQA, and MMLU, for every architecture, a split the usual task-averaged number conceals. Proposer-Critic captures the arithmetic gains, scaling steepest and, in aggregate, surpassing every other architecture at the largest budget (item-clustered intervals exclude zero), though it ranks among the weakest elsewhere, and no architecture wins across tasks.
  We explain these trajectories with an exact generate-transform decomposition. Partitioning any workflow into proposal coverage and a downstream transform, any accuracy change splits exactly into an extensive coverage dividend and an intensive transformation change. The decomposition diagnoses each task: arithmetic offers coverage headroom that a critic-guided transform converts, whereas the multiple-choice benchmarks either saturate in coverage or fail to convert it, and on open-ended code generative recovery nearly vanishes so accuracy tracks coverage. At equal call budgets token cost still varies 2.1x. Extra calls therefore create candidate opportunity that only some architectures, on some tasks, convert. Team scaling is a task- and architecture-specific bet, not a uniform lever.

</details>


### [56] [The Layer Mystery of VLA: An Information-Theoretical Analysis of VLA Latent Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36118)
*Yuxiang Liu,Lizhi Yang,Fengze Xie,Aaron Ames,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在冻结的预训练视觉语言骨干上，最优单层接口往往优于多层融合；用Action-conditioned InfoNCE作为层质量代理能高效、稳定地选择层，极大节省计算并保持近似最优策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在VLA策略中，通过预训练视觉语言骨干到动作头的潜在接口暴露哪个层并不清楚；不同层可能含有不同任务相关表征，错误选择会显著降低策略性能，而穷尽搜索代价高昂，需找到高效的层选择方法。

Method: 在三个预训练视觉语言模型和两个操作基准（LIBERO, CALVIN）上，对冻结骨干进行单层选择与多层融合的系统性实验。比较三种融合机制、三种层子集策略和三种随机种子，总计54配置。提出并解析信息瓶颈目标下的Action-conditioned InfoNCE与动作预测的加权等价性；评估四种代理指标与策略成功率的相关性。

Result: 47/54配置中多层融合不及最佳单层；最佳层随骨干和基准显著变化；统计检验显示融合优势有限；InfoNCE与最终策略成功率的相关性最稳定，使用InfoNCE选择层比穷尽策略训练节省9–33倍GPU计算，将平均选择遗憾从深层固定选择的17.89个百分点降到3.71点，接近使用所有基准回溯优化的固定层启发式的3.28–3.50点。

Conclusion: 多层融合并未普遍优于最佳单层接口；选择暴露哪个层非常重要但昂贵；InfoNCE 可作为有效的层质量代理，显著减少计算并降低选择遗憾，接近离线优化的固定层启发式效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies connect a pretrained vision-language backbone to an action head through a latent interface, but which backbone layers this interface should expose remains unclear. We study single-layer selection and multi-layer fusion for frozen backbones across three pretrained models and two manipulation benchmarks, LIBERO and CALVIN, with three policy-training seeds per configuration. Across three fusion mechanisms and three layer-subset strategies, 47 of 54 configurations underperform the best observed single-layer policy. Our stastical analysis further confirms that fusion's advantage is very limited. However, the best layer varies substantially across backbones and benchmarks, making layer selection consequential and exhaustive policy sweeps expensive. We further derive a reweighting equivalence between the proposed information-bottleneck objectives for action-conditioned InfoNCE and action prediction, motivating InfoNCE as a proxy for layer quality. Empirically, InfoNCE provides the most consistent positive association with policy success among four evaluated proxies. Selecting the layer with the highest InfoNCE score requires 9-33 times less GPU compute than exhaustive policy sweeps and reduces mean selection regret from 17.89 percentage points for deepest-layer selection to 3.71 points across six settings. Its mean regret is close to the 3.28-3.50 points achieved by fixed-layer heuristics optimized retrospectively using all six oracle sweeps, without requiring closed-loop evaluations during selection.

</details>


### [57] [AdaST: Adaptive Coupling for Spatial-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36119)
*Zhenyu Lei,Chenghao Liu,Yushun Dong,Qi R. Wang,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决时空预测中耦合异质性，AdaST提出分解-重组框架，利用异质专家和相关性自适应重组器动态调节时空建模，从而在多种耦合模式下实现更鲁棒的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时空数据存在不同耦合机制（时间主导、空间主导、强耦合），现有方法统一建模会引入虚假依赖，性能在主导关联存在时下降，因此需动态调节时空建模。

Method: 提出了decompose-recompose范式：使用异质专家将输入分解为不同耦合模式的分量；用角色对齐模块对各分量建模；用基于相关性的自适应重组器将分量融合用于最终预测。

Result: 在多组实验中AdaST显著优于最先进基线，说明自适应方法能更好应对耦合异质性；并通过消融和可视化验证了分解、角色对齐和相关性重组的必要性。

Conclusion: AdaST通过自适应地分解-重组时空信息，显著提升在不同耦合模式下的预测性能，验证了按耦合结构动态调制模型的重要性。

Abstract: Spatial-temporal (ST) forecasting underpins many real-world systems such as traffic, climate, and energy networks. While existing methods implicitly assume strong spatiotemporal coupling, we observe that real-world ST data exhibits distinct coupling regimes, ranging from temporal-dominated and spatial-dominated to strongly coupled patterns. This mismatch causes current models to suffer from spurious dependencies and degraded performance when one correlation dominates. To overcome this limitation, we aim to dynamically modulate spatial and temporal modeling based on the data's inherent coupling structure. However, three key challenges exist: unknown coupling structure, heterogeneous coupling dynamics, and suboptimal spatial modeling. We propose AdaST, an adaptive ST forecasting framework that tackles these challenges through a decompose-recompose paradigm. AdaST factorizes inputs into components capturing different coupling patterns using heterogeneity-aware experts. Each component is processed by role-aligned modules, and a correlation-informed adaptive recomposer integrates them for final prediction. Extensive experiments confirm that AdaST significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the necessity of an adaptive approach.

</details>


### [58] [Memory Is a Derivation: The Distributed-Evidence Paradox in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36130)
*Hongjun Liu,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DerivAudit 将记忆与写入时历史的推导问题制度化审计：证据扩展能恢复多数表面无支持的记忆，但仍有显著比例不被支持，且写入时的接纳可靠性不由证据扩展自动保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的 LLM 代理将交互历史压缩为持久记忆以便重用，但压缩或引用缺失可能导致记忆与历史之间出现推导不合——既可能是真实支持却未被引用，也可能是由个别事实拼凑出历史未确立的更强断言。需要判定记忆在写入时是否由当时可得历史推导而来。

Method: 定义三项耦合需求（证据范围、组合有效性、写入可靠性），设计 DerivAudit 框架将审计拆分为三类问题：检查作者提供的引用是否遗漏支持证据；检测合成的记忆是否引入不被历史支持的含义；评估写入时的接纳决策如何影响后续使用。对两个自然记忆语料进行实证：比较引用内证据与更广泛的写入前历史、用多种验证模型评估支持性并统计恢复率与残留不支持率。

Result: 通过扩大写入时可用历史，审计为近60% 在引用层面看似不受支持的记忆找回了支持；但仍有约17–21% 的记忆在扩展后仍不受支持。同时，即便扩展证据，未经支持的记忆仍常被写入/接纳，且在两个验证骨干上证据扩展反而降低了接纳判别的可靠性。

Conclusion: DerivAudit 能有效揭示持久记忆与写入时交互历史之间的推导差距；通过扩展写入时可用的证据，约60% 看似无支持的记忆可以被找回支持，但仍有17–21% 在扩展后仍不受支持，且扩展证据并不能保证写入决策的可靠性（在部分骨干模型上反而恶化）。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents compress past interactions into persistent memories that may be reused as premises for later tasks. This creates a distinct derivation problem: whether the memory actually follows from what the interaction history supports. Relevant evidence may be scattered across earlier interactions, while compression can introduce relations or event status that the history never established.
