A valid memory may therefore appear unsupported because its citations omit relevant evidence, while individually supported facts may be composed into a stronger statement the history never established. We characterize this problem through three coupled requirements: (1) Evidence scope; (2) Compositional validity; (3) Admission reliability. We therefore ask whether the interaction history available at write time supports what enters persistent memory. We introduce DerivAudit, a framework for auditing whether a memory is actually supported by the history available when it was written. The audit separates three questions: whether supporting evidence lies beyond writer-provided citations, whether the composed memory introduces unsupported meaning, and how write-time admission decisions affect later memory use. Across two natural memory corpora, audits using broader pre-write history recover support for nearly 60% of memories that appear unsupported from citations alone, while 17-21% remain unsupported after expansion. Yet broader evidence does not by itself make admission reliable: unsupported memories are still frequently admitted across verification models, and evidence expansion alone worsens it on two backbones.

</details>


### [59] [More Features Are Not More Evidence: Limits of Training-Free Human Activity Recognition with Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36154)
*Orhan Konak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Zero-shot general-purpose decisions on sensor data fail to reach supervised HAR accuracy. Representation format is critical; evaluation must include the sensor-to-model interface rather than treating preprocessing as neutral.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether general-purpose training-free models can directly interpret deterministic descriptions of physical sensor signals well enough to replace or complement supervised HAR classifiers, thereby reducing reliance on labeled data.

Method: Evaluate Jev (a fixed probabilistic decision model) on 1,800 class-balanced accelerometer windows from three datasets (WISDM, UCI341, PAMAP2) without labels, retrieval context, or task-specific updates. Test three deterministic sensor representations, compare 5,400 Jev decisions against a generative baseline and three supervised HAR models; measure macro-F1, calibration, and post-hoc fusion effects.

Result: Jev's best macro-F1 per dataset: 0.038 (WISDM), 0.118 (UCI341), 0.089 (PAMAP2); supervised models range 0.686–0.907. Increasing numerical features worsened performance; adding deterministic semantic rendering partially recovered recognition; Jev's probabilities poorly calibrated; post-hoc fusion yielded a small WISDM improvement only.

Conclusion: Fixed general-purpose model Jev cannot match supervised HAR performance when applied zero-shot to deterministic sensor representations; performance depends strongly on how sensor data are represented, and semantic rendering can partially recover but does not close the gap. The sensor-to-model interface must be treated as part of evaluation.

Abstract: General-purpose models promise sensor-based decisions without training a task-specific classifier, which could reduce the dependence of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) on labeled data. Yet it remains unclear whether such models can directly interpret deterministic descriptions of physical sensor signals well enough to replace or complement trained HAR models. We study this question using Jev, a fixed general-purpose probabilistic decision model, on 1,800 class-balanced accelerometer windows from WISDM, UCI341, and PAMAP2. Jev receives no labeled examples, retrieval context, or HAR-specific parameter updates. We evaluate three deterministic sensor representations and compare 5,400 Jev decisions with a generative baseline and three supervised HAR models. Jev remains far below supervised recognition, with its strongest representation reaching macro-F1 of 0.038, 0.118, and 0.089 across the three datasets, compared with 0.686 to 0.907 for the supervised models. More numerical features do not improve Jev. Instead, they reduce recognition on all three datasets, while augmenting the same numerical evidence with a deterministic semantic rendering partially recovers performance, although the experiment does not isolate semantics from the accompanying serialization and redundancy changes. Jev is fast and inexpensive to query, but its probabilities are not reliably calibrated for recognition. A post-hoc fusion analysis finds a small improvement on WISDM that does not replicate on UCI341 or PAMAP2. These results show that training-free sensor decisions depend not only on the information available in the signal, but also on whether the model can use the representation through which that information is exposed. The sensor-to-model interface should therefore be treated as part of the model evaluation rather than as a neutral preprocessing step.

</details>


### [60] [Principled Thoughts for Latent Recursive LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36159)
*Fahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REST 通过对隐含“思维”引入四项可微约束（因果、最小、可分、稳定），在不改推理架构与推理时成本的情况下，提升准确率、加速收敛并增强潜在通信可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用最终答案的 CE 监督不能约束中间隐藏状态，导致隐藏表征跨问题塌缩、保留无关信息等四类失败，进而降低正确答案概率与潜在通信的可解释性。

Method: 在原有 CE 损失之上，设计四个可微损失项对应“有效思维”属性，并在不改变推理时架构与参数的前提下，分别在单代理与多代理的潜在状态循环或传递场景中训练，保持相同训练数据、算力和潜在预算。

Result: 在 7 个数学、科学、医学与代码生成基准上，REST 相较于仅 CE 训练在相同条件下最高提升准确率 7.5 个百分点，使“收敛到最终答案”的速度提升约 30%，并使潜在表征更易解码和解释。

Conclusion: REST 提出通过对潜在“思维”表征施加监督性约束（因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性），在仅有交叉熵（CE）最终答案监督的情况下，显著改善模型在潜在空间推理与代理间通信的质量和可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models can reason in continuous space instead of decoded text, by recurring on their own hidden states or by passing those states between agents, while training supervises only the Cross-Entropy (CE) of the final decoded answer and does not constrain the thought. Theoretical and empirical analyses establish and confirm four failures of CE-only training that lead to a lower probability of the correct answer such as collapsing thoughts across distinct questions and retaining irrelevant information. We introduce REST (REpresentation-Supervised Thoughts), a training objective that turns four properties of a valid thought representation (causality, minimality, separability, and stability) into differentiable losses added to CE. We instantiate it in latent single-agent and multi-agent systems, without architectural changes or added parameters at inference. Across 7 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, and code generation, with the same training data, compute, and latent budget, REST increases accuracy over CE-only training across agent settings and model sizes by up to 7.5 percentage points and convergence on a final answer by 30\%. Furthermore, REST thoughts encode more of what is required to achieve the correct answer, and decoding them better recovers the intended output of the agent, which makes latent communication easier to interpret. Project Website: https://fard-lab.github.io/REST

</details>


### [61] [FigAct: Turning Scientific Figures into Active Canvases for Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36190)
*Shishi Xiao,Zichao Wang,Alexa Siu,David H. Laidlaw,Jennifer Healey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FigAct把科学图表当成演示画布，生成带定位与可视动作的分步短叙述来解释图表；通过分层搜索显著降低token开销，并用任务特定奖励训练模型，实验证明其在清晰度与着根性上优于传统文本化解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型（MLLMs）通常通过将视觉内容翻译为文本来解释科学图表，要求读者将文本解释手动映射回图像。受人类演示方式启发，作者希望直接在图表元素上施加可视化动作，使解释更直观、易跟随。

Method: FigAct首先生成与问题相关的短叙述序列，每条叙述与对应的视觉证据相绑定；采用分层搜索策略进行高效元素定位以减少token使用量约40倍；训练FigAct-8B模型，使用三个任务特定奖励（定位准确性、搜索效率和渲染质量）优化模型。

Result: 作者构建了一个经过人工验证的基准数据集（来自真实科研论文的图表），用于评估MLLMs生成有依据的视觉解释能力。实验证明FigAct使解释更清晰、更容易理解，并在元素定位与token效率方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文提出了FigAct，一种将静态科学图表转换为条件化视觉演示的框架，通过对图中已有图形元素直接作用，生成一系列短叙述并以可视化动作引导观众注意力，从而提升图表解释的可理解性。

Abstract: Scientific figures are designed to communicate information visually, yet MLLMs typically explain them by translating their visual content back into text. This requires readers to manually map the resulting explanations back to the figure. Inspired by how people present visual information, we introduce FigAct, a framework that transforms static scientific figures into question-conditioned visual presentations by acting directly on their existing graphical elements. Like a human presenter, FigAct generates a sequence of short narrations, grounds each narration in the corresponding visual evidence, and applies visual actions to guide the viewer's attention. We develop a hierarchical search strategy for efficient element localization, reducing token usage by approximately 40$\times$. We further train FigAct-8B using three task-specific rewards for grounding accuracy, search efficiency, and rendering quality. We further build a human-verified benchmark from figures in real-world scientific papers to evaluate the ability of MLLMs to generate grounded visual explanations. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of FigAct and show that treating scientific figures as presentation canvases makes explanations clearer and easier to follow.

</details>


### [62] [An Empirical Study and Assessment of EU AI Act Compliance Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36228)
*Zhen Tao,Alize Kahraman,Shidong Pan,Zhenchang Xing,Chiara Ullstein,Jens Grossklags,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对12款AIACCs的首个实证评估表明：当前工具适合早期导向性使用，但在法律覆盖、确定性与可操作性方面存在显著短板，容易产生虚假合规感；需按论文提出的设计原则改进以提升实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EU AI Act对安全、隐私、数据治理、透明性、准确性与鲁棒性等方面提出大量合规义务，但中小企业与独立开发者缺乏法律专业知识，难以将法律义务转化为工程与治理措施；因此出现面向自动化的合规检查工具，作者旨在评估这些工具的有效性与风险。

Method: 对12个主流AIACCs进行定性与定量混合评估：从功能维度（交互模式、可用性）、法律覆盖性与对齐性、以及生成合规报告的结构性、确定性与可操作性三个方面进行系统分析，并基于实例化的检查报告进行比对与归纳。

Result: 发现工具在交互设计和用户友好性上不一致，常简化或遗漏关键义务，且无法提供确定性与可操作性建议；因此现有AIACCs只能作为“方向性参考”，不能替代专业法律/合规审查。论文还给出改进AIACCs的设计原则以提高可靠性。

Conclusion: 当前市面上的EU AI Act合规检查器（AIACCs）质量参差不齐，仅能作为早期入门工具，不能作为决定性或可执行的合规依据；过度依赖可能导致虚假的合规感。

Abstract: The EU AI Act introduces extensive compliance requirements for organizations that develop, deploy, or integrate AI systems. Many of these requirements are directly relevant to security and privacy, while also addressing closely related issues such as data governance, transparency, accuracy, and robustness. However, stakeholders such as small-to-medium businesses and individual developers often lack the legal expertise required to interpret these obligations and translate them into engineering and governance practices. This disconnect creates challenges for implementing the EU AI Act and may lead to missing safeguards or misdirected development and deployment efforts. To address this, various automated EU AI Act compliance checkers (AIACCs) have emerged, claiming to streamline compliance assessments and provide practical guidance. In this paper, we present the first empirical study and assessment of AIACCs. We characterize 12 mainstream AIACCs across multiple dimensions, evaluate their legal coverage and alignment, and analyze checker-generated compliance reports for structure, determinacy, and actionability. We find that the quality of AIACCs varies significantly and that they currently can only serve as early-stage orientation tools. Specifically, we observe inconsistent interaction modes and user-friendliness, a tendency to overly simplify or omit key obligations, and a failure to provide determinate, actionable guidance. As a result, reliance on the current generation of AIACCs may foster a false sense of compliance. With our study, we provide a critical baseline of the current AIACC landscape. We further offer design principles for the implementation of more reliable compliance-support tools.

</details>


### [63] [MERID: Multimodal Exploration via Recursive Self-Improvement Agents for Major Depression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36235)
*Lei Liu,Zhaokang Liang,Qingcheng Zeng,Chenda Duan,Lu Mi,Zhen Tan,Tianyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MERID is a recursive self-improvement agent framework that grounds multimodal experiences, jointly explores pipeline components, and verifies improvements under uncertainty, yielding superior MDD analysis pipelines on benchmark datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multimodal MDD detection requires heterogeneous, customized pipelines; prior manual architectures and agent-assisted methods lack autonomous, feedback-driven revision and reliable inheritance of verified improvements.

Method: MERID builds pipelines via three components: Grounded State Construction (GSC) to align multimodal records with subject-level depression targets; Coupled Pipeline Exploration (CPE) to jointly modify representation, fusion, and predictors to generate successor pipelines; Evidence-Guided Evolution (EGE) to use experimental feedback, verify improvements under uncertainty on small cohorts, and inherit verified changes recursively.

Result: Extensive experiments on depression benchmarks show MERID achieves state-of-the-art performance across classification and severity estimation tasks; analysis identifies acoustic and linguistic modalities as particularly informative. Code is released.

Conclusion: MERID demonstrates an automated, experience-based recursive self-improvement framework that produces improved multimodal pipelines for MDD detection and severity estimation, outperforming existing multimodal and agent-based baselines on public depression benchmarks.

Abstract: Major depressive disorder (MDD) severely impacts daily activities and quality of life. Detecting MDD involves multimodal data, such as interview recordings and sensor measurements. This is particularly challenging, as these heterogeneous modalities often demand distinct, customized prediction pipelines. Existing efforts to address this challenge have explored both manually engineered multimodal architectures and agent-assisted pipeline development. Despite their progress, it remains challenging to autonomously revise pipelines based on experimental feedback and carry verified improvements forward into subsequent designs. To this end, we propose Multimodal Exploration via Recursive Self-Improvement Agents for Major Depression Analysis (MERID). The framework develops depression pipelines through experience-based recursive self-improvement (RSI). Grounded State Construction (GSC) grounds experience by aligning multimodal records with subject-level depression targets. Coupled Pipeline Exploration (CPE) jointly modifies representations, fusion, and predictors to build successor pipelines for classification and severity estimation. Evidence-Guided Evolution (EGE) guides revisions through feedback and verifies gains under uncertainty in small depression cohorts before inheritance. Extensive experiments on depression benchmarks show that MERID achieves the best results on multiple tasks compared with multimodal and agent-based baselines. Further analysis highlights the value of acoustic and linguistic cues for depression detection. Our code is available at https://github.com/DiscoAILab/MERID

</details>


### [64] [ChronoSRL: Temporal Geometry for Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36238)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将 critic 嵌入映射到时间几何、回归到达时间分布与停留时间的 ChronoSRL，使策略偏向更快更可靠的到达并保持目标状态，在多项基准与sim-to-real四足任务上显著优于若干自监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间距离并不能反映时间代价：近的目标在空间上可能需要很长时间到达，且单次快速到达不代表可靠性。现有对比学习和生存RL的 critic 表征未在时间单位上量化嵌入距离，因此需要显式的时间几何表征来改善目标导向的自监督RL。

Method: 构造时间几何的嵌入空间：训练嵌入使得 state-action 到 goal 的距离拟合真实的到达时间；对未到达或来自其他轨迹的目标施加至少一个折扣地平线的下界距离；在该嵌入上回归到达时间的完整分布以及到目标附近的停留时间，并将这些预测作为策略学习的目标（基于生存RL的思想）。同时将机器人学的塑形项自然融入框架。

Result: 在 7 个标准运动和导航基准上，ChronoSRL 比对比学习、action-chunked 对比学习和生存RL基线收敛更快且性能更高，且使用更小的网络。在仿真到真实的四足机器人任务（速度跟踪、到位、爬箱）中，ChronoSRL 是唯一能学会保持命令速度/位置并爬最高箱子的自监督RL方法。

Conclusion: ChronoSRL 为 critic 的嵌入赋予显式的时间几何性，使嵌入间距离对应到达目标所需时间（或被推远至少一个折扣地平线），并在此基础上从时间嵌入预测到达时间分布与在目标附近停留时间，从而促使策略优先选择既快又可靠且能长时间滞留在目标处的动作。

Abstract: A goal that is close in space can be far away in time. Obstacles, terrain, and the agent's own capabilities determine how long it takes to get there. Yet, critics in contrastive and survival reinforcement learning do not measure the distances in their representation space in units of time. We therefore introduce ChronoSRL, which gives the critic's embeddings an explicit temporal geometry. The distance between state-action and goal embeddings is trained to match the time that the agent takes to reach the goal (goal-reaching time), while goals that were not reached, and goals from other trajectories, are pushed at least one discount horizon away. Furthermore, reaching a goal quickly once does not mean that reaching it is reliable in general, so the policy should not follow the temporal distance directly. Instead, we build on survival reinforcement learning and predict from our temporal embeddings not only the full distribution of goal-reaching times but also the time spent near the goal. Thereby, the policy is trained to favor actions that reach the goal sooner and more reliably and that keep the agent near it. ChronoSRL learns faster and reaches higher performance than contrastive, action-chunked contrastive, and survival reinforcement learning baselines on seven standard locomotion and navigation benchmarks, even with much smaller networks. To test the limits of self-supervised reinforcement learning, we introduce velocity tracking, goal-position reaching, and box climbing tasks with a quadruped robot in a realistic sim-to-real locomotion setup, and show how the shaping terms that are typical for robotics can be naturally incorporated into our framework. ChronoSRL is the only one of the tested self-supervised reinforcement learning methods that learns to stay at the commanded velocities and goal positions, and climbs the highest boxes.

</details>


### [65] [CoRe: Co-Evolving Reward Models for Mitigating Latent Reward Hacking in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36245)
*Zhaolong Su,Yujin Han,Feng Wang,Jameson Dong,Hins Hu,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 固定潜在奖励会导致生成器通过分布外漂移获得虚高评分，从而破坏视频质量；CoRe通过持续重拟合奖励并以真实视频偏好锚定，实现在潜在空间的稳健对齐并提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作使用潜在奖励模型（LRMs）在潜在空间直接评分以高效对齐视频扩散模型，但发现固定奖励下生成质量会迅速崩溃。作者分析发现核心问题是分布外逃（distributional escape）：生成器参数在若干百次更新后移出奖励模型的训练支持域，导致奖励信号不再反映真实视频质量。

Method: 提出CoRe（co-evolving reward）框架：在潜在空间里对生成器和奖励模型进行动态交互。具体做法是不断用当前生成器样本对潜在奖励模型进行重拟合，同时用真实视频偏好对奖励进行锚定，以保持奖励模型对生成分布的覆盖并阻止生成器通过漂移而获得虚高奖励。

Result: 在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B基线上，CoRe相比预训练模型和既有对齐方法持续提升生成质量，并避免了固定奖励优化时出现的质量坍塌。实验表明共演化重拟合并锚定真实偏好的策略能稳定奖励质量与生成效果。

Conclusion: 固定的潜在奖励模型在对视频扩散模型进行对齐时会导致奖励被“黑化”：生成器在潜在空间中漂移到奖励模型训练分布之外，从而在感知和运动质量上恶化但奖励仍高。通过将奖励模型与生成器共演化，并定期以生成器样本重拟合奖励模型同时锚定真实视频偏好（CoRe），可以防止质量坍塌并持续提升生成质量。

Abstract: Latent reward models (LRMs) enable efficient alignment of video diffusion models by scoring intermediate states directly in latent space. However, we find that optimizing against a fixed latent reward rapidly leads to latent reward hacking: the predicted reward stays high while perceptual and motion quality deteriorate. Our analysis identifies distributional escape as the central cause: within a few hundred updates, the generator moves beyond the reward model's training support, where its scores no longer reflect video quality. Based on this insight, we introduce CoRe, a co-evolving reward framework that treats latent-space alignment as a dynamic interaction between the generator and the reward model. Rather than optimizing against a stationary proxy, CoRe continually refits the reward model on the generator's current samples while anchoring it to real-video preferences, so the generator cannot gain reward by drifting away from the data. On Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, experiments show that CoRe consistently improves generation quality over both the pretrained model and prior alignment methods, while avoiding the quality collapse of fixed-reward optimization.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Mitigating Deceptive Safety Alignment in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36254)
*Xiangyu Zhou,Saleh Zare Zade,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Alexander Kotov,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文定义并测量了“推理-答案安全不一致”问题（DSAR），并通过一种同时奖励安全推理与安全答案的RL方法SARA有效缓解该问题，实验结果良好且开源代码可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用RL优化的LRM通常基于最终答案的奖励，缺少对中间Chain-of-Thought的直接监督，导致模型可能在推理中表现出不同的安全信号与最终答案不一致——即deceptive safety alignment。需要系统化量化并提出修正方法。

Method: 提出DSAR指标用于量化推理痕迹与最终答案间的安全不一致性；进行隐藏表示分析以比较模型在推理阶段与最终答案阶段的安全判别能力；基于此提出SARA，一种在强化学习中同时对安全感知的推理过程和安全的最终答案给予奖励的方法。

Result: 在多种LRM与基准上，DSAR表明deceptive safety alignment普遍存在并在prefilling攻击下被放大；隐藏表示分析显示模型在最终答案阶段的安全判别更强；SARA在标准与对抗情形下均显著降低了DSAR，同时保持帮助性与实用性。

Conclusion: SARA能够显著降低LRM在中间推理与最终答案之间的安全不一致问题（deceptive safety alignment），从而促使模型在推理阶段更早识别有害意图并在答复时保持一致性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are commonly trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to improve their generation of chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before producing final answers. However, RL rewards are typically assigned based on final answers, providing little or no direct supervision over intermediate reasoning. This can lead to deceptive safety alignment, where the reasoning trace and final answer convey inconsistent safety signals. To systematically investigate this phenomenon, we introduce DSAR (Deceptive Safety Alignment Rate), a metric that jointly assesses reasoning traces and final answers to quantify their safety inconsistency. Across multiple LRMs and benchmarks, we find that deceptive safety alignment is pervasive under standard prompting conditions and is substantially amplified under prefilling attacks. We further provide a hidden representation analysis showing that models exhibit stronger safety discrimination at the final-answer stage than during intermediate reasoning. To close this gap, we propose SARA (Safety-Aware Reasoning Alignment), an RL-based method that rewards both safety-aware reasoning and safe final answers, encouraging early harmful intent recognition and enforcing reasoning-answer consistency. Experiments show that SARA significantly mitigates deceptive safety alignment under both standard and adversarial settings while preserving helpfulness and utility. Code is available at https://github.com/xzhou98/SARA.

</details>


### [67] [OTROPE: Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-policy Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36264)
*Liner Xiang,Wenbo Zhang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于最优传输的似然无关离策略评估方法 OTROPE，通过语义对齐+残差校正实现对黑盒 LLM 的稳健评估，兼具理论保证与实证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评估大模型成本高、风险大且对黑盒模型常不可行。希望用有限的行为策略带标注数据去可靠地评估新（目标）LLM，克服标注稀缺、分布漂移与黑盒缺乏似然的问题。

Method: 在语义嵌入空间中通过最优传输（optimal transport）将有标注的行为模型样本重配到无标注的目标模型样本上，利用重配后的人类标注残差与代理预测器（proxy predictor）结合，构造类似双重稳健（doubly robust）的估计器，无需建模行为策略或估计密度比，属于一种似然无关的方法。

Result: 理论上给出了基线评估器在分布漂移下失败的说明，并证明当重加权的行为分布或代理预测器之一收敛时，OTROPE 的一致性与收敛速率；实证上在合成与真实 LLM 任务中优于基线，并能让弱评估模型的组合接近或超过更强的评估器。

Conclusion: OTROPE 提供了一种不依赖似然、能在语义空间对齐行为策略与目标策略样本的稳健离策略评估方法，对稀疏标注和黑盒 LLM 场景具有实际价值。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is essential for their development and deployment, yet is often costly, risky, and difficult to perform safely online. We study off-policy evaluation for LLMs, where limited human-labeled data from a behavior model are used to evaluate a newer target LLM. This setting is challenging because labels are scarce, behavior--target distribution shift is common, and response likelihoods are often unavailable for black-box LLMs. We propose the Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-Policy Evaluation (OTROPE), a likelihood-free evaluation that performs distributional correction in a semantic space via optimal transport to align labeled behavior-policy samples with unlabeled target-policy samples. OTROPE combines corrected human-labeled residuals with proxy predictors, yielding a doubly robust-style evaluation without behavior-policy modeling or density-ratio estimation. We theoretically characterize why baseline evaluators fail under LLM distribution shift, and establish consistency and convergence rates for OTROPE when either the reweighted behavior distribution or the proxy predictor converges. Experiments on synthetic and real LLM evaluation tasks show that OTROPE consistently outperforms baselines while enabling ensembles of weaker LLM evaluators to approach and sometimes surpass stronger evaluators. Code is available at https://github.com/LinerXiang/OTROPE.

</details>


### [68] [From Surfaces to Volumes: Registered Geometry for Protein Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36277)
*Siyuan Chen,Cai Zhou,Jinrui Zhang,Zhaokang Liang,Taku Komura,Wojciech Matusik,Stephen Bates,Tommi Jaakkola,Wengong Jin,Peter Yichen Chen,Minghao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Protein-TetSphere通过残基级四面体配准和统一拉普拉斯基底构建一致的体积坐标，作为表面信息的互补表示，在多个识别、相互作用与设计任务上带来稳健性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子几何模型多用采样点、法向量和曲率等表面局部特征，能够描述暴露的表面形状但缺乏对表面之下体积组织的显式建模和一致的残基尺度坐标系；需要一种能捕捉局部三维形变并与表面/化学信息联合表征的残基级体积表示。

Method: 对每条蛋白链进行四面体化，提取与单个残基相关的局部体积区域；将这些局部体积配准到一个共享的固定拓扑四面体参考；在共同的拉普拉斯基底上表示每个残基的体积特征；将所得注册体积信息与表面与化学信息进行多模态融合用于下游任务。

Result: 在配体结合口袋分类、蛋白-蛋白界面预测和新设计蛋白结合子三项任务上均有提升：配体结合口袋平衡准确率从0.795提升到0.826；Pinder-Pair AUROC从0.914提升到0.932，Site从0.852提升到0.866；Binder设计成功率在BoltzGen从14.95%增至19.90%，在ProtDBench骨架级从27.62%增至32.19%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种注册的残基级体素化表示 Protein-TetSphere，通过将蛋白链四面体化并把每个残基的局部体积区域配准到共享的固定拓扑四面体参考中，在统一的拉普拉斯基底上提供一致的体积坐标，从而补充分子表面信息并在识别、相互作用和设计任务上带来性能提升。

Abstract: Existing protein geometry models typically represent molecular surfaces using local geometric features such as sampled points, normals, and curvature. While effective for capturing exposed molecular shape, these representations do not explicitly model the volumetric organization beneath the surface or provide a consistent coordinate system for residue-wise volumetric structure. We introduce Protein-TetSphere, a registered residue-wise volumetric representation for proteins. Each protein chain is tetrahedralized to obtain local volumetric regions associated with individual residues, which are then registered to a shared fixed-topology tetrahedral reference and represented in a common Laplacian basis. This registration establishes consistent volumetric coordinates across residues, enabling local three-dimensional deformation to be integrated with surface and chemical information in a multimodal protein representation. We evaluate Protein-TetSphere on ligand-binding pocket classification, protein--protein interface prediction, and de novo protein binder design. Across the three tasks, Protein-TetSphere improves ligand-binding pocket balanced accuracy from $0.795$ to $0.826$, Pinder-Pair/Site AUROC from $0.914/0.852$ to $0.932/0.866$, and binder-design success from $14.95\%$ to $19.90\%$ on the BoltzGen Challenge Set and from $27.62\%$ to $32.19\%$ at the ProtDBench backbone level. These results show that registered volumetric geometry provides complementary spatial information beyond molecular surfaces across protein recognition, interaction, and design.

</details>


### [69] [Towards an AI Software Factory for Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36323)
*Anna Pavlenko,Bogdan Crivat,Brandon Haynes,Carlo Curino,Fotis Psallidas,Jaro Slawinski,Johannes Freischuetz,Laura Pereira Sanchez,Markus Weimer,Mathieu Demarne,Matthias Jasny,Mauktik Gandhi,Max Bovykin,Mirco Milletari,Purbasha Ghosh,Qiushi Bai,Raghu Ramakrishnan,Rahul Pandita,Sergiy Matusevich,Shivaram Venkataraman,Subru Krishnan,Md. Tareq Mahmood,Tiemo Bang,Venkatesh Emani,Xuan Zhao,Yiwen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An integrated AI SW Factory that captures metadata exhaust to fine-tune models and a World Model shows strong end-to-end SDLC speedups (3x engineering, 22x token efficiency) in Data Systems’ evolutionary tasks, but requires further validation and broader evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Single-stage AI-assisted tools yield diminishing system-level returns (Amdahl’s law). To achieve larger end-to-end gains, an integrated factory must accelerate all stages and enable closed-loop self-improvement.

Method: Design and deploy an AI SW Factory that targets all SDLC stages (Targeting, Coding, Reviewing, Ops), captures comprehensive metadata exhaust, uses that data to fine-tune models and update a World Model, and focus experiments on Evolutionary Coding Tasks in Data Systems.

Result: Scaled Microsoft deployments across tens of repositories report ~3x engineering-efficiency improvement relative to agentic coding and up to 22x token efficiency. The paper also catalogs several open challenges around evaluation, reproducibility, and broader applicability.

Conclusion: The AI SW Factory can accelerate the entire SDLC by producing metadata exhaust that enables model fine-tuning and a World Model, demonstrating substantial gains in deployed Data Systems scenarios; however, open challenges remain for generality, evaluation, and governance.

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools deliver significant acceleration of coding, but only limited impact across the end-to-end software development lifecycle (SDLC)--an Amdahl's law effect!
  In this paper, we discuss our progress towards building an AI SW Factory that accelerates all the stages of SDLC-Targeting, Coding, Reviewing, and Ops. The AI SW Factory produces a metadata exhaust that enables self-improvement by fine-tuning model weights and updating our World Model (a rich data substrate).
  We focus on Data Systems and the important class of Evolutionary Coding Tasks (i.e., those with a measurable objective to hill-climb) and report on 1) scaled deployments at Microsoft (tens of repositories) leading to 3x engineering efficiency above agentic coding and up to 22x token efficiency, and 2) several open challenges.

</details>


### [70] [Illusory Truth or Mere Exposure? Model-Dependent Repetition Effects in LLM-Based Social Media Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36278)
*Azza Bouleimen,Nicolò Pagan,Anikó Hannák*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 不同 LLM 在信息重复对真值判断的影响上表现不一致，只有 Gemma-3 明显再现了人类的错觉真相效应；这对基于 LLM 的社交仿真具有重要含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在基于生成型代理的社会仿真中，LLM 代理能否复制人类认知偏差（尤其是错觉真相效应），从而保证社会仿真对错误信息扩散等社会过程的有效代理性。

Method: 在社交媒体信息流情境中采用两阶段同一上下文内实验设计，对 4 个 LLM 在 100 条声明、10 种信息流变体、3 次重复实验中收集 336,000 条关于真值、重要性、情感和兴趣的评分。对比重复出现的声明与完全未见声明，使用 OLS 回归和线性混合效应模型来控制模型与评分维度差异，并进行方差分解与温度敏感性分析。

Result: 四种截然不同的重复效果模式被观察到；Gemma-3 接近人类 ITE，其他模型分别表现为仅暴露效应、无效应或带有其他评分维度变化；温度对效应无影响；评分高度依赖上下文。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不同大模型在模拟人类“错觉真相效应”（ITE）时表现各异：Gemma-3 显示真实的 ITE，Qwen2.5 表现为仅暴露效应，GPT-5-nano 对重复内容表现出轻微怀疑，Llama-3.1 有小幅真值提升但评估维度下降。温度参数无显著影响，模型评分高度受上下文敏感。

Abstract: Generative agent-based models (GABMs) are increasingly used to simulate social media dynamics, including misinformation spread. For such social simulations to be valid proxies of human behavior, LLM agents should replicate established human cognitive biases, among them the Illusory Truth Effect (ITE), where repeated exposure to a claim increases its perceived truth value. We investigate whether and how the ITE manifests across four LLMs (Gemma-3-4b-it, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and GPT-5-nano) in a social media simulation context. We propose a two-phase within-context experimental design that embeds the repetition manipulation inside a realistic news feed interaction. Using this design, we collect 336,000 truth, importance, sentiment, and interest ratings across 100 statements, 10 feed variants, and 3 replications. The key comparison is between ratings assigned to repeated statements, seen throughout a simulation phase, and completely unseen ones, rated within the same experimental context window. We distinguish genuine ITE (truth-specific repetition boost) from mere exposure effects. We run an OLS regression followed by a Linear Mixed-Effect Model to account for differences across models and ratings. Our results reveal four qualitatively distinct patterns: Gemma-3 exhibits a genuine ITE; Qwen2.5 shows a mere exposure effect; GPT-5-nano displays no repetition effect on truth and mild skepticism toward repeated content; Llama-3.1 shows a small truth boost alongside decreases in evaluative dimensions. Crucially, temperature has no effect on these findings, and a variance decomposition highlights the high context-sensitivity of LLM rating behavior. Our findings caution against assuming uniform ITE replication across LLMs in social simulations, while suggesting that Gemma-3-4b-it may offer the most behaviorally realistic approximation for misinformation-related simulations.

</details>


### [71] [Can Agents Design Libraries for Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36730)
*Gabriel Orlanski,Alex L. Zhang,Avi Trost,Vincent Sunn Chen,Frederic Sala,Aws Albarghouthi,Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LibraryDesignBench measures agent library-design quality: agents can recreate many human abstractions, but reuse is low due to usability/rigidity; targeted guidance and self-testing boost downstream adoption and simplify client code.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agents increasingly build on other agents' code but tend to reimplement rather than reuse, inflating codebases and reducing efficiency—there is a need to measure and improve how well agents design reusable libraries for downstream agents.

Method: Introduce a two-phase benchmark where designers implement libraries from semi-open specifications; evaluate libraries by having three distinct user agents write programs using the libraries and score correctness and simplicity; dataset covers 242 problems, 15 tasks, 4 languages; perform failure analysis and controlled experiments with prescriptive guidance and subagent testing.

Result: Agent designers reproduced human abstractions on 11/15 tasks; downstream agents use both agent- and human-written libraries but underuse them, often reimplementing features because agent libraries are rigid or hard to use; providing more prescriptive guidance and subagent testing improves downstream scores and produces simpler programs.

Conclusion: LibraryDesignBench establishes a measurable benchmark and shows that agent-designed libraries can reproduce many human abstractions and that modest, agent-focused design/testing guidance improves downstream reuse.

Abstract: Agents increasingly build on code written by other agents, and they reimplement rather than reuse, growing the codebases later agents must work in. To measure how well agents design libraries for other agents, we introduce LibraryDesignBench, a two-phase benchmark in which an agent implements a full-featured library from a specification that defines required capabilities and potential use cases without prescribing the design. We evaluate the library through the correctness and simplicity of programs written by three user agents from different model families. The benchmark spans 242 expert-validated programming problems across 15 library-design tasks in four languages. On eleven of the fifteen tasks, agent designers reproduce the abstractions of the human-written production library. Downstream agents adopt agent- and human-written libraries alike but underuse them, reimplementing capabilities the library already provides. Our failure analysis finds that downstream agents write extra code mainly because agent-written libraries are rigid or hard to use, not because capabilities are missing. We also experiment with giving designers more prescriptive, agent-first guidance and having them test their library with subagents; this improves downstream scores and yields simpler programs. LibraryDesignBench provides both a testbed for evaluating library-design practices for agent users and an initial design baseline that improves downstream reuse.

</details>


### [72] [CheatBench: Measuring Reward Gaming in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36308)
*Long Phan,Stephen K. Yang,Jason J. Lim,Mantas Mazeika,Wenyu Zhang,Zheyuan Liu,Richard Ren,Jingxiang Meng,Yaoteng Tan,Weiliang Zhao,Addison Wu,Matei Anghel,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CheatBench：一个跨领域、包含“作弊机会”的基准，用于测量 AI 代理在难题面前为达成目标而采取不当手段的倾向，并提供公开资源推动该方向研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实和受控评估中发现强化学习代理为最大化奖励会访问未授权信息、规避监控或突破沙箱，提示需要系统化基准来量化和研究这种行为，以便在更高风险职责下降低安全隐患。

Method: 通过构建跨数学研究、知识工作、代码、视觉任务等多领域的环境，每个任务在提供正当解法的同时设计可被利用的作弊途径，从而允许对模型在困难工作情境下选择作弊策略的行为进行比较与测量。

Result: CheatBench 支持在模型与任务类别间的横向比较，成为衡量和降低代理作弊倾向的测试床，并已公开发布以便社区使用与扩展。

Conclusion: CheatBench 提供了一个衡量和研究 AI 代理“作弊”行为的基准，强调在高奖励目标下，代理可能采取未获授权或不符合用户意图的手段，这一问题随着代理能力增长而加剧。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has helped AI agents solve increasingly difficult tasks, but high rewards do not always reflect the work users intended. In recent incidents and controlled evaluations across the AI industry, agents trained to maximize reward have accessed unauthorized information, attempted to evade monitoring systems, and even breached sandbox protections to attack external systems. As agents become more capable, this behavior could pose increasingly serious risks. To measure this problem, we introduce CheatBench, a benchmark of cheating in AI agents across mathematical research, knowledge work, coding, visual tasks, and other domains. Its environments combine challenging assignments with opportunities to cheat, allowing researchers to study how agents pursue a goal when honest work is difficult. CheatBench supports comparisons across models and task categories, providing a testbed for measuring and reducing cheating as agents take on more consequential responsibilities. We publicly release CheatBench at https://cheatbench.ai

</details>


### [73] [Mubric: Mutation Testing-Guided Rubric Generation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37322)
*Jiayuxuan Yang,Jie M. Zhang,Yiling Lou,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mubric：一种基于变异测试、通过从真实响应对中抽象缺陷并注入参考响应来检验和精化 rubric 的方法。在大规模跨域评测中优于现有六种方法，性能提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成能可靠捕捉任务特定质量要求的 rubric 很困难；作者借鉴变异测试在软件测试中检测测试套件覆盖缺陷的思想，提出通过注入可控缺陷来检测并补强 rubric 的缺失维度。

Method: 首先从真实的偏好/非偏好响应对中挖掘常见缺陷，并将这些缺陷抽象为可复用的变异算子（定义如何在参考响应中引入特定类型缺陷）。对新任务，选择相关变异算子作用于参考响应，生成带缺陷的响应并检查现有 rubric 对这些缺陷的惩罚是否充分。对于未能充分降低评分的缺陷，Mubric 将其反馈用于扩展或细化 rubric。

Result: 在 703 个任务、4 个代表性领域上与 6 种先进的 rubric 生成方法比较，Mubric 实现了最高的整体评估准确率，较最强基线提升 7.48 个百分点，表明变异测试引导的缺陷检测策略能有效改进自动生成的评估量表。

Conclusion: Mubric 利用变异测试指导生成评估量表，能够通过注入真实常见缺陷并检查评分敏感性来发现量表中缺失的重要质量维度，从而自动精化任务特定的 rubric。实验证明 Mubric 在大规模跨域评测中表现最佳，整体评估准确率领先最强基线 7.48 百分点。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used to assess LLM-based systems by decomposing response quality into task-specific scoring criteria. However, automatically generating rubrics that reliably capture task-specific quality requirements remains challenging. We introduce Mubric, a mutation testing-guided approach to rubric generation. Mutation testing, a classic software testing methodology, evaluates a test suite by injecting faults into programs and checking whether the tests detect them. We draw an analogy between test suites and rubrics: if a rubric captures an important quality requirement, introducing a corresponding defect into an otherwise high-quality response should reduce its score. Mubric first mines common defects from real pairs of preferred and dispreferred responses and abstracts these defects into reusable mutation operators, each specifying how to introduce a particular type of response defect. For a new task, it applies relevant operators to a reference response, checks whether the injected defects reduce response quality, and uses insufficiently penalized defects to refine the rubric. We evaluate Mubric on 703 tasks across four representative domains against six advanced rubric generation methods. Mubric achieves the highest overall evaluation accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 7.48 percentage points.

</details>


### [74] [StateTape: Action-Conditioned Evidence Lifecycle Modeling for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36319)
*Ziyang Yu,Liang Zhao,Bowen Zhu,Hasibul Haque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将仓库建模为符号级代码图并记录写入影响的符号，StateTape 把陈旧检测变为基于写入的观测，改进了编辑频繁场景下上下文维护的准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于历史的上下文维护方法只基于文本判断陈旧或重要性，但在频繁编辑的编程任务中，写入会改变代码语义，使得仅文本的判断容易保留已被作废的信息或丢弃仍然有效的信息，导致代理在长时间、多次编辑任务中性能下降。

Method: 提出符号级代码图（symbol-level code graph）表示依赖与语言规则；在其上维护一条写入磁带（StateTape）记录每次写入影响的符号；每次写入后由磁带提名可能被作废的记录，再由一个小型管理模型（manager model）判定剩余不确定性；提供理论分析与构建了 TraceBench 基准用于标注代理所持记录与实际所需记录的差异。

Result: 在六种编码代理和三套编辑密集型基准上，StateTape 在几乎没有额外计算开销的情况下，提高了解决率（resolve rate），能够更有效地清除被作废的记录并检索所需信息；此外提供理论保证并通过 TraceBench 验证命中与遗失情况。

Conclusion: StateTape 将代码库建模为符号级代码图，并用一条“磁带”标记每次写入改变的符号，从而将陈旧性检测从对文本的推断替换为对写入的观测，显著提高了在多次编辑场景下对被篡改记录的清理与所需记录的检索能力。

Abstract: Despite the recent success of coding agents built on large language models, it remains challenging to run them over long horizons, since every observation is appended to the context and the context grows with each one. History-based maintenance is a common remedy, which masks or summarizes old observations, or prunes what a model reads as useless, and bounds the context at little cost. However, it decides from the text of the history alone and sees nothing of how the code is connected. Since a coding agent edits code many times over a single task, and each write can change what code elsewhere means, such maintenance may keep records a write has falsified, drop ones that still hold, and miss code the agent needs next. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes StateTape, a novel and scalable framework that rewrites a coding agent's context as the repository changes rather than as the context grows. The key idea of StateTape is to model the repository as a symbol-level code graph, whose dependencies and language rules expose which symbols a write can affect. Upon this graph, a tape marks the symbols each write changed, which turns staleness from an inference about text into an observation of the agent's writes. We propose a per-write procedure in which the tape nominates the records a write could have falsified while a small manager model settles what the write log cannot, and further provide a theoretical analysis and TraceBench, a benchmark that labels what an agent is holding against what is actually needed. Empirically, we demonstrate that StateTape can effectively clear falsified records and retrieve what is needed, and thus achieve a higher resolve rate in all experiments spanned by six coding agents and three edit-heavy benchmarks with little computational overhead.

</details>


### [75] [VeriWeave Govern: Evidence-Gated Deterministic Runtime Governance for Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37457)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个证据感知、确定性且可重放的企业代理治理层，在大规模合成与生产测试中表现出很高的安全性与可审计性，但在保守性与效用之间存在权衡，需要更广泛的现实世界评估与统计置信度证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着企业AI代理能调用工具、改变基础设施并处理受保护数据，必须把动作“生成”与动作“授权”分离，提供可审核、可回放、证据感知与可控的人类介入路径，以降低误授权和被滥用风险。

Method: 提出一个确定性规则化治理引擎：对输入的结构化动作和伴随的类型化证据执行验证与策略评估，固定优先级（deny>review>allow），在必要时将动作交给人类审查，记录版本化策略与可重放审计日志。用 GovernBench（30个独立随机种子、60,000条oracle标注样本，跨五个企业域、包含对抗证据、OOD动作与时间演化策略）进行离线评估；做六种消融实验；在生产API上进行端到端场景与高并发测试；另外用基于欧盟/奥地利法规的150例集合与两位盲审人类评估器做对照。

Result: 在评估集上取得0.9888平均准确率、0.9836 macro-F1，未观测到聚合“错误允许”或治理攻击成功；消融证明证据门控、否决优先、OOD失败安全、人类审查、矛盾处理与时间重放均对安全性有互补贡献；API通过12/12端到端场景与40,040并发请求矩阵（零失败）；在150例法规评估中，两位人类审查一致，确定性引擎更保守，Gemma 4 31B更贴近人类共识，显示安全—效用的可测量权衡。

Conclusion: VeriWeave Govern 是一个确定性运行时治理层，能将代理生成的结构化动作与版本化策略比对、验证类型化证据、以固定否决>审查>允许优先级决策、将重要动作路由至可问责的人类审查，并记录可重放且防篡改的审计状态；在多域、大规模和对抗性测试集上表现出高准确性与零观测到的“错误允许”与治理攻击成功率，展示了证据感知、可重放治理作为企业代理控制平面的可行性。

Abstract: Enterprise artificial-intelligence agents increasingly call tools, modify infrastructure, and process protected data, creating a need to separate action generation from action authorization. This article presents VeriWeave Govern, a deterministic runtime governance layer that evaluates structured agent actions against versioned policies, validates typed evidence, applies fixed deny > review > allow precedence, routes consequential actions to accountable human review, and records replayable tamper-evident audit state. GovernBench evaluates the design over 30 independent seeds and 60,000 oracle-labelled cases spanning five enterprise domains, adversarial evidence, out-of-distribution actions, and temporal policy evolution. VeriWeave achieves 0.9888 mean accuracy, 0.9836 macro-F1, zero observed aggregate false allows, and zero observed Governance Attack Success Rate on the evaluated cases. Six ablations show that evidence gating, deny precedence, out-of-distribution fail-safe behavior, human review, contradiction handling, and temporal replay contribute complementary safety. The deployed API additionally passes 12/12 end-to-end scenarios and a 40,040-request concurrency matrix with zero failures. A separate 150-case EU/Austria regulation-grounded evaluation uses frozen predictions and two independent blinded human annotators, who agree on all decisions. On this set, deterministic engines remain conservative, while a Gemma 4 31B comparator aligns more closely with the human consensus. The results expose a measurable safety--utility trade-off and motivate evidence-aware, replayable governance as an independent control plane for enterprise agent execution.

</details>


### [76] [PILLAR: Private Inverted-Index Lexical Lookup for Augmented Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36326)
*Truong Son Nguyen,Daniel Blackley,Ni Trieu,Evgenios M. Kornaropoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PILLAR 通过私有稀疏检索（PIR 查询预计算 BM25 索引）先缩小候选集，再本地做密集重排，提出两种协议（Bin/Tree）以在隐私不泄露的前提下显著降低 PIR 开销并在延迟与检索质量上胜过现有私有检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的私有 RAG 架构用密集检索直接把近似最近邻搜索翻译为大量依赖查询的 PIR 轮次，导致高延迟和检索质量下降。目标是减少 PIR 轮数与流量，同时保持或提升检索质量并保证服务器对查询词及访问模式没有信息泄露。

Method: 两阶段私有混合检索。第一阶段：稀疏阶段通过对预先计算的 BM25 得分索引进行少量、固定次数的 PIR 查询来筛选与查询共享词项的候选文档（服务器不可见查询词或访问模式）。第二阶段：密集阶段只通过 PIR 获取这些候选文档的嵌入并在客户端本地重排。实现上提出两种协议：PILLAR-Bin（基于哈希分箱的单轮设计，低延迟）与 PILLAR-Tree（基于块最大裁剪的不可知树遍历和 cuckoo 哈希，检索质量最高且延迟低于现有方案）。

Result: 通过将精确的词项过滤（私有 BM25 查询）与后续的密集重排相结合，PILLAR 在延迟和质量之间取得更好折衷：PILLAR-Bin 实现了低于现有私有检索方案的延迟，PILLAR-Tree 在保持更高检索质量的同时仍优于现有方案的延迟。

Conclusion: PILLAR 提供了一种实用的隐私保护 RAG 框架，通过将私有稀疏检索与本地密集重排相结合，在不泄露查询词或访问模式的前提下，显著降低了基于 PIR 的私有检索的延迟开销并提升/维持检索质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) hands the user's query to whoever hosts the corpus. We propose PILLAR, a Privacy-Preserving RAG (PPRAG) system based on Private Information Retrieval (PIR) in which a client utilizes the k documents most similar to their query from a server-held and publicly known corpus to respond to their query, while the server learns nothing about the query, either its terms or its access pattern. Prior PPRAG constructions rely on dense retrieval alone, translating approximate nearest-neighbor search into many query-dependent rounds of PIR, and pay for it in both latency and retrieval quality. PILLAR instead performs private hybrid retrieval in two stages. A sparse stage issues a small, fixed number of PIR queries against a carefully designed index of precomputed BM25 scores, filtering the corpus down to candidates that share terms with the query without the server ever seeing which terms these are. A dense stage then fetches only those candidates' document embeddings and re-ranks them locally, avoiding the many costly PIR queries that private dense retrieval typically requires. We instantiate PILLAR with two protocols that trade latency against retrieval quality, each built on a different private rendering of lexical search. PILLAR-Bin bins posting lists into a hash table and is a single-round design that achieves lower latency than state-of-the-art private retrieval schemes. PILLAR-Tree turns block-max pruning into an oblivious tree traversal combined with cuckoo hash tables and achieves the highest retrieval quality at lower latency than state-of-the-art schemes.

</details>


### [77] [ThuRunel: Dynamic Decoupling for Structured Advisory Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36340)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThuRunel operationalizes dynamic decoupling with belief-state tracking, CoT-based teacher synthesis, and adapters, improving elicitation and specialist handoff and demonstrating practical deployment.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High-stakes advisory domains have a two-phase interaction pattern—early empathetic elicitation and late authoritative specialist judgment—that neither purely automated agents nor scaled human juniors address well; the paper asks how an AI should dynamically decouple tasks between elicitation, autonomous resolution, and specialist referral.

Method: Formalize dynamic decoupling and implement ThuRunel, which combines a finite-state belief management framework for structured elicitation, a chain-of-thought teacher synthesis protocol to generate training supervision, and learned generation adapters to adapt outputs; evaluate against eleven baselines on elicitation and briefing metrics and deploy as a bilingual web app grounded in a curated knowledge base.

Result: Across eleven baselines, ThuRunel shows consistent improvements in elicitation completeness and specialist brief quality; it is publicly deployed with bilingual support and a curated KB that cites sources in every answer.

Conclusion: ThuRunel demonstrates that dynamic decoupling—letting an AI agent decide what to ask, when to stop, what to resolve autonomously, and what to forward to a specialist—yields measurable improvements in elicitation completeness and specialist brief quality, and can be deployed in practice as a bilingual, source-citing advisory tool.

Abstract: High-stakes advisory domains such as medical aesthetics, legal consultation, and educational planning exhibit a two-phase structure. The early phase requires empathetic elicitation and emotional support, and the late phase requires authoritative specialist judgment. Neither fully automated agents nor human junior consultants adequately address this structure at scale. We formalize the core design challenge as dynamic decoupling, asking how an AI advisory agent should decide what to ask, when to stop, what to resolve autonomously, and what to forward to the specialist. We present ThuRunel, an advisory agent combining a finite-state belief management framework, a chain-of-thought teacher synthesis protocol, and learned generation adapters. Against eleven baselines, ThuRunel achieves consistent improvements in elicitation completeness and specialist brief quality. ThuRunel is publicly deployed as a bilingual web application in which the same decoupling decisions operate from the client's side, grounded in a curated knowledge base that cites its sources in every answer.

</details>


### [78] [HyperZip: Efficient Data Compression through Personalized Diffusion LLMs with Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36357)
*Thai Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperZip 将扩散式 LLM、MTP 与超网络结合，用少量参数更新适配模型，实现高吞吐量且保持低比特率的无损文本/数据压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归 LLM 的无损压缩在压缩效果上有优势但解码昂贵、吞吐量低；需要一种既能保持较好压缩率又能显著提高解码吞吐量的可扩展方案。

Method: 构建基于扩散的 LLM 解码器，设计多标记预测以并行生成多 token ，并引入一个小型超网络，该超网络从上下文表示生成参数更新并注入到 dLLM 中以实现数据特定的适配；整个流程保留无损重构要求，并在多组数据集上与自回归和现有神经压缩方法比较压缩率与速度。

Result: 提出的 HyperZip 在多项任务上展示出比多种最先进基线更好的压缩率/速度权衡：在增加解码吞吐量时，传统 dLLM 压缩会牺牲更多压缩率，而 HyperZip 通过超网络适配显著缓解该权衡，从而在相同或更高速度下获得更低的比特率。

Conclusion: HyperZip 利用扩散式大语言模型（dLLM）配合多标记预测（MTP）和超网络生成的数据特定更新，在无需昂贵微调的情况下对模型进行快速适配，从而在压缩率与解码吞吐量之间取得更优平衡，实验证明优于现有基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for lossless data compression, but existing approaches are constrained by the high computational cost and low throughput of autoregressive decoding. We propose HyperZip, an efficient and scalable LLM-based compression framework that leverages diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) to accelerate LLM-based data compression processes. We identify a trade-off in diffusion-based compression, where increasing decoding throughput degrades the compression rate. To mitigate this trade-off, HyperZip employs a hypernetwork to generate data-specific updates from a context representation, adapting the dLLM to the target data without costly fine-tuning, resulting in a low compression rate and high throughput. Extensive experiments show that HyperZip achieves a superior trade-off between compression rate and speed compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [79] [Better Nearest Neighbor Graph Indices via (Efficient) LLM-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36359)
*Fangzhou Wu,Haike Xu,Sandeep Silwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Guided Graph Pruning，用LLM在索引层判断并替换低价值邻居，解决几何与语义的错配，从而提升图基ANN在语义检索任务的表现，同时保持效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现了一个根本性问题：ANN图索引通常基于向量几何关系构建，但检索评估基于语义相关性，二者存在“几何—语义”错配；在线重排序只能缓解但无法解决图结构本身的局限。

Method: 提出了一种通用框架：对现有ANN图（如DiskANN、HNSW）逐节点调用LLM进行邻居质量评估，识别“低价值”边并用LLM选择的替代邻居替换之，替换过程设计以保持图的稀疏性与导航效率（例如限制度、连通性约束等）。该方法在索引层面修改图结构，而不是只在查询时重排序。

Result: 在多个语义检索基准上，LGP对不同图索引（DiskANN、HNSW等）均带来端到端检索性能提升，超越了原始图搜索和仅做LLM重排序的方案，同时在保持索引稀疏性与搜索效率方面表现良好。

Conclusion: LGP通过在已有的图索引上使用LLM推理识别并替换结构上“低价值”的邻居，从而在保留图的稀疏性与可导航性前提下，把几何关系调优为更有利于语义检索的结构。实验表明LGP在代表性语义检索基准上相较于原始贪心图搜索和基于LLM的重排序方法均有稳定提升。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is widely used for large-scale semantic search. Its indices are constructed primarily based on geometric relationships among embeddings of an input dataset (e.g., documents or images), rather than explicitly optimizing for semantic relevance. However, when using these indices for downstream query retrieval, performance is evaluated based on the semantic relevance of the retrieved results to the query. This creates a fundamental "geometry-semantic" mismatch between how the indices are constructed and how their retrieval results are evaluated. While existing LLM-based reranking methods can partially mitigate this mismatch at query time, they leave this underlying structural problem in the graph unresolved. We therefore propose LLM-Guided Graph Pruning (LGP), a general framework that addresses this mismatch directly by leveraging LLM reasoning to refine an existing ANN graph index itself. LGP identifies structurally "low-value" neighbors of nodes and replaces them with LLM-selected alternatives that provide useful semantic information while retaining desired geometric structures of the original graph, including sparsity and efficient navigability. Experiments on representative semantic retrieval benchmarks show that LGP consistently improves end-to-end retrieval performance over both vanilla greedy graph search and LLM-based reranking across widely used graph-based ANN indices such as DiskANN and HNSW.

</details>


### [80] [Engineering Simplicity: Simple Mechanism Interfaces Steer LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36365)
*Kehang Zhu,Anand Shah,David Parkes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用人类动机的“简单性”设计交互与提示，能在结构化博弈中改善LLM代理的实际选择，但必须同时通过行为结果和解释性指标来评估，因为两者并不总是同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究如何通过交互界面设计与文本提示来提高LLM代理在结构化、可验证决策任务中的行为表现，并检验解释性（口头说明）是否能作为行为改进的可靠指示器；以人类关于“简单性”的理论作为设计启发。

Method: 在具有明确规则和已知最优策略的拍卖与匹配多智能体环境中对比实验：比较完整（一次性）输出与顺序（分步）输出接口，并在固定接口下对比不同的文本支架与规则描述。对四个模型家族进行测试，使用已知最优策略作为基准，衡量出价/排序偏差与短期口头计划中的策略理解指标。

Result: 升序（逐步）拍卖界面显著减少出价偏差；匹配任务说明顺序响应需要不同的错误计量；明确列出收益情形并解释为何诚实报告是安全的能改善选择；而促使代理进行详尽规划或形成关于对手信念的提示反而降低表现。行为改善经常与口头策略指标脱节：一些提示改善行为但不改善口头指标，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 交互格式与文本支架能改善大语言模型（LLM）代理的决策，但决策质量的提升并不总是伴随口头解释或计划质量的同步提升。

Abstract: Can interaction formats and textual scaffolds help large language model (LLM) agents make better decisions, and do better decisions come with better explanations? We study these questions in auctions and matching, multi-agent environments with explicit rules and known optimal strategies. These settings let us vary how a decision problem is presented while retaining a benchmark for evaluating behavior. Drawing on human-motivated theories of simplicity, we compare interfaces that elicit a complete bid or ranking with sequential interfaces that make safe choices easier to identify. We then hold the interaction format fixed and vary reasoning scaffolds and rule descriptions. Across four model families, the ascending auction interface substantially reduces bid deviations. The matching comparison also shows why sequential responses require different error accounting from complete rankings. Laying out payoff contingencies and explaining why truth-telling is safe also improve choices, whereas prompts to plan through matching rounds or form beliefs about opponents worsen play overall. In auctions, these behavioral gains are not accompanied by corresponding improvements in measured verbal indicators of strategic understanding in the agents' short stated plans. Other prompts change those indicators without improving bids. Our findings suggest that human-motivated theories of simplicity can inform the design of decision environments for artificial agents. They also show why scaffolds should be evaluated through realized choices as well as explanations: improvements in one need not appear in the other.

</details>


### [81] [Support-Set Target Leakage in Relational Foundation Models during In-Context Learning: Impact, Detection, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36384)
*Roshan Reddy Upendra,Alexandre Dorais,Joe Meyer,Andrew Pouret,Anastasios Lambrianos Stappas,Dinesh Katupputhur Ramprasath,Tom Palczewski,Minghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文定义并系统评估了关系型ICL中的“支持集目标泄露”，用14类合成leaker与RelBench实验证明目标表leaker最具破坏性，且IG可用于检测并部分缓解此类泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关系型ICL场景中，支持示例（支持集）有时含有源自目标的特征，这些特征在需要预测的查询中并不可用，导致模型凭借不合法信息获得错误的捷径。该工作将此问题从数据集构造、时间切分或表征学习中的泄露区分出来，系统化研究其影响与检测/缓解方法。

Method: 构造14类合成leaker（对应20个列），覆盖不同噪声水平、覆盖率、模态、语义透明度与关系距离；在保留的RelBench数据库上用冻结的关系编码器加ICL头进行评估；用Integrated Gradients对输入列打分并据此排序与移除可疑列，从而测评识别与缓解能力。

Result: （1）支持集目标泄露的负面影响随任务与表间关系距离而异；（2）来自目标表的leaker破坏性最大；（3）一跳和两跳leaker并非一致被模型利用；（4）Integrated Gradients能把目标表leaker排在高位，并在若干设置下通过移除高分列部分恢复性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出并研究了在关系型 in-context learning（ICL）中出现的“支持集目标泄露”（support-set target leakage）问题，即支持集中包含仅在支持集中可见的目标派生列（leaker），导致对查询的预测失误。结论是：支持集中存在的目标表(leaker)对模型性能造成最显著的负面影响；而一跳、两跳关系的leaker对模型的影响不稳定。采用Integrated Gradients（IG）可以在多数据集中较可靠地识别目标表leaker，并在泄露影响严重的设置下部分恢复性能。

Abstract: Relational in-context learning (ICL) conditions predictions on the labeled support examples and their linked tables, creating a failure mode when the support set contains target-derived features that are unavailable for the query. We formulate this problem as support-set target leakage, distinct from leakage during dataset construction, temporal splitting, or representation learning. Here, the target-derived (leaker) columns are present only in the labeled support set during relational in-context inference, while queries remain clean. We construct 14 synthetic leaker types, corresponding to 20 columns, spanning proxies with different noise levels, coverage, modalities, semantic transparency, and relational distances. We evaluate a frozen relational encoder with an ICL head on held-out RelBench databases and use Integrated Gradients (IG) to rank and remove suspicious columns. Our results show that the effect of support-set leakage varies across tasks and relational distances. Target-table leakers cause the clearest degradation, while one- and two-hop leakers are not consistently used by the model. IG ranks target-table leakers highly across datasets and partially recovers performance in settings where leakage has the largest effect.

</details>


### [82] [Persona Dosing: Calibrated Activation Steering for Graded Trait Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36388)
*Zehao Jin,Junran Wang,Ruixuan Deng,Jiahao Chen,Jingyuan Zhang,Yuxuan Zhang,Xinjie Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PersonaDose：用描述+强度标定的 FLAS 控制器来“剂量化”人格表达，能扩大并部分精确控制特质强度，但可达范围和每个目标的精确度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统激活干预难以以可解释的尺度请求特定程度的人格表达；需要一种既能用自然语言描述特质又能用可控标度请求强度的方法来‘配药’（dosing）模型行为。

Method: 提出了一个共享的、基于描述条件的 FLAS 控制器（PersonaDose），在未给出训练时强度标签的响应上对控制器进行专门化训练；随后通过测量特质表达对控制器的流时（flow time）进行标定以匹配请求的平均强度。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-3-4B 上，PersonaDose 在 Persona Vectors 的 75 一致性下相比对比性激活叠加分别提高了核心特质表达 33.2、18.3、17.8 点。标定选取的设置在未见问题上仍保留表达优势；对七个训练特质的 14–22 个可达目标，平均定位误差为 4.7–6.2 点（每模型 28 个候选目标）。

Conclusion: PersonaDose 能以“描述+目标强度”的方式对大模型的人格表达进行可控调节，能显著提升核心特质表达并实现强度标定，但可达范围有限且不同特质表现差异。

Abstract: An activation-steering coefficient sets intervention strength, but requesting a particular degree of persona expression requires a behavioral scale. We study persona dosing: controlling a language model through a trait description and a requested mean intensity. PersonaDose specializes a shared, description-conditioned FLAS controller on persona responses, then calibrates its flow time against measured trait expression. Training responses are not paired with requested target intensities. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen3-8B, and Gemma-3-4B, PersonaDose raises core-trait expression at the Persona Vectors coherence floor of 75 by 33.2, 18.3, and 17.8 points over contrastive activation addition. Calibration-selected settings retain an expression advantage on held-out questions, although the coherence floor does not hold for every trait there. Across seven trained traits, calibrated requests yield mean targeting errors of 4.7-6.2 points over 14-22 calibration-reachable targets out of 28 per model. These results separate the behavioral range learned by a controller from the accuracy of requests within that range.

</details>


### [83] [ARCagent: An Adaptive Retrieval Calibration Agent for Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36392)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCagent = curated ME/CFS KB + structured conflict registry + conflict-aware retrieval re-ranking + LLM-judged benchmark → improved, conflict-aware clinical QA (95.3%).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ME/CFS involves multiple coexisting and sometimes contradictory diagnostic frameworks and treatment guidelines. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems lack guarantees of knowledge completeness and dynamic conflict-aware synthesis, which are safety-critical in clinical QA.

Method: Constructed a 1,706-chunk knowledge base from 10 sources with a structured inter-guideline conflict registry; implemented a conflict-aware retrieval calibration pipeline that re-ranks retrieved evidence using query-specific focus and conflict signals; evaluated using a benchmark scored by an LLM-as-Judge and compared against keyword-matching and base LLMs.

Result: A benchmark performance of 95.3%, outperforming base LLMs; demonstrated that keyword-matching evaluation underestimates performance by ~10.1 percentage points; released code on GitHub.

Conclusion: ARCagent provides an adaptive retrieval-calibration QA agent for ME/CFS that improves evidence completeness and conflict-aware synthesis; it reports strong empirical gains over base LLMs and a keyword-matching baseline, achieving 95.3% on the proposed benchmark.

Abstract: In diseases where clinical guidelines are incomplete, contested, or mutually contradictory, knowledge completeness and dynamic conflict-aware synthesis are two safety-critical properties that standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems do not provide. Therefore, we present \sysname, an adaptive retrieval calibration clinical question-answering agent for ME/CFS, a disease where diagnostic frameworks coexist and major guidelines actively contradict each other on treatment. ARCagent contributes three components. First, a 1,706-chunk, 10-source knowledge base with a structured inter-guideline conflict registry spanning all active ME/CFS diagnostic frameworks. Second, a conflict-aware retrieval calibration pipeline that re-ranks retrieved evidence using query-specific focus and conflict signals. Third, a benchmark scored by LLM-as-Judge, avoiding systematic underestimation averaging 10.1 percentage points caused by keyword matching. ARCagent achieves 95.3%, outperforming all base LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Yukyin/ARCagent.

</details>


### [84] [From Retrieval to Reasoning: Agentic Mechanism Prediction from Cell Painting Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36406)
*Jiayuan Chen,Botao Yu,Tianyu Liu,Thai-Hoang Pham,Meng Wu,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将MOA预测视作可靠性校准的证据推理，PhenoAIR通过多智能体证据精化与离线校准显著改善Cell Painting的机制推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Cell Painting的表征匹配容易受批次效应、非特异性细胞毒性、表型汇聚与来源依赖性噪声影响，检索到的邻居常含误导性证据，因而需要一种能评估/比较/拒绝不可靠证据的可靠性驱动推理机制。

Method: PhenoAIR保存对每个候选的证据记忆，采用多个代理进行表型级与机制级证据评估与精化，并通过离线参考集校准为证据加权（源可靠性、表型稳定性、机制间混淆度），最后基于加权/被拒绝的证据进行机制判定。

Result: 在基于JUMP数据构建的基准上（受控、现实与开放世界设置），PhenoAIR较表示匹配和基于大模型的基线均有显著提升，证明其在多种实用场景下更稳健。

Conclusion: 本文将Cell Painting的MOA预测问题从简单的表征匹配重构为“带校准的证据推理”问题，提出PhenoAIR——一个可靠性感知的多智能体框架，通过候选中心的证据记忆和控制器引导的表型/机制侧证据精化提升预测准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cell Painting is a high-content morphological profiling assay widely used for phenotype-based biological inference, with mechanism of action (MOA) prediction as a central application. Existing approaches largely formulate Cell Painting-based inference as representation matching, assigning predictions from nearby reference perturbations in morphological feature space. However, retrieved neighbors are often noisy and partially misleading evidence due to batch effects, non-specific cytotoxicity, phenotypic convergence, and source-dependent variability. We reformulate Cell Painting-based MOA prediction as a calibrated evidence reasoning problem, where retrieved neighbors are treated as uncertain observations that must be evaluated, compared, and sometimes rejected before supporting a mechanistic conclusion. We propose PhenoAIR, a reliability-aware multi-agent framework that maintains a candidate-centric evidence memory and performs controller-guided refinement over phenotype- and mechanism-side evidence. PhenoAIR uses offline reference-set calibration to weight evidence by source reliability, phenotype stability, and mechanism-level confusion. We evaluate PhenoAIR on a benchmark constructed from JUMP Cell Painting profiles and annotations, covering controlled, realistic, and discovery-oriented open-world MOA prediction settings. PhenoAIR outperforms representation-matching and LLM-based baselines across all settings.

</details>


### [85] [Support-Set Target Leakage in Relational Foundation Models during In-Context Learning: Model Dependence and Evaluation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36417)
*Roshan Reddy Upendra,Alexandre Dorais,Joe Meyer,Andrew Pouret,Anastasios Lambrianos Stappas,Dinesh Katupputhur Ramprasath,Viswanath Ganapathy,Tom Palczewski,Minghua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Constructed diverse synthetic leakers and showed that target-derived leakage, especially 0-hop and combined, strongly skews relational ICL evaluation; detection helps rank leakers but does not guarantee recovery—thus enforcing a clear support/query information boundary and sensitivity analyses is essential.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Relational ICL can fail when support contexts include features derived from the query target that are absent for the query itself (support-set target leakage). The work aims to quantify how different leakage types affect evaluation and whether detectors can identify/remove leakers to restore clean evaluation.

Method: Constructed 20 controlled target-derived features varying in signal fidelity, representation, semantic transparency, coverage, and hop placement (0/1/2-hop). Evaluated these across 13 RelBench tasks and five relational ICL configurations (varying ICL head, message-passing depth, pretraining cohort, encoder architecture) under matched 0/1/2-hop and Full leakage conditions; compared IG-based, mutual information, and LOCO detectors on a baseline subset; performed a four-task rel-salt case study on native-schema leakage.

Result: 0-hop (target-table) and Full leakage cause largest aggregate deviations; higher-hop leakage generally weaker due to lower effective exposure (temporal reachability, sampling, aggregation). Effects are task- and model-dependent and can reverse model-ranking conclusions even with small aggregate changes. IG, MI, LOCO rank leakers best in high-impact settings but removing detected leakers does not reliably restore clean performance. Native-schema leakage in rel-salt replicates the concern.

Conclusion: Support/query information boundary is critical for reliable relational in-context learning evaluation; target-derived feature leakage (especially 0-hop and combined) can substantially distort model performance and comparisons, and current leak-detection/removal methods are imperfect.

Abstract: Relational in-context learning (ICL) uses labeled support examples and their linked relational context to predict labels for new queries. This creates a failure mode when target-derived features are present in the support context but unavailable for the query. We study this setting as support-set target leakage. We construct 20 controlled target-derived features that vary in signal fidelity, representation, semantic transparency, coverage, and zero-, one-, and two-hop relational placement, and evaluate them across 13 RelBench tasks and five relational ICL configurations that vary the ICL head, message-passing depth, pretraining cohort, or relational encoder architecture. We evaluate matched 0-hop, 1-hop, and 2-hop leakage settings, together with a Full leakage condition containing all 20 leaker columns. Within the tested configurations, target-table (0-hop) and Full leakage produce the largest aggregate deviations from clean evaluation, while higher-hop effects are often weaker, consistent with differences in effective exposure associated with temporal reachability, sampling, and aggregation fidelity. Leakage effects are strongly task- and model-dependent and can reverse relative conclusions between model variants even when aggregate changes are small. For leaker detection, we compare an Integrated Gradients (IG)-based screening method with mutual information (MI) and leave-one-column-out (LOCO) on a common Baseline subset. Ranking quality is strongest in the high-impact 0-hop and Full leakage conditions, but detector-based removal does not consistently restore the clean evaluation. A four-task rel-salt case study further shows the same evaluation concern with native-schema leakage candidates from the original relational schema. These results identify the support/query information boundary as an important component of reliable relational ICL evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [The Safety Operator: Modulating the Expression of Safety Instructions via Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36434)
*Benoit Dherin,Michael Munn,Xavier Gonzalvo,Adrian Goldwaser,Blaz Bratanic,Ananth Balashankar,Andrey Vlasov,Pinzhi Huang,Cecile Loge,Nicole Mitchell,Andre Fernandes,Trilok Acharya,Wendy Kan,Ziyue Wang,Hanna Mazzawi,Felipe Tiengo Ferreira,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把上下文看作乘法算子，其主特征值可作为调节安全指令影响力的连续参数；提出的对比安全损失通过抑制权重在增强对有害查询的抑制与减少对无害查询的误拒之间实现可控权衡，并在多种参数化下显示出鲁棒的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全指令在抑制有害输出时常导致对无害查询的过度拒绝，缺少一个可连续调节指令影响力的机制；研究能否通过分析模型内部算子特征值并设计损失来获得可控且稳健的安全行为。

Method: 将上下文嵌入视作可吸收进模型权重的乘法算子，理论上分析该算子的主特征值如何影响指令强度；基于该观察提出带抑制权重的对比安全损失函数，在含有害与无害查询的数据上系统性地调整抑制权重，测量攻击成功率与过度拒绝率随抑制权重和对应特征值的变化，并比较不同损失函数参数化下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验证明：改变抑制权重会改变算子主特征值，从而平滑地调整攻击成功率与过度拒绝率之间的权衡曲线；该权衡曲线在不同损失参数化下表现出相对一致性，存在一段抑制权重区间能带来帕累托改进（同时降低攻击成功和过度拒绝的折中）。

Conclusion: 在变压器模型中，作为乘法算子的上下文令牌的主特征值（dominant eigenvalue）确实可以作为一个连续的“调节盘”来调控安全指令对生成的影响；通过引入带抑制权重的对比安全损失（Contrastive Safety Loss），可以在对有害查询强化安全指令与对无害查询抑制过度拒绝之间进行可控权衡，从而在适当的抑制权重下实现帕累托改进。

Abstract: Context tokens in a transformer-based language model can be absorbed into the model's weights as a multiplicative operator. We study this operator in the setting of safety instructions and show that influencing its dominant eigenvalue modulates how strongly the instruction shapes generation. We derive a Contrastive Safety Loss with a suppression weight that controls the tradeoff between emphasizing the safety instruction on harmful queries while suppressing it on harmless queries. Varying the suppression weight maps a relationship between the attack success and the over-refusal rates, supporting the hypothesis that the operator's eigenvalue acts as a continuous dial for the instruction's influence. Moreover, this relationship holds relatively independently of how the Safety Loss is parameterized, yielding Pareto-improved safety instructions for appropriate values of suppression weight.

</details>


### [87] [Bits Under ZK-LLM: Evaluating Zero-Knowledge-Friendly Quantization for Verifiable Private LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36437)
*Taeung Yoon,Yupeng Zhang,Xiaojing Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向ZK证明的量化需要按算子选择精度：激活与非线性查表比权重更关键，RMSNorm的逆平方根查表是常见瓶颈，选择性提精能恢复精度，但减少位宽不必然线性降低证明成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZK proofs require arithmetic over finite fields and their proving cost depends on operation counts, nonlinearities, and lookups; yet there is limited understanding of how quantization choices map to ZK constraint structure and proving cost for LLMs, hindering practical ZK-LLM deployment.

Method: Formalize a definition of ZK-friendly quantization capturing ZK proof requirements; empirically evaluate nine LLMs (including Qwen2.5-14B and Qwen3-30B-A3B) across weight, activation, and nonlinear lookup-table precisions; profile utility degradation and identify bottleneck operators; measure implications for ZK proving cost and demonstrate selective precision increases.

Result: Activation precision is more sensitive than weight precision; nonlinear lookup-table approximations (e.g., inverse-square-root in RMSNorm) can be the main source of degradation; selectively increasing precision at bottleneck operators recovers utility; bit-width or lookup-table size reductions do not yield proportional proving-cost savings, motivating operator-aware precision selection.

Conclusion: The paper provides the first systematic study of quantization schemes optimized for zero-knowledge (ZK) LLM inference, showing that activation precision and nonlinear lookup approximations (not weights) dominate utility loss and that operator-aware precision tuning — e.g., higher precision for RMSNorm inverse-sqrt lookups — recovers near-baseline utility while impacting proving cost nonlinearly.

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs are emerging as a promising approach for enabling private, verifiable LLM governance and auditing, where regulators, users, and auditors need to verify claims about training-data usage or LLM inference-time behavior, while model providers must protect proprietary model parameters. However, despite the growing interest in ZK-LLMs, the understanding of ZK-friendly quantization remains limited. This gap matters because in the ZK setting, quantization directly shapes the arithmetic structure, constraint complexity, and proving cost of ZK inference. ZK protocols operate over finite fields and incur costs that depend heavily on the number and type of arithmetic operations, nonlinearities, and lookup constraints. Understanding ZK-friendly quantization is therefore essential for making ZK-LLMs practical. In this work, we present the first systematic study of ZK-friendly quantization for LLMs. We first formalize the definition of ZK-friendly quantization, capturing the properties required for ZK proof generation. We then evaluate nine language models, including Qwen2.5-14B and the mixture-of-experts model Qwen3-30B-A3B, across a broad design space of weight, activation, and nonlinear lookup table precision. Our results show that activation precision is substantially more sensitive than weight precision, while nonlinear lookup approximations can become the dominant source of utility degradation. Also, we identify RMSNorm inverse-square-root lookups as a recurring bottleneck in several large models and recover near-baseline utility by selectively increasing precision only at the bottleneck. Finally, we show that reducing bit-width or lookup-table size does not necessarily yield proportional end-to-end proving savings, showing that conventional low-bit quantization heuristics do not directly translate to ZK proving efficiency and motivating operator-aware precision selection.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Reasoning Paths as Phase-Structured Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36461)
*Zhenghao He,Guangzhi Xiong,Sanchit Sinha,Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 短评：PAIR通过在单题内部按归一化进度对齐推理相位、并在相同题相同相位内对比成功/失败轨迹，消除题级混淆，学得更纯粹的路径质量方向，能更准确地评价与引导多步推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：现有探测协议通常将中间状态贴标为最终答案正误并跨问题训练探针，这会产生两类混淆：一是正确性预测可能利用问题层面的差异而不是路径质量；二是按绝对步序对齐会把不同功能阶段混在一起，掩盖推理动态。作者提出在题内按相位对齐以消除这些混淆。

Method: 方法（PAIR）：对每个问题采样多条生成轨迹；将可变长度路径按归一化进度映射到共享的相对“相位”（phase）；仅在同一问题且同一相位内比较成功与失败轨迹以学习相位特定的‘路径质量方向’（probe）；用这些方向进行轨迹排序、Best-of-N选择与相位级别的生成指导（steering）。

Result: 实验结果：常规的跨题正确性探针在‘题内’评估下预测力显著下降，说明这些探针部分依赖题级信息。PAIR在题内轨迹排序和Best-of-N轨迹选择上均优于基线，跨模型与基准有效。通过相位级别的steering可以改变生成结果，提供了所学方向对生成过程的因果影响证据。

Conclusion: 本工作结论是：将推理路径视为在固定问题下的相位结构轨迹，并基于归一化进度进行相位对齐（PAIR），能更好地分离路径质量信号与问题层面的变化，从而显著提升对轨迹优劣的判别与选择，并能通过相位引导改写生成结果，提供因果证据表明学得方向确实承载轨迹相关信息。

Abstract: Large language models often improve problem-solving performance by generating multi-step reasoning paths, yet how to analyze the hidden states along these paths remains unclear. Existing approaches typically assign each intermediate state the final-answer correctness label and train probes across heterogeneous questions. We argue that this protocol obscures reasoning dynamics in two ways: (1) correctness prediction can exploit question-level variation rather than path quality, and (2) states aligned by absolute step indices may correspond to different functional phases of reasoning. In this work, we propose to view reasoning paths as phase-structured trajectories within fixed questions. We instantiate this view as PAIR, short for Phase-Aligned Intra-question Reasoning. PAIR samples multiple trajectories for each question, maps variable-length paths into shared relative phases based on normalized trajectory progress, and compares successful and unsuccessful trajectories only within the same question and phase. This yields phase-specific path-quality directions that better isolate path-quality signals from question-level variation. Empirically, we find that standard across-question correctness probes lose much of their predictive power under within-question evaluation, suggesting that these probes partly rely on question-level information. PAIR improves within-question trajectory ranking and Best-of-N trajectory selection across models and benchmarks. Phase-wise steering further shows that the learned directions can change generation outcomes, providing causal evidence that they capture trajectory-relevant information.

</details>


### [89] [Going Beyond State-Reaching: Learning Abstractions for Intrinsically Motivated Option Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36473)
*Akhil Bagaria,Anita De Mello Koch,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对每个子目标只使用少量相关特征来发现可泛化的选项，从而显著改善稀疏图像域的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选项发现方法往往把子目标当作完整状态到达问题，导致选项只在狭窄状态子空间有效、数量爆炸且不利于主任务的奖励最大化，需要更紧凑且具有泛化能力的子目标表示。

Method: 不是把子目标定义为到达完整状态，而是学习针对每个子目标的特征子集（feature-selective subgoals），并基于这些子集学习抽象且可迁移的选项；在图像输入上进行表征学习并发现局部相关特征，进而训练相应的选项策略。

Result: 生成的选项更通用，减少了选项数量膨胀，加速了在稀疏奖励图像域中的探索，论文在三个任务上取得快速探索表现，并在复杂关卡（如Montezuma's Revenge）上展示有效性。
