Conclusion: 提出通过为每个子目标识别一小部分相关特征，生成具有广泛泛化能力的选项，从而改进稀疏奖励、图像化环境中的探索；实验证明在三个图像域（包括Montezuma's Revenge）中能加速探索。

Abstract: Temporal abstraction via options can improve exploration in large environments. However, existing option discovery algorithms find subgoals that target all aspects of the state simultaneously. This state-reaching approach produces options that only apply in narrow regions of the state-space, eventually causing an explosion in the number of options that overwhelms the agent, and impedes progress on its primary task of reward maximization. We introduce an algorithm that instead identifies a small, relevant subset of features for each subgoal, yielding options that generalize broadly and accelerate exploration. Our approach learns abstract, transferrable options and achieves rapid exploration in three sparse-reward, image-based domains, including the Atari game MontezumasRevenge.

</details>


### [90] [Learning to Harvest Without Collapse in a Regenerative Commons: A Lagrangian Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36478)
*Jose Tupayachi,Xueping Li,Soham Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将再生公地表述为受约束马尔可夫博弈并采用非平稳拉格朗日构造，论文给出从无约束求解器构造受约束策略序列的理论保证，并在渔业实验上验证耗竭预算对系统行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合作或优化目标未明确规定应保留多少共享资源，导致公地悲剧。需要一种可指定耗竭预算并能在多智能体强化学习设定下保证资源保存的通用方法。

Method: 将再生公地建模为带耗竭预算的受约束马尔可夫博弈/多智能体MDP；引入非平稳拉格朗日框架和平均-纪元（average-epoch）解概念以处理重置情形、折扣奖励与终端代价；使用拉格朗日乘子和基于无约束求解器（如IPPO/MAPPO）的子问题迭代构造策略序列；理论上证明可行性证书、合作可行性、对可行策略混合的近似最优性，以及对无偏采样代价的扩展；为自利智能体给出约束纳什证书并量化价格分散项。

Result: 在理论上给出一系列证书（奖励无关的可行性证书、合作可行性、近似最优性、约束纳什证书），并在Gordon-Schaefer渔业仿真实验中展示受约束IPPO/MAPPO如何根据耗竭预算调整渔业存量、采收收益与价格适应性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能将公地悲剧的“保存”要求显式化为带约束的马尔可夫博弈，并通过非平稳拉格朗日方法从无约束求解器构造策略序列，给出可行性、近似最优性和纳什约束性等理论保证。

Abstract: The tragedy of the commons poses a multi-agent safety problem: reward-seeking agents can deplete a shared resource, and cooperation among its users does not itself specify how much must be preserved. We make preservation an explicit requirement by formulating a regenerative commons as a constrained Markov game or a constrained multi-agent MDP with a designer-specified depletion budget. We develop a nonstationary Lagrangian framework that constructs a policy sequence from solutions of unconstrained games or cooperative control problems. Extending earlier time-average constructions, we introduce average-epoch solution concepts for reset episodes with discounted rewards and terminal costs. We prove a reward-independent feasibility certificate, cooperative feasibility and approximate optimality against feasible policy mixtures, and an extension to unbiased sampled costs. For self-interested agents, a constrained Nash certificate quantifies the price-dispersion term introduced by deviations that redistribute budget across epochs. Under the stated assumptions on solver accuracy and multiplier updates, these results give constrained policy-sequence guarantees using solutions of unconstrained problems. Experiments with constrained IPPO and MAPPO in a Gordon-Schaefer fishery examine how depletion budgets shape stock retention, harvest rewards, and price adaptation.

</details>


### [91] [Human-AI Collaboration: From Paradoxes to Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36481)
*Michael Weiss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用“自治性×主动性”两维并通过悖论分析，系统化地导出了四种人机协作模式及其生成过程，为协作系统设计提供理论性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有证据表明人类与AI协同优于单独工作，但缺乏系统化的设计维度与模式分类。作者旨在用自治性与主动性两维度分析人机协作，并通过揭示内在矛盾为设计者提供可操作的模式库。

Method: 以概念性/理论性分析为主：提出一个表面张力的过程（surface tensions）、将问题映射为悖论（map paradoxes）、再从悖论映射中导出模式描述；文中通过框架化和推理来构建模式集合，缺少或未必包含大量实证数据。

Result: 提出并定义了四种人机协作模式（Instruction、Delegation、Assistance、Co-creation）；给出从识别问题到映射悖论再到生成模式描述的过程；强调悖论透视在理解和设计协作交互中的价值，为后续实证研究与设计实践提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：以自治性（autonomy）和主动性（initiative）为关键维度，可以将人机协作归纳为四种模式（Instruction、Delegation、Assistance、Co-creation）；采用悖论（paradox）视角有助于揭示设计中的内在张力，并以此映射出可操作的协作模式，有利于为人机协作系统设计提供理论与方法指导。

Abstract: Evidence shows that humans and AI systems perform better together, by collaborating, than alone. This paper examines two key design dimensions of human-AI collaboration (autonomy and initiative) and explores the collaboration patterns that they generate. Documenting these patterns starts with identifying the underlying problems and solutions, followed by examining the internal tensions within the problems. The paper uses a paradox perspective to analyze those tensions. It describes a process for surfacing the tensions and mapping the underlying paradoxes. It also illustrates how the pattern descriptions can be derived from mapping these paradoxes. Finally, the paper documents four human-AI collaboration patterns: Instruction, Delegation, Assistance, and Co-creation.

</details>


### [92] [AVIO: Learning to Add and Remove Sounding Objects in Audiovisual Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36503)
*Weihan Xu,Kan Jen Cheng,Koichi Saito,Jingyu Shi,Tingle Li,Yisi Liu,Liming Wang,Masato Ishii,Takashi Shibuya,Gopala Anumanchipalli,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AVIOBench提供大规模配对视听编辑训练数据，AVIO通过源条件特征调制和参考帧课表实现单模型的非语音对象视听增删编辑，评估显示出良好编辑效果与可选视觉指引控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对局部非语音视听编辑的配对监督稀缺，因为需要视觉和声学编辑同时锁定同一对象并把其声音与重叠声源分离，缺乏大规模标注的配对训练集与相应模型。

Method: 自动化管线通过视觉定位与跨模态一致性筛选目标声音移除候选，并对视听对进行联合精修；在此数据上，用源条件特征调制（source-conditioned feature modulation）微调预训练文本到视听生成模型，并采用参考帧课表（reference-frame curriculum）逐步减少参考框架条件，实现可选视觉引导的一体化增删编辑。

Result: 构建了含37.9小时、1,878个目标对象名称的AVIOBench，数据中每个目标对象有共享身份与视觉掩膜以链接视觉存在与声学贡献；所提出的AVIO模型在定量与定性评估中能有效完成对象声学的移除与添加，且在添加任务中可通过可选参考帧提供外观与位置控制。

Conclusion: 提出了AVIOBench数据集和AVIO模型，实现了在视频中局部非语音声音源的添加与移除，且能在保留场景的同时对目标对象的视觉与声学贡献进行联合编辑。

Abstract: Adding or removing a sounding object requires coordinated changes to visual content and sound while preserving the surrounding scene. Yet paired supervision for localized non-speech audiovisual editing remains limited, as visual and acoustic edits must target the same object and isolate its sound from overlapping sources. To address this gap, we introduce \textit{AVIOBench}, a dataset comprising 37.9 hours of paired audiovisual examples spanning 1{,}878 target-object names. AVIOBench links the visual presence and acoustic contribution of each target object through a shared identity and visual mask. Our automated pipeline uses visual grounding and cross-modal consistency to select target-sound removal candidates, then jointly refines the audiovisual pairs to improve perceptual quality and cross-modal consistency. Building on this dataset, we propose \textit{AVIO}, which adapts a pretrained text-to audiovisual generation model through source-conditioned feature modulation to jointly learn object addition and removal. A reference-frame curriculum gradually reduces reference conditioning during training, enabling one model to perform instruction-only editing with optional visual guidance. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate effective audiovisual object removal and addition, with optional reference guidance providing appearance and placement control for addition.

</details>


### [93] [BRIDGE: Bilevel Retrieval-Credit-Aware Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36505)
*Quan Xiao,Mingda Liu,Gaowen Liu,Katsuki Fujisawa,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为缩小检索与策略间的信息-信用差距，提出双层优化视角与内存友好的一阶解法BRIDGE，显著提升检索增强Agentic RL在多项QA任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARL方法只优化LLM生成的tokens，把检索到的证据视作环境观测，导致信息归因不清（信息-信用差距）：证据缺失或误导造成的失败被错归因于策略，从而需要联合训练检索器与LLM策略。

Method: 把检索增强的Agentic RL表述为双层优化问题（外层优化策略，内层优化检索器），基于对RL与检索目标的损失景观分析，提出BRIDGE——一种内存节省的一阶双层优化算法来实现协同训练。

Result: 在七个开放域QA基准上，BRIDGE以3B和7B骨干分别取得最高平均准确率，多跳任务相比最强基线提升9.6和3.4 EM点；在医学QA上也获得最佳平均答案准确率与推理质量。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了将检索器-策略学习建模为双层优化的观点，通过先适配检索器再训练策略能够获得更大回报；设计了内存高效的一阶双层方法BRIDGE，有效促进检索器与LLM策略的协同进化。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (ARL) with verifiable rewards improves the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle knowledge-intensive tasks by learning to interleave search and reasoning. However, most existing ARL methods optimize only LLM-generated tokens and treat retrieved evidence as environment observations. This creates an information-credit gap: failures caused by missing or misleading evidence are attributed to the LLM policy rather than to the retriever, which motivates training the LLM and the retriever jointly. In this paper, we show that retrieval and LLM policy learning are order-sensitive: adapting the retriever before optimizing the policy yields a larger reward gain than the reverse order. To preserve this hierarchy while allowing both components to co-adapt, we formulate retrieval-augmented agentic RL as a bilevel optimization problem. To solve it efficiently, we introduce BRIDGE, a memory-efficient first-order bilevel method motivated by a loss-landscape analysis of the RL and retrieval objectives. Across seven open-domain QA benchmarks, BRIDGE achieves the highest average accuracy with both 3B and 7B backbones, improving the multi-hop average over the strongest baseline by 9.6 and 3.4 EM points, respectively. It also achieves the best averaged answer accuracy and reasoning quality across medical QA benchmarks.

</details>


### [94] [MAADBench: The Refreshable Paradigm for Anomaly Detection in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36556)
*Lei Ma,Dennis Hofmann,Haowen Xu,Joshua DeOliveira,Peter VanNostrand,Lei Cao,Elke Rundensteiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个可刷新、多骨干适配的MAS异常检测基准MAADBench，发布5,200步标签的数据集，并证明了现有AD方法在MAS场景下的显著短板，推动MAS专用AD研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究指出LLM驱动的多代理系统失败率高（41%–87%），但缺乏支持系统性MAS异常检测的基准。构建此类基准面临任务泄露、轨迹与异常模式随骨干更新而过时、为每次刷新可靠标注的难题。作者希望通过可刷新设计与大规模生成策略解决这些挑战。

Method: MAADBench由三部分组成：1) 在约10^37的任务空间中采用采样与耦合的生成式任务以降低任务泄露风险；2) 在可配置的LLM骨干下可刷新地生成代理交互轨迹（traces）；3) 自动提供确定性的、零成本的步级标签以支持细粒度异常检测评价。同时作者在五种先进LLM骨干上运行生成流程并发布包含5,200个步级标签轨迹的MAADBench-Full数据集。

Result: 作者发布了MAADBench-Full数据集，并在其上评估了25种异常检测方法，结果表明当前方法在MAS场景中普遍存在依赖监督、对MAS特有细微异常识别能力差、对不同LLM骨干缺乏稳健性等问题。研究指出了MAS特定AD的研究空白并提供了可持续的基准平台。

Conclusion: 作者提出了MAADBench，一套可刷新（refreshable）的多代理系统（MAS）异常检测（AD）基准，旨在针对不断演进的大规模语言模型（LLM）骨干进行系统化评估。基准显示现有AD方法在MAS场景中存在显著不足，尤其依赖监督、对细微MAS异常识别能力弱、对不同LLM骨干不鲁棒。

Abstract: Recent studies report that LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) fail at rates of 41%-87%, yet to our knowledge, no benchmark to date supports systematic anomaly detection (AD) for them. Building MAS AD benchmarks is hard because they must remain fresh as LLM systems evolve: tasks may leak into training data and thus be memorized by LLMs, traces and anomaly patterns expire as backbones evolve, and labels must be provided reliably for each refresh. To address these challenges, we present MAADBench (MA: multi-agent; AD: anomaly detection), the first refreshable MAS AD benchmark designed for diverse, evolving LLM backbones underlying the agents. MAADBench combines (1) sampled-and-coupled generative tasks over an approximately 10^37-task space to mitigate task leakage, (2) refreshable trace generation under configurable LLM backbones, and (3) automated provision of cost-free, deterministic step-level labels for fine-grained AD evaluation. Beyond offering the paradigm itself, we run MAADBench with five state-of-the-art LLM backbones and release the MAADBench-Full dataset with 5,200 step-labeled traces. Benchmarking 25 AD methods on the MAADBench dataset reveals substantial limitations in current approaches: they rely heavily on supervision, struggle with subtle MAS-specific anomalies, and lack robustness across LLM backbones. These gaps point to a rich research agenda for MAS-specific anomaly detection, with MAADBench providing a systematic and refreshable testbed for method development and evaluation. We open-source MAADBench-Full at https://huggingface.co/datasets/hww123/MAADBench-full.

</details>


### [95] [Visual sensitivity is not claim retractability: persistence-aware credit assignment for multimodal reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36572)
*Zhongan Bi,Kepeng Lin,Xuanang Gao,Yuhan Sun,Lianrun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对回答在干预图像上的固定回放重评分，本文分离出视觉敏感性与断言坚持性，发现某些RL算法会增加不被图像支持断言的坚持性（SPD）。提出PACG按断言坚持性门控奖励，能在无需标签与额外开销下减少幻觉并提升多基准表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于结果的RL奖励将整个回答作为单一实体评分，导致即便回答正确、其中包含的与图像不符的视觉断言也被整体奖励——加剧多模态模型的“幻觉”问题。需要一种能在不依赖人工标注的情况下，分离视觉敏感性与断言可撤回性，并在RL信用分配中有选择性地削弱对不支持断言的奖励。

Method: 提出一种fixed-rollout counterfactual diagnostic：对同一回答在“干预后”的图像下重新评分，分解出Evidence-Function Sensitivity (EFS，预测随图像改变的强度)与claim persistence（模型是否继续支持相同视觉断言）；在此诊断基础上设计PACG机制，在RL回报阶段对异常坚持的视觉断言降低正向信用，而不改变其它信用分配。实验在Qwen2.5-VL-7B及更大/更新骨干模型上，使用DAPO、VPPO、GRPO等算法，在九个基准和HallusionBench上评测。

Result: 诊断发现SPD：DAPO与VPPO提升EFS同时使不被支持的断言更具坚持性，而GRPO仅提升EFS且无此恶化。引入PACG后，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，九基准三随机种子平均准确率：DAPO从58.1%提升到59.9%，VPPO从59.8%提升到60.9%；同时使不被支持断言更易撤回。效果在更大模型与新骨干上可迁移，HallusionBench准确率亦稳定提升。PACG无额外推理成本且不需支持/不支持标签。

Conclusion: 本文揭示并验证了“敏感性-坚持性解耦”(SPD)现象：在视觉强化学习微调（如DAPO、VPPO）下，模型对图像干预的预测更加敏感（EFS上升），但对于不被图像支持的视觉断言却可能变得更难撤回（claim persistence上升）；相反，GRPO提高了EFS但没有造成不良的坚持性上升。基于此，提出了Persistence-Aware Credit Gating (PACG)，对异常坚持的视觉断言削弱正向奖励，从而在不增加推理成本且无需额外标签的情况下，提升模型在多基准上的整体表现并减少幻觉性断言。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has been extended to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), and perception-aware methods further encourage policies to rely on visual evidence. Yet relying on the image does not guarantee that visual claims are supported by it. Before RL training, 27.81% of the correctly answered responses of Qwen2.5-VL-7B on four multimodal reasoning benchmarks contain at least one direct visual claim that the image does not support. Since outcome-level RL rewards each response as a whole, these claims inherit the positive credit of the correct answer. We introduce a fixed-rollout counterfactual diagnostic that re-scores the same response under an intervened image to separate Evidence-Function Sensitivity (EFS), how strongly the model's predictions change, from claim persistence, whether the model keeps supporting the same claim rather than retracting it. The diagnostic reveals Sensitivity-Persistence Decoupling (SPD): under DAPO and VPPO, EFS increases and claims become more retractable overall, yet unsupported claims become significantly more persistent, whereas GRPO raises EFS without this deterioration. We therefore propose Persistence-Aware Credit Gating (PACG), which attenuates positive credit for unusually persistent visual claims and leaves all other credit unchanged. It requires no supported/unsupported labels and adds no inference cost. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, PACG raises the nine-benchmark average over three seeds from 58.1% to 59.9% with DAPO and from 59.8% to 60.9% with VPPO, while making unsupported claims more retractable. The gains extend to a larger model, a newer backbone, and the accuracy of HallusionBench also improves consistently. These results suggest that visual sensitivity and claim retractability are complementary dimensions of multimodal credit assignment.

</details>


### [96] [Divide and Inject: Can Agents Reconstruct an Indirect Prompt Injection from Fragments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36576)
*Michael Lee,Zhipeng Wei,Yue Dong,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种把恶意目标分片并用自适应检索+迭代优化促使代理重构执行的新攻击（AdaLCPI），在实验中大幅超过若干基线，提示需要在安全评估中考虑片段化重构威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大模型的代理系统广泛使用检索与工具，攻击者可以利用长上下文与检索结果中的零碎信息间接注入目的，而无需单处放置完整恶意指令，扩大了提示注入攻击面。研究者需要量化并建模这类新威胁。

Method: AdaLCPI将攻击目标拆分为不完整片段，嵌入在通过代理工具检索到的外部内容中；设计重构提示（reconstruction cue）引导代理把片段合并并执行；以OpenEvolve进行迭代优化，使用分级评分与目标代理的自然语言执行反馈改进片段与提示。

Result: 在所报实验中，AdaLCPI在宏平均攻击成功率（ASR）上达到61.4%，显著高于Trojan Hippo风格的32.8%和AgentVigil的30.0%，表明该方法在所设评估下更容易促成代理重构并执行有害目标。

Conclusion: 本文提出的AdaLCPI展示了一种新的对话代理攻击向量：通过将恶意目标分割成分散在长上下文中的不完整片段，并利用自适应检索与重构提示促使代理拼合这些片段，从而实现间接提示注入。实验表明此攻击比已有自适应基线更有效，提示安全评估需覆盖片段化重构情形。

Abstract: Agentic systems are now being widely used to orchestrate tools and reason over long contexts. However, the improving capabilities of the large language models powering these agents also create new attack surfaces for indirect prompt injection. In particular, an attacker may not need to place a complete malicious instruction in retrieved content if the agent can reconstruct the objective from incomplete fragments distributed across a long context. In this work, we introduce adaptive long-context prompt injection (AdaLCPI), which combines long-context fragmentation with adaptive search. AdaLCPI splits an attack objective into incomplete fragments, embeds them in external content retrieved through the agent's tools, and uses a reconstruction cue to prompt the agent to combine them. It then iteratively refines the fragments and cue with OpenEvolve using graded scoring and natural-language execution feedback from the target agent. Empirically, AdaLCPI achieves higher attack success than strong adaptive baselines, reaching 61.4\% macro-average ASR compared with 32.8\% for Trojan Hippo-style and 30.0\% for AgentVigil. Safety evaluations should therefore test whether agents remain robust when harmful objectives must be reconstructed from incomplete fragments.

</details>


### [97] [SafeCoEvo: Co-Evolving Safety Harnesses and Guards for LLM Agents at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36580)
*Yu Cheng,Yongkang Hu,Shuaijie Ma,Zhihang Lin,Weicheng Meng,Jingyang Qiao,Jiuan Zhou,Yushuo Zhang,Yihang Chen,Weilin Luo,Kun Shao,Dong Li,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeCoEvo 通过短期显式知识快速适应与长期参数化能力巩固的协同进化，在测试时持续利用运行经验提高 LLM 代理的安全性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法依赖可重复访问的固定任务分布并多轮优化，与现实部署中只能利用过去任务经验改进未来决策的测试时适应场景不符。

Method: 在测试时在线积累经验，S-Harness 将近期运行时经验快速外化为可更新的显式安全知识以立即影响后续任务；GuardVPO 在更长时域上将累计经验内化为参数化的风险判断模型（例如通过强化或持续训练），两者协同更新以兼顾快速适应与稳健能力增长。

Result: 在实验证明下，SafeCoEvo 在最强基线之上将不安全结果率降低了 10.05%，同时将任务成功率提升了 12.15%，表明在安全与任务效用上实现了协同提升。

Conclusion: SafeCoEvo 提出了一种在测试时从运行时经验中持续适应的 Harness-Guard 协同进化框架，联合提升短期可更新的显式安全知识（S-Harness）和长期参数化风险判断能力（GuardVPO），从而实现同时降低不安全率并提高任务成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed in real-world environments continually encounter new tasks and safety risks, while execution feedback typically becomes available only after each task is completed. However, existing self-evolving approaches commonly rely on multiple rounds of optimization over fixed and repeatedly accessible task distributions, fundamentally differing from test-time adaptation in real-world deployment, where only experience accumulated from past tasks can be used to improve safety decisions on future unseen tasks. To address this limitation, we propose SafeCoEvo, a test-time Harness-Guard co-evolution framework for LLM agent safety that enables the external safety system to continually adapt from accumulated runtime experience. SafeCoEvo jointly improves two complementary safety capabilities at different timescales: S-Harness rapidly externalizes recent runtime experience into updatable explicit safety knowledge that can promptly influence subsequent tasks, while GuardVPO internalizes accumulated runtime safety experience over a longer timescale into parametric risk-judgment capabilities. By combining short-term rapid adaptation with long-term capability consolidation, SafeCoEvo continually improves the agent's safety capabilities, reducing the unsafe outcome rate by 10.05% while improving the task success rate by 12.15% over the strongest baseline, thereby achieving simultaneous gains in safety and task utility.

</details>


### [98] [MemEvo: Automatic Discovery of Streaming Video Memory Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36581)
*Guohong Liu,Jialei Ye,Shanhui Zhao,Yunxin Liu,Yuanchun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过DSL描述记忆原语并用预训练LLM指导搜索可执行记忆程序，MemEvo自动发现训练-free、因果且有界的高效流式视频记忆机制，在基准上展示出性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在查询未知的流式视频理解中，必须在查询到来前将无限增长的视觉流压缩到有界内存，记忆机制（何时保留、如何表示与整合、如何检索）决定性能。手工设计单一记忆架构难以覆盖巨大异构的设计空间，故将设计问题形式化为可执行程序的搜索。

Method: 提出一个包含表示、接纳、保留、整合、预算和检索原语的领域专用语言（DSL），在因果和有界内存约束下构建可执行程序空间；用预训练LLM作为语义感知的生成器提出候选程序，基于实际执行的实验反馈迭代生成和精化；使用确定性评估流水线验证并评价候选设计，视觉-语言模型保持冻结，最终输出训练免费记忆机制。

Result: 在StreamingBench和OVO-Bench上的大量实验表明，所发现的记忆机制在流式视频理解任务上取得了较强性能，同时在上下文与推理效率上有显著提升，且整个发现过程无需对基础视觉-语言模型进行训练。

Conclusion: 作者将有界记忆机制的设计视为可执行“记忆程序”搜索问题，提出一种轻量级DSL表达记忆机制并借助预训练大模型（LLM）作为语义感知的提案器，通过迭代生成—评估—优化的自动化流程（MemEvo）发现训练免费、满足因果和有界内存约束的高效流式视频理解记忆机制。

Abstract: Query-agnostic streaming video understanding requires vision-language models to continuously compress an indefinitely growing visual stream into a bounded memory before future queries are known. The performance depends critically on the memory mechanism--what observations to preserve, how to represent and consolidate them, and what information to retrieve when a query eventually arrives. Rather than designing a single memory architecture by hand, we formulate memory design as a search problem over executable memory programs. We introduce a lightweight domain-specific language that expresses memory mechanisms through structured primitives for representation, admission, retention, consolidation, budgeting, and retrieval, while enforcing causal and bounded-memory constraints. Although structured, the derived program space remains large and contains heterogeneous, conditionally dependent design choices whose effects can only be assessed via downstream execution. We therefore propose MemEvo, an LLM-driven auto-research framework that uses pretrained LLM as a semantics-aware proposal model to iteratively generate and refine candidate memory programs based on accumulated experimental feedback. At runtime, a deterministic evaluation pipeline validates and evaluates each candidate, while the underlying vision-language model remains frozen throughout discovery. We finally produce a training-free, bounded-memory mechanism. Extensive experiments on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench demonstrate strong streaming video understanding performance together with substantial context and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [99] [Transformers Stop Thinking Too Early, and a Tiny LoRA Fixes It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36585)
*Zehao Jin,Ruixuan Deng,Junran Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一枚极小的、放在早期层的LoRA能在不改动主模型权重的前提下，显著扩展变换器对长链引用的跟踪能力，机制为中层的“接力”式信息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练变换器在利用深层计算跟踪上下文引用方面表现不足，研究者希望通过极小的可逆编辑（LoRA）解锁模型深层尚未被使用的计算能力，低成本扩展模型的链式推理长度并理解其内部机制。

Method: 测量13个基础预训练模型在链式引用任务（按行计）上的基线表现；在一个早期层训练rank-8 LoRA（其他权重冻结），通过多次循环应用LoRA并记录准确率变化；使用中间层/注意力分析、父行注意力消融和冻结模型的干预定位方法来揭示机制；将相同方法应用到任务特定数据（MuSiQue）以验证通用性。

Result: 基础模型只可靠跟随1.4–3.6行；在早期层加入rank-8 LoRA后，Qwen3-8B在24行链条任务上从15.5%提升到99%，进一步训练达50行；Ouro-1.4B在4次循环后达到60行，8次至少160行；分析显示LoRA在中层启动接力传递链身份，冻结的注意力头逐层读取更远的行，移除父行注意力会中断接力；冻结模型的干预测量能在多数持出模型中定位最后有用的干预层；任务特定LoRA也使MuSiQue表现提升。

Conclusion: 在冻结主模型权重的条件下，向一个早期层注入小规模（rank-8）的LoRA就能极大扩展变换器模型对多行引用/链式追踪的能力：LoRA在中层启动“接力”式传播，使后续冻结的注意力头能够读取更远的链条位置，从而将可跟踪行数从几行扩展到几十甚至上百行。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers use little of their depth to follow references in context. Thirteen base models reliably follow only 1.4-3.6 lines, and extra pretrained loops add little. A task-trained rank-8 LoRA at one early layer extends this computation with all model weights frozen. Qwen3-8B improves from 15.5% to 99% exact accuracy on 24-line chains; a longer-trained LoRA reaches 50 lines. Ouro-1.4B reaches 60 lines after four loops and at least 160 after eight. The LoRA starts a relay: program lines pass on their chain identity through a short range of middle layers. Frozen heads read progressively further up the chain, and removing parent-line attention stops the relay. A frozen-model measurement locates the last useful intervention layer within tolerance in three of four held-out models. Task-specific LoRAs also improve MuSiQue. Default answers therefore understate the computation accessible through a tiny edit. Code and an interactive demo are available at https://lunamos.github.io/stop-thinking-too-early/

</details>


### [100] [SAKI: Maximal-Coupling-Routed Teacher Supervision for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36601)
*Miteto Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Zehao Chen,Jiajun Chai,Sichao Liu,Li Wang,Haoyuan Xu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAKI 用 KL 约束的最大耦合机制把接受/修正事件转化为有界且自适应的 token 级监督分配，在不改变目标轨迹分布的前提下提高训练效率与数学推理任务上的解题性能，同时通过引擎层的验证器显著提升吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 on-policy distillation（OPD）通过让学生基于自身轨迹训练来缓解训练-测试状态不匹配，但当学生较弱时会访问与教师不一致的前缀，导致监督信号在这些位置不具代表性，亟需一种冲突自适应的监督分配机制。

Method: 引入 KL 约束的教师引导 rollout，采用最大耦合（maximal coupling）机制在每步决定接受（sampled-token、reverse-KL）或修正（teacher argmax、直接监督）；在耦合下修正概率等于两分布的总变差距离 TV(p_t,q_t)，因此同一信任域半径既控制 rollout 偏离也上界介入与专门监督频率；并实现了一个驻引擎的 speculate verifier，保留 exact-q 轨迹分布与耦合语义，同时将 matched-workload 下的 rollout 吞吐提升 4.22x。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准上，相对于匹配的教师引导基线，SAKI 在 1.7B 与 0.6B 学生模型上均提升了 Mean@8 和 Pass@8；实现细节包括修正概率为 TV(p_t,q_t) 的解析性保证和 4.22× 的 matched-workload 吞吐改进；放置控制与固定前缀分析进一步支持“修正触发路由”作为冲突自适应监督信号。

Conclusion: SAKI 通过基于 KL 约束的教师引导 rollout 与最大耦合，将基于位置的“接受/修正”事件复用为 token 级监督路由策略，从而在保证轨迹分布（exact-q）和耦合语义的前提下，自适应地将更强监督施加于与教师偏差更大的位置，减少训练-测试不匹配并提升学生性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-test state mismatch by training a student on its own generated trajectories, but weak students may visit teacher-misaligned prefixes where supervision is less representative. We introduce SAKI (Supervision Allocation with KL-constrained Interpolation), which combines a KL-constrained teacher-guided rollout with maximal coupling and reuses realized accept/correction events to route token-level supervision. Accepted positions retain sampled-token reverse-KL supervision, while correction positions receive direct supervision on the teacher's highest-probability token. Under maximal coupling, the correction probability is exactly TV(p_t, q_t), so the same trust-region radius controls rollout deviation and upper-bounds intervention and specialized-supervision frequency. We further implement an engine-resident speculative verifier that preserves the exact-q trajectory distribution and coupling semantics while improving matched-workload rollout throughput by 4.22x. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SAKI improves the matched teacher-guided baseline in Mean@8 and Pass@8 for both 1.7B and 0.6B students. Placement controls and fixed-prefix analysis further support correction-triggered routing as a conflict-adaptive supervision signal.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-Channel Mitigation of Source-Trust Shortcuts in Fact-Checking RL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36611)
*Jianchang Su,Yiwei Yang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入TrustSwap揭露证据可信度标签能被模型作为改变裁决的捷径；简单的数据增强TSA在4B上能减弱这种捷径但在8B上未见效，尺度问题值得进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理应把源标签用于调整模型对证据置信度与检索决策，而非直接改变最终事实裁决；需要一种系统化的反事实测试来检测模型是否把标签当捷径。

Method: 提出TrustSwap反事实测试：在保持证据文本不变的前提下交换、降低或移除源标签，分别测量三条输出通道（裁决/verdict、置信度/confidence、是否继续检索/search decision）。在不同模型（未训练和RL训练）、尺度（4B/8B）、数据集和提示下运行测试。为缓解问题，提出信任交换数据增强（TSA）：在GRPO微调时对每个实例同时用原始与标签交换的证据训练，并保持相同金标准裁决；还评估了一致性奖励和其他基于奖励的替代方法。

Result: 在49/50次比较中，置信度与检索决策按标签变化做出预期响应；但仅改变标签就能使Qwen3模型在4–23%（未指明情形）的自信裁决上翻转，已有RL训练的事实核查器在部分情形下达50%。标准GRPO微调在8B上放大了这一捷径。TSA在4B上使裁决翻转率在四个（共六）设置中相对下降7–35%，保留准确率和期望的置信/检索响应，在主设置上优于基于奖励的替代方法，并能迁移到未见的标签移除扰动；加入一致性奖励对训练过的交换有帮助，但对未见扰动无效；在8B上TSA未表现出可检测效应。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：证据来源的可信度标签（HIGH/LOW）在检索增强的事实核查系统中被模型作为“捷径”使用——虽然标签应影响模型的置信度与是否继续检索，但标签改变会不恰当地改变最终裁决。TrustSwap 能揭示这一问题；通过简单的数据增强（TSA）在中等规模（4B）上能显著降低裁决被标签操纵的翻转率，但在更大模型（8B）上未见效果，尺度成为开放问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented fact-checkers often receive a reliability label, such as HIGH or LOW trust, for each evidence source. These labels should adjust the model's confidence and its decision to search for more evidence, while the verdict should follow the evidence content. We introduce TrustSwap, a counterfactual test that swaps, lowers, or removes source labels while keeping every evidence text fixed, and measures its three output channels (the verdict, the confidence, and the search decision) separately. Across untrained and RL-trained models at two scales, three datasets, and two prompts, confidence and search respond to the labels as intended in 49 of 50 comparisons, yet a label change alone alters 4-23% of confident verdicts for Qwen3 models and up to 50% for an existing RL-trained fact-checker. Standard GRPO fine-tuning amplifies this shortcut at 8B in all six settings. To reduce it, we propose trust-swap augmentation (TSA), which trains GRPO on each claim with both its original and its label-swapped evidence under the same gold verdict. At 4B, TSA lowers the verdict flip rate by 7-35% (relative) in four of six settings, keeps accuracy and the intended confidence and search responses, outperforms reward-based alternatives in the main setting, and carries over to an unseen label-removal perturbation. An added consistency reward helps on the trained-on swap but not on unseen perturbations. At 8B, TSA's effect is not detectable, which makes scale the main open question.

</details>


### [102] [Neural Structural Reasoner: A Brain-inspired Architecture for Reasoning over Structured Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36620)
*Zixing Jia,Yuhang Pan,Ni Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NSR把关系结构直接编码到神经连接与动力学中，借生物启发机制实现高可解释性的结构推理，在若干知识图谱基准上表现竞争且训练更快，能显性提取层次与规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将拓扑关系压缩为平坦嵌入，难以发现隐含结构且可解释性差。作者旨在提出一种既能保留关系拓扑又具可解释性的神经结构推理模型。

Method: NSR受三种生物机制启发：多层结构用于编码分层知识、实体与概念的稳定表示、以及用于输入驱动状态推断的路径积分。推理时并行枚举候选关系结构并用置信度加权分数执行链路预测；推理过程由可读的神经元激活序列构成。

Result: 在标准知识图谱基准上，NSR取得了具有竞争力的准确率（但并非在所有数据集上领先），训练时间低于若干神经基线。模型能提取潜在的关系层次和组合规则，并通过中间神经激活序列提供原生可解释性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Neural Structural Reasoner (NSR) 通过将关系结构直接保存在耦合神经元群的连接和动力学中，实现了一种解释性强、效率高的结构推理方法。

Abstract: Structural reasoning, the ability to recognize and make inferences over the relational structure between objects and concepts, is a hallmark of human cognition, yet prevailing methods often collapse relational topology into flat embeddings, cannot discover hidden structure and lack interpretability. We introduce Neural Structural Reasoner (NSR), a brain-inspired network that preserves relational structure directly in the connectivity and dynamics of coupled neuronal populations. NSR draws inspiration from three biological mechanisms: multi-layered architecture for encoding hierarchical knowledge, stable representations of entity and concepts, and path integration for input-driven state inference. At query time, NSR parallelizes computation over candidate relational structures and leverages confidence-weighted scores to perform link prediction. Across standard knowledge-graph benchmarks, NSR achieves competitive accuracy without leading on every dataset, and has lower reported training times than several neural baselines. Because reasoning is implemented through sequences of human-readable neuron activations, NSR affords native interpretability by tracking intermediate inference steps. The model further extracts latent relational hierarchies and compositional rules, demonstrating the brain-inspired architecture as an effective, efficient, and highly interpretable substrate for structural reasoning.

</details>


### [103] [DualTrack: Synchronized speech-gesture generation via symmetric coupling of pretrained priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36624)
*Yuanzhuo Hu,Zehan Liu,Xiaoyi Qin,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualTrack，通过因果适配器与当前状态融合在统一低帧率时间线上耦合语音与动作先验，实现在多语与说话人外推设置下优于GELINA的手势合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在配对语音—手势数据稀缺的条件下，需要在两个模态之间建立更强的双向协调（bidirectional interaction）、扩展语言覆盖并保留细粒度的身体与手指表示。现有方法在交互性、语言范围或人体细节表达上存在不足。

Method: 在12.5Hz的统一时间线上耦合了预训练的语音与动作先验；使用因果适配器在包间交换历史信息，使用当前状态融合在并行完成各自的十六码本分包前协调两条流；分别输出动作码（16码本包）并重建动作序列。评测采用43条BEAT2录音，覆盖四种语言，训练/验证时对说话人做外推。

Result: 在不使用语音或动作前缀的情形下，DualTrack在共享英/西双语输入上相比所评估的GELINA基线，取得更低的词错误率（WER）、更低的全动作Fréchet Gesture Distance（FGD）、更高的beat一致性与语音自然度。评测数据为多语、多说话人外推设置。

Conclusion: DualTrack有效整合预训练语音与动作先验，在共享12.5Hz时间线上通过因果适配器与当前状态融合实现双向流交互，从而在多语言与受检者外推设置中优于GELINA基线。

Abstract: Joint speech-gesture synthesis must coordinate two modalities despite limited paired data. Existing approaches often lack bidirectional interaction, have limited language coverage, or simplify body and finger representations. We present DualTrack, which couples pretrained speech and motion priors on a shared 12.5 Hz timeline. Causal adapters exchange previous-packet information, while current-state fusion coordinates the streams before they separately complete sixteen-codebook packets. We evaluate 43 BEAT2 recordings in four languages, with speakers held out from joint training and validation. On the shared English/Spanish inputs, without speech or motion prefixes, DualTrack achieves lower word error rate and full-motion Fréchet Gesture Distance, higher beat consistency and speech naturalness than the evaluated GELINA baseline.

</details>


### [104] [Semantic Projection for Continual Self-Evolution of Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36626)
*Ziyu Liu,Jun Chen,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SSPE 将正交梯度投影的思想引入行为/语义空间，通过识别并保留先前能力来构建兼容更新，从而在动态异质任务流中实现稳定的语言技能演化、降低遗忘并提升迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理需要持续的、可被修订的自然语言技能以适应动态任务，但共享技能在非平稳、异质任务流中易发生覆盖性干扰；传统方法如正交梯度投影依赖参数梯度与向量空间，无法直接用于无梯度、行为导向的技能修订，因此需要一种在行为层面保护既有能力的机制。

Method: 对未经约束的技能修改视为候选更新，通过在行为（语义）空间识别该更新可能影响到的既有能力，基于观测到的增益与回退构建一个兼容的修订——即在行为空间上执行‘语义范围投影’，保留先前能力同时接纳有益改动，而非简单拒绝更新。

Result: 在受控的合成任务流与异质的真实代理基准上，SSPE 相较于强基线提升了最终跨域能力、减轻了遗忘，并且在将演化后的技能迁移到不同执行模型时仍表现为最高的平均性能，表明语义投影是稳定自我演化的可行原则。

Conclusion: 将梯度投影的功能性原则从参数空间迁移到行为空间，SSPE 允许共享的自然语言技能在非平稳、异质任务流中稳定演化，减少遗忘并提高跨领域总体能力，同时保持在迁移到不同执行器模型后的强劲表现。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on persistent natural-language skills to adapt beyond their frozen model parameters. When a shared skill is repeatedly revised from a non-stationary, heterogeneous task stream, however, improvements for new tasks can overwrite procedures needed for earlier ones. In continual learning, Orthogonal Gradient Descent (OGD) addresses analogous interference by projecting a new-task gradient onto a subspace that locally preserves prior predictions. Natural-language skill revisions, however, have neither gradients nor a canonical vector space in which such a projection can be performed. We introduce \emph{Semantic-Scope Projected Evolution} (SSPE), which transfers the functional principle of gradient projection from parameter space to behavior space. SSPE treats an unconstrained skill revision as a proposed update, identifies acquired capabilities with which it may interfere, and uses the observed gains and regressions to construct a compatible revision rather than merely rejecting the update. This enables one shared skill to evolve across latent and recurring task contexts without exposing semantic domain identities to the evolution model. Across controlled synthetic streams and heterogeneous real-agent benchmarks, SSPE improves final cross-domain competence and mitigates forgetting relative to strong skill-evolution baselines. The evolved skill also retains the strongest average performance after transfer to a different executor model. These results establish semantic projection as a promising principle for stable and adaptive self evolution of language agents.

</details>


### [105] [Distilling Agentic Systems: A Roadmap across Models, Artifacts, and Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36630)
*Ziluowen Luo,Senzhang Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Jun Yin,Hao Yan,Ming Cheng,Chenxu Wang,Songyang Liu,Litian Zhang,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent Distillation概念与基于保留位置的分类，以及关联因果贡献与部署效用的评估框架，为跨组件知识迁移提供理论和评估基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能代理依赖记忆、工具和执行逻辑，能力已超出模型参数，传统的知识蒸馏（关注学生如何模仿教师）不足以描述和指导跨组件的知识转移。

Method: 主要采用概念建模与范畴化方法：定义知识迁移的保留位置、分离转移证据与结果、并构建一个将保留与因果贡献和部署效用关联的评估框架。

Result: 给出一套组织视角（按保留位置分类）、一个用于量化保留与因果与效用关系的评估框架，并阐明了如何在代理组件间追踪知识流动，提出研究基础以促进可控的代理蒸馏实践。

Conclusion: 本文提出“Agent Distillation”概念，主张将知识迁移按保持位置（模型内、工件、执行框架、基底）进行组织，并提出了把“保持”与“因果贡献”和“部署效用”关联的评估框架，从而为复杂代理系统的可靠、可维护和安全开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Modern agents increasingly rely on memories, tools, and execution logic, so their competence extends beyond model parameters. This shift exposes a limitation of conventional knowledge distillation, which asks how a student model imitates a teacher model. We define Agent Distillation as the persistent transfer of task-solving knowledge from a teacher agent to a student agent. Our study organizes the field by where transferred knowledge is retained: within the model, as artifacts, through the execution harness, or across substrates. This perspective separates transfer evidence from its outcome and clarifies how knowledge moves between agent components. We develop an evaluation framework that relates retention to causal contribution and deployed utility. Together, these contributions establish a foundation for the reliable, maintainable, and safe development of increasingly complex agentic systems.

</details>


### [106] [RankBuffer: Efficient Ranking-Based Rewards for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36652)
*Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Qi Liu,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Liyi Chen,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankBuffer builds a reusable, query-specific judged-response buffer to convert coarse+local comparisons into efficient rank rewards, achieving high performance with much lower judging cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pointwise rewards are hard to calibrate for open-ended generation; direct ranking of rollouts gives better relative signals but full ranking is costly. A reusable, local ranking approach can lower judging costs.

Method: Introduce RankBuffer: maintain a per-query ordered buffer of judged responses; new rollouts are placed into an anchor interval via a coarse judgment; only rollouts within the same interval are locally fine-ranked; complete order converted to bounded rank rewards; buffer updated by boundary expansion, local refinement, and inactive-anchor pruning.

Result: Across four open-ended benchmarks, RankBuffer outperforms pointwise baselines and nearly matches the best ranking-based method while substantially reducing judging cost. Ablations show local fine ranking and anchor content matter; buffers extend and refine quality coverage over time.

Conclusion: Reusing previously judged responses as query-specific ordered anchors enables efficient construction of reliable relative rewards for open-ended generation, reducing human judging cost while maintaining strong learning performance.

Abstract: Open-ended generation lacks canonical answers, making pointwise rewards difficult to calibrate for group-based reinforcement learning. Directly ranking same-query rollouts provides a more suitable relative reward signal, but existing ranking-based reward methods can incur substantial judging cost. We introduce RankBuffer, which maintains an ordered, query-specific buffer of previously judged responses as a reusable quality scale. Each rollout is first inserted into an anchor interval through an independent coarse judgment, after which only rollouts assigned to the same interval undergo local fine ranking. The resulting complete order is converted into bounded rank rewards, while boundary expansion, local refinement, and inactive-anchor pruning adapt the buffer as the policy evolves. Across four open-ended benchmarks, RankBuffer consistently outperforms all pointwise baselines. It also achieves nearly on-par performance with the strongest ranking-based reward baseline while substantially reducing judging cost. Ablations demonstrate the importance of both local fine ranking and anchor response content, while buffer analyses show that rollout-derived anchors progressively extend and refine the covered quality scale. These results establish response reuse as an effective approach to efficient relative reward construction.

</details>


### [107] [FineSID: Scalable and Efficient Semantic Identifier Learning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36670)
*Song-Li Wu,Weinan Gan,Zhaocheng Du,Xianquan Wang,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对整个码本做软、可微的梯度分配，FineSID 有效解决了 Top-1 带来的梯度稀疏与 SID 冲突问题，从而提升码本利用率与生成式推荐的准确性，且不依赖复杂初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SID 学习依赖 Top-1 硬分配导致梯度稀疏：频繁被选的码字获得大量梯度，绝大多数码字得不到充分训练，造成严重的 SID 冲突；现有启发式手段（聚类初始化、冲突后处理）会破坏端到端语义对齐且不能根本解决梯度稀疏问题。

Method: 将标准的 Top-1 向量量化替换为对全部码字进行软分配的可微机制——不是只更新被选中的单个码字，而是以连续、可导的方式将梯度分配到整个码本，从而实现全局平衡的码本优化并严格保持语义一致性。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，FineSID 对初始化配置鲁棒，能显著提升码本覆盖率和推荐性能，稳定大型高维码本的训练过程。

Conclusion: FineSID 提出了一种去除 Top-1 硬分配限制的统一量化框架，通过软性、可微的梯度分配使整个码本都能接收学习信号，从而缓解语义标识（SID）冲突、稳定训练并提升码本利用率与推荐准确性，且对初始化不敏感。

Abstract: A critical prerequisite of generative recommendation is designing semantic identifiers (SIDs) that are both scalable to large item sets and efficiently learnable. Existing SID learning methods fundamentally rely on Top-1 hard assignment during vector quantization. While heuristic strategies -- such as clustering-based initialization or forced post-hoc collision resolution -- can artificially inflate codebook coverage, they often disrupt end-to-end semantic alignment and fail to address the underlying optimization bottleneck: sparse gradient propagation. In standard Top-1 assignment, gradients concentrate on a narrow subset of frequently selected codewords, leaving the majority inherently under-trained and causing severe SID collisions. To overcome this limitation natively without relying on complex initialization priors, we propose FineSID, a unified quantization framework that moves beyond Top-1 assignment by enabling fine-grained gradient propagation across the entire codebook. Instead of updating only a single selected codeword, FineSID distributes learning signals to all codewords in a soft, differentiable manner. This design promotes globally balanced codebook optimization while strictly preserving semantic consistency, effectively alleviating SID collisions and stabilizing training in large, high-dimensional codebooks. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that FineSID is robust to initialization configurations and consistently improves both codebook utilization and recommendation accuracy. Our work provides a principled, initialization-agnostic solution for semantic identifier learning, advancing the practicality of generative recommendation.

</details>


### [108] [FairDiff: Mitigating the Self-Reinforcing Matthew Effect in Diffusion Recommender Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36671)
*Song-Li Wu,Xianquan Wang,Zhaocheng Du,Weinan Gan,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRMs structurally amplify popularity bias during generation; FairDiff (PCG + SC) corrects this at inference time and via one-step calibration, improving fairness and retaining SOTA performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address a novel and underexplored problem: DRMs not only reflect but exacerbate the Matthew Effect and filter bubbles, causing severe under-generation of niche items; need a plug-and-play method that corrects generative bias without retraining.

Method: Diagnose two compounding mechanisms—(1) popularity-dominated optimization loss and (2) prior mismatch between data terminal distribution and standard Gaussian that leads reverse diffusion to collapse toward popular items. Propose FairDiff with two components: Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG), an inference-time reweighting of the score gradient to penalize high-popularity regions, and a Semantic Calibration (SC) module that aligns forward and reverse distributions via one-step optimal transport.

Result: Comprehensive experiments report state-of-the-art recommendation quality while significantly reducing popularity amplification and improving niche-item generation, demonstrating practicality and generality of FairDiff as a fairness-aware plug-in for DRMs.

Conclusion: Diffusion recommender models (DRMs) do more than inherit popularity bias; they amplify it via self-reinforcing generative dynamics. Introducing FairDiff, the paper shows one can mitigate this amplification and recover niche-item generation while maintaining strong recommendation quality.

Abstract: While the "Matthew Effect" and filter bubbles are widely recognized outcome-level biases in recommender systems, we reveal that Diffusion Recommender Models (DRMs) uniquely compound this issue through their generative dynamics. Rather than merely inheriting data imbalances, DRMs trigger a self-reinforcing amplification of popularity bias. We identify that this phenomenon is driven by two compounding mechanisms. First, while optimization loss is universally dominated by high-frequency items across recommenders, DRMs suffer from a unique structural prior mismatch during generation. Because the forward terminal distribution of long-tailed data deviates significantly from the standard Gaussian prior, reverse sampling trajectories inherently collapse toward high-density popular items, fundamentally suppressing niche item generation. To dismantle this self-reinforcing loop, we propose FairDiff, a plug-and-play fairness-aware diffusion framework. To overcome the popularity-dominated loss, we introduce Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG). Rather than altering the training objective, PCG acts as an inference-time distributional reweighting mechanism, mathematically reshaping the score-based gradient field to penalize high-popularity regions and guide trajectories toward niche semantics. Furthermore, we design a Semantic Calibration (SC) Module to bridge the prior mismatch, aligning the forward and reverse distributions via one-step optimal transport. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that FairDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance while effectively mitigating the self-reinforcing Matthew Effect, highlighting its value as a general framework for DRMs.

</details>


### [109] [MLToolBench: Learning Tool-Augmented Agents for Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36679)
*Xin Yu,Lizhu Zhang,Jiamu Bai,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang,Serena Li,Weiwei Li,Lingzhou Xue,Xiangjun Fan,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolMLBench + SPICE-enabled SFT+RL teaches agents to use diagnostic tools effectively, substantially improving success rates on synthetic ML experimentation tasks and providing some generalization to held-out tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML experimentation is costly in time and compute; synthetic environments and tool interfaces can lower cost but agents need to learn when and how to use diagnostics, which is not solved by providing tools alone.

Method: Introduce ToolMLBench (executable diagnostic tools) and train agents with supervised fine-tuning followed by RL; propose SPICE, a reward that measures how privileged context changes the likelihood of a sampled tool action and uses that delta as a turn-level intrinsic reward in addition to final outcome.

Result: On 80 training tasks and evaluations on 25 in-domain + 10 out-of-domain tasks, training with ToolMLBench and SPICE raised in-domain success from 24.8% to 52.4% for Qwen3-8B and from 35.6% to 69.2% for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B; the latter model also improved out-of-domain from 31% to 48%.

Conclusion: Training agents to use diagnostic tools via a combined SFT+RL pipeline (with a turn-level reward SPICE) significantly improves ML experiment success in synthetic benchmarks, enabling better in-domain and some out-of-domain generalization compared to unadapted models.

Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) agents have made substantial progress, but learning through ML experimentation remains costly in time and computation. Synthetic environments reduce these costs while introducing variations in data and experimental settings that require task-specific diagnosis. Access to diagnostic tools alone does not ensure that agents learn when to use them or how to act on their findings. We introduce ToolMLBench, a suite of executable tools for data inspection, code verification, and experiment diagnosis, together with an SFT and RL pipeline for learning their use. Diagnostic calls acquire evidence whose value depends on subsequent decisions, so final outcomes provide limited guidance on which calls to reinforce. We address this challenge with SPICE, which measures how privileged context changes the likelihood of a sampled tool action and uses this difference as a turn-level reward alongside the final outcome. We train on 80 synthetic tasks and evaluate on 25 in-domain and 10 out-of-domain tasks. Providing tool interfaces and descriptions alone yields inconsistent gains across unadapted models. With the same diagnostic interface, our training pipeline raises in-domain success from 24.8% to 52.4% for Qwen3-8B and from 35.6% to 69.2% for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. The latter also improves from 31% to 48% out-of-domain, supporting learned diagnostic tool use on held-out sources and targets.

</details>


### [110] [JudgeProfile: Understanding and Steering Subjectivity in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36705)
*Qi Cao,Kangning Liu,Xuan Kan,Shunwen Tan,Yang Pei,Dake Chen,Yatai Ji,Zixuan Ye,Yuanpeng Tu,Daniel Li,Junbiao Tang,Pengtao Xie,Zihao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LLM评判的主观性分解为感知与优先级，构建大规模对比数据集并通过估计与学习属性权重证明重加权能有效调整评判器以提升与参考标准的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为评判者在成对比较中常有主观差异，且很多情况并不存在客观“正确”答案。需要系统化地理解主观性来源并提供可控手段以对齐评判结果。

Method: 提出JudgeProfile框架，将评估拆解为感知与优先级两部分；构建SubjectiveSet（50,013对响应，来自17个公开来源，21位LLM评判器，87个属性）；基于属性判断估计每个评判器的权重；用参考标签学习新的权重并重加权以适配目标标准；与微调和rubric prompting比较并在held-out数据上评估一致性。

Result: 发现感知存在隐藏共识——评判器在属性层面常常一致，即便总体选择不同；同一属性判断下不同评判器的优先级（权重）显著差异；通过重加权感知属性，平均对参考标签的held-out一致率由66.48%提升到71.97%，优于微调与rubric prompting。

Conclusion: LLM评判器的主观性来源于两个独立维度：感知（对属性的比较判断）和优先级（属性在最终选择中的权重）。通过分离这两者，可以揭示评判者间的共同感知并以重加权方式有效调整其决策，从而更好地对齐目标评价标准。

Abstract: LLM judges are inherently subjective, often favoring different responses in pairwise comparison when neither option is objectively wrong. To study this subjectivity, we introduce JudgeProfile, a framework that dissects LLM evaluation into perception (how a judge compares two responses across specific attributes like clarity, correctness, and detail) and prioritization (how much each attribute influences the final choice). We curate SubjectiveSet, a dataset of 50,013 response pairs from 17 public data sources, evaluated by 21 LLM judges across 87 attributes. We find a hidden consensus in perception: judges frequently agree on attribute judgments even when their overall choices diverge. Building on this separation, we first characterize each judge's prioritization using attribute weights estimated from its own overall choices. These weights differ across judges even when estimated from the same attribute judgments. We then learn new weights from reference labels to adapt their decisions to a target evaluation standard. Reweighting perceived attributes improves average held-out agreement with reference labels from 66.48% to 71.97%, outperforming fine-tuning and rubric prompting. Our findings show that understanding and steering the subjectivity of LLM judges requires attention not only to what they perceive, but also to how they prioritize it.

</details>


### [111] [Can AI Scientists Change Their Minds? Prior-Evidence Conflict in Synthetic Universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36726)
*Kargi Chauhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A benchmark shows that predictive success does not imply correct mechanism identification; distinct tests are required for predictive adequacy vs mechanism recovery.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test whether a scientific agent can distinguish laws it truly inferred (mechanism recovery) from laws it merely recognizes via surface predictive fit.

Method: Introduce a controlled benchmark (Synthetic Universes) pairing canonical worlds with nearby noncanonical twins; evaluate reported laws twice—predictive rollout on held-out continuations/transfer and independent check for recovery of the generating mechanism; pre-specified 60-cell study with 22 graded trials.

Result: In 20 twin trials: 8 pass predictive verification, 5 recover the generator. Dissociation is bidirectional: 6 outputs predict well but miss mechanism; 3 recover mechanism but fail predictive rollout. Examples: Drag shows 5/5 predictive pass but 1/5 mechanism recovery; Gravity shows 1/5 predictive pass but 4/5 mechanism recovery. No conclusive causal prior-conflict effect due to incomplete controls and sweeps.

Conclusion: Predictive adequacy and mechanism recovery are distinct scientific claims; current results do not support a confirmatory causal prior-conflict effect.

Abstract: Can a scientific agent distinguish a law it inferred from evidence from one it merely recognizes? We introduce Synthetic Universes, a controlled benchmark that pairs canonical famous worlds with matched twisted twins governed by nearby noncanonical mechanisms. We evaluate each reported law twice: by executing it on held-out continuations and transfer settings, and by independently checking whether it recovers the generating mechanism. In the current checkpoint of a pre-specified 60-cell study, 22 trials were graded and one additional run ended in infrastructure failure. Among 20 twin trials, 8 pass predictive verification while 5 recover the generator. The dissociation is bidirectional: six parsable outputs predict successfully while missing the mechanism, whereas three recover the mechanism but fail predictive rollout. Drag exhibits the first pattern (5/5 predictive pass, 1/5 mechanism recovery); Gravity exhibits the second (1/5 predictive pass, 4/5 mechanism recovery). Because matched famous controls, the corrected identifiability sweep, and the Evidence Ladder remain incomplete, we do not claim a confirmatory causal prior-conflict effect. Instead, the completed runs establish a narrower verification result: predictive adequacy and mechanism recovery are distinct scientific claims and require distinct tests.

</details>


### [112] [BiFE: Search-Efficient Discovery of CPU-Only Branching Policies via LLM-based Bi-Fidelity Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36735)
*Ce Zhang,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu,Hao Chen,Xinyue Lu,Shanwei Fan,Yingxuan Teng,Guoliang Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种双保真进化框架，用低保真仿真快速预筛并对精英进行高保真评估，从LLM生成的代码空间中发现了在CPU上表现优越的轻量级分支规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分支变量选择对混合整数线性规划的B&B效率至关重要。现有神经网络策略通常需GPU推理，而符号化、CPU友好的表达能力有限；LLM生成的代码提供了丰富的可执行规则候选，但逐一在真实实例上完整评估代价极高，且离线模仿学习易受分布偏移影响。

Method: 以LLM生成可执行分支规则代码作为搜索空间，采用进化算法产生候选规则；利用低保真仿真（imitation）评分做快速筛选，仅对表现优异的精英个体进行高保真（完整B&B实例）评估，从而节省评估成本并引导搜索向高性能规则收敛。

Result: 实验验证了BiFE在搜索效率上的优势以及其发现规则的竞争力：所学规则在CPU环境下优于SCIP与其他基线，并超过部分需GPU推理的神经策略，表明BiFE能高效地在可执行代码空间中找到实际可用的分支规则。

Conclusion: BiFE通过在进化搜索中结合低保真仿真评分的快速预筛选与对精英候选的高保真实例上完整分支定界评估，有效权衡了搜索开销与性能可靠性，从而发现出基于LLM生成代码的轻量级分支规则，这些规则在CPU上超越了SCIP及其他基准，甚至优于部分基于GPU的神经策略。

Abstract: In branch-and-bound (B&B) for mixed-integer linear programming (MILP), branching variable selection critically impacts efficiency. Existing neural branching policies often require GPU inference, while CPU-efficient symbolic expressions lack the representational capacity for complex logic. Large Language Model (LLM)-generated code provides a flexible search space for designing lightweight branching rules with diverse algorithmic logic. To discover effective rules within LLM-based evolutionary frameworks, a core challenge arises: full B&B evaluation on real instances is prohibitively expensive, whereas offline imitation learning suffers from distribution shift. To address this, we introduce a Bi-Fidelity Evolutionary framework (BiFE). It employs low-fidelity imitation scores as a rapid pre-screener and selectively applies high-fidelity on-instance evaluation only to elite candidates, effectively balancing search efficiency with performance reliability. Experiments validate both the search efficiency of BiFE and the competitiveness of its discovered rules, which outperform the SCIP solver and other baselines on CPUs, and even surpass certain GPU-based neural policies.

</details>


### [113] [Distinguish or Homogenize: Last-Chance Policy Identification and Risk-Budgeted Recovery under Irreversible Resource Depletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36741)
*Yibo Guo,Xiaodan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LCPI范式与LIM边界，给出确定性Exact-LIM和有噪声下的RBCP算法；实验证明通过使模型共享可接受策略可以在严格失败预算下改善恢复效果，收益来自改变可接受策略集合而非额外资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源不可逆耗尽（例如修复预算、时间、物理资源）情形下，传统诊断和规划方法未把“通过行动改变哪些策略被视为对某些模型可接受”显式化。智能体需要在耗尽前决定是继续辨别模型还是通过改变系统状态使多模型共享可接受策略，从而终止进一步诊断。

Method: 形式化Last-Chance Policy Identification (LCPI)，将正确性评估从初始状态转为到达状态；引入Last Identifiable Margin (LIM)作为区分与同质化可行性的边界；对确定性诊断图给出Exact-LIM递归；对有噪声的有限视界恢复问题提出Risk-Budgeted Compatibility Planning (RBCP)，在硬性最坏情况失败预算约束下搜索兼容性感知的前沿策略。

Result: 在抽象微服务拓扑的事故恢复和MiniGrid的潜在损伤导航实验中，RBCP在满足最坏情况失败预算的前提下提升了风险可行的恢复率。对照实验使用成本匹配但不改变模型不兼容性的“伪控制”消除了增益，验证了收益来自于改变模型—策略的可接受性而非额外搜索或预算。

Conclusion: LCPI提出了一个将诊断与控制结合的新范式：在资源不可逆耗尽的设置中，智能体可通过区分潜在故障模型（distinguish）或改变系统状态以使剩余模型拥有共同可接受继续策略（homogenize），从而在到达某状态时确保正确性。该范式补充并超越了以往把“从模型到可接受策略的映射”视为既定事实的工作。

Abstract: Under irreversible resource depletion, an agent can spend resources to distinguish among latent fault models, or to change the system state so that the remaining models admit a common acceptable continuation--at which point further diagnosis becomes unnecessary. This distinguish-or-homogenize principle identifies a path that existing frameworks for identification, planning, and diagnosis do not make explicit: prior formulations treat the mapping from fault models to acceptable policies as a given, whereas LCPI makes it a function of the agent's own actions. We formalize this principle through Last-Chance Policy Identification (LCPI), where correctness is evaluated at the state the agent reaches rather than at the initial state. The Last Identifiable Margin (LIM) marks the feasibility boundary between distinguishing and homogenizing. For deterministic diagnostic graphs we provide the Exact-LIM recursion; for noisy finite-horizon recovery we propose Risk-Budgeted Compatibility Planning (RBCP), which searches a compatibility-aware frontier under a hard worst-case failure constraint. Across incident recovery on abstract microservice topologies and latent-damage navigation in MiniGrid, RBCP improves risk-feasible recovery while satisfying the failure budget. A sham control--cost-matched actions that preserve model incompatibility--eliminates the gain entirely, confirming that the benefit comes from changing which policies are acceptable for which models, not from extra search or additional budget.

</details>


### [114] [SIPO: Unifying Reinforcement Learning with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36742)
*Zhenrui Yue,Huimin Zeng,Yueqi Wang,Yaokun Liu,Fengran Mo,Jinghan Zhang,Mung Yao Jia,Gyuseok Lee,Yang Zhang,Na Wei,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对比式自教师的SIPO：用组内错误构造双教师上下文，取两者log-prob差作为逐标记优势，实现稠密的token-level信用分配并保留任务回报方向，在推理与代码生成任务上优于RLVR/OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR提供全局序列级奖励但缺乏逐标记归因，OPSD通过特权上下文自蒸馏给出密集信号但易受自教师过度自信与对长推理轨迹的过度惩罚影响。需要一种既能保持任务回报直接优化方向又能提供可靠逐标记信用分配的方法。

Method: SIPO在每次迭代中对同一提示从当前策略采样多条rollout，按环境回报对它们打分；对每条rollout构造两种教师上下文（将参考答案与组内其他rollout的错误配对），让模型在这两个上下文下重新评估自身输出，利用两者教师log-prob之差作为逐标记反馈。该差值在期望上可抵消两上下文共享的偏差，使得每个token获得基于奖励方向的优势（advantage）重分配。

Result: SIPO在多项推理和代码生成基准上，相较于仅有序列回报的RLVR和带单一自教师的OPSD，展现出更好的性能；不依赖外部教师或额外生成；在组内所有rollout均失败时仍能提供学习信号。

Conclusion: SIPO在保留任务回报直接优化的同时，通过对比式自教师提供密集的逐标记（token-level）反馈，有效缓解了传统RLVR稀疏奖励与OPSD自教师过度惩罚长轨迹的问题，从而在推理和代码生成基准上优于RLVR和OPSD基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for improving large language models (LLMs) on various tasks, yet its sparse outcome rewards lack token-level credit assignment for intermediate steps. To address this, on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) leverages a self-teacher with privileged context to provide additional dense learning signals. However, because the self-teacher is often overconfident and imposes excessive penalties on long reasoning trajectories, OPSD frequently struggles in practice. To mitigate this, we propose self-instructing policy optimization (SIPO) with a contrastive self-teacher to provide dense credit. At each iteration, SIPO samples multiple rollouts per prompt from the current policy, scores them with environment rewards, and constructs two teacher contexts for each rollout by pairing the reference answer with mistakes made within the group. The model then re-evaluates its own responses under both contexts, using the difference between the two teacher log-probabilities as token-level feedback, so that biases shared by both contexts are expected to largely cancel. The resulting objective yields a token-level advantage for every rollout: the reward still sets the main direction of each update while the self-teacher redistributes credit across tokens. Even in groups where every rollout fails and group-relative advantages vanish, SIPO still provides a learning signal. By preserving direct optimization of the task reward while providing dense, token-level feedback, this approach bridges reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation. Extensive experiments across multiple reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that SIPO outperforms both RLVR and OPSD baselines without an external teacher or additional generation.

</details>


### [115] [EASE: Behavior-Adaptive Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36746)
*Zhen Xiong,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EASE trains a single curator that adapts its curation decisions to observed executor behavior via an online behavioral profile and RL, achieving better cross-executor generalization and efficiency compared to per-executor or behavior-agnostic curators.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learned skill curators optimize for curation independent of executor behavior, causing curators to specialize to their training executors and perform poorly when paired with different executors. The goal is to build a curator that adapts to varied executor behaviors so that a single curator can serve many executors robustly without per-executor finetuning.

Method: Introduce EASE: maintain an online behavioral profile of recent executor behaviors; condition a single curator on this profile, current trajectory, and retrieved skills; use reinforcement learning to jointly train the curator across multiple frozen executors; apply retrieval-aware and behavior-aware temporal attribution to assign credit to curation actions with observable downstream effects; support add/modify/remove operations on a skill repository.

Result: EASE outperforms strong skill- and memory-based baselines across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, with executors including Qwen3-8B/32B, GPT-OSS-120B and unseen models (Kimi K2.6, DeepSeek V4 Flash, Gemini 3.5 Flash). Quantitative gains: 34.5–41.0% fewer skills maintained, 36.3–38.7% improved skill retrieval, 51.8–60.0% higher edit utility, and 9.1–14.5% reduction in deployment-time inference tokens.

Conclusion: Behavior-adaptive skill curation (EASE) effectively addresses executor-dependent degradation in learned skill curators by conditioning a shared curator on an online behavioral profile; it generalizes across diverse frozen and unseen executors and yields substantial gains in skill compactness, retrieval accuracy, edit utility, and inference efficiency without per-executor finetuning.

Abstract: Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for self-evolving agents to accumulate reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, existing learned skill curators typically optimize curation without explicitly modeling downstream executor behavior. We show that this can cause systematic cross-executor degradation: curators trained with different executors perform best when paired with their own training executor, indicating that effective skill curation is executor-dependent. We formulate behavior-adaptive skill curation and introduce EASE, a framework that learns a single curator that adapts its decisions to different executor behaviors. EASE maintains an online behavioral profile of recent execution patterns and conditions the curator on this profile, the current trajectory, and retrieved skills to add, modify, or remove skills from an evolving repository. We train the shared curator jointly across multiple frozen executors with reinforcement learning, using retrieval-aware and behavior-aware temporal attribution to focus optimization on curation actions with observable downstream influence. Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and WebShop, with executors ranging from Qwen3-8B/32B and GPT-OSS-120B to unseen Kimi K2.6, DeepSeek V4 Flash, and Gemini 3.5 Flash, EASE outperforms strong skill- and memory-based baselines without per-executor finetuning. EASE also maintains 34.5--41.0% fewer skills, improves skill retrieval by 36.3--38.7% and measured edit utility by 51.8--60.0%, and reduces deployment-time inference tokens by 9.1--14.5%. These results establish behavior-adaptive skill curation as an effective principle for building self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [116] [Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing via Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36748)
*Dahyun Jung,Suhyune Son,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIME: lifelong knowledge editing combining preference optimization, replay, and gradient constraints to improve generalization and retain prior edits.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing knowledge editing methods are designed for one-off edits and often overfit to specific prompts in lifelong scenarios, which harms generalization and overall model performance. There is a need for methods that enable continual updates without degrading the model.

Method: GLIME integrates preference optimization over generated outputs with targeted editing. It uses replay-based editing and applies a gradient constraint during updates to maintain previously edited knowledge.

Result: Experiments show GLIME substantially improves knowledge generalization in lifelong editing settings, retains editing accuracy, and preserves general model capabilities compared to baselines.

Conclusion: GLIME provides a practical lifelong editing framework that improves generalization of edited knowledge while preserving prior edits and model capabilities.

Abstract: Knowledge editing enables rapid updates of specific factual knowledge in large language models (LLMs) without full retraining. However, more realistic scenarios call for a lifelong framework that handles continual updates rather than one-off modifications. In such settings, existing editing methods often overfit to target prompts, significantly degrading both the generalization of the edited knowledge and the model's general capabilities. To address this issue, we propose GLIME (Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing), which combines knowledge editing with preference optimization over generation behavior. GLIME further incorporates replay-based editing and a gradient constraint to preserve previously edited knowledge. Experimental results show that GLIME significantly improves knowledge generalization in lifelong editing settings while maintaining both editing performance and general capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [Emergent Specialization in Populations of Self-Supervised Collaborative Vision Experts Without a Shared Gate or Cross-Agent Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36770)
*Aram Davtyan,Pablo Acuaviva,Sebastian Stapf,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在完全去中心化、无梯度共享的条件下，通过本地路由与无梯度通信，神经网络群体可自发形成有用的分工，显著改善预测任务性能，DISCO为实现这种协作提供了可行机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在无中心门控与无跨代理梯度信息的严格去中心化条件下，是否以及如何出现有用的专家/专长分配，这对分布式预测预训练和大规模自治系统设计有重要意义。

Method: 作者构造了多个初始相同但独立训练的代理，使用冻结的DINOv3特征进行掩码预测，在包含六个视觉域的异构无标签数据上微调。代理可通过前向传递请求他人帮助。研究逐步移除中心控制，最终提出DISCO机制：每个代理本地选择一个帮手，通过无梯度通道读取其内部状态，并仅将路由器的奖励与“帮助带来的改进”挂钩。通过衡量“最佳代理是否与潜在域对齐（专业化）”和“责任是否在代理间分布（利用率）”来评估分工。

Result: （1）专业化确实自发出现并有助于性能；（2）随机路由的群体不如单个通用模型，但语义路由的群体优于通用体，表明性能提升来源于专业化而非仅仅是群体规模；（3）即使没有中心路由器，专业化依然存在；（4）无梯度通信允许非专家利用出现的专家，DISCO下被专家帮助的随机代理可达到或超过单体通用模型，专家在许多情况下更优，包括超出训练混合的数据；（5）本地路由器在98%输入上选择了出现的专家；（6）这些发现对群体规模、模型容量、数据不均衡和微调随机种子具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 在没有共享门控或跨代理梯度的情况下，神经网络群体仍能自发形成有用的分工，且这种分工可被用于提高预测预训练的效果。

Abstract: Can a population of neural networks develop a useful division of labor without a shared gate or gradients between agents? We study a setting where each network has its own weights, trains independently on the same heterogeneous data, and can ask another agent for help through a forward pass. Unlike mixtures of experts, where a jointly trained gate assigns inputs to experts, specialization here must emerge without central control. We test this in a small scale proxy for predictive visual pretraining. Initially identical agents are finetuned on an unlabeled mixture of six visual domains using masked prediction of frozen DINOv3 features. We measure specialization by asking whether the best agent for an input aligns with its latent domain, and utilization by asking whether responsibility is distributed across agents. We progressively remove central control, ending with DISCO (DIStributed COllaboration) where each agent locally selects a helper, reads its internal state through a gradient free channel, and rewards its router only for the improvement that help provides. Specialization emerges and is useful. Randomly routed populations underperform a single generalist, while semantically routed populations outperform it, showing that specialization rather than population size drives the gain. Specialization persists without a central router, and gradient free communication lets nonexperts exploit emergent expertise. In DISCO, a random agent helped by the expert matches the solo generalist, while experts surpass it, including on data outside the specialization mixture. Local routers select the emergent expert for 98% of inputs. These effects persist across population size, model capacity, data imbalance, and finetuning seeds, providing measurable evidence for the dynamics needed by decentralized predictive pretraining.

</details>


### [118] [Code4Scene: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Constructing and Editing 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36777)
*Xiaokang Ye,Siddhant Hitesh Mantri,Zimeng Chen,Edward Zhang,Zhaoxu Zheng,Yuanheng Li,Yizhao Chen,Tianyang Huang,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Code4Scene evaluates coding agents on engine-native 3D scene generation/editing and reveals that while agents can produce plausible renders, they frequently fail to ensure correct spatial relations and precise scene state; top agents vary by task and overall reliability remains limited.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rendered images can hide incorrect spatial relations, intersections, or unintended edits; current LLM-driven code agents might not truly understand 3D structure or control scene state reliably. A benchmark that evaluates the actual engine-native scene (not just rendered output) is needed to quantify these gaps.

Method: Introduce Code4Scene: a benchmark of 190 Unreal Engine cases (95 public) derived from human-assembled scenes, with a unified execution interface. Two task families: Construction (open-ended language specifications allowing multiple valid realizations) and Editing (precisely recover a target scene from reference images while preserving other content). Evaluation inspects engine-native scene artifacts for task fulfillment, artifact integrity, and static physical validity; edits additionally compared to withheld ground truth. Benchmarked 14 coding-agent configurations and report per-category performance and correlations.

Result: Across agents, construction and editing performance are strongly correlated but distinct (Spearman ρ=0.78). Different agents lead in different tasks (Claude Fable 5.1 for construction, Gemini 3.8 Flash for editing, GPT-6 Astra overall). Spatial Composition is the weakest construction category. Editing is imprecise: best Repair F1=0.527; 35.8% of fully recovered edits still introduce unintended changes elsewhere. These results indicate meaningful limitations in current coding agents' spatial reasoning and state-control capabilities.

Conclusion: The paper concludes that current code-generating agents produce visually plausible 3D scenes but often lack reliable spatial reasoning and precise state control. Despite some agents performing well in either construction or editing, none achieve robust, artifact-free edits; significant unintended modifications and low Repair F1 indicate a gap between rendered plausibility and correct engine-native scene state.

Abstract: Frontier coding agents can now write and execute code that authors 3D environments, but whether they reliably understand 3D structure and precisely control scene state remains unclear. The generated 3D scene is a persistent, executable artifact: a convincing render can hide incorrect spatial relations, intersecting objects, or unintended modifications. We introduce Code4Scene, a benchmark of 190 Unreal Engine cases built from human-assembled scenes that evaluates coding agents on two complementary settings under a shared execution interface. Construction tests scene-level spatial reasoning from open-ended language specifications, where many realizations are valid; editing tests precise control of scene state, where the agent must recover the target scene from reference images while preserving everything else. Rather than scoring code or rendered views, Code4Scene evaluates the generated engine-native scene for task fulfillment, artifact integrity, and static physical validity, with edits additionally compared against withheld ground truth. Across 14 coding-agent configurations on the 95-case public set, construction and editing performance are strongly correlated but not interchangeable (Spearman $ρ= 0.78$): Claude Fable 5.1 leads construction, Gemini 3.8 Flash leads editing, and GPT-6 Astra narrowly leads overall. Spatial Composition is the weakest construction category for every agent, while editing remains imprecise: the best Repair F1 is only 0.527, and 35.8% of edits that fully recover the target still introduce unintended changes elsewhere in the scene. These results expose a gap between plausible 3D generation and reliable spatial reasoning and state control.

</details>


### [119] [Aperture: Merge-Consistent Rotary States for Compressed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36781)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Aperture：以傅里叶矩保留被合并 tokens 的加权位置信息，理论上达成最小维度与可加更新，数值与任务实验表明其可行但在下游任务上未必优于已有合并策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 transformer 的 token 压缩（merging）中，旋转位置编码（rotary position embeddings）通常给合并后 token 分配单一位置坐标，导致位置信息丢失。问题是：在后续合并与交互中需要保留哪些位置信息，如何用最小维度的连续表征保存这些信息以支持期望的旋转交互。

Method: 提出 Aperture 表征：对每个被压缩的 token 集合计算其加权支撑的傅里叶矩并在旋转频率处存储；证明这些矩在维度上最小且能支持所选期望旋转交互；将表征解释为“表示质量”从而使更新为加法形式；将注意力归一化视为独立的读出选择；解析均匀区间导致的中心旋转乘以 sinc 增益，并构造例子说明中心不能总是确定区间宽度。进行了数值实现验证与在时间序列读者和原生视频问答任务中的比较。

Result: 理论上给出最小实维表征及其性质；数值实现验证了加权支撑的实现方法；在训练的时间读者里，压缩迁移性能随增益校准和特征放置变化；在一个预先设定的视频问答对比中，存储支撑的办法达到 65.63% 准确率，对比已部署合并规则的 67.12%，说明在某些任务上精确位置保存与下游效用可分离。

Conclusion: Aperture 提供了一种在压缩多个 token 时保留必要位置关系的紧凑表征，通过存储 token 的加权支撑在模型旋转频率处的傅里叶矩（Fourier moments），在连续状态空间中实现了对期望旋转交互（rotary interactions）的最小实维表示。

Abstract: Token compression combines content from several positions, yet rotary position embeddings usually assign the merged token one coordinate. We ask what positional information must survive later merges. Aperture stores Fourier moments of the token's weighted support at the model's rotary frequencies. We prove that these moments have minimal real dimension among continuous states sufficient for the selected expected rotary interactions. Represented mass makes updates additive; attention normalisation remains a separate readout choice. Uniform intervals give a centre rotation times a sinc gain. We characterise when centres determine interval widths and construct matched examples where they do not. Numerical checks verify the weighted-support implementation. In trained temporal readers, compression transfer varies with gain calibration and feature placement. In a prespecified native video question-answering comparison, stored support reaches $65.63\%$ accuracy versus $67.12\%$ for the deployed merging rule. These results separate exact positional preservation under compression from downstream benefit.

</details>


### [120] [AI as a Compiler: Compiling Triton kernels without the Triton compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36800)
*François Costa,Charly Castes,Thomas Bourgeat,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-driven lowering from Triton to PTX can be competitive or superior to hand-tuned lowering for many GPU kernels, but success depends critically on a strong evaluation and verification infrastructure and careful modeling of modern tensor-core features.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Compiler backends are costly to build and maintain for evolving programming models, workloads and accelerators; the authors ask whether LLMs can replace conventional lowering and optimization pipelines to reduce engineering effort and accelerate support for new hardware.

Method: An LLM-based agent translates Triton kernels into PTX; an evaluation environment executes and measures candidate PTX; verification is performed via an extended PTX verifier (Volta extended to support Blackwell tcgen05 tensor-core primitives, managed tensor memory, descriptor-based layouts, and asynchronous commit/wait semantics).

Result: Across 22 kernels (12 common kernels on Ada/Hopper/Blackwell and 10 from recent ML papers), AI lowering reached 0.83×–3.34× the performance of autotuned Triton. Notable wins include BitDelta (3.34×), FlashAttention (1.37×), and overlapping-convolution reuse (up to 2.23×). The work also develops and extends a formal PTX verifier to handle modern tensor-core interfaces and asynchronous execution semantics.

Conclusion: AI lowering — using LLM agents to translate high-level GPU kernels (Triton) directly into PTX — can match or exceed autotuned conventional pipelines on many workloads, yielding up to 3.34× speedups for certain kernels, while requiring a robust verification/evaluation harness and substantial extensions to PTX verification for modern tensor-core features.

Abstract: Compiler backends are expensive to build and maintain as programming models, workloads, and accelerators evolve. We investigate whether large language models can replace the conventional optimizing and lowering pipeline, a process that we call AI lowering. We study AI lowering from Triton to NVIDIA PTX: an LLM agent translates Triton kernels directly into PTX. We build an environment that evaluates candidate PTX, and an agentic harness in which an LLM translates Triton kernels into PTX. Across twelve common kernels on Ada, Hopper, and Blackwell GPUs and ten kernels from recent ML papers, AI lowering achieves 0.83x-3.34x the performance of autotuned Triton. The largest gains come from transformations that Triton's lowering pipeline does not perform, such as decoding packed binary weights directly into Tensor Core operands (3.34x on BitDelta), assigning each thread a complete softmax row in tensor memory (1.37x on FlashAttention), and reusing overlapping convolution windows (up to 2.23x). These results rely on a robust evaluation harness with comprehensive verification support. We build on Volta, an existing PTX verifier, and substantially extend it to support modern GPU architectures by introducing support for Blackwell's tcgen05 Tensor Core interface.
