This requires modeling three architectural features: managed tensor memory, descriptor-based operand layouts, and asynchronous execution coordinated through commits, waits, memory barriers, and proxy fences. We discuss the challenges involved in formalizing them, as well as the current limitations. Our results suggest an emerging future in which AI compilers replace custom-written intermediate representations and checkers, reducing the time and engineering effort required to bring up software for new general-purpose and custom chips.

</details>


### [121] [UpliftMem: Learning Set-Level Uplift for Agent Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36805)
*Mengkun Liang,Haoran Qiang,Guannan Liu,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UpliftMem learns retrieval by optimizing for execution uplift and uses an EVSI-based probing policy to spend rollout budget where it most improves local retrieval choices, yielding better success with fewer rollouts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Learning which external-memory sets help an LLM agent requires outcome feedback, but evaluating alternatives needs extra rollouts; the goal is to make retrieval-learning efficient under tight rollout budgets.

Method: Train a shared memory-set scorer using uplift labels computed as execution improvement over a frozen executor without memory; use a theoretical analysis to derive an EVSI-based probe selection rule (closed-form under a correlated Gaussian model) to allocate limited rollouts to probe alternative memory sets; at test time the frozen scorer selects sets without probes.

Result: Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and BigCodeBench, UpliftMem achieves highest success rates among baselines on main evaluations. Controlled fixed-store and matched probe-budget tests show it makes better memory-use decisions and uses execution feedback more effectively.

Conclusion: UpliftMem enables more effective memory retrieval for LLM agents by learning from set-level execution uplift versus a no-memory baseline, improving task success while limiting costly rollouts.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents reuse external memory to guide new tasks, but effective retrieval requires learning which memory sets improve execution. Such learning relies on costly outcome feedback: ordinary retrieval observes only executed sets, while evaluating alternatives requires additional rollouts. We introduce \textsc{UpliftMem}, which learns memory retrieval from set-level execution uplift relative to the same executor without memory. A theoretical analysis of how retrieval preferences restrict feedback coverage motivates targeted probing of alternative memory sets. Probe selection follows an expected value of sample information (EVSI) criterion, derived in closed form under a correlated Gaussian model, to allocate limited training rollouts according to their expected improvement in local retrieval decisions. The shared scorer is trained with a frozen executor and selects memory sets without test-time probes. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and BigCodeBench, \textsc{UpliftMem} achieves the best success rates among evaluated baselines on the main evaluation sets. Controlled fixed-store and matched probe budget evaluations further demonstrate improved memory-use decisions and more effective use of execution feedback.

</details>


### [122] [CAD-Native Transformer Operators for AI-Aided Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36806)
*Daniel Leibovici,Nikola Borislavov Kovachki,Dawon Ahn,Ruben Ohana,Ira J. S. Shokar,Abouzar Ghasemi,Semih Akkurt,Rishikesh Ranade,Neil Ashton,Jan Kautz,Jean Kossaifi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将NURBS参数化CAD直接token化并输入Transformer神经算子的方案，无须网格化即可在任意查询点预测连续表面与体域场，实验证明在多项气动基准上精度优异且支持可微逆设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值仿真需要昂贵且脆弱的网格化，且大多数神经代理继承了离散化带来的表征差距。目标是直接在连续参数化CAD表示上构建可微、快速且高精度的物理场替代器，以减少人工介入并加速设计评估与优化。

Method: 建立了从几何流形到物理场函数空间上学习算子的理论框架；将参数化补片（以NURBS表示：控制点、节点向量和权重）进行序列化token化，构建基于Transformer的神经算子；该模型在任意查询位置输出连续的表面与体域场预测。

Result: 在四个汽车与飞机气动基准（AhmedML、WindsorML、DrivAerML、HiLiftAeroML）上，CANTO在大多数表面与体域预测任务上达到或超过现有最佳方法；在HiLiftAeroML的表面压强相对L2误差上比AB-UPT降低19.8%。模型对CAD参数可微，支持基于梯度的逆设计：在AhmedML上找到在相同体积和升力约束下比数据集中最佳设计降低4.4%至20.4%阻力的设计，并用相同CFD设置验证了改进。

Conclusion: CANTO能够直接从连续CAD几何到物理场进行映射，无需对输入几何进行网格化，既提高了预测精度又保留了对CAD参数的可微性，从而支持基于梯度的逆设计优化。

Abstract: Modern engineering systems, from automobiles to aircraft, are designed by using precise, continuous parametric computer-aided design (CAD) models. Evaluating design changes through numerical simulation requires meshing the continuous geometry, a computationally expensive and often brittle process that can require manual intervention and replaces the continuous representation with a discrete approximation. Most neural surrogates accelerate the simulation, but inherit this representation gap by relying on meshes, point clouds, voxels, or other sampled approximations of geometry. We introduce CANTO, a transformer neural operator that maps directly from continuous CAD geometry to physical fields, without meshing the input geometry. We develop a theoretical framework for learning operators from geometric manifolds to function spaces of physical fields, representing geometry through sequences of parametric patches. CANTO instantiates this framework by directly tokenizing non-uniform rational B-spline (NURBS) patches from their control points, knot vectors, and weights, and predicts continuous surface and volume fields at arbitrary query locations. We evaluate CANTO on four automotive and aircraft aerodynamics industry benchmarks: AhmedML, WindsorML, DrivAerML, and HiLiftAeroML. CANTO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on most evaluated surface and volume prediction tasks, including a 19.8% reduction in surface-pressure relative $L_2$ error compared with AB-UPT on HiLiftAeroML. Differentiability with respect to CAD parameters further enables gradient-based inverse design of designs. On AhmedML, CANTO identifies designs with 4.4 to 20.4% lower drag than the best dataset designs satisfying the same volume and lift constraints, with the improvements verified using the same CFD setup used to generate the original dataset.

</details>


### [123] [Geometry-Conditioned Fixed-Scaffold Encoders for Time-Warp Robust Sequence Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36809)
*Cassandra Yang,Yufan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoPatch通过固定分块支架与连续几何调制，解决了几何驱动的token化不稳定问题，在时间伸缩下提升检索稳定性，但在局部匹配与非重叠检索目标间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对长序列集合做基于嵌入的检索时，项可一次编码后用最近邻检索，但实际信号常有非规范时钟（心率、语速等）导致时间伸缩，使得基于几何的patch分界在同一变形下发生改变，破坏模型应有的时间稳健性，因而需要一种在几何变化下保持标记化稳定的方法。

Method: 提出固定支架（fixed-scaffold）patch编码器：保持token支持不随信号几何变化而改变，同时将斜率、曲率、加速度、仿射残差和置信度作为连续的条件变量进行调制；在机理层面给出稳定性分析，分离边界漂移、仿射时间变换、置信度加权的几何扰动和检索边际效应；在ECG、语音和多元时序检索任务上进行实验，支持全局嵌入检索与后期交互评分匹配评测协议。

Result: 在存在时间变形的ECG、语音和多元时序数据集上，GeoPatch提升了early-rank检索性能；同时展示出几何调制允许表示随表面变化而改变但不会引起token支持重排，从而带来局部匹配能力与严格非重叠检索鲁棒性之间的可观察权衡。

Conclusion: GeoPatch通过固定支架的分块编码器将几何诱导的边界变化从离散的标记化转化为连续的特征调制，从而在存在时间拉伸/压缩的情况下保持标记支持（数量、顺序、覆盖）不变，最终提高了在时间变形情形下的检索稳定性并暴露出局部表面匹配与严格非重叠检索之间的权衡。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval is attractive for long sequence collections because each item can be encoded once and searched by nearest-neighbor ranking. The difficulty is that the objects being indexed are often observed under a noncanonical clock: cardiac cycles stretch with rate, speech changes with tempo, and sensor traces reach comparable states at different speeds. This paper studies a specific source of instability in patch-based encoders for this regime. If patch boundaries are chosen from signal geometry, then the tokenization can change under the same temporal deformation that the representation is expected to tolerate. We propose GeoPatch, a fixed-scaffold patch encoder that keeps token support independent of geometry and uses slope, curvature, acceleration, affine-residual, and confidence descriptors only as continuous conditioning variables. The design turns boundary variation into feature modulation: geometry can change the embedding through a controlled pathway, but it cannot change the number, order, or support of local tokens. We formalize this distinction through a mechanism-level stability analysis that separates boundary drift, affine timing variation, confidence-weighted geometry perturbation, and retrieval-margin effects. The same local tokens support global embedding retrieval and late-interaction scoring, so the scoring rule can be matched to the evaluation protocol. Across ECG, speech, and multivariate time-series retrieval tasks, GeoPatch improves early-rank retrieval under timing variation while exposing a clear trade-off between local surface matching and strict non-overlap retrieval.

</details>


### [124] [Calibrate the Decisions That Change the Future: On-Policy Post-Training Quantization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36828)
*Wenxiao Fan,Jingling Fu,Lichen Ma,Yu He,Luohang Liu,Jinbao Xue,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OnPTQ：基于 on-policy 轨迹和短期反事实回滚的量化校准框架，通过 Decision–Consequence 风险优先处理关键状态，理论上证明并在多模态模型上实证提升性能并减少错误翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练量化（PTQ）通常用固定序列和局部目标进行重建，忽视了自回归生成中一个 token 的改变会改变前缀并影响后续状态的“在线反馈”效应。因此需要基于实际被量化策略访问的轨迹进行校准，并关注那些真正改变未来生成的决策。

Method: 在共享前缀处识别因量化而侵蚀的决策边界，对有竞争性的 token 通过短期反事实（counterfactual）回滚评估其分支后果，将当前决策差异与分支后果结合为 Decision–Consequence 风险并以此优先校准；同时采用上下文锚定与轨迹刷新以保持多模态行为一致并与更新的策略对齐。还给出一个将行为偏差与当前策略差异及动作条件的未来价值跨度联系起来的理论上界。

Result: 在多种低位宽设置下，对视觉-语言与全模态 Qwen 系列模型进行实验，OnPTQ 在不修改部署推理图的情况下提升了下游性能，并相较于对应的 Dense/FP16 参考模型产生更少的正确性翻转（correctness flips）。

Conclusion: OnPTQ 是一种在不改变推理图的前提下，通过在当前量化策略所访问的轨迹上进行在线（on-policy）校准，优先修复对后续生成影响较大的决策，降低量化引起的行为偏差并提升下游任务性能的方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) lowers deployment cost for multimodal large language models, but calibration typically reconstructs fixed sequences with local objectives. This overlooks autoregressive feedback: a quantization-induced token change redirects the prefix and changes future states. Yet on-policy coverage alone is insufficient because many decision mismatches barely affect future generation. We propose OnPTQ, an on-policy framework that calibrates on trajectories visited by the current quantized policy. On shared prefixes, OnPTQ identifies quantization-eroded boundaries, evaluates competing tokens through short counterfactual rollouts, and combines current discrepancy with branch consequence into a Decision--Consequence risk. The risk prioritizes critical states, while context anchoring and trajectory refresh preserve multimodal behavior and keep calibration aligned with the updated policy. We further derive a Decision--Consequence bound linking behavioral deviation to current policy discrepancy and action-conditioned future-value span. Across vision--language and omni-modal Qwen models under multiple low-bit settings, OnPTQ improves downstream performance and yields fewer correctness flips against the corresponding Dense/FP16 references, without changing the deployed inference graph.

</details>


### [125] [The Default Trap: Rethinking Plan Evaluation in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36829)
*Xueqi Li,Jingjie Ning,Yibo Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出“默认陷阱”：仅看移除默认计划时的小变化会误判模型对优先级的控制。大量窗口实验表明优先级切换确实能强力重定向行为，但默认效应大受呈现顺序影响，故应同时报告优先级响应、呈现依赖的默认效应与任务成功/成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清和量化语言模型或执行器对指令中优先级变化的真实控制能力，同时揭示仅用“移除默认计划后小变化”这一对比可能掩盖对优先级的强烈敏感性的盲点。

Method: 用三类任务（Retail、Airline、AgentDojo）构造成对计划（强调不同信息目标）并以共享的无计划（no-plan）作为参照；推导一个会计恒等式将两种行为对比联系起来；在不同设置下运行大量决策窗口实验（共计数千窗口），并做账户列表顺序反转、单条优先语句的成分分析及完整任务集的成功率评估。

Result: 优先级切换在模型选择上产生了强烈重定向（效应接近100个百分点）；但计划与默认的对比呈现差异性：反转账户列表顺序时默认目标选择变化显著（63.3–98.3个百分点），而优先级切换效应在任意顺序下保持96.7–100.0个百分点。成分研究显示单条优先语句可产生强控制效果，额外文本的影响随组与方向不同而异。完整任务测量显示相对于无计划的成功率差异分布在-19.4至+8.3点之间：Retail与Airline的成功区间包含零，AgentDojo为四个固定应用世界的结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了“默认陷阱”（default trap）：当移除与默认对齐的整体计划时，执行者对信息选择概率的变化很小，但在切换优先级时会产生强烈响应，这可能导致将前者误判为对优先级不敏感。针对这一风险，作者建议在报告中同时呈现优先级响应性、依赖呈现方式的默认效应以及任务成功率与成本。

Abstract: An executor can respond strongly to a change in a supplied plan's priority while showing a small change in the same information-selection probability when a default-aligned whole plan is removed. We call the risk of interpreting the latter as weak responsiveness to alternative priorities the default trap. We compare paired plans that prioritize different information targets with a shared no-plan reference. An accounting identity relates these distinct behavioral contrasts. Across 3,200 decision windows on 160 selected Retail, Airline, and AgentDojo tasks, switching priorities strongly redirects two models' choices, while the two plan-versus-default contrasts differ. In 2,160 additional windows, reversing account-list order shifts default target selection by 63.3-98.3 percentage points; priority-switching effects remain 96.7-100.0 points in either order. A separate 3,240-window component study finds strong control under single priority sentences, with effects of additional text varying by group and direction. Finally, 1,080 full-task episodes yield observed success differences of -19.4 to +8.3 points relative to no plan. All Retail and Airline success intervals include zero; AgentDojo results describe four fixed application worlds. These findings support joint reporting of priority responsiveness, presentation-dependent defaults, and task success and cost.

</details>


### [126] [Where Does Staleness Accumulate? Pool Aware Effective Staleness Control for Asynchronous RL in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36830)
*Chenliang Li,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei,Alfredo Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE 通过将池内占用转化为自适应拒绝预算并基于前缀感知的有效陈旧性对轨迹排序筛选，在不显著牺牲并行效率的情况下减少异步 RL 的策略滞后，从而提高学习性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全异步 RL 可以重叠 rollout 生成与策略优化以提高资源利用率，但会引入策略滞后（policy lag），随轨迹生命周期累积的陈旧性会损害训练效果。目标是在不放弃异步带来墙钟时间优势的前提下，控制陈旧性对学习的负面影响。

Method: 作者将轨迹陈旧性分解为 Generation Staleness（在生成期间累积）和 Waiting Staleness（生成完成后进入池中累积），基于此提出 PACE：将过剩的池占用转换为自适应拒绝预算，并对已完成轨迹按“有效陈旧性”评分（结合 Waiting Staleness 与前缀感知的 Generation Staleness）进行排序和筛选，避免因跨多个策略版本而惩罚长或被中断的 rollout。

Result: 在单轮数学推理上，PACE 在相同墙钟预算下比未过滤的异步 RL 平均提升 18.7% 验证准确率，并以 47.1% 更少 GPU 时间匹配同步 RL 性能。在多轮工具集成推理中也超越了同步与未过滤异步 RL。对混合专家模型和另一种 RL 算法的实验显示方法在架构与算法上有一定泛化。

Conclusion: PACE 在保留异步执行带来的时间效率的同时，有效控制轨迹陈旧性，从而显著提升 RL 在数学推理和多轮工具集成推理任务上的验证表现，并在多种模型架构与算法上展示出一定的通用性。

Abstract: Fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) improves resource utilization in large language model post-training by overlapping rollout generation with policy optimization, but it also introduces policy lag as trajectories are generated and queued while the trainer continues to update. We study how this lag accumulates over a trajectory's lifetime and how it can be controlled without sacrificing the wall-clock benefits of asynchronous execution. We decompose trajectory staleness into Generation Staleness, accumulated before rollout completion, and Waiting Staleness, accumulated after a completed trajectory enters the pool. Motivated by this decomposition, we introduce PACE (Pool-Aware Control of Effective Staleness). PACE converts excess pool occupancy into an adaptive rejection budget and ranks completed trajectories using an effective-staleness score that combines Waiting Staleness with prefix-aware Generation Staleness. This avoids penalizing long or interrupted rollouts solely because they span multiple policy versions. In single-turn mathematical reasoning, PACE improves the six-benchmark average validation accuracy by 18.7\% over unfiltered asynchronous RL at the same wall-clock budget and matches synchronous RL performance with 47.1\% less GPU time. PACE also improves validation performance in multi-turn tool-integrated reasoning, outperforming both synchronous and unfiltered asynchronous RL. Further experiments with the mixture-of-experts model and an alternative RL algorithm support its applicability across model architectures and training algorithms.

</details>


### [127] [ARC-KV: Amortizing Anchor Search for Reconstruction-Based KV Cache Compaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36835)
*Zheyu Shen,Guanhua Wang,Dezhan Tu,Mengchi Zhang,Yanjia Li,Adnan Aziz,Chunqiang Tang,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARC-KV 通过训练一次性可重用的锚点选择器并结合凸包约束的键合并，实现了显著更快且更准确的 KV cache 紧缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache 随序列长度线性增长对长上下文推理成为瓶颈；迭代锚点搜索（如 OMP）在重构式方法中虽有效但代价高昂，特别是对可复用前缀，需要更快的锚点选择方案。

Method: 引入“选择性摊销”原则：训练一个 value-aware 索引器在一次评分中选取真实键锚点；对剩余键做凸包约束的合并，并拟合 attention-mass 偏置与紧凑值（compact values）以匹配完整缓存。推理时使用冻结的索引器对每个上下文构建紧凑缓存并重用。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上的 QuALITY、RULER、LongBench 实验中，ARC-KV 在大多数设置优于已有紧缩方法。示例：QuALITY 上 10% KV 保留时，相比 Attention Matching，准确率由 0.6409 提升到 0.6474，紧缩时间从 959.8 s 降至 37.3 s（加速 25.73 倍）。

Conclusion: ARC-KV 提出了一种可复用锚点选择器并结合重构式 KV cache 紧缩的方案，在保持重构精度的同时显著降低了紧缩时间，从而在多个长上下文基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is bottlenecked by KV caches that grow linearly with sequence length. This burden is especially severe for long, reusable context prefixes, whose cache must serve many downstream queries. Reconstruction-based methods such as Attention Matching achieve strong downstream task performance with compact KV caches. However, iterative anchor search dominates the compaction cost of OMP-based Attention Matching. This motivates our selective amortization principle of learning a reusable anchor-selection policy across contexts while retaining context-specific reconstruction. In this work, we propose ARC-KV, a novel reconstruction-based KV cache compaction method that follows this principle. To this end, we first train a value-aware indexer to select real-key anchors in a single scoring pass. ARC-KV then applies convex-hull-constrained key merging and fits an attention-mass bias and compact values against the full cache. At inference time, ARC-KV builds the compact cache once per context using the frozen indexer and reuses it for all subsequent queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARC-KV outperforms reported compaction methods in most settings across QuALITY, RULER, and LongBench on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. In particular, at 10% KV retention on QuALITY, ARC-KV improves accuracy from 0.6409 to 0.6474 over Attention Matching while reducing compaction time by a factor of 25.73, from 959.8 s to 37.3 s.

</details>


### [128] [Automated Screw Planning for Reduced Pelvic Fractures Based on Statistical Shape Models and Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36847)
*Yang Gao,Sutuke Yibulayimu,Yanzhen Liu,Zian Zhao,Yudi Sang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一种基于患者三维解剖的全自动经皮髂骶螺钉术前规划流程，显著提高规划效率并小幅提升安全边距，临床接受率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经皮髂骶螺钉放置受限于复杂解剖及狭窄通道，传统术前人工测量耗时且依赖主观经验，存在安全性和效率问题，故需自动化、客观且高效的术前规划工具。

Method: 基于患者特异性三维解剖建模，自动检测骶髂区域解剖特征，算法搜索并优化可行且安全的螺钉通道，输出四种螺钉类型的插入轨迹；方法在200例临床骨盆骨折病例上进行验证，并与传统人工测量对比。

Result: 在200例临床验证中，提出的自动化流程使四类螺钉的安全通道安全边距平均提升约2%，平均术前规划时间下降超过90%，临床接受率达95%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种用于骨折患者的经皮髂骶螺钉术前规划的全自动化流程，可基于患者特异性的三维解剖结构自动识别安全螺钉通道并生成个体化的插入轨迹，从而提高手术规划的效率与安全性。

Abstract: Percutaneous iliosacral screw fixation is an important minimally invasive treatment for unstable pelvic fractures. Because the sacroiliac region has complex anatomy and narrow screw corridors, the accuracy and safety of screw placement directly affect surgical outcomes. Accurate and reliable preoperative screw planning is therefore essential to improve surgical success and reduce intraoperative risks. Conventional preoperative planning typically requires surgeons to determine screw trajectories through manual measurements, a labor-intensive process that depends on subjective clinical experience. To address these challenges, we propose a fully automated pipeline for preoperative iliosacral screw planning in patients with pelvic fractures. Using patient-specific three-dimensional anatomy, the pipeline automatically identifies safe screw corridors and generates individualized insertion trajectories to support clinical preoperative planning. We evaluated the proposed pipeline on 200 clinical cases of pelvic fractures. Compared with conventional manual measurements, the safety margin of the safe insertion corridors increased by 2% across the four screw types, the mean planning time decreased by more than 90%, and the clinical acceptance rate reached 95%.

</details>


### [129] [When Upstream Messages Override Correct Answers: A Controlled Study of Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36855)
*Yaxin Gong,Gangyi Zhang,Chongming Gao,Leyang Shen,Chenxiao Fan,Jiakai Wang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Wenjie Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Inter-agent messages can both help and harm; most harmful changes are due to downstream agents copying upstream wrong answers, so selective, reliability-aware communication and evidence-aware filtering are needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To disentangle benefits of inter-agent communication from harms caused by incorrect messages and to identify how and when message passing changes downstream answers.

Method: Controlled experiments across five benchmarks and five receiver models. For each downstream task with fixed evidence, compare three conditions: no message, the upstream agent's original message, and a message with the opposite conclusion. Audit harmful cases to identify mechanisms.

Result: Messages improve accuracy when downstream agents would otherwise err, but incorrect upstream messages change correct downstream answers in up to 32% of cases. In 94% of audited harmful cases the downstream agent copies the upstream's specific wrong answer (answer substitution). Filtering unreliable messages partially recovers accuracy, implying selective communication is beneficial.

Conclusion: Communication in multi-agent LLM systems is double-edged: it can help correct downstream errors but also frequently causes downstream agents to adopt incorrect upstream answers (answer substitution), so communication must be selective based on upstream reliability and downstream evidence.

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems rely on message passing among specialized agents to accomplish complex tasks. However, an upstream agent may provide useful information or an incorrect answer that causes a downstream agent to override a correct answer supported by its own evidence. Prior work has not clearly separated the benefits of communication from the damage caused by incorrect messages. We study this problem with controlled experiments across five benchmarks and five receivers, keeping the downstream task and evidence fixed while comparing answers under three conditions: no message, the upstream agent's original message, or a message with the opposite conclusion. Our experiments reveal three key findings. First, messages often help when the downstream agent would otherwise answer incorrectly. Second, messages can also hurt: when the downstream agent would answer correctly without a message, an incorrect upstream message changes the answer in up to 32% of cases. Third, in 94% of audited harmful cases, the downstream agent copies the upstream's specific wrong answer--a pattern we term answer substitution. Removing unreliable messages recovers part of the lost accuracy, suggesting that communication should be selective based on upstream reliability and the evidence already available to the downstream agent.

</details>


### [130] [IronLLM: Forging Compact Edge-Native Language Models for Real-Time Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36860)
*Changdi Yang,Fengquan Jiao,Haochih Lin,Haoran Yang,Jing Xiao,Liangyu Huo,Suxin Lu,Tiance Chen,Wei Liu,Yinggan Xu,Yunxiang Lu,Zai Zheng,Zhirui Xie,Zhongyang Che,Ziyan Tang,Zuoxiang Zhao,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向端侧低延迟部署的 654M 模型，通过 X-MTP 与轻量化设计显著加速解码并保持与更大模型可比的性能，Light 版优化了量化适配与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向资源受限的终端设备与低延迟场景，追求在受限算力/内存下仍能实现高吞吐和较好模型能力的实用化部署解决方案。

Method: 采用混合注意力结构与 X-MTP（共享 KV 的多 token 预测）以消除逐层 KV-cache 重放；引入轻量级验证头实现无回滚的草稿回退；设计为 Instruct-Only 模式以降低延迟。预训练语料约 6.2 万亿 token，随后使用多域 On-Policy 知识蒸馏整合领域教师能力。IronLLM-0.6B-Light 用 Dynamic Tanh 取代 RMSNorm 并简化若干昂贵组件以改善推理与量化效率。

Result: 实现 1.48× 解码加速；在多项评测上对比 Qwen3.5-0.8B、MiniCPM5-1B 等更大模型保持竞争力；在生成长度/简洁性方面常表现出更简明的回复；Light 版本在量化与推理效率上进一步改进。

Conclusion: IronLLM-0.6B 在权衡性能与推理效率方面取得了显著进展：通过架构与推理设计优化获得约 1.48× 的解码加速，并在与更大模型比较时保持竞争力。Light 版本通过更换归一化和简化组件进一步提高了量化与推理效率。

Abstract: We present IronLLM-0.6B, a 654M-parameter language model designed for efficient on-device inference. IronLLM-0.6B combines a hybrid attention architecture with X-MTP, a lightweight shared-KV multi-token prediction design that eliminates per-depth KV-cache replay and employs a lightweight verification head for rollback-free drafting, achieving a 1.48x decoding speedup. The model is pretrained on approximately 6.2 trillion tokens using a quality-oriented data pipeline and is further post-trained with Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation to integrate capabilities from domain-specialized teachers. To better meet the low-latency requirements of on-device scenarios, IronLLM-0.6B adopts an Instruct-Only design. Evaluations show that IronLLM-0.6B achieves competitive performance relative to larger models such as Qwen3.5-0.8B and MiniCPM5-1B, while producing more concise responses on many tasks. We further present IronLLM-0.6B-Light, which replaces RMSNorm with Dynamic Tanh and simplifies several computationally expensive components to improve inference and quantization efficiency. Together, the IronLLM models provide an effective performance-efficiency trade-off for resource-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [131] [State Trace Rationale As Auxiliary Task in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36867)
*Muhammad U. Nasir,Alex Vogt,Steven D. James,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用环境生成的短文本轨迹作为辅助预测任务，可显著提升稀疏奖励迷宫类任务中RL的性能并提供可解释性，同时改善表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类空间导航的启发，利用类似地标/路线/全景的多模态结构化描述来强化表征学习与次级监督，以改善在稀疏奖励场景中的探索、信用分配和策略可解释性。

Method: 在标准策略网络上增加一个辅助head，训练目标为预测一段描述智能体当前状态的短文本轨迹（包含地标、路线、全局视图、位置、物品、目标和即时进展），这些文本由环境规则在线生成，无需人工标注。总体训练为主任务的RL损失与该辅助文本预测损失的联合优化。

Result: 在60个稀疏奖励XLand‑MiniGrid任务上，STRAT解决了标准RL失败的复杂环境，得到更紧凑的表征、避免秩坍塌，并在推理阶段以零额外代价提供可读的轨迹预测。

Conclusion: STRAT通过让强化学习智能体预测一段由环境规则生成的简短文本轨迹，能够在稀疏奖励的XLand‑MiniGrid任务集中显著提升解题能力，同时压缩状态表征并防止表征秩坍塌，并且额外产出可读的智能体信念描述。

Abstract: We propose STRAT, an auxiliary task that trains deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents to predict a short textual trace of their own state. Inspired by human spatial navigation, the description combines landmark, route, and survey knowledge, tracking the agent's position, inventory, goals, and immediate progress. Environment rules generate this text online without human labelling. Our method adds a single auxiliary head to a standard policy. Across 60 sparse-reward XLand-MiniGrid tasks, STRAT solves complex environments where standard RL fails outright, while compacting state representations and preventing rank collapse. Beyond performance gains, the predicted trace provides a readable account of agent beliefs at every step for no extra cost.

</details>


### [132] [WEFT: Scaling Tool-Use Post-Training for General-Purpose Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36887)
*Bo Mao,Hang He,Linting Wang,Lizhi Lin,Maosen Zhou,Guanming Liu,Jinxiu Liu,Tianyu Huai,Chaoyun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Kepeng Lei,Guanting Dong,Zhou Shao,Rui Zheng,Hang Yan,Jie Zhou,Chengcheng Wan,Tao Gui,Liang He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WEFT is a whole-system, execution-driven post-training framework that iteratively fixes interaction-system failures and applies stability-focused optimization; it outperforms environment-only scaling baselines across multiple benchmarks and model sizes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work focused on scaling executable environments alone, but isolated environment scaling fails to guarantee improved model performance because reliable learning signals require coherent interactions among environment, task, agent harness, and evaluator. WEFT aims to scale the entire agentic interaction system and close the loop between execution outcomes and system revision.

Method: WeFT builds three pillars: (1) whole-system scaling of agentic interaction components (environment breadth, task complexity, interaction diversity), (2) execution-driven self-evolution that uses execution traces and state evidence to attribute failures, revise specific components, and re-evaluate with fresh rollouts, and (3) stable post-training techniques (prefix-preserving sampling, atomic-turn credit assignment, and MegaMCP for isolated recoverable state during concurrent rollouts).

Result: Extensive experiments show WEFT-8B and WEFT-14B outperform matched-size environment-scaling baselines on BFCL V4, τ^2-Bench, and Claw-Eval. WEFT-14B improves over Agent-World-14B by +6.41, +2.23, and +12.27 percentage points on those benchmarks respectively. WEFT-35B-A3B extends gains to long-horizon workflow benchmarks like Toolathlon-Verified and AutomationBench.

Conclusion: WEFT shows that coupling scalable, whole-system construction of agentic interactions with execution-driven self-evolution and stability-focused post-training yields consistent and sizable gains in tool-use post-training across model sizes and benchmarks, outperforming environment-only scaling baselines.

Abstract: Recent efforts to scale tool-use post-training have largely centered on the synthesis of executable environments, which constitute only one component of a broader agentic interaction system comprising the environment, task, agent harness, and evaluator. Scaling environments in isolation, however, does not guarantee commensurate gains in model performance, because reliable learning signals depend on coherent interactions among all components of the agentic interaction system. To address this problem, we introduce WEFT (Whole-system Evolution For Tool-use Post-training), which couples scalable agentic interaction system construction, execution-driven self-evolution, and stable post-training. WEFT scales agentic interaction system construction across environment breadth, task complexity, and interaction diversity. Execution-driven self-evolution iteratively uses execution traces and state evidence to attribute failures and revise the responsible components, with fresh rollouts evaluating the changes and providing evidence for subsequent evolution rounds. For stable post-training at scale, WEFT addresses both optimization and execution reliability: prefix-preserving sampling retains verified progress and atomic-turn credit assignment localizes learning signals, while MegaMCP maintains isolated, recoverable state across concurrent rollouts over shared tool services. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of WEFT for tool-use post-training. WEFT-8B and WEFT-14B outperform all evaluated matched-size environment-scaling baselines on BFCL V4, $τ^2$-Bench, and Claw-Eval. In particular, WEFT-14B improves over Agent-World-14B by 6.41, 2.23, and 12.27 percentage points. WEFT-35B-A3B further extends these gains to more challenging long-horizon workflow benchmarks, including Toolathlon-Verified and AutomationBench.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Sub-Gaussian Detector Scores: Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection for Human-LLM Text Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36888)
*Wan Tian,Zhongyi Li,Yawen Li,Rui Zhang,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种对检测器分数鲁棒的加权剖面损失变点检测（RWCP），通过截断权重、Huber 增益和 narrowest-over-threshold 搜索在理论与实践上均优于标准加权方法，尤其提高了孤立变化的边界恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合人类与大模型（LLM）的文档中，基于检测器分数定位作者切换需要对不同文本单元可信度加权，但现有加权均值对极端分数敏感，而直接使用鲁棒中心则难以解析误置边界如何改变总体目标。为此，需要一种既鲁棒又能保持变点可识别性的检测框架。

Method: 提出了 Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection (RWCP)：通过（1）对可靠性权重截断（capped weights），（2）使用 Huber 型剖面增益（profile gains）来抵抗极端分数，以及（3）在可靠性坐标上采用 narrowest-over-threshold 的搜索策略来定位分割点。理论分析将真实分割与误置分割间的目标差异表达为一个合并成本（merge cost），从而规避对混合段非线性中心的封闭形式求解；并在二次损失极限下恢复加权平方 CUSUM。还提出 RWCP-R——一种在解码时在不相邻片段间共享源中心的变体。

Result: 理论结果给出在显式曲率、间距和依赖性假设下的恢复与定位一致性；算法上，核心 RWCP 在五类回溯缓存分数基准家族上将 family-macro WindowDiff 平均降低约 17.6%（相比传统加权变化点方法），RWCP-R 在共享中心的设置上进一步降低错误，边界恢复在孤立变化场景最为明显，但对于协作式或密集交替的文本配置仍存在漏检问题。

Conclusion: 在明确的曲率、间距和依赖性条件下，RWCP 能够恢复变化点个数并局部化其边界；其二次损失极限对应加权平方 CUSUM，RWCP-R 在共享源中心的设置下能进一步降低错误率。总体上，相对已有加权变化点检测方法，核心 RWCP 将 family-macro WindowDiff 平均降低约 17.6%，RWCP-R 进一步改善边界恢复，尤其对孤立变化最明显。

Abstract: Mixed human-LLM documents require locating authorship transitions from detector scores whose reliability varies across text units. Existing weighted mean contrasts are vulnerable to extreme scores, while directly replacing means with robust centers obscures how a misplaced boundary changes the population objective. We propose Robust Weighted Profile-Loss Change Point Detection (RWCP), which combines capped reliability weights, Huber profile gains, and narrowest-over-threshold search in reliability coordinates. Our key analysis expresses the population gap between a true and a displaced split as a merge cost, avoiding a closed-form solution for the nonlinear center of a mixed segment. Under explicit curvature, spacing, and dependence conditions, core RWCP recovers the number of changes and localizes their boundaries; its quadratic-loss limit recovers squared weighted CUSUM. We also study RWCP-R, a separately evaluated decoder that shares source centers across nonadjacent passages. Across five retrospective cached-score benchmark families, core RWCP reduces family-macro WindowDiff by 17.6\% relative to weighted change-point detection, and RWCP-R lowers it further. Boundary recovery improves most clearly for isolated changes, while both fixed configurations miss changes in collaborative and densely alternating text.

</details>


### [134] [Harness Evolution as Learning: Approximation, Generalization, and Optimization Limits of Self-Improving Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36892)
*Zeyu Gan,Zixuan Gong,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: tldr：论文把个人代理的个性化问题聚焦到harness设计与演化，结合偏好基准的实证研究与学习理论分析，说明了架构、规模和算法三方面如何限制或促进个性化，并给出指导性结论和设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：随着LLM能力提升，如何把能力转化为对个人用户有用的持续个性化行为成为关键问题；当基础模型固定时，所能依赖的是围绕模型的harness层，理解和优化该层的演化规律是实现可靠个性化的核心。

Method: 方法：提出偏好导向基准，系统化地评估harness的架构选择、规模扩展与自我进化算法的表现；同时把harness演化形式化为学习问题，分别分析可达策略空间、有穷交互证据下的容量限制和带偏更新动力学，结合实证与理论解释观察到的现象。

Result: 结果：实证部分通过新基准揭示了不同harness设计在个性化任务上的能力与局限；理论部分用逼近-泛化-优化三类误差解释了规模与交互样本量对性能的影响，并给出关于可达性、容量与偏差更新的理论结论，二者共同限定了通过harness演化可达的个性化水平。

Conclusion: 论文结论：通过实证与理论分析相结合，论文揭示了在固定基础模型下，通过设计和演化“harness”（上下文、记忆、工具、执行层）实现个性化的能力与局限，说明了架构、规模与自我演化算法三者对个性化效果的影响，并用逼近、泛化与优化误差构建了统一解释框架，从而为未来harness设计提供指导。

Abstract: As the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) continue to advance, increasing attention is turning to how to translate their abilities into useful behavior. Personal agents bring this question into everyday settings, where models are expected to serve individual users and continually adapt to their preferences. With the underlying model held fixed, such adaptation relies on harness engineering: designing and evolving the surrounding layer that manages context, memory, tools, and execution. Despite rapid progress, the factors governing effective harness evolution remain insufficiently understood. To narrow this gap, we investigate three central questions concerning harness architecture, harness scale, and self-evolution algorithms through complementary empirical and theoretical analyses. Empirically, we introduce a preference-oriented benchmark and systematically characterize the capabilities and limitations of personal agents associated with these three dimensions. Theoretically, we formulate harness evolution as a learning problem and explain these phenomena through approximation, generalization, and optimization errors. Analyses of reachable policies, capacity under finite interaction evidence, and biased update dynamics provide theoretical accounts of the observed phenomena. Together, these results offer a unified perspective on the limits of personalization through harness evolution and inform future harness design.

</details>


### [135] [HorizonFlow: Variable-Length Planning for Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36896)
*JunHyeok Oh,Zian Jang,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HorizonFlow是一种将计划长度作为生成目标的层次化轨迹生成方法，通过插入式生成与流匹配联合产出子目标、动作前缀与长度信息，从而自适应地避免过短或过长的规划并在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成的轨迹修补方法通常需要事先指定规划时域(horizon)，但合适的时域依赖具体路线：过短导致不可行跳转，过长造成冗余动作。需一个能同时产生内容与长度的规划器以提高效率与可行性。

Method: 构建两层架构：一个子目标路由(planner)和一个动作前缀控制器(controller)，两者均采用基于插入的生成机制结合流匹配(flow matching)，在生成过程中同时输出连续的计划token与对应的长度信息，并利用部分已生成的计划引导后续插入操作。生成得到的长度信息被复用用于候选方案筛选与偏好较短计划的引导，无需额外学习的价值模型。

Result: 在Maze2D、Multi2D和OGBench的导航与视觉操控基准上，HorizonFlow在比较方法中取得最高的平均性能，表明其在多种任务环境下有效地生成合适长度的规划并提升成功率。

Conclusion: HorizonFlow提出了把计划长度作为生成输出而非预先设定的层次化规划器，能够自适应选择轨迹长度并生成连续的子目标与动作前缀，从而减少不可行或冗余的过短/过长计划。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative planning have made trajectory inpainting a promising approach to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning. However, these methods typically specify the planning horizon before generating plan content, even though the appropriate horizon depends on the route itself. A horizon that is too short can force infeasible transitions, whereas one that is too long can introduce redundant motion. We introduce HorizonFlow, a hierarchical planner that treats plan length as an output of generation rather than a prescribed input. Its subgoal route planner guides its action-prefix controller through a sequence of latent subgoals. Both components combine insertion-based generation with flow matching to jointly generate continuous plan content and length, using the partially generated plan to guide token insertion. HorizonFlow reuses the resulting length information to select candidates and steer generation toward shorter plans without a separate learned value model. Across Maze2D, Multi2D, and OGBench navigation and visual manipulation benchmarks, HorizonFlow achieves the highest average performance among the compared methods.

</details>


### [136] [STAR-GRPO: Canonical Anchoring and Reliability-First Advantages against Representation-Dependent Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36900)
*Wan Tian,Zhongyi Li,Xiang Xu,Minhao Zou,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAR-GRPO通过基于成对评估的可靠性量化，先进行鲁棒的群体归一化再按群体可靠性衰减优势，从而限制不受支持的奖励影响并改善真实质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略优化中，脆弱的奖励接口或过于宽松的代理目标会导致奖励劫持（reward hacking）；在群体相对优化框架下，这种劫持更严重，因为不受支持的奖励可以改变群体基线并影响其他rollout的更新，而事后或纯相对加权无法表达群体范围内的不确定性。

Method: 提出Self-Tuned Anchored Reliability Group-Relative Policy Optimization (STAR-GRPO)：对同一次rollout进行成对评估以得到分数分歧作为可靠性度量；用相对可靠性进行自适应的鲁棒位置-尺度拟合（加锚定）进行群体归一化；再通过绝对群体可靠性对所得有界优势值进行衰减。

Result: 给出了坐标与二阶矩界、通过加权位置方程精确中心化的刻画，以及针对异常奖励的可靠性依赖衰减保证；在两种奖励劫持场景（token接口被利用和用于医学推理的rubric代理过优化）上实证显示：防止部署接口分数无界增长、提升规范质量信号、缩小代理与裁判评估差距并降低过度宣称。

Conclusion: STAR-GRPO能在保留任务奖励目标的同时，通过可靠性优先的归一化与缩减策略限制不受支持的奖励对群体基线和策略更新的影响，从而减少奖励劫持并提升真实质量指标。

Abstract: Reward hacking occurs when policy optimization exploits a brittle reward interface or an overly permissive proxy objective, improving the training score without improving the underlying response quality. This phenomenon is amplified in group-relative policy optimization: an unsupported reward can shift the group baseline and alter the updates of other rollouts, while post-hoc or purely relative weighting cannot represent group-wide uncertainty. We propose \emph{Self-Tuned Anchored Reliability Group-Relative Policy Optimization} (STAR-GRPO), a reliability-first advantage estimator based on paired assessments of the same rollout. STAR separates the quality signal from its learning influence: score disagreement determines rollout reliability, relative reliability enters a self-tuned robust location--scale fit before group normalization, and absolute group reliability attenuates the resulting bounded advantage. The analysis establishes coordinate and second-moment bounds, characterizes exact centering through the weighted location equation, and gives reliability-dependent attenuation guarantees for outlying rewards. We evaluate STAR-GRPO in two complementary reward-hacking regimes. In token-interface exploitation, STAR prevents runaway optimization of the deployed-interface score while improving the canonical quality signal. In rubric-proxy overoptimization for medical reasoning, STAR improves independent semantic evaluation, narrows the proxy--judge discrepancy, and reduces overclaim while optimizing the same task proxy. Together, these results show that reliability-first normalization offers a principled way to limit unsupported reward influence on both group baselines and policy updates, while retaining the task reward as the optimization target.

</details>


### [137] [PrecogUI: Proactive GUI Agents via Pre-cognitive Simulation and Experience Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36923)
*Bin Kang,Jiarui Ouyang,Li Jiang,Bin Chen,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于经验缓存+动作前瞻模拟+融合控制的预感知GUI代理，能在长时程强扰动任务中显著提高鲁棒性，但需更多细节与广泛消融/泛化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式GUI代理在长时程交互中遇到意外扰动时容易注意力转移和级联失败，缺乏前瞻能力和对反复异常模式的记忆，因而需要从被动执行转向主动预测与准备以提高鲁棒性。

Method: 三部分协同：1) Proactive Experience Pool (PEP)：以“状态-动作-结果”三元组形式在双重记忆库中缓存重复出现的异常与成功模式，用于检索经验先验；2) Proactive Simulation Executor (PSE)：学习在候选动作条件下预测下一步符号化的UI布局，从而提前识别并避免异常，并据此为候选动作打分排序；3) Pre-cognitive Execution Controller (PEC)：融合PEP的经验先验与PSE的预测，优先处理可预见的异常，并通过闭环错误修正保证执行鲁棒性。为评估还设计了AutoTraj自动数据生成器并构建了含强扰动的InterfereBench基准。

Result: 在作者构建的InterfereBench上，PrecogUI超越了当前最先进的方法；在若干公开基准上保持竞争性能。论文宣称将公开代码以利复现。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种“预感知”GUI代理架构（PrecogUI），通过缓存经验、基于候选动作的前瞻模拟以及融合先验与预测的执行控制，显著提高了在长时程、强干扰交互任务中的鲁棒性。总体思路合理且创新，实验证明在作者设计的扰动基准上优于现有方法，但论文仍需补充若干实现细节和消融/泛化实验以增强可信度。

Abstract: Existing reactive Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents often fail in long-horizon, dynamic scenarios, where unexpected disturbances trigger attention-diverting and cascading failures. To address this, we propose PrecogUI, a pre-cognitive architecture that shifts the paradigm from reactive execution to proactive decision-making. Specifically, we design a Proactive Experience Pool (PEP), which caches recurring anomaly and success patterns as "state-action-result" tuples in a dual-memory repository. Furthermore, we introduce a Proactive Simulation Executor (PSE) that learns to forecast the next symbolic UI layout given a candidate action, enabling early anomaly avoidance and ranking candidate actions by predicted reliability. Finally, a Pre-cognitive Execution Controller (PEC) fuses these priors and predictions, prioritizes handling of foreseen anomalies, and ensures execution robustness through a closed-loop error correction mechanism. For robust evaluation, we develop AutoTraj, an automatic data-generation engine, to construct InterfereBench, a benchmark for long-horizon tasks with strong disturbances. Experiments demonstrate that PrecogUI surpasses state-of-the-art methods on InterfereBench while maintaining competitive performance on public benchmarks. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [138] [Neuro-Symbolic Computer Use: Learning Reusable Policies for Reliable and Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36927)
*Hyewon Suh,Thanh Minh Nguyen,Chih-Lun Lee,Darrow Hartman,Lizhao Liu,Xin Eric Wang,Ang Li,Jiachen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将稳定决策符号化并用神经模块处理感知相关决策，配合迭代生成与修订策略，论文在重复性桌面/科学工作流上实现了更高通过率和更低运行成本与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多计算机任务是重复性工作流——相同流程多次运行但输入和起始状态不同。传统代理每次重新规划导致计算代价高且不可靠，需寻找可重用且更高效的执行策略。

Method: 提出“神经-符号策略迭代”：从单一代理轨迹出发，反复执行当前策略、用任务完成与步级判别器定位失败、借助编程模型（参考代理在失败点的后续）修订代码；通过生成参数与初始状态变体以增强可重用性，并在部署时使用步骤前验证器保护每个改动步骤。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified与ScienceBoard四种设置上，学得的策略在所有方法中Pass^3最高，比基线代理高3.6–15.8点；每次运行成本降低15–217倍，延迟降低3.4–5.1倍；在OSWorld-Verified上仅用变体训练的策略能迁移到留出原始任务，Pass^3比AutoRPA高8.6–17.5点。

Conclusion: 引入的神经-符号计算机使用方法通过把跨运行稳定的决策固化为可执行代码并将观察依赖决策交给神经组件，从而在重复性工作流上显著提高可靠性和效率；所学策略在多个基准上获得最优Pass^3并显著降低运行成本与延迟。

Abstract: Many computer tasks recur: the same workflow runs many times, with new inputs and from different starting states. Current computer-use agents re-plan every step of every run, which makes them costly and unreliable on such tasks. We introduce neuro-symbolic computer use, in which a recurring workflow is executed by a learned policy rather than re-derived by an agent on each run. The policy fixes the decisions that are stable across runs (ordering, variables, loops, and branches) in executable code, and delegates observation-dependent decisions, such as grounding and state checks, to neural models. We learn these policies with neuro-symbolic policy iteration: starting from one agent trajectory, it executes the policy, diagnoses failures with task-completion and step-level judges, and revises the code with a coding model informed by an agent's continuation from the point of failure, without access to the benchmark evaluator. Iterating on generated parameter and initial-state variants makes the policy reusable, and a pre-action verifier guards each state-mutating step at deployment. On OSWorld-Verified and ScienceBoard, the learned policies achieve the highest Pass^3 of all methods in all four settings, 3.6-15.8 points above the base agent, while cutting per-run cost by 15-217$\times$ and latency by 3.4-5.1$\times$. On OSWorld-Verified, policies built only on variants transfer to the held-out original tasks, exceeding AutoRPA by 8.6-17.5 points in Pass^3.

</details>


### [139] [Learn from the Gap: Differential-Aware Advantage Pruning with Adaptive Rollout Sampling for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36932)
*Jiahua Yang,Zhiwei Yang,Xianpeng Zhang,Dongyu Chen,Xing Chen,Tianhuang Su,Haonan Lu,Quanlong Guan,Kai Tang,Chuangchuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FastRL 通过优势感知剪枝与自适应采样，减少冗余 rollouts 并提升梯度信息密度，从而在保持兼容性的情况下显著加速 GRPO 系列方法并带来小幅性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 GRPO 类方法虽能提升策略优化效果，但存在每题多次 rollout、逐 token 概率重复计算导致的高计算开销；此外，低信息或高度同质的轨迹会削弱学习信号效率，限制最终性能。

Method: 提出两大核心机制：1) 优势感知剪枝（advantage-aware pruning），选择性保留高优势且多样的轨迹以提高梯度信息含量；2) 自适应 rollout 采样机制，根据历史剪枝分布动态调整不同训练阶段的采样规模，以平衡探索与计算效率。将这些模块无缝集成到 GRPO、DAPO、GSPO 中。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 和 GeoQA8K-R1V 视觉推理数据集上，FastRL 平均获得约 2.07× 的训练加速，并带来约 1.64% 的平均准确率提升；代码将开源。

Conclusion: FastRL 是一个可插拔的强化学习框架，通过对轨迹进行基于优势的剪枝和自适应采样，在保持或提升策略学习效果的同时显著减少计算开销，提高训练效率。

Abstract: Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and its variants have been developed for policy optimization and demonstrated notable performance gains. However, these methods usually incur substantial computational overhead due to per-question multi-rollout sampling and repeated per-token probability evaluation across rollouts. Furthermore, low-information or highly homogeneous trajectories can degrade downstream learning signal efficiency, hindering model optimization and limiting final performance. To address these issues, we propose FastRL, a novel plug-and-play reinforcement learning framework that simultaneously improves training efficiency and the effectiveness of policy learning. Specifically, 1) We introduce an advantage-aware pruning strategy to selectively preserve high-advantage trajectories while maximizing inter-trajectory gradient diversity. 2) Then, we design an adaptive rollout sampling mechanism to dynamically adjust the sampling scale across different training stages based on historical pruning distributions, balancing exploration adequacy and computational efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that FastRL can be seamlessly integrated into GRPO, DAPO, and GSPO variants, achieving an average 2.07$\times$ training speedup on Geometry3K and GeoQA8K-R1V, along with an approximately 1.64\% improvement in average accuracy on visual reasoning benchmarks. Source codes will be available at https://github.com/Nicozwy/FastRL.

</details>


### [140] [VLALight: A Vision-Language-Action Model for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36934)
*Pan Zhang,Siqi Lai,Kemu Dong,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLALight: first VLA model for end-to-end multi-view video-based traffic signal control, combining supervised cold-start, cooperative RL, and adaptive reasoning to improve network-wide traffic efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing TSC methods depend on hand-crafted traffic states or separate perception modules, creating a gap between visual observations and control decisions; the paper aims to close this gap by an end-to-end VLA model that uses raw roadside video for control.

Method: Direct mapping from multi-view video to signal actions via multi-target spatiotemporal reasoning and topology-aware cooperative perception; two-stage training (supervised cold-start for perception and decision priors, then cooperative agentic reinforcement learning for joint local/network optimization); adaptive fast/slow reasoning with balanced mode-aware rollouts and relative advantage optimization to trade off decision quality and inference cost.

Result: On seven real-world traffic-flow datasets across three urban networks, VLALight consistently outperforms multiple baselines; ablations show benefits of cooperative perception, network-level optimization, and adaptive reasoning; code is released.

Conclusion: VLALight demonstrates that a vision-language-action model can perform end-to-end traffic signal control from multi-view roadside videos, achieving coordinated, topology-aware control and outperforming transportation-based, RL-based, and LLM/VLM-based baselines on multiple datasets.

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is essential for improving urban mobility and reducing congestion. Although roadside cameras are widely deployed at signalized intersections and provide rich visual observations of evolving traffic, existing TSC methods typically rely on manually engineered traffic states or separate perception modules, creating a gap between physical observations and control decisions. We present VLALight, the first vision-language-action (VLA) model for end-to-end traffic signal control from multi-view roadside videos. VLALight directly maps visual observations to coordinated signal actions through multi-target spatiotemporal traffic reasoning and topology-aware cooperative perception across intersections. To establish this capability, we develop a two-stage supervised cold-start training strategy for visual traffic understanding and signal decision-making, followed by cooperative agentic reinforcement learning that jointly optimizes local control and network-wide traffic efficiency. Furthermore, VLALight introduces adaptive fast and slow reasoning modes, enabling the policy to allocate deeper reasoning only when additional deliberation provides sufficient control benefits. Through balanced mode-aware rollouts and relative advantage optimization, VLALight learns to trade off decision quality and inference cost. Extensive experiments on seven real-world traffic-flow datasets across three urban networks demonstrate that VLALight consistently outperforms transportation-based, RL-based, and LLM/VLM-based baselines. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of cooperative perception, network-level optimization, and adaptive reasoning. These results demonstrate the potential of VLA models for real-world physical traffic control. Our project is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/VLALight.git.

</details>


### [141] [CoEM: Empowering Long-Context Reasoning with Commit-on-Evidence Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36935)
*Jingguang Li,Yebo Wu,Zuyi Guo,Kailang Ma,Xianjie Dai,Han Zheng,Benwang Chen,Li Li,Can Rong,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEM提出“证据驱动提交”的记忆管理，通过保留原文摘录并用学习策略+验证器决定何时压缩，显著改善长上下文推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中模型性能随上下文变长下降，现有按块处理并立即压缩信息的方法会在早期丢弃后续推理所需的关键细节。

Method: 在固定的上下文-记忆预算下，CoEM维护一个“待决（pending）”原文摘录集合，随后按步接收新的上下文；一个训练得到的策略对每个待决摘录判断“提交/保留/丢弃”，并由一个冻结的验证器确保仅当当前保留摘录和上下文支持时才接受为已提交记忆；策略通过强化学习训练，结合细粒度的步级证据奖励与最终答案奖励。

Result: 在大规模长上下文评测上（6,400 文档长上下文输入），CoEM较最强记忆基线在 Qwen3.5-9B 上获得约 10.4–11.4 点 F1 提升，表明在保留关键证据并选择性提交方面效果显著。

Conclusion: CoEM通过在压缩前保留可疑证据片段并在后续上下文到来时基于学习策略决定是否提交为记忆，从而减少关键信息被过早丢弃，显著提升长上下文推理性能。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning is essential for complex and long-horizon tasks, yet the performance of large language models (LLMs) degrades as context length increases. Recent approaches address this by processing input chunk by chunk while maintaining a bounded textual memory in model context. However, premature information compression can discard critical details essential for subsequent reasoning. In this paper, we introduce Commit-on-Evidence Memory (CoEM), which learns when to convert source evidence into compact memory facts. Specifically, under a fixed context-memory budget, CoEM preserves potentially useful source excerpts verbatim in a pending set, allowing subsequent context to clarify their relevance before irreversible compression. As new context arrives, a learned policy revisits each pending excerpt and decides whether to promote it to the committed memory, retain it for further consideration, or discard it. A frozen verifier ensures proposed facts are accepted only if supported by retained excerpts and current context. To further guide effective memory management, we train this policy using reinforcement learning by combining fine-grained, step-level evidence rewards with final answer rewards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoEM consistently improves long-context reasoning. When evaluated on 6,400 documents long-context input, CoEM outperforms the strongest memory baseline by 10.4-11.4 F1 points on Qwen3.5-9B. Code repository: https://github.com/benmagnifico/CoEM.

</details>


### [142] [SCA: Spatial Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning of GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36939)
*Shengtian Yang,Ziyu Xiong,Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Gai Kun,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce Spatial Credit Assignment: use click coordinates to refine group-relative RL credit, improving action-prediction and grounding across GUI benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Binary group-relative reward signals fail to distinguish spatially different failed clicks and give no relative information when all sampled clicks fail; spatial coordinates can provide finer-grained supervision.

Method: Predict each held-out response's reward from the other responses in mixed-success groups, use the prediction residual to adjust group credit; when all sampled clicks fail, rank by distance to annotated target; these spatial cues are used only during training.

Result: In controlled synthetic studies SCA reduces error and aligns policy updates closer to the true return gradient; across GUI grounding and offline action-prediction benchmarks it achieves consistent and often best performance among reinforcement-fine-tuned models.

Conclusion: SCA refines group-relative credit by incorporating screen-coordinate information of sampled clicks, yielding better policy updates and improved performance on GUI grounding and offline action-prediction benchmarks.

Abstract: GUI agents automate tasks on digital devices by grounding language instructions in visual interfaces. Existing group-relative reinforcement learning improves GUI action prediction by comparing the rewards of multiple responses sampled from the same GUI state. However, binary evaluation treats spatially different failed clicks as identical and provides no relative signal when all sampled clicks fail. To address these limitations, we propose Spatial Credit Assignment (SCA), which uses the screen coordinates of sampled clicks to refine group-relative credit. Specifically, SCA predicts each held-out response's reward from the other responses in groups containing both successes and failures, then uses the prediction residual to adjust credit. When all sampled clicks fail, SCA instead orders them by distance to the annotated target. These spatial references are used only to construct the training update; the deployed policy remains unchanged. We evaluate whether this correction improves the policy update itself by comparing its error and directional alignment with the exact return gradient in a controlled synthetic study. Across GUI grounding and offline action-prediction benchmarks, SCA improves grounding across professional domains and achieves the strongest results among reinforcement-fine-tuned models on most action-prediction metrics, with consistent gains across the reported GUI suites.

</details>


### [143] [Dual-Channel Robust Group-Relative Policy Optimization via Advantage and Sequence-Weight Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36944)
*Zhongyi Li,Wan Tian,Xiang Xu,Yutian Xiao,Yikun Ban,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个分离处理奖励与比率异常的双通道鲁棒GSPO变体（RoVR-GSPO），在多项任务与扰动测试中表现出比GSPO更强的鲁棒性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于组的策略优化（GSPO）同时依赖于奖励推导的优势估计与序列级似然比权重，这两类信号对局部异常值（极端奖励或token级log-ratio扰动）敏感，导致训练不稳定或性能下降。

Method: 提出双通道优化器：奖励通道采用稳健参考估计（robust reference estimation）+ 有界残差记分（bounded residual credit），以限制极端奖励对优势信号的破坏；比率通道采用可微SoftRoVR聚合构造序列权重，减少token级log-ratio扰动导致的剪裁/重权问题。并针对两通道给出稳定性与效率分析。

Result: 在数学推理、长上下文摘要和工具调用标注等任务上，RoVR-GSPO较GSPO持续改进；受控扰动试验显示其对奖励污染和token比率异常更鲁棒，且在稳定性与计算效率方面有理论或经验支持。

Conclusion: RoVR-GSPO通过在奖励通道和比率通道分别使用稳健参考估计与有界残差记分、以及可微SoftRoVR聚合，显著提升了对奖励污染和令牌比率异常的鲁棒性，相较于GSPO在多项任务上表现更稳健且具有稳定性与效率保证。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization relies on reward-derived advantages and sequence-level likelihood weights, both of which can be sensitive to localized outliers. Extreme rewards can collapse the contrast among clean responses after group normalization, while token-level log-ratio perturbations can alter sequence weights and clipping decisions. We introduce RoVR-GSPO, a dual-channel robust optimizer that addresses these failure modes separately. Its reward channel combines robust reference estimation with bounded residual credit, while its ratio channel uses differentiable SoftRoVR aggregation to construct robust sequence weights. We provide stability and efficiency analyses for both channels. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, long-context summarization, and tool-call annotation show consistent improvements over GSPO, while controlled perturbation studies demonstrate stronger robustness to reward contamination and token-ratio anomalies.

</details>


### [144] [REALHOP: Rethinking Multi-Hop Reasoning Evaluation via Behavioral Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36984)
*Jiawen Tao,Xiaokun Yuan,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Mengzhou Wu,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者通过BNR揭示了现有多跳基准中模型不依赖每跳证据的普遍问题，并用REALHOP构建可验证的多跳问题，使得模型对每一步证据的依赖率显著提升，强调多跳评估必须同时验证行为必要性和答案准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳评测通常用预先定义的推理链构造问题，并以正确答案假定模型利用了该链。但仅靠答案正确无法保证模型依赖每个中间证据，可能靠记忆、短路径或部分证据完成。需要一种方法验证模型是否确实依赖于每一步的证据。

Method: 本文提出并使用行为必要率（Behavioral Necessity Rate, BNR）来度量去除目标证据后模型能否仍恢复出原先正确答案；在诊断不足后，作者提出REALHOP——一个 diagnose-construct-verify 框架：重新绑定实体、因式分解选定关系、添加完整的竞争路径并将证据放在可追踪位置；在结构和语义检查通过后冻结数据并进行行为干预测试。

Result: 在五个既有基准上，原始数据的面板平均BNR在16.6%到48.9%之间；在790个MuSiQue配对问题上，REALHOP将面板平均BNR从27.4%提升到94.4%，同时保持高的整体准确率；在REALHOP-FRAMES和REALHOP-LONGBENCH上也得到高BNR；在216个长上下文问题上，16个模型的多项选择区分度从13.9点增至59.2点，并在重复的开放式评估中保持。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：仅有概念上连贯的推理链和正确的最终答案不足以证明模型进行了每一步的多跳推理；必须通过行为级别的干预验证每个预期跳跃的必要性，才能可靠地评估多跳能力。

Abstract: Complex questions often require multi-hop reasoning that connects facts distributed across sources or distant regions of a long context through intermediate steps. Benchmarks commonly evaluate this ability with questions built around predefined reasoning chains, treating a correct answer as evidence that the intended composition was used. Yet answer correctness alone leaves open whether success depends on the evidence associated with each intended step: models may instead rely on memorized associations, shorter paths, or partial evidence. We examine this dependence using the Behavioral Necessity Rate (BNR), which measures how often targeted evidence removal prevents answer recovery on initially correct instances. Across five existing benchmarks, panel-mean BNR ranges from 16.6% to 48.9%, exposing a substantial gap between annotated structure and observed dependence. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce REALHOP, a diagnose-construct-verify framework that rebinds entities, factorizes selected relations, adds complete competing paths, and places evidence at traceable locations. Structural and semantic checks precede freezing; behavioral interventions follow. On 790 paired MuSiQue questions, REALHOP raises panel-mean BNR from 27.4% to 94.4% while retaining high Full accuracy. It also yields high BNR on REALHOP-FRAMES and REALHOP-LONGBENCH. On 216 long-context questions, the matched multiple-choice spread across 16 models grows from 13.9 to 59.2 points and persists under repeated open-ended evaluation. Together, these results show that a conceptually coherent chain and a correct final answer do not by themselves establish multi-hop reasoning. Verifying that success depends on every intended hop is therefore as fundamental to multi-hop evaluation as measuring answer accuracy itself.

</details>


### [145] [CF-LoRA: Decoupled Factor Aggregation and Adaptation-Aware Client Clustering for Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36986)
*Mengjun Yi,Langxing Yang,Suhan Guo,Furao Shen,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Decouple A/B aggregation and cluster clients by B similarity: preserves low-rank structure, reduces negative transfer, improves accuracy and maintains communication efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard federated LoRA averaging causes structural mismatch (breaking low-rank factorization) and statistical mismatch (single global adapter harms heterogeneous clients), so need aggregation that preserves low-rank structure and enables collaboration among similar clients.

Method: Learn a shared global A factor and retain personalized B_i factors; compute cosine similarity among B_i to form clusters; freeze A and perform intra-cluster aggregation of B factors; communicate one LoRA factor per round.

Result: On four language tasks and four vision datasets with RoBERTa and ViT, CF-LoRA achieves the highest average accuracy across modalities while communicating only one LoRA factor per optimization round.

Conclusion: CF-LoRA mitigates federated LoRA’s structural and statistical mismatches by decoupling LoRA factor aggregation and clustering clients by adaptation patterns, yielding improved accuracy and communication efficiency.

Abstract: Federated LoRA fine-tuning enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained models without sharing private data, but suffers from two fundamental mismatches under heterogeneous client data: a structural aggregation mismatch caused by independently averaging LoRA factors, and a statistical collaboration mismatch caused by enforcing a single global adapter across divergent clients. To address these issues, we propose CF-LoRA, a clustered federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that combines decoupled factor aggregation with adaptation-aware client clustering. CF-LoRA first learns a globally shared $A$ factor while retaining personalized $B_i$ factors, then identifies clients with similar adaptation patterns based on the cosine similarity of their learned $B_i$ factors, and finally performs intra-cluster $B$-factor aggregation with a frozen $A$ factor. By decoupling LoRA factor aggregation, CF-LoRA preserves the low-rank structure and mitigates the structural aggregation mismatch, while adaptation-aware clustering promotes collaboration among clients with similar adaptation patterns and reduces negative transfer caused by statistical heterogeneity. Experiments on four language tasks and four vision datasets with RoBERTa and ViT show that CF-LoRA achieves the highest average accuracy in both modalities while communicating only one LoRA factor per optimization round.

</details>


### [146] [ImbalancE: Inference-Time Latent Search Against Degree Imbalance in Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36996)
*Alberto Bernardi,Luca Costabello,Christophe Gueret*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 面向度数不平衡三元组的推理时潜在空间搜索可在不重训练的情况下显著改善KGE模型对关键角落案例的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到现有KGE模型的表示质量在图的不同区域差异很大，且这种差异与测试三元组中实体的度数不平衡高度相关：当目标实体度远小于锚实体度时，模型预测效果最差。这类不平衡三元组在推荐系统等应用中往往是关键但被忽略的角落案例，因此需要专门的修正策略。

Method: 提出一种推理时的潜在空间搜索优化方法：以预训练KGE模型为基础，在评估阶段对实体嵌入空间进行探索与优化，融合已知信息与带外（out-of-band）信息以缓解度数不平衡偏差，专门针对度数极端不平衡的三元组进行处理，提高目标实体的检索/排序概率。该方法为推理级别的优化，无需修改训练过程或模型参数。

Result: 在常用基准数据集的高度不平衡三元组上进行实验，所提出的推理时潜在搜索方法显著优于常规方法（基于作者描述），在这些关键角落案例上提升了预测质量，证明了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该论文结论是：在知识图嵌入（KGE）模型中，针对测试三元组中实体度数不平衡（尤其是目标实体度远小于锚实体度）会导致预测性能显著下降；通过在推理阶段对潜在空间进行搜索优化（inference-time latent search optimization），可以在不重新训练模型的前提下明显改善对这些高度不平衡三元组的预测效果，从而提高KGE模型在关键角落案例上的可用性。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embedding models have been extensively used to learn representations of entities and relations in Knowledge Graphs for predicting missing links. However, the quality of the learned representations varies a lot across different areas of the graph. If previous research has loosely linked the problem to relation types or degree bias, we show that it is more widespread and it correlates with the degree imbalance of the entities in test triples. In particular, the prediction of a target entity that has a degree much smaller than the degree of the anchor entity is extremely problematic. This is critical in recommender systems and other use cases, where these triples represent important corner cases. To address this issue, we propose an inference-time latent search optimization method capable of significantly improving model predictions on the most imbalanced triples. Built on top of a pre-trained model, it explores the embedding space at evaluation time, blending known and out-of-band information to mitigate the degree imbalance bias. We show the value of our approach on imbalanced triples from common benchmark datasets, where we outperform conventional methods, opening the door to the successful adoption of Knowledge Graph Embedding models on these critical corner cases.

</details>


### [147] [CADOC: Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37012)
*Junjie Yao,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CADOC: an online, cache-aware batching algorithm that compresses agent context into retrievable Cards by balancing waiting vs reconstruction cost, cutting input cost ~40% while retaining near-full-context performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long histories are a bottleneck for long-horizon agents: large prompts, degraded reasoning with growing history, and loss of cache reuse when editing history. Existing recovery/edit timing methods rely on forecasts or fixed intervals and can break cache reuse.

Method: Formulate replacement scheduling as an economic order quantity-style trade-off between accumulated waiting cost and shared cache reconstruction cost; implement an online batch-scheduling rule (CADOC) that selects integer batch sizes optimal under stationarity and performs batched card-replacements while maintaining retrievability of original contents.

Result: Across evaluations, CADOC achieves the lowest aggregate input cost among compared configurations, reducing input cost by ~40% on average while maintaining task performance close to using the full context; it recovers optimal integer batch sizes under stationary assumptions and demonstrates cost-aware scheduling improves compression effectiveness.

Conclusion: CADOC is an online scheduling algorithm that compresses long-horizon agent context by replacing structured objects with compact ‘Cards’ while preserving exact on-demand recovery; it schedules batched replacements by trading off waiting cost versus cache-reconstruction cost and empirically reduces input cost roughly 40% while keeping task performance near full-context.

Abstract: For a long-horizon agent, context is the bottleneck: the history is resent with every request, the window caps task length, and reasoning degrades as the history grows. Replacing structured objects with compact retrieval Cards shortens the prompt and keeps the exact originals retrievable, but editing the history can break prefix-cache reuse, and prior recoverable methods time their edits by forecasts of future reuse or by preset intervals. We propose CADOC (Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context), an online algorithm that replaces structured objects with compact Cards while preserving exact, on-demand retrieval of their original contents. CADOC schedules replacements in batches by balancing accumulated waiting cost against shared cache-reconstruction cost. Its scheduling rule follows from an economic order quantity trade-off, recovers the optimal integer batch under stationary assumptions. Across evaluation, CADOC consistently achieves the lowest aggregate input cost among the compared configurations, which reduces input cost by approximately 40\% on average while maintaining task performance close to full context. CADOC thus provides a cost-derived approach to compressible context management, demonstrating that efficient compression depends not only on shortening prompts but also on scheduling edits to preserve cache reuse.

</details>


### [148] [Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination for Hospital Patient Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37022)
*Guoqing Zhang,Rafik Hadfi,Takayuki Ito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将BCMP作为物理先验融入去中心化多智能体强化学习，通过局部动作指纹与空间奖励分解实现跨科室协作，有效降低医院系统延迟并兼顾可解释性与部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BCMP解析模型在状态依赖性动态和路由变化下失效，而集中化强化学习难以适应医院部门间权责分散、观测局部性与目标异质性的现实治理结构，因此需一个兼顾可解释性与分散决策能力的方法。

Method: 构建一个基于Dec-POMDP的去中心化多智能体框架“Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination”，将经校准的BCMP网络作为结构化先验，允许各部门智能体在耦合资源约束下协作进行患者路由与动态服务扩展；通过沿网络边交换局部动作指纹并采用空间分解的奖励函数，缓解环境非平稳性与通信开销。

Result: 在基于MIMIC-IV的真实病人轨迹驱动仿真中，所提方法在累计系统延迟指标上优于静态马尔可夫近似、若干启发式调度和独立多智能体方案，同时保持临床安全约束未被违反。

Conclusion: 将经验校准的BCMP排队拓扑作为物理先验嵌入去中心化多智能体强化学习框架，可在保留临床安全约束下显著降低系统总体延迟，相较于静态马尔可夫近似、启发式调度和独立多智能体基线具有优越性。

Abstract: Efficient patient flow coordination across autonomous hospital departments is critical for mitigating overcrowding and balancing resource utilization. While classical queueing theory, specifically open Baskett--Chandy--Muntz--Palacios (BCMP) networks, provides an interpretable mathematical topology for healthcare operations, analytical models rely on stationary assumptions and fixed routing matrices that degrade under state-dependent real-world dynamics. Conversely, centralized reinforcement learning approaches struggle to accommodate the decentralized structure of hospital governance, where individual clinical departments function with localized observations, heterogeneous resources, and divergent operational objectives. In this paper, we present a Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) framework titled \emph{Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination}, which embeds empirically calibrated BCMP queueing topologies as physical priors within a decentralized multi-agent reinforcement learning architecture. Formulated as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) under coupled resource constraints, our method enables autonomous departmental agents to cooperatively negotiate patient routing and dynamic service scaling. To mitigate environmental non-stationarity without inducing excessive communication overhead, agents exchange localized action fingerprints along network edges and optimize a spatially decomposed reward structure. Empirical evaluations driven by real-world MIMIC-IV patient trajectories indicate that this cooperative multi-agent approach substantially reduces cumulative system delay compared to static Markovian approximations, heuristic dispatching, and independent multi-agent baselines, while maintaining clinical safety constraints.

</details>


### [149] [Language as the Interface: Foundation-Model Contrastive Learning Links Transcriptomes and Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37024)
*Junbo Shen,Jinying Gao,Bo Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LangPatch uses language-based contrastive alignment on Patch-seq data to map GenePT transcriptomic embeddings to electrophysiological phenotypes, improving cross-modal prediction and transferability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Build multimodal foundation models that connect molecular (transcriptomic) and functional (electrophysiological) neuron representations; leverage Patch-seq paired measurements and language interfaces for flexible cross-modal alignment.

Method: Use paired Patch-seq gene-expression and intrinsic electrophysiology data. Gene descriptions and verbalized electrophysiological profiles are embedded by a single frozen text encoder; a context adapter and projection modules bridge modalities. Paired contrastive learning aligns GenePT representations with electrophysiology embeddings.

Result: Outperforms evaluated foundation-model and representation-learning baselines in transcriptome-to-electrophysiology correlation across mouse visual, mouse motor, and human cortex. Improves held-out alignment (FOSCTTM 0.107/0.135 vs 0.208/0.222 for JAMIE). Predicts family/type/layer/marker genes from electrophysiology and transfers to other areas/species with retained correlation (~70% to mouse motor, 47% to human cortex; 58% on acute-slice recordings).

Conclusion: LangPatch successfully aligns pretrained GenePT transcriptomic representations with electrophysiological phenotypes using a language-based contrastive learning framework, improving cross-modal prediction and showing cross-area and cross-species transfer.

Abstract: Integrating transcriptomic and electrophysiological data is essential for building multimodal foundation models for neuroscience. Patch-seq provides paired measurements of gene expression and intrinsic electrophysiology from the same neuron, establishing a basis for training cross-modal models. Here we introduce LangPatch, a foundation-model-based contrastive learning framework that uses paired Patch-seq data to align pretrained GenePT representations with electrophysiological phenotypes through a language-based interface. Gene descriptions and verbalized electrophysiological profiles are embedded by the same frozen text encoder. A context adapter and projection modules connect the modalities through paired contrastive learning. Across mouse visual, mouse motor, and human cortical cohorts, LangPatch achieves the highest mean transcriptome-to-electrophysiology prediction correlation among the evaluated foundation-model and representation-learning methods. It also improves held-out cross-modal alignment in the two mouse cohorts (FOSCTTM 0.107/0.135 vs. 0.208/0.222 for JAMIE, an existing cross-modal Patch-seq imputation method). It predicts transcriptomic family, type, cortical layer, and marker-gene expression from electrophysiology, exceeding other baselines on most endpoints. More importantly, the method transfers across brain areas and species: a model trained on mouse visual cortex predicts electrophysiology in motor cortex with approximately 70% correlation retention and in human cortex with 47% (58% on acute-slice recordings). Together, these results demonstrate alignment between molecular and functional representations of neurons, providing a building block for multimodal foundation models in neuroscience.

</details>


### [150] [AnyAct: Universal Action for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37025)
*Lingrui Xu,Yangqin Jiang,Jiachang Zhang,Xubin Ren,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnyAct is a reliability-aware, hierarchical action abstraction layer that unifies multi-modal observations and prunes large tool sets at test time, improving agent efficiency and robustness in dynamic, large-scale tool ecosystems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address three practical challenges for deployed LLM agents: (1) scale dilemma where huge tool sets exceed LLM context windows; (2) non-stationarity of tool quality due to updates/outages; (3) heterogeneity of feedback formats creating information silos.

Method: AnyAct builds a hierarchical progressive retrieval pipeline to filter task-relevant actions, applies test-time reliability evolution to prune unreliable actions, unifies multi-modal feedback via a heterogeneous observation grounding module, defines a hybrid action space combining primitive and semantic actions, and optimizes action selection for a trade-off between success rate and execution cost.

Result: Demonstrated state-of-the-art performance on LiveMCPBench and a newly introduced OSMCP benchmark; notable gains for models with limited native capabilities; on OSMCP achieved 77.27% overall success using 50 steps (roughly half the steps of competitors).

Conclusion: AnyAct proposes a unified, self-evolving action layer that constructs a filtered, reliability-aware action space and a heterogeneous observation grounding mechanism, claiming improved efficiency and robustness for LLM agents in large, dynamic tool ecosystems.

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) advance, AI agents are increasingly deployed in open-world environments to tackle complex sequential tasks (e.g., document processing, cross-application collaboration), relying heavily on actions ranging from GUI operations to semantic APIs. However, three core challenges persist: the "scale dilemma" of massive tool ecosystems exceeding LLM context windows, the "non-stationarity" of tool quality due to updates or outages, and the "heterogeneity" of feedback formats (pixels, text, structured data) creating information silos. To address these, we propose AnyAct, a universal action layer that unifies available capabilities into a self-evolving action space, enabling agents to operate efficiently and reliably in large-scale, dynamic tool ecosystems. AnyAct's core design focuses on two objectives: constructing this action space via hierarchical progressive retrieval (filtering task-relevant actions) and test-time reliability evolution (pruning unreliable actions), and enabling reliability-aware action orchestration through a heterogeneous observation grounding module that unifies multi-modal feedback. Additionally, it defines a hybrid action space (primitive + semantic actions) and optimizes for a balance between task success rate and execution cost. Evaluations on LiveMCPBench and OSMCP (a new benchmark we developed for multi-granularity action collaboration) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. AnyAct delivers substantial performance gains over baseline methods across various LLM base models on LiveMCPBench and improvements are particularly notable for models with constrained native capabilities. On OSMCP, it achieves 77.27% overall success with only 50 steps, which is half the steps required by most competitors.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Low-Rank Parameterization: Narrowing the Gap Between LoRA and Full Fine-Tuning via Gradient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37027)
*Yihao Ouyang,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Zhuoqi Hu,Jinglun Yu,Yichen Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用几何视角刻画LoRA可达梯度空间并做正交分解；GDLoRA提取并施加完整梯度中LoRA不可及的法向分量到基座权重上，改善了PEFT的性能而不增加优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA及其变体改善了低秩因子的初始化或优化，但其可达的一阶方向受限于当前参数化，因而与FFT仍有性能差距。目标是在不放弃PEFT内存优势的前提下，补偿LoRA不可及的梯度分量，从而提升性能。
