Method: Design a reward using solver-internal signals (branch-and-bound node counts and post-cut relaxation gaps) to score feasibility and hardness; employ an asymmetric challenger-solver self-play where an LLM generates instances and a solver verifies them; fine-tune Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3.5-4B with GRPO and a size curriculum to create OptiScribe-12B and OptiScribe-4B.

Result: OptiScribe models significantly increase solver difficulty on tested problems: OptiScribe-12B raises median SCIP nodes by 1.7–5× and post-cut gaps by 1.1–1.7×, improves feasibility rates by 9–19 points; OptiScribe-4B raises median nodes up to 15.6×. Generated instances span broader difficulty ranges and follow density/difficulty instructions.

Conclusion: Optimization-specific rewards in an asymmetric self-play setup enable LLMs to generate feasible and computationally hard MILP instances without requiring seed instances, producing benchmarks that expand difficulty ranges and can tune solver behavior.

Abstract: Generating optimization instances that are both feasible and computationally challenging is crucial for benchmarking solvers and training learning-based optimization algorithms. Existing non-LLM generators rely on seed instances or parameter tuning, resulting in high test-time computational cost, while existing LLM generators lack explicit hardness measures. Recent reinforcement learning methods with verifier feedback evaluate only binary correctness, which is misaligned with generating challenging problems. We note that an optimization solver reports the cost of solving at several stages of its pipeline, and leverage this to design a reward that scores both the solvability and the hardness of generated problems, measured by branch-and-bound nodes and post-cut relaxation gaps. Our key idea is a challenger-solver asymmetric self-play approach, where an LLM challenger generates progressively harder instances and the solver verifies feasibility and hardness, so no seed or training MILP instances are required. We fine-tune Gemma-4-12B and Qwen3.5-4B with GRPO and a size curriculum into OptiScribe-12B and OptiScribe-4B, which generate feasible yet challenging MILP problems from natural language instructions. On capacitated facility location and max-cut, OptiScribe-12B raises median SCIP search nodes by 1.7-5x and post-cut gaps by 1.1-1.7x over its base model and improves the feasibility rate on facility location by 9-19 points, while OptiScribe-4B raises median nodes by up to 15.6x. The problems cover a wider difficulty range than public benchmarks of the same size, follow instructions on density and difficulty, and can tune solver settings for families that public libraries lack. These results indicate that optimization-specific rewards, used in self-play mode, can teach LLMs to generate high-difficulty optimization benchmarks. We will release our code and models publicly on acceptance.

</details>


### [182] [Pretrain Once, Route Anywhere: Towards a Foundation Model for LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37362)
*Guannan Lai,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RouteFM 通过在异构环境上预训练一个基于行为上下文的路由器，实现了对匿名候选模型能力的推断与跨环境复用，显著提升了少量观测下的路由效果，支持“pretrain once, route anywhere”的路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器通常为特定查询工作负载和候选池进行本地拟合，需要在环境变化时重新监督或重训练。研究者希望构建一种可复用的、跨任务、跨候选模型和部署条件通用的路由能力，以提升质量-效率折中并减少重复训练成本。

Method: 通过情景式（episodic）预训练在异构路由环境上训练一个路由器，使其学会从候选模型的行为样本（匿名）中推断其面向特定任务的能力；训练后保持路由器参数冻结，仅用新的行为上下文适配不同环境，不依赖模型身份或额外监督。

Result: 在多种迁移场景下均表现出显著迁移能力。尤其在行为证据有限的情形下收益最大。在未见的 MMR-Bench 上，使用每个候选模型仅 8 个观测，RouteFM 比最强基线高出 2.23 个质量点。代码已公开。

Conclusion: RouteFM 提出了一种可迁移的 LLM 路由能力：通过行为上下文表征匿名候选模型并推断其面向目标的能力，从而实现“预训练一次、随处路由”的范式。相比于基于本地拟合的路由器，RouteFM 在不同域、模态、候选池和上下文预算变化下具有更好的泛化性与数据效率，在少量观测时收益最大。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing aims to assign each query to the most suitable model from a heterogeneous candidate pool, improving the quality--efficiency trade-off of LLM inference. Existing routers are typically learned through local fitting: a router is optimized for a particular query workload and candidate pool, and often requires additional supervision or retraining as the routing environment changes. We ask whether LLM routing can instead be approached from a foundation-model perspective, learning a reusable routing capability that generalizes across tasks, candidate models, and deployment conditions. To this end, we introduce RouteFM, which learns to characterize anonymous candidate models from behavioral context and infer their target-specific capabilities, rather than binding routing decisions to fixed model identities or a single environment. Through episodic pretraining across heterogeneous routing environments, this capability can be reused by a frozen router and adapted to new environments through context alone. Experiments demonstrate transfer across changes in domains, modalities, candidate pools, and context budgets, with the largest gains when behavioral evidence is limited. On MMR-Bench, which is excluded from pretraining, RouteFM outperforms the strongest baseline by 2.23 quality points with only eight observations per candidate. These results support moving LLM routing from repeated local fitting toward a pretrain once, route anywhere paradigm. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LAMDA-Model-Reuse/RouteFM.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Prompt Count: How Data Shapes Transfer in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37377)
*Jiaxuan Wang,Jiafei Lyu,Yuchen Cai,Siye Wu,Pengyuan Wang,Jiashun Liu,Xiang Cheng,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPD在样本效率上表现出色，但提示不是可互换的资源；提示选择的有效性高度依赖教师-学生配对和目标任务，随机采样常为稳健基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD以学生自身采样的反馈训练尚普遍采用，但尚不清楚提示的数量、来源与选择如何在不同教师-学生配对中影响知识迁移与性能，因此需要系统化研究提示对迁移效果的作用机制。

Method: 系统性实验：在多种教师-学生配对（包含RL和SFT继续训练对、跨模型情形）中，控制并比较提示(prompt)的数量、来源与选择策略；度量学习性能（例如数学成绩）并分析参数变化与功能(预测)对齐；对比少量提示与大池提示、不同来源提示的相对效果，以及选择/过滤策略和继续OPD的效果。

Result: 主要发现包括：1) 少量提示即可接近大池性能（例如4个DAPO提示可匹敌3,840个DeepMath提示的数学分数）；2) 提示效用依赖于教师——仅更换教师即可逆转不同类型提示（数学 vs 代码）的相对有效性；3) 功能对齐（预测变化）在不同提示支持与任务间波动，参数层面的对齐可弱于功能对齐；4) 在不利迁移后继续OPD可恢复性能；5) 有针对性的提示选择并不稳定优于均匀随机采样，且从源中过滤单独表现不佳的子集并不能保证持续改进。

Conclusion: On-policy distillation (OPD) exhibits high prompt efficiency but prompt utility is relational—dependent on the specific teacher-student pair—so prompts are not universally interchangeable. Continued OPD on effective supports can recover from poor transfer, and simple random sampling of prompts is often a strong baseline compared to targeted selection.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains students using teacher feedback on their own sampled responses, yet how prompt choice shapes transfer across teacher-student pairs remains poorly understood. We systematically study prompt quantity, source, and selection across RL- and SFT-continuation pairs and cross-model settings. We find that OPD can be highly prompt-efficient: a few prompts can approach large-pool performance, with four DAPO prompts matching the observed mathematics score of 3,840 DeepMath prompts. However, prompt utility is relational rather than intrinsic: changing only the teacher can reverse the relative effectiveness of mathematics and code prompts. To characterize these transfer differences, we analyze parameter and functional changes across prompt supports and model pairs. Functional alignment with the teacher varies across supports and target tasks; in continuation pairs, teacher-aligned prediction changes can coexist with weak parameter alignment. Continued OPD on effective supports can restore performance after unfavorable transfer. Finally, targeted selection does not consistently outperform uniform random sampling, and filtering out a source that performs poorly alone yields no consistent gain across three paired support draws. Overall, our results distinguish prompt efficiency from prompt interchangeability and show that effective data choice depends on the teacher-student pair and target capability, with random sampling providing a competitive baseline in the studied settings.

</details>


### [184] [Direct Experience World-Model Optimization: Learning the World Beyond Action Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37398)
*Xiangcheng Zhan,Zirui Chen,Yicheng Zhao,Ziteng Gao,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEWO在部署阶段通过对世界模型的视觉自我监督与进度驱动引导，提高了WAM在仿真与真实灵巧操控任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署后学习常只改进行为而不改进世界预测；在高维灵巧操作中，执行误差会累积并使交互偏离世界模型训练分布，阻碍策略优化。

Method: 在动作模仿的同时对世界表示进行视觉监督优化：识别交互转折点，利用成功与失败的未来视频进行训练以支持无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance），并增加一个从视频表示估计任务进展的value head，在推理时当进展停滞则激活引导。

Result: 在五个DexJoCo任务上对三种WAM格式均提高了平均成功率；消融显示来自成功与失败续帧的视觉监督优于仅有动作监督；在Wuji和Sharpa四个真实任务中，两轮部署学习使在有初始成功的格子单元的平均成功率从51.0%提升到71.7%。

Conclusion: DEWO通过在部署后利用视觉经验持续优化世界模型，从而提升动作生成质量，使世界建模成为WAM自适应的一部分。实验表明在仿真与真实环境中均能显著提高成功率。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with predictions of how physical interactions unfold. However, current post-deployment learning paradigms typically improve behavior without requiring better world predictions. Especially in dexterous manipulation, small execution errors can compound in high-dimensional action spaces, hindering policy improvement and pushing interactions beyond the world model's training distribution. Motivated by this, we propose Direct Experience World-Model Optimization (DEWO), a post-deployment learning paradigm for WAMs that, alongside action imitation, refines world representations through visual experience to better condition action generation. Specifically, it identifies interaction turning points and learns from successful and failed futures to support classifier-free guidance. An additional value head estimates task progress from video representations and activates guidance when progress stalls during inference. Across five DexJoCo tasks, DEWO improves average success across all three WAM formulations. Ablations show that visual supervision from successful and failed continuations improves both prediction and control beyond action supervision alone. On four real-world tasks across Wuji and Sharpa, 3 x 3 grid evaluations show that two rounds of deployment learning increase success from 51.0% to 71.7% in cells with at least one initial success, a gain of 20.7 percentage points. These findings support continued predictive learning for improving control through deployment experience, making world modeling an active part of WAM adaptation.

</details>


### [185] [Routing Should Pay for Itself: Sparse Supervision for Economical LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37402)
*Guannan Lai,Gelin Bian,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Long Chen,Jian-Dong Liu,Zhi-Hao Tan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 路由的稀疏监督方案，通过选择性采样与能力共享，在大幅减少监督开销的同时保持路由性能，显著改善经济回报曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 路由器需要在部署前对历史查询执行多候选模型以获得监督信号，这带来显著的前期成本；同时观测到路由性能在收集全部反馈之前即趋于饱和，提示存在过度监督的经济浪费。

Method: 提出一种稀疏监督框架（SaveRouter），通过有选择地获取信息性模型反馈并在相关查询之间共享模型能力信息，同时保留对单个查询的细粒度精调，从而减少需要执行的候选模型次数。评估时将监督开销与后续服务时间节省共同计入经济效益。

Result: 在四个路由基准上，SaveRouter 在主设定下只使用约33–41%的可用训练反馈即可维持或超越传统稠密监督路由质量，并相比最快的传统路由器将回本部署量缩小约1.9–9.5倍。此外发现最小化长期服务成本所需的监督力度与实现最早回本的监督力度可能并不相同。

Conclusion: SaveRouter 显著降低了为训练路由器而收集查询--模型质量反馈的监督成本，同时在路由质量上保持与甚至优于稠密监督方法的水平，从而在多项基准上将部署回本所需的查询量大幅缩小。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing reduces serving cost by assigning each query to an appropriate model while preserving response quality. Learning such a router, however, often requires executing multiple candidate models on historical queries to collect query--model quality feedback, creating a nontrivial supervision cost before deployment. Existing work largely focuses on serving-time efficiency, overlooking whether the resulting savings are sufficient to recover this upfront expenditure. We further observe that routing quality often saturates well before all query--model feedback is collected, suggesting that dense supervision can be economically over-provisioned. We propose SaveRouter, a sparse-supervision routing framework that selectively acquires informative model feedback and shares capability information across related queries, while retaining query-level refinement for fine-grained routing. We evaluate routing by jointly accounting for supervision expenditure and subsequent serving-time savings. Across four routing benchmarks, the main setting uses only about 33--41% of available training feedback while maintaining competitive or better routing quality, and reduces the break-even deployment volume by approximately 1.9--9.5 times compared with the fastest conventional router. Further analysis shows that acquiring more supervision is not always economically preferable: the supervision level that minimizes serving cost can differ from the one that achieves the earliest payback. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LAMDA-Model-Reuse/SaveRouter.

</details>


### [186] [Demistifying Data and Simulator Assumptions in Supervised Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37446)
*Pingchuan Ma,Rui Ding,Bojun Huang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文把监督因果发现方法按预测目标、编码器和训练范式系统化，强调区分模型内可识别性与训练后泛化，并建议用与可识别目标对应的评估与分布转移测试，同时要求对真实基准披露因果证据与不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督因果发现常用模拟器生成带标签训练集，模拟器同时提供监督信号并隐含关于因果图、生成机制和噪声的假设。由于观测数据往往兼容多种因果图，必须明确模拟器的假设如何补充或限制来自观测数据的信息，以便正确解释方法输出并评估其在部署时的可靠性。

Method: 对至2026年6月的代表性方法进行梳理与分类：按预测目标与粒度、编码器类型、结构解码器和训练范式组织方法，并基于该框架分析每类方法如何利用观测数据与模拟器监督来形成预测。通过理论/实验论证识别性与泛化的差异，并给出匹配评估与基准化的建议。

Result: 给出方法的分类体系；阐明“可识别性（在假设模型类内）”与“训练后泛化（跨分布转移）”是两个不同问题；证明机制和噪声的限制可以使模棱两可的方向可识别，但这类识别性不隐含分布外有效性；提出评估应匹配可识别的图目标并包含图/机制/噪声变化的稳健性测试；建议真实数据基准需记录外部证据与不确定性。

Conclusion: 监督因果发现高度依赖用于生成带标签训练集的模拟器假设；必须把“在假设模型类下可识别”与“训练的预测器能否超出训练分布泛化”区分开来。仅在受限机制/噪声下得到高精度并不能保证在机制或噪声改变时仍然有效。为此需按可识别的图目标选择评估指标，并通过在图、机制、噪声发生变化的情形下测试转移性与适应性；对真实数据的基准还需披露外部因果证据与参考图的不确定性。

Abstract: Supervised causal discovery learns to infer causal structure for a new dataset from training datasets paired with structural labels. These training pairs are typically simulated, making the simulator both a source of supervision and a carrier of assumptions about causal graphs, mechanisms, and noise. Understanding the resulting predictions therefore requires examining how these assumptions supplement the information available in observational data, which may be compatible with multiple causal graphs. This paper examines that relationship across representative methods available through June 2026. We organize these methods by prediction target, prediction granularity, encoder, structural decoder, and training regime to relate what each method predicts to how it uses data and simulator-based supervision. Using this framework, we distinguish two questions: whether the target is identifiable under the assumed model class, and whether a trained predictor generalizes beyond its training distribution. Restrictions on mechanisms and noise can make otherwise ambiguous causal directions identifiable, but predictive accuracy under those restrictions does not establish transfer when they change. This distinction motivates evaluation that matches metrics to the identifiable graph target and tests changes in graphs, mechanisms, and noise between training and deployment. Extending such evaluation to real data also requires documenting the external causal evidence and uncertainty behind benchmark reference graphs. Together, these analyses guide method comparison and identify open questions in transfer, test-time adaptation, and uncertainty assessment.

</details>


### [187] [Commitment Hierarchies under Intent Revision: A Belief-Revision Account of Salvage in Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37453)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把意图变更作为一次性全局分类并用确定性层传播决策，可在保证安全的前提下实现成本最优的子结果回收，实验证明比逐节点询问显著更好（节省43%，100%正确）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当用户在任务中途改变意图时，已付费并缓存的子结果应如何处置（保留/修补/丢弃）以避免浪费，同时保证答案反映新意图；现有按节点决策不可靠，需设计既安全又成本最优的策略。

Method: 将规划建模为承诺层级（commitment hierarchy），将意图变更建模为满足AGM公设的信念修正算子；对比两种角色设计——逐节点询问与单次全局分类+确定性传播；理论证明不可性定理；在三个环境上对两家供应商的三种模型进行实证评估，测量成本节省与正确率。

Result: 单次全局分类配合确定性传播在所有测试模型与环境中实现了成本最优（达成理论上可做到的全部节省），在实验中比重启便宜43%，并达成100%正确性；理论上证实任意仅观察局部视角的策略无法同时保证安全性与成本最优性。

Conclusion: 通过将意图变更判定为一次性、全局分类并用确定性层传播该决策，系统在三个不同模型和环境中均能达到成本最优策略并保证正确性，显著优于逐节点询问；理论上证明了仅凭局部视角不可同时满足安全性与成本最优性，而单次全局分类可满足二者。

Abstract: When a user changes their mind partway through a task, an agent that has already split the task into sub-goals and paid for tool calls must decide, per cached sub-result, whether to keep, patch, or discard it (salvage), restarting wastes valid work and continuing unchanged answers the old question. Our main finding is that salvage quality is a matter of role design rather than model capability: a language model asked the keep/patch/discard question one node at a time is unreliable, but asked to classify the revision once, with a deterministic layer propagating the decision, it reaches the cost-optimal oracle on all three models tested, from two vendors. Modeling the plan as a commitment hierarchy and the intent change as a belief-revision operator with AGM style postulates, we prove that no policy observing only a node's local view can be both safe and cost optimal, while the single classification design is both. Across three environments the policy recovers the full achievable savings, 43% cheaper than restart, at 100% correctness.

</details>


### [188] [Governing the Edge: Automating Commercial Property and Casualty Insurance Underwriting via a Hybrid Local-Cloud Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37454)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Gautam Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A privacy-aware multi-agent edge/cloud workflow that accelerates P&C underwriting, enforces deterministic compliance for hard stops, preserves data residency, and achieves 70% compliance accuracy on a small synthetic benchmark; further validation and robustness work needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Underwriters spend large fractions of time on administrative tasks; submissions are time-consuming (~40 minutes). The goal is to automate administrative layer processing without exfiltrating sensitive submission data, enforce compliance deterministically where possible, and provide auditability while speeding up throughput.

Method: Design of a 13-node LangGraph multi-agent workflow: 11 agents + 2 deterministic control nodes; on-device agents (Gemma 2) handle raw submission data via logged tool interfaces; anonymized scores/non-identifying fields are passed to Claude Sonnet in cloud; compliance encoded as conditions on graph edges; prototype uses synthetic tool stubs and datasets; evaluation on a 20-scenario benchmark and a detailed commercial-auto scenario.

Result: Prototype processes a manual 40-minute submission in a few minutes on a single edge device, deterministic enforcement of hard-stop rules with reproducible outcomes (temperature 0), overall compliance accuracy 70% on 20 scenarios with remaining errors concentrated in softer judgment tiers; full audit trail maintained; code and synthetic artifacts released.

Conclusion: Governing the Edge demonstrates a practical multi-agent, two-tier workflow that preserves data residency by running sensitive processing on-device and sending only anonymized outputs to the cloud; it reduces manual underwriting time substantially and enforces deterministic hard-stop compliance, but overall compliance accuracy (70%) leaves room for improvement in judgment-based tiers and the evaluation uses limited, synthetic scenarios.

Abstract: Underwriters in commercial Property and Casualty (P&C) insurance spend 30 to 40% of their time on administrative work rather than risk judgment, and a single submission takes about 40 minutes by hand. We present Governing the Edge, a multi-agent framework for that layer, organized around a data-residency constraint: sensitive submission data must not leave the perimeter. Eleven agents and two deterministic control nodes, one a human-escalation interrupt, form a 13-node LangGraph workflow across two tiers. Agents touching raw submissions run locally on Gemma 2, each bound through a tool interface logging every invocation (synthetic stubs in the current prototype); only anonymized scores and non-identifying fields cross to Claude Sonnet in the cloud. Compliance rules are encoded as conditions on graph edges, so a non-compliant submission never reaches pricing. We walk through one full scenario end to end, a commercial auto submission whose principal driver carries serious violations, showing every agent call, every tool invocation, and the resulting routing decision. The framework processes a 40-minute manual submission in a few minutes on a single edge device, dominated by serialized on-device inference. On the hard-stop violation tier the framework enforces every rule correctly and reproducibly, since hard stops are deterministic predicates over fields extracted at temperature 0; overall compliance accuracy across the 20-scenario benchmark is 70%, with the remaining gap concentrated in softer, judgment-based tiers. It keeps a complete audit trail and respects the privacy boundary throughout. We release all code, compliance rules, tool stubs, and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [189] [CRASM-Gate: Deterministic-First Constraint- and Role-Aware Semantic Mapping with Selective Model Assistance Across Heterogeneous Industrial Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37458)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在受控设置下，基于约束与角色的确定性方法已能高精度、低延迟地完成标准间语义映射，模型辅助作为受控且不可越权的补充价值有限但可测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业标准在层级、标识符、角色与结构约束上各异，简单的词汇或嵌入匹配常常在技术上不可接受，因此需要兼顾结构与角色约束的可验证映射策略，并将模型的生成能力限定在受控候选空间内。

Method: 提出CRASM——基于约束和角色感知的确定性语义映射框架，包含标准特定的规范化、有界检索、确定性规则、目标-源角色判定、语义与结构排名、歧义拒绝与目标验证；及其扩展CRASM-Gate，在置信/不一致门控下可调用候选受限的大模型，且最终仍由确定性验证决定。

Result: 在受控数据集（6个有向标准对、3个难度等级、10种配置，共14,400条样本）上：CRASM-Gate平均F1=0.9938、结构有效率=1.0000；确定性CRASM在无生成模型调用下F1=0.9931；模型单独基线F1=0.5347。相较基线，CRASM将top-1错误从670降至10；延迟方面CRASM为0.0688s/样本，模型单独为24.5263s/样本，CRASM-Gate为15.7666s/样本，且CRASM-Gate仅带来1个额外正确决定（每1,440条样本）。

Conclusion: 支持“确定性优先、模型辅助可选”的互操作性架构：以确定性规则和结构验证为最终权威，必要时通过受限候选项与置信/不一致门调用生成模型。

Abstract: Industrial standards often encode the same engineering concept through incompatible hierarchies, identifiers, roles, and structural constraints, so the nearest lexical or embedding match can still be technically inadmissible. This article presents CRASM, a deterministic constraint- and role-aware semantic mapping method, and CRASM-Gate, its selectively model-assisted extension. The framework separates standard-specific canonicalization, bounded retrieval, deterministic rules, destination-versus-origin role interpretation, semantic and structural ranking, ambiguity refusal, and target validation. CRASM-Gate adds a confidence/disagreement gate that may invoke a candidate-constrained large language model, while final authority remains with deterministic validation. A controlled artifact covers six directed industrial-standard pairs, three difficulty levels, and ten configurations, yielding 14,400 sample-level decisions. With a fixed local model endpoint, CRASM-Gate reaches mean F1 0.9938 and an implemented structural-validity rate of 1.0000; deterministic CRASM reaches 0.9931 without generative-model calls; and the model-only baseline reaches 0.5347. Relative to the model-only baseline, CRASM reduces top-1 errors from 670 to 10 while exhibiting 0.0688 s/sample rather than 24.5263 s/sample observed latency. CRASM-Gate improves CRASM by one additional correct decision out of 1,440, with observed latency increasing to 15.7666 s/sample. The results support a deterministic-first interoperability architecture in which model assistance is optional, measurable, candidate-bounded, and unable to bypass structural validation.

</details>


### [190] [Authority Before Utility: Non-Compensatory Control for Persistent LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37474)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在含有项级“忘记”标注的数据上，秩归一化的硬排除在防止已撤销/已删除信息泄露方面比基于固定惩罚的软控制显著更有效，且不损害当前值召回；证据来自 Qwen3-8B 的 185 个 heldout 单元，簇自助法给出稳定的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机来自持久记忆控制问题：检索相关性本身不足以处理记忆在更新/删除/撤销后仍然有用但已不应被纳入当前答复的情形，需要将“有用性”与“是否可被引用的权威性”分离，并寻求尺度不变的控制方法。

Method: 作者形式化区分“效用（utility）”与“权威（authority）”并证明：对未归一化分数施加固定有限惩罚无法在任意正仿射重参数化下保证排除，而秩归一化具有尺度不变性，因而可作为更稳健的比较器。实验上，原先的 TIDE/LongMemEval 比较被隔离，转而在 Memora Remembering 数据集上进行诊断性替代：用包含项级更新/删除状态的数据集，在 Qwen3-8B 上用 DEV 选择 lambda=0.6（十点归一化 SOFT 家族），在 28 个依赖簇的 185 个 HELDOUT 单元上比较 HARD 排除与锁定 SOFT；采用 20,000 次簇自助法计算 95% 置信区间。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上，HARD 排除的平衡构造错误率为 4.04%，而锁定 SOFT 为 19.66%，成对差异为 15.61 个百分点；20,000 次簇自助法的 95% 区间为 [13.07, 18.76]。错误主要由已忘记值的泄露驱动，同时保留了对当前值的召回。作者同时声明这只是同一问答操作下的对比证据，并非对标量控制普适性或学习型权威推断的评价，也不是对下游危害端点的独立评估。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：在需要排除不再可接受记忆的场景下，基于秩归一化的补偿（HARD 排除）比对未归一化实用性分数施加固定惩罚（SOFT）更鲁棒，能显著降低因“已忘记”值泄露而产生的错误。

Abstract: Persistent memory creates a control problem that retrieval relevance alone does not solve: a memory can remain highly useful after an update, deletion, or revocation makes it inadmissible for the current answer. We formalize this as a separation between utility and authority. A fixed finite penalty applied to an unnormalized utility score cannot guarantee exclusion under arbitrary positive-affine reparameterization of that score; by contrast, rank-normalized compensation is scale-invariant and therefore forms a stronger empirical comparator.
  Our prospectively frozen TIDE/LongMemEval primary was quarantined before a valid HELDOUT comparison because the materialized TIDE adapter conflated historical age with query-relative inadmissibility and the aligned LongMemEval split left no DEV set for the predeclared penalty selection. We therefore report a post-primary replacement diagnostic on Memora Remembering, where update/delete operations provide item-level forgetting state.
  On Qwen3-8B, DEV selected lambda = 0.6 from a ten-point normalized SOFT family. Across 185 HELDOUT units in 28 dependency clusters, HARD exclusion yields 4.04% balanced construct error versus 19.66% for locked SOFT, a paired difference of 15.61 points with a 20,000-replicate cluster-bootstrap 95% interval of [13.07, 18.76].
  The effect is driven primarily by forgotten-value leakage while current-value recall is preserved. This is same-Q operator-comparison evidence, not a universal claim that scalar control fails, not an evaluation of learned authority inference, and not an independent downstream-harm endpoint.

</details>


### [191] [Boundary-State Control for Tool-Using Language-Model Agents: Commit-Time Consistency under State Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37475)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BSC-R is an effective, deterministic commit-time boundary mechanism with strong results on in-domain and curated suites, but it fails on some broader attack sets; the claim should be scoped to commit-time consistency rather than universal agent safety.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agents can propose actions when a security-relevant state holds and then execute them later after that state has changed (proposal-to-commit gap). The goal is to prevent execution that would be unsafe at commit time while preserving benign behavior.

Method: Introduce BSC-R: a deterministic effect-boundary kernel that binds a single-use commit authorization to (1) the exact action and (2) a semantic projection of the authorization state that justified the proposal; accept/reject logic checks for exact match or identified classes of change/replay.

Result: On multiple benchmarks: preserves original agent behavior on AgentDojo (80.576% utility; 1.616% attack success), accepts all unchanged commits on 10,302 frozen proposals and rejects all ten specified changed/replayed classes; in boundary-drift test commits 0/4,403 invalid contexts and retains 5,899/5,899 valid contexts; on CONTINUITY suite retains 700 benign, prevents 1,200/1,200 represented non-replay invalid effects, handles 160 replay lifecycles, withholds 200 ambiguous cases. However, on a broader external attack suite BSC-R commits invalid effects in 25% of 2,560 attacks vs 0% for CONTINUITY, showing limits.

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates a scoped, deterministic commit-time consistency mechanism (BSC-R) that enforces single-use, action-bound commit authorizations tied to semantic projections of authorization state; it improves commit-time attack rejection in many evaluated suites but is not a universal safety solution.

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents can decide that an action is permissible and execute it only after security-relevant state has changed. We study this proposal-to-commit gap and introduce BSC-R, a deterministic effect-boundary mechanism that binds a single-use commit authorization to the exact action and to a semantic projection of the authorization state that justified it. On 2,847 attacked AgentDojo episodes, the boundary kernel preserves the unprotected agent's behavior exactly (80.576% utility; 1.616% attack success). On 10,302 frozen proposals, it accepts every unchanged commit and rejects every instance of ten prospectively specified changed or replayed classes. In an independently generated boundary-drift experiment, full joint binding commits 0/4,403 invalid contexts while retaining 5,899/5,899 valid contexts. A prospective external evaluation on the 3,460-scenario CONTINUITY suite retains all 700 benign cases, prevents 1,200/1,200 represented non-replay invalid effects, handles 160/160 replay lifecycles correctly, and withholds 200/200 ambiguous no-release cases. The broader external suite also exposes the method's limit: across all 2,560 attacks, BSC-R has a 25% invalid-effect commit rate versus 0% for CONTINUITY. The result is therefore a scoped commit-time consistency mechanism, not a universal agent-safety claim.

</details>


### [192] [SkillGym: Training Skill-Use Agents with Automatic Verifiable Environment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37539)
*Renxi Wang,Mingshan Hee,Fajri Koto,Timothy Baldwin,Haonan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGym 自动构建可验证环境并生成高质量轨迹，用以微调 LLM，使其能更可靠地识别与调用技能，进而在多个基准上提升表现，且小模型经过训练可在若干任务上超越更大的未训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代理范式广泛引入“技能”以解决长周期、复杂任务，但如何自动化构建可靠训练数据以及如何训练模型有效调用技能尚未充分研究。

Method: 构建 SkillGym：1) 爬取大量线上技能并筛选出可在离线可复现实行的技能；2) 采用 builder–reviewer 流程构造难度可控的四类任务，每个任务包含参考解法和可执行的验证器；3) 基于该流程构建 6.8k 个环境并收集 19k 条经验证的成功轨迹；4) 用这些轨迹对不同规模（2B–122B 参数）的 LLM 进行有监督微调并在四个技能使用基准上评估。

Result: 在 6.8k 个环境和 19k 条验证轨迹上微调后，各规模模型均有提升。Qwen3.5-9B SFT 在两个基准上优于未训练的 397B 模型。训练显著提升模型读取并调用相关技能的概率（从 28% 提升到 96%），并表现出跨推理结构、对训练集中少数任务类型及训练中未见技能的泛化。

Conclusion: SkillGym 提供了一个自动化、可验证的流水线，用以构建可复现的技能环境、采集经验证的成功轨迹并用于有监督微调，从而显著提升 LLM 代理的技能调用能力与任务完成率。

Abstract: Skills equip LLM agents with professional knowledge and guidance to complete long-horizon and complex tasks. Although skills have been widely adopted in recent agent paradigms and harnesses, how to synthesize reliable training data and how to train agents for skill use remain underexplored. In this work, we propose SkillGym, an automatic pipeline to build verifiable environments, collect trajectories, and train skill-use agents. SkillGym first crawls a large volume of skills from the internet, then keeps those whose workflows can run reproducibly offline. A builder-reviewer pipeline is used to construct difficulty-controlled tasks, spanning four task types, each with a reference solution and an executable verifier. With this pipeline, we build 6.8k environments and collect 19k verified successful trajectories for supervised finetuning. Finetuning on these trajectories improves LLMs of different families and sizes, from 2B to 122B parameters across four skill-use benchmarks; Our Qwen3.5-9B SFT model outperforms the 397B untrained model on two of them. Further analysis shows that training teaches agents to invoke skills, raising the rate of reading the relevant skill from 28% to 96%, and that the gains hold across reasoning structures, extending to task types that form a minority of the training data and to skills held out from training

</details>


### [193] [How Can Recommendation Feedback Evolve Agent Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37544)
*Shanwen Mao,Mingming Li,Hao Zhang,Zhiheng Li,Yige Wang,Penghua Yu,Junxiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIDE通过轨迹感知的责任与时序归因，把延迟推荐反馈用于记忆进化，显著提高记忆对未来任务的增益（MEG）并在离线/线上实验中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中印象、点击、转化和负反馈等真实结果信号虽可用于记忆进化，但存在延迟、噪声、多重记忆共同作用和受受众/投放策略混淆等归因困难，现有方法多依赖即时反馈或语义检索，难以稳定将推荐结果映射到记忆质量。

Method: 提出TIDE（Trajectory-Informed Directed Memory Evolution）框架，将记忆视为有容量约束的经验群体，利用时间和语义归因估计上下文适应度，责任归因将延迟结果信号分配到生成时引用的记忆，并以强化、交叉、变异和淘汰等进化操作更新记忆。引入Memory Evolution Gain（MEG）作为衡量进化后记忆相对于无记忆基线在严格未来任务上的效用提升的指标。

Result: 在电商会员营销内容生成任务上，TIDE在离线时序重放中实现+7.75个百分点的MEG，线上A/B测试显著提升独立点击率（UCTR）和激活率；在延迟标签基准上获得最低MAE和RMSE以及最高MEG，优于比较方法。

Conclusion: TIDE显著改善了基于延迟推荐反馈的外部记忆进化，使内容生成代理能在严格的未来任务上获得更高的记忆效用（MEG），并在离线和线上评估中均带来实质性提升。

Abstract: Content-generation agents continuously receive impressions, clicks, conversions, and negative feedback from recommendation systems, providing real-world outcome signals for memory evolution. However, these signals are delayed and noisy, confounded by audience composition, placement, and recommendation policies, and may result from the combined influence of multiple memories, making accurate attribution difficult. Existing methods rely primarily on immediate feedback or semantic retrieval and therefore struggle to reliably translate recommendation outcomes into memory fitness. To address this challenge, we propose TIDE (Trajectory-Informed Directed Memory Evolution), an external memory evolution framework driven by delayed recommendation feedback. We further introduce Memory Evolution Gain (MEG), which measures the utility improvement of evolved memory over a no memory baseline on strictly future tasks. TIDE treats memory as a capacity-constrained population of experiences: temporal and semantic credit assignment estimates contextual fitness, while responsibility credit distributes outcome signals according to the memories referenced during generation. These signals are then used to reinforce, crossover, mutate, or evict memories. On an e-commerce membership marketing content-generation agent, TIDE achieves a +7.75-percentage-point MEG in offline temporal replay and significantly improves both unique click-through rate (UCTR) and activation rate in an online A/B test. On a delayed-label benchmark, TIDE achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) and the highest MEG among the compared methods, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [194] [Rational Clarification by Assistive Agents via Value-of-Information Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37588)
*T. Duy Nguyen-Hien,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee,Tan Zhi-Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息价值推理的澄清决策框架 REVOIR，通过在推理时估计提问对下游回报的期望提升，有效权衡提问成本与执行收益，在多个任务上实现更高性能与更少提问，且能自适应考虑后续用户纠正的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言助理经常面对模糊请求；直接执行可能导致误对齐，而盲目提问会增加成本并影响用户体验。现有基于不确定性降低或信息增益的方法忽视了对下游任务回报的影响、提问代价以及用户在未被询问时可能的纠正行为，因此需要一种能在这些权衡间自适应决策的机制。

Method: 提出 REVOIR（Rational Enquiry via Value-of-Information Reasoning），在推理时计算每个候选澄清问题的期望信息价值，即收到答案后对下游任务回报的预期提升，并据此权衡提问成本与立即执行的收益，从而决定是否询问与询问哪个问题。

Result: 在两个任务（CondAmbigQA 和 ADAPT）上，REVOIR 在提问更少的情况下取得更高成功率：在 ADAPT 上，REVOIR 在无需训练的条件下，将偏好满足率提升 13–15% 相较于微调的澄清策略，同时提问次数减少约 5 倍。当允许在行动后获得廉价用户纠正时，REVOIR 会自适应地减少提问；而普通的推理型代理无法实现这种自适应，且随着推理投入增加反而减少澄清。

Conclusion: REVOIR 能在不额外训练的前提下，通过推理阶段的信息价值（value-of-information）评估来选择是否询问澄清问题，从而在保留用户偏好和任务回报的前提下减少不必要的提问并提高任务成功率。

Abstract: Users of language-based assistive agents often make ambiguous requests. In response, an assistant can either directly act on its interpretation of the request --- risking misalignment with the user --- or ask a clarifying question. Which option is the most safe and helpful? A common approach is to ask questions that minimize uncertainty about the user's intent until a threshold is reached. However, this neglects the impact of uncertainty reduction on downstream performance, the costs of asking versus acting immediately, and the possibility that users may provide corrections without being asked. To navigate these trade-offs, we introduce Rational Enquiry via Value-of-Information Reasoning (REVOIR). REVOIR makes clarification decisions via inference-time reasoning about the value-of-information of a question, which captures the expected improvement in task reward due to the answer received. In two assistive tasks --- ambiguous question answering (CondAmbigQA) and preference-aligned household task planning (ADAPT) --- we show that REVOIR achieves greater success with fewer questions than approaches based on prompting, chain-of-thought, fine-tuning, or information gain, improving preference satisfaction on ADAPT by 13-15% over a fine-tuned clarification policy while requiring no training and asking five times fewer questions. Furthermore, when the assistant can receive cheap user corrections after acting, REVOIR naturally infers that asking questions is not always efficient, demonstrating the adaptivity of our approach. In contrast, we find that vanilla reasoning agents fail to adaptively clarify user requests, and request fewer clarifications as reasoning effort increases.

</details>


### [195] [FOCUS: Training-Free Decision-Preserving Context Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37590)
*Shantanu Dixit,Anson Bastos,Xuchao Zhang,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOCUS 是一种无需训练、在测试时基于因果贡献选择保留历史交互的模块化上下文压缩方法，在多任务上同时提高效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互历史随任务长度线性增长，导致推理成本按二次方增长并因注意力稀释而损害性能；现有上下文压缩方法依赖离线对比、蒸馏或策略训练，昂贵且在测试时不可动态适配。

Method: 通过在测试时评估过去交互对未来决策的因果影响来选择保留单元，不需要离线数据收集或微调；实现为一个架构无关的压缩层，可插入闭环 API 模型和工具调用流程中以删除不重要历史。

Result: 在多种自治代理基准（API 与工具调用、问答、网络域、多轮对话）上达到新的最优，峰值上下文缩减最多 48%，依赖度减少 73%，任务成功率相较未压缩执行提升最多 8.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: FOCUS 提出了一种训练免费、仅在测试时运行的上下文压缩范式，将压缩问题重写为对离散交互单元的因果决策保留问题，从而以模块化方式附着于任意封闭 API 前沿模型，显著降低上下文规模并提升任务成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents accumulate interaction histories that grow linearly with task length, causing quadratic inference cost scaling and performance degradation from attention dilution. Existing context-compression methods learn what to discard offline: by contrastively optimizing guidelines, distilling compressors, or training compression policies. This incurs a substantial cost. Further, the compression policy is learned a priori and is not dynamically conditioned on the evolving test-time trajectories. In this paper we ask a complementary question: Which past interactions causally shape the agent's future decisions? We recast context compression as a causal decision preservation problem over discrete interaction units and introduce FOCUS, a training-free context compression framework that operates entirely at test time. Our method requires no offline data collection or fine-tuning, and is architecture-agnostic, attaching to any closed-API frontier model as a modular compression layer. We evaluate FOCUS on diverse agentic benchmarks including API and tool-calling, QA, web domain and multi-turn dialogue. Our method establishes new state of the art performance, cutting peak context by up to 48% and dependency by 73% while improving task success by up to 8.9 percentage points over uncompressed execution.

</details>


### [196] [XU-RS: Explaining Credal Width in Random-Set Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37594)
*David Achara,Maryam Sultana,Alexander D. Rast,Fabio Cuzzolin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 XU-RS，把 randomset 分类器的 credal width 用 Expected Gradients 归因到输入 token，上述归因在 MedQA（SmolLM3-3B、Llama-2-7B）上通过零掩码干预验证有效，但受归一化和数值问题影响，需要额外诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的不确定性分数只能告诉我们“有多不确定”，却不能说明“为什么不确定”。需要把不确定性和输入特征关联起来，以验证模型是否基于和任务相关的特征产生不确定性、并用于定位模型训练/数值问题。

Method: 在基于预训练语言模型的 randomset 分类器框架下，定义每个答案的上下概率区间并以二者之差（credal width）表示表征数据匮乏引起的认知不确定性。提出 XU-RS：用 Expected Gradients 对 credal width 进行特征归因，得到每个输入 token 对 credal width 的贡献得分；通过对排名靠前的 token 进行零掩码（将其 embedding 置零）并比对 credal width 变化来评估归因质量。按 MedQA 数据集在 SmolLM3-3B 和 Llama-2-7B 上进行实验。

Result: 在 MedQA 上的实验证明：对 XU-RS 排名前列的 token 执行零掩码会比随机掩码引起更大的 credal width 变化，支持 XU-RS 对不确定性的解释性贡献；此外发现归一化步骤会把其他答案组的概率信息混入某一答案的宽度，使归因结果可能受全局概率分配影响；还发现某些高归因 token 只是掩盖了数值不稳定或实现错误。论文给出若干诊断检查以区分有意义的语义归因与噪声/数值影响。

Conclusion: XU-RS 成功把随机集（randomset）分类器的答复不确定性（credal width）归因到输入 token，从而使模型对不确定性的解释变得可检验。实证结果表明，XU-RS 排名靠前的 token 在被零掩码后会显著改变 credal width，优于随机掩码；同时揭示了归一化项会引入其他答案组的影响、token 归因可能掩盖数值误差，并给出诊断检查方法。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimates tell us how unsure a model is, but not why. Without knowing which parts of an input influences a model's uncertainty, we cannot tell whether that uncertainty score depends on input features that are relevant for the task. We study this problem in randomset classifiers built using pretrained language models. These classifiers assign probability to individual answers and to groups of answers, producing lower and upper probabilities for each answer; The difference between these probabilities, called credal width, is used to represent epistemic uncertainty about an answer arising from limited training data. We propose XU-RS, a framework that attributes an answer's credal width to the input tokens (words or word pieces) supplied to a language model. XU-RS uses Expected Gradients (a standard feature attribution method) to estimate how input tokens contribute to credal width. The proposed framework is evaluated on a MedQA dataset using SmolLM3-3B and Llama-2-7B models, demonstrating that setting the embedding of a token ranked highly by XU-RS to zero (zero-masking) causes larger changes in credal width than zero-masking randomly selected tokens. In addition, we show that normalisation can cause other answer groups to influence an answer's width, reveal how token attribution can mask numerical errors, and provide diagnostic checks to verify whether a token ranked highly by XU-RS meaningfully explains model uncertainty.

</details>


### [197] [Flattening the Connectome Spectrum: A Spectral Filter for FC Induces a Pretraining Target for fMRI Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37642)
*Giovanni Marraffini,Victoria Shevchenko,Carlo Alberto Barbano,Demian Wassermann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对功能连接矩阵谱的重校准并据此进行自监督预训练，作者构建了一个轻量级的脑编码器，弥补并超越了KRR与现有BFM在表型预测与指纹识别上的差距，且模型更小、对短扫描和小队列更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自监督预训练在语言与视觉领域成功，但现有BFM在基于功能连接的表型预测上仍落后于简单的KRR。作者旨在理解并解决这一差距：识别KRR依赖的谱加权机制，并利用谱重校准来恢复被低估的个体信息，从而为更优的自监督表征学习提供监督信号。

Method: 首先分析KRR对FC的加权机制，发现其受FC特征值（谱）错配影响；提出一种高效的光谱滤波器，对每个被试的FC特征值进行重校准以增强可利用的个体间变异。基于重校准后的连接矩阵相似性，使用约4,000小时、来自162个公开fMRI数据集的分片进行自监督预训练：训练小型编码器使得片段嵌入间的两两相似度匹配重校准后的连接相似度。最后在5个数据集、11种分区和6个预测任务上与KRR及6个已发布BFM进行横向比较。

Result: 重校准光谱后，KRR性能得到提升，且在所测试的数据集、分区和任务上能匹配或超越原始KRR基线。基于重校准相似性的自监督预训练得到的小型编码器在与6个已发布BFM比较中表现相当或更好，参数量少一数量级；在短时扫描、样本量较少的队列和指纹识别任务中优于传统基于FC的方法。作者还开源了预训练权重、代码以及预处理并分区好的预训练数据。

Conclusion: 通过对功能连接矩阵（FC）特征值进行光谱重校准，并在此基础上通过自监督预训练对短时fMRI片段进行嵌入对齐，可以弥补并超越传统核岭回归（KRR）在个体表型预测任务上的优势。所提出的轻量级编码器在多数据集、多分区方案和多预测目标上达到或优于现有最佳脑基础模型（BFM），在短扫描与小样本队列上表现尤为突出，并在指纹识别任务中性能更优，同时模型参数规模显著更小。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining reshaped prediction in language and vision, and brain foundation models (BFMs) inherited its promise. Representations learned from large unlabelled corpora should capture individual functional dynamics and generalise across cohorts. However, kernel ridge regression (KRR) fitted on functional connectivity (FC) matrices still predicts individual phenotypes more accurately than any BFM we tested. In this paper, we show that KRR is weighted by the eigenvalues of the FC which are miscalibrated for phenotype prediction. We apply an efficient spectral filter to recalibrate the eigenvalues of each subject's FC matrix, enabling the model to exploit more inter-individual variance. Across the 5 datasets, 11 parcellations and 6 prediction targets we tested, we match or exceed the KRR baseline. Based on this finding, we then pretrain a small encoder model on about 4,000 hours of fMRI from 162 open datasets, whereby we align the pairwise similarities between the embeddings of recording snippets with those between the recalibrated connectomes. Our model performs on par with the best of the 6 published BFMs we tested while having an order of magnitude fewer parameters. Our encoder performs better than FC on short scans and in smaller cohorts, especially in fingerprinting. We release the pretrained model weights, the code and the pretraining data, preprocessed and parcellated.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond a single latent space: a dual-latent world model for long-horizon planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37644)
*Delin Zhao,Zhengrong Yue,Shaobin Zhuang,Junlin He,Xiaoyu Chen,Zikang Wang,Yuxin Liu,Limin Wang,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用分层潜在表征（低层短期/高层长期）加上带指数衰减权重的多时域自回归监督，可有效缓解递归误差与距离集中问题，从而提升长时域潜在规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规模型在短期预测上准确但难以做长时域规划：一方面递归展开会累积误差，另一方面高维潜在空间会发生距离集中使得目标间难以区分，因此需要不同的表示与动力学来分别承担局部执行与全局规划职责。

Method: 提出 Dual-Latent World Model：低层表示学习动作条件的短期转换，高层学习宏动作并在更长时间尺度上进行规划。引入 LoRe（Long-Horizon Representation Learning with Weighted Rollout）：对自生成的短期与长期预测施加监督，采用指数衰减的多时域权重（分别针对高/低层）以抑制误差传播和强化长期表征。规划时高层生成潜在子目标，低层将子目标细化为可执行动作。

Result: 在五个从零开始训练的目标导向视觉控制任务上，在不使用 actor 指导的情况下，Dual-WM 在步偏移 50 和 100 时的平均成功率分别从 75.9% 提升到 84.4% 和从 61.4% 提升到 69.5%。在偏移 100 时，Dual-WM 在所有五个任务上均优于最强基线，对比 LeWM 平均提高 30.8 个百分点。消融与分析显示更具判别力的目标评估表征与递归预测下更高的一致性。代码公开。

Conclusion: 通过在潜在空间中将短期执行与长期规划分离（低层动作条件动态与高层宏动作动态）并对自回归预测在多时域上进行有权重的监督，Dual-WM 与 LoRe 显著提高了长时域目标导向视觉控制任务的成功率，减缓了递归误差积累并提升了目标判别能力。

Abstract: Latent world models often struggle with long-horizon planning despite accurate short-term predictions. Recursive rollouts accumulate errors, while distance concentration in high-dimensional latent spaces can weaken goal discrimination. We introduce the Dual-Latent World Model (Dual-WM), which separates local execution and long-range planning through distinct state representations and dynamics models. The low-level model predicts action-conditioned transitions, while the high-level model uses learned macro-actions to plan over longer temporal spans. We also propose Long-Horizon Representation Learning with Weighted Rollout (LoRe), which supervises self-generated predictions at both levels. An analysis of recursive error propagation motivates exponential horizon weights with separate decay rates for the two temporal scales. During planning, the high-level model generates latent subgoals that the low-level model refines into actions for precise execution. We evaluate from-scratch Dual-WM on five goal-conditioned visual control tasks against the task-wise strongest baselines without actor-guided proposals. At goal offsets of 50 and 100 environment steps, mean success increases from 75.9% to 84.4% and from 61.4% to 69.5%, respectively. At offset 100, Dual-WM outperforms these baselines on all five tasks and improves mean success over LeWM by 30.8 percentage points. Ablations and supporting analyses provide evidence of more informative representations for goal evaluation and greater consistency under recursive prediction. These results highlight the value of separating temporal roles and training across multiple horizons for reliable latent planning. Our core implementation is available at https://github.com/DeLin1001/Dual-WM-Official.

</details>


### [199] [EnterpriseBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Enterprise-Level Strategic Reasoning and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37658)
*Min Yang,Yichen Pan,Jinghua Piao,Dandan Song,Yongshun Gong,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnterpriseBench通过统一套件与三类专业交互模拟，评估LLM代理在企业级长时与交互决策上的能力，结果显示现有方法仍不足以实现稳定与跨任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准侧重静态能力（信息抽取、数值计算、领域知识等），但企业应用常涉及信息缺失、不确定性、多轮反馈与长期权衡，需专门测试交互式与长时决策能力。

Method: 将现有企业与金融QA数据集重组为统一基础套件并按能力与难度标注；新增三类专业交互场景（Consulting、Beer Game、Enterprise Digital Twin）；在四种骨干模型上比较九种代理方法。

Result: 实验表明当前代理在多任务、长时序与交互式决策场景中表现不稳定；EnterpriseBench能作为实际企业战略推理与决策评估的实用基准。

Conclusion: EnterpriseBench填补了现有企业/金融基准在交互性与长时决策评估方面的空白，显示当前LLM代理在企业级情景下尚未实现稳定、跨任务的可靠性。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to support enterprise workflows, where tasks often involve missing information, uncertainty, feedback, and long-term trade-offs. However, existing enterprise and financial benchmarks mainly test static capabilities such as information extraction, numerical calculation, domain knowledge, and financial QA, leaving interactive and long-horizon decision-making underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce EnterpriseBench, a benchmark that evaluates LLM agents across this spectrum, from static question answering to dynamic decision-making. Specifically, EnterpriseBench reorganizes existing enterprise and financial QA datasets into a unified foundational suite annotated by capability and difficulty, and introduces three professional interactive settings: Consulting, based on management-consulting-style business cases for client problem diagnosis through multi-turn information seeking; the Beer Game, adapted from a classic supply-chain management simulation for inventory control under delayed feedback; and Enterprise Digital Twin, a project-based business simulator for workforce, risk, and project planning. Experiments with nine agent methods under four backbone models show that current agents have not yet achieved stable, comprehensive, and cross-task reliability in enterprise scenarios. These results show that EnterpriseBench provides a practical benchmark for evaluating LLM agents in realistic enterprise strategic reasoning and decision-making.

</details>


### [200] [MeanFlowAdvantage: Stable Reward Fine-Tuning for Few-Step Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37670)
*Haocheng Tang,Tianchi Xie,Xingqiao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A corrected advantage-weighted least-squares objective (MeanFlowAdvantage) that reconciles reward fine-tuning with MeanFlow’s average-velocity representation, empirically yielding strong gains in both image and DNA design tasks while retaining few-step inference efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow’s interval-average velocity parameterization is efficient for few-step generation but mismatches standard advantage-based reward fine-tuning objectives defined on instantaneous velocities or x0-space; this mismatch prevents direct transfer of reward improvements to the deployed average-velocity flow map.

Method: Introduce a signed advantage-weighted least-squares loss for average-velocity generators, using a shared, detached MeanFlow derivative correction to express reward objectives in prediction (x0-equivalent) space while making rollout and reference regularization exact penalties on the average-velocity network used at inference.

Result: On SD3.5-Medium, MeanFlowAdvantage improves all eight reported metrics compared to a matched four-step MeanFlowNFT baseline and, with four NFEs, matches or exceeds the 40-step DiffusionNFT baseline on six of eight metrics. The objective also applies to DNA promoter design, supporting teacher-free on-policy RL on a manifold and teacher-guided reward-graded distillation, with the latter achieving the lowest one-step Sei profile MSE among baselines.

Conclusion: MeanFlowAdvantage provides a principled advantage-weighted least-squares objective that aligns reward fine-tuning with MeanFlow’s interval-average velocity representation, enabling direct transfer of reward improvements to the deployed few-step sampler while preserving the original inference map.

Abstract: MeanFlow enables efficient few-step generation by predicting interval-average velocities, but this representation creates a mismatch for reward fine-tuning: existing advantage-based objectives are typically defined on instantaneous velocities or equivalent $x_0$-space predictions, whereas inference directly uses the learned average-velocity map. We introduce MeanFlowAdvantage, a signed advantage-weighted least-squares objective for average-velocity generators. Our key construction uses a shared, detached MeanFlow derivative correction to express the reward objective in prediction space while making rollout and reference regularization exact penalties on the average-velocity network deployed at inference. The resulting formulation preserves MeanFlow's native few-step sampler and provides a direct mechanism for transferring reward improvements to the deployed flow map. On SD3.5-Medium, MeanFlowAdvantage improves all eight reported metrics over the matched four-step MeanFlowNFT baseline and, with only four NFEs, matches or exceeds the 40-step DiffusionNFT baseline on six of eight metrics. The same objective also transfers to DNA promoter design, where it supports both teacher-free on-policy RL for a generator defined on a manifold and teacher-guided reward-graded distillation, with the latter yielding the lowest one-step Sei profile MSE among the compared configurations.

</details>


### [201] [KUPAS MASTER: Distilling the Tacit Expertise of Master Practitioners into Agent-Ready Experience Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37673)
*Changmian Wang,Yuchao Ma,Xuchao Lu,Chen Zhang,Ping Sun,Jiazheng Wang,Shan Wang,Xuanwen Chen,Yihe Sun,Ziyu Lu,Jianqiang Huang,Hongzhi Li,Ziqing Xia,Kaihua Tang,Xian-Sheng Hua,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个九层认知语料构建与六库资产管理的平台，将默会经验结构化为可调用技能，显著提升LLM代理在专业任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规工作记录缺乏对专家在判断与行动过程中的默会知识的保留，导致LLM代理无法充分利用专业经验；因此需要一种工程化平台把这些隐性知识结构化并与代理执行闭环连接。

Method: 通过构建“六要素”（上下文、线索、判断、行动、边界、结果）和‘九层’提取维度，把异构工作记录与访谈分解为可索引的经验记录，并将资产归入六类库（规则、约束、最佳实践、负例、角落情形、技能）；结合语义对齐、个体蒸馏、组织整合与交叉复审流程，最终封装为带明确输入、步骤、依赖与终止条件的可调用技能，并通过RAG等方法在LLM代理中运行与评估。

Result: 用20名随机选取的从业者授权样本（1,576个源文件）生成23,024条个人经验记录与13,113条组织级资产；在跨领域评测中，在同一任务输入与评分标准下，基础模型、原始语料RAG与KUPAS MASTER代理的得分分别为70.63、79.75、89.58，KUPAS MASTER在七个评分维度均优于原始RAG。

Conclusion: KUPAS MASTER将从个体实践中难以形式化的“默会经验”结构化为可追溯、可调用的技能资产，显著提高了基于LLM的任务执行质量，提供了从个人经验到组织知识与代理能力的实用路径。

Abstract: Experienced professionals know more than just facts and conclusions. They know which cues matter, why a judgment is reasonable, and which action to take. Routine work records often leave out this tacit knowledge, making it difficult for Large Language Model (LLM) agents to use professional experience effectively. We introduce KUPAS MASTER, an experience engineering platform built around nine-layer cognitive corpus construction. It turns heterogeneous work records and practitioner interviews into traceable, reusable experience corpora for agents. Six case elements preserve the task process: context, cues, judgment, action, boundaries, and outcomes. Nine-layer cognitive corpus construction organizes tacit experience along nine extraction dimensions and stores the resulting assets in six libraries: rules, constraints, best practices, negative examples, corner cases, and skills. Semantic alignment, individual experience distillation, organizational consolidation, and cross-review preserve source evidence, conditions of use, and unresolved disagreements. The platform packages these assets into callable skills with explicit inputs, steps, dependencies, and stopping conditions, connecting experience collection to task execution and evaluation feedback. Using authorized samples from 20 randomly selected practitioners, the platform processed 1,576 source files into 23,024 individual experience records and 13,113 organizational assets. The evaluation spans multiple professional domains. Under common task inputs and scoring criteria, the base model, raw corpus retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and KUPAS MASTER agent scored 70.63, 79.75, and 89.58, respectively. The KUPAS MASTER agent improved on raw-corpus RAG in all seven scoring dimensions. The platform provides a practical path from individual tacit experience to organizational knowledge and agent capabilities.

</details>


### [202] [Learning from Shared-Control Overrides: Context-Driven Acceleration Profile Prediction for Personalized Overtaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37684)
*Ruizheng Xu,Lounis Adouane,Javier Ibañez-Guzmán,Clément Zinoune*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用共享控制下的越权动作作为监督信号，采用聚类+分类+残差回归的混合框架实现情境驱动的个性化ACC，实验表明可更接近驾驶员期望并降低介入需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ACC为平均驾驶员标定，在临界超车等时序敏感场景与个体预期不匹配，驾驶员通过越权操作隐含反馈系统需学习个性化行为。

Method: 提出CoP-ACC混合流水线：1) 无监督分层聚类从越权事件提取代表性加速度轨迹；2) 上下文分类器将事前驾驶情境映射到合适轨迹；3) 残差回归器在所选轨迹上微调生成平滑个性化加速度曲线。

Result: 基于真实路测数据、与被保留的强制ACC基线比较，展示了高重构保真度和生成的加速度趋向驾驶员期望，有潜力减少人工干预、提高舒适性。

Conclusion: 将驾驶员对ACC的油门/加速越权视作监督信号，通过数据驱动方法可实现情境感知的个性化ACC，从而在潜在越权场景中生成更贴合驾驶员期望的加速度策略，降低人工干预并改善乘坐舒适性。

Abstract: Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) systems are typically calibrated for an average driver, often resulting in a mismatch between vehicle behavior and individual expectations during time-critical maneuvers such as highway overtaking. When the ACC is perceived as too conservative and inconsistent, drivers intervene through throttle overrides, providing implicit feedback on the system's behavior. This paper reframes these override actions as human-in-theloop supervisory signals and proposes a data-driven framework for personalized vehicle adaptation, termed Context-driven Personalized ACC (CoP-ACC). Rather than relying solely on end-to-end regression, which tends to over-smooth dynamic responses, we introduce a hybrid pipeline combining: (i) unsupervised hierarchical clustering to extract representative acceleration profiles from override events; (ii) a context classifier that maps pre-maneuver driving conditions to the appropriate profile; and (iii) a residual regressor that refines the selected profile into a smooth, personalized acceleration profile tailored to the immediate context. Evaluated on real-world public-road data against a withheld forced-ACC baseline, the approach demonstrates high reconstruction fidelity and generates acceleration profiles that tend toward the driver's expected behavior in potential override contexts. The results highlight the potential of learning from shared-control overrides to enable anticipatory, personalized ACC behavior, reducing manual interventions and improving ride comfort.

</details>


### [203] [EngiWorld: What Can Frontier Agents Deliver in Professional Engineering Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37686)
*Hongcheng Gao,Hailong Qu,Yu Lei,Henghui Sun,Haoyang Li,Yipeng Wei,Naihao Xue,Xiaohan Yu,Zhuo Tao,Yihe Zang,Yajiao Wang,Jingyi Tang,Yi Li,Jingjing Zhou,Jie Luo,Bohan Zeng,Chengyu Shen,Hao Jiang,Chong Chen,Bowen Qu,Olive Huang,Zeqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EngiWorld 提供首个覆盖完整设计闭环的工程软件基准与程序化制品验证方法，体现了当前模型在专业工程自动化任务上的明显不足，并为后续研究提供评测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管通用自动化代理在日常电脑使用上进展迅速，但专业工程设计仍难以实现可靠自动化；工程工作流程需要跨软件与设计阶段保持几何与物理约束与依赖，亟需一个能端到端评估代理在真实工程软件中表现的严谨基准。

Method: 构建包含1,301个专家策划任务的任务集，支持GUI与CLI操作接口，定义6类任务，从软件选择到开放式设计；引入制品中心的评价方法与统一域验证器，用程序化检查最终与中间制品的几何有效性、物理可行性与规则遵从性，并通过规格达成度对定量设计任务进行连续评分。

Result: 在七个先进模型上评估，最强模型仅获EngiScore 44.3，且仅有3.6%的多软件尝试成功，表明当前模型在工程软件端到端自动化方面能力仍有巨大差距。

Conclusion: EngiWorld 是一个面向工程软件端到端设计流程的首个综合基准，覆盖6个工程领域、26款专业软件与多种任务类型，通过基于制品的评价与统一的域验证器套件对几何与物理有效性进行程序化检查，揭示当前通用模型在工程自动化上的显著不足。

Abstract: Autonomous agents have made rapid progress in general-purpose computer use, but reliable automation of professional industrial engineering remains out of reach, as engineering workflows demand reasoning over geometric and physical constraints and dependencies preserved across software and design stages. We present EngiWorld, the first benchmark structured around the complete design loop: 1,301 expert-curated tasks spanning 6 engineering domains (CAD, CAE, CAM, BIM, EDA, and 3D visualization) and 26 professional software platforms, with both GUI and CLI interfaces and 6 task types ranging from software-selection to open-ended tasks. We further introduce an artifact-centric evaluation methodology built on a unified domain-verifier suite, which programmatically checks the geometric validity, physical feasibility, and rule compliance of final and intermediate artifacts, and scores quantitative design tasks continuously by specification attainment rather than binary success. Evaluation of seven frontier models reveals a substantial capability gap: the strongest model achieves an EngiScore of only 44.3, and just 3.6% of multi-software attempts succeed. EngiWorld provides the first rigorous foundation for measuring progress toward agents that operate professional engineering software end to end.

</details>


### [204] [WISE-ATTA: When to Ask for Labels in Budgeted Active Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37687)
*Muhammad Huzaifa,Lea Schönherr,Thorsten Eisenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出预算化主动测试时自适应（budgeted ATTA）并设计WISE-ATTA，通过在线轻量信号做出“何时标注”的决策并结合漂移驱动的样本选择，在节省大量标注的同时保持或提升适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动测试时自适应方法通常隐含假设能对每个输入批次请求监督，这在长时序测试流中会造成高昂注释成本。将可用标注限制为测试批次的一小部分，关键挑战从“批内选什么标注”转为“何时应用监督”，以在预算约束下最大化适应收益。

Method: 提出“预算化ATTA”问题设定，并设计WISE-ATTA：1) 在线计算轻量级信号以估计当前批次监督的潜在收益，从而在测试流上按优先级分配有限的标签预算（决定何时请求标签）；2) 在选择批次进行监督时，采用基于适应漂移的样本选择准则，挑出表现出未收敛适应动力学的样本，以便用单个标注样例进行高效更新。

Result: 在合成扰动（ImageNet-C）和自然分布移位（ImageNet-R/K/A）上评估，WISE-ATTA在多种设定下以显著更少的标注量实现了与或优于近期ATTA方法的性能，表明在预算受限场景中按时机分配监督是有效的策略。

Conclusion: 在有预算限制的在线测试场景中，监督时机比批内样本选择更为关键。WISE-ATTA通过基于轻量级在线信号的监督分配和基于漂移的样本选择，在使用显著更少标签的情况下，能够达到或超越现有主动测试时自适应方法的性能。

Abstract: Active test-time adaptation (ATTA) improves robustness under distribution shift by updating a deployed model during inference while selectively querying supervision. However, most existing ATTA methods implicitly assume that supervision can be requested for every incoming test batch, which can incur substantial annotation cost over long test streams. In this work, we introduce \emph{budgeted ATTA} in which labels are available for only a fraction of test batches. This formulation shifts the central challenge from deciding \emph{what} to label within a batch to deciding \emph{when} supervision should be applied over time. To address this challenge, we propose a budget-aware approach \emph{WISE-ATTA} that allocates supervision over the test stream based on lightweight signals computed online, prioritizing periods where supervision is likely to be most useful. When a batch is selected for supervision, we further employ a drift-based sample selection criterion that targets samples exhibiting ongoing, unconverged adaptation dynamics, enabling effective updates from a single labeled example. We evaluate this approach on synthetic corruptions (ImageNet-C) and natural distribution shifts (ImageNet-R/K/A). Across settings, WISE-ATTA achieves competitive or improved performance compared to recent ATTA methods while requiring substantially fewer labels. Overall, we find that the timing of supervision is a key, yet underexplored, aspect of active test-time adaptation. Code: https://github.com/Muhammad-Huzaifaa/WISE-ATTA

</details>


### [205] [Locating Answer-Correctness Signals in Frozen Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37700)
*Yuansen Liu,Yixuan Tang,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在单次前向传播上系统搜索并融合多类内部读出，作者定位出答案正确性主要集中在答案标记并证明多信号融合能提高鲁棒性与下游检索控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探测器常局限于单一信号族或层，且在分布转移或检索增强场景下脆弱；需要回答“正确性在何处可读”“哪类内部信号携带正确性”“如何组合这些信号”的问题。

Method: 在冻结模型的单次前向传播中，搜索并比较不同信号族（隐藏态、标记概率、残差流特征、注意力）及其融合，分别在闭卷与有上下文检索场景下评估，并将选定读出视为预测器而非机械定位。

Result: 发现正确性信息集中在答案跨度内，可从答案标记中恢复，且各信号族提供互补信息；在分布外情形下融合多信号最有效；该协议在两个不同主干模型上均有效，并能用于检索控制器的上游判定。

Conclusion: 提出了一种系统性协议，用作‘预测性测量’来定位与答案正确性相关的内部信号，并证明通过融合多种信号可提升在分布转移下的判别性能。

Abstract: Language models expose internal signals that predict whether an answer is correct, readable from a single forward pass of a frozen model without additional generations. Yet existing probes often commit to one signal family or layer and can be brittle under distribution shift; in retrieval-augmented settings, many specialized detectors instead target passage faithfulness, which can diverge from correctness when retrieved evidence is unhelpful or conflicting. We therefore ask where answer correctness is readable, which internal signal families carry it, and how they should be combined. We search over hidden states, token probabilities, residual-stream features, attention, and their fusion, treating the selected readouts as a predictive measurement rather than a mechanistic localization. We run this analysis separately in closed-book and with-context settings, since context can change which readouts are informative. A consistent anatomy emerges: correctness concentrates in the answer span, recovered from the answer tokens even under retrieval, and the families carry it complementarily, so fusing them helps most out of distribution, where a single signal is weakest. The protocol is effective across two backbones and gates a retrieval controller as one downstream use.

</details>


### [206] [Generative Interactions: Weaving Multiparty Human Motion with Bilevel Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37708)
*Ojas Shirekar,Yash Surange,Agustinas Jučas,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种层次潜变量模型（BRAID），显式分离组级与个体级表征，实现了跨数据集、跨观测条件的多人社交动作生成与适配，并在多项任务与度量上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交运动模型多数只生成合理轨迹但未显式建模交互状态，限制了跨群体、跨任务及部分观测场景的泛化与可控性；需要一个能显性表示交互状态、支持不同观测稀疏度并便于下游使用的模型。

Method: 提出 Bilevel Representations for Agent Interaction Dynamics (BRAID)，使用组级潜变量表示共享交互动力学，个体级潜变量表示基于演化组上下文的个人行为；将社交运动生成视作元迁移学习问题，从多个数据集中学习共享交互先验并通过任意上下文集进行适配；在统一的 SMPL 表示下评估多项任务（预测、跟踪、补全、响应生成），并用重建、真实感、多样性、时序对齐与人际协调度量进行评估。

Result: BRAID 在统一表示下的多任务评估中表现出能够在不同观测条件下生成连贯、多样且具协调性的社交动作；层次化潜空间可分离捕获群体与个体结构，社交状态向量对下游体感代理系统有实用价值。

Conclusion: BRAID 提出了一种分层序列潜变量模型，通过显式建模群体级与个体级潜变量，实现了对多人社交动作的生成与迁移学习，能在全观测、稀疏或部分观测下协调生成并提供紧凑的社交状态向量供下游系统使用。

Abstract: Human social behaviour is not a collection of independent motions, but a jointly organised process in which group dynamics and individual variation continuously shape one another. Yet existing social motion models often prioritise plausible trajectories while leaving interaction state implicit, limiting their ability to transfer across groups, tasks, and partial-observation regimes. To address this gap, we introduce Bilevel Representations for Agent Interaction Dynamics (BRAID), a hierarchical sequential latent-variable model for generative multi-person interaction. BRAID explicitly formulates social motion generation as a meta-transfer learning problem: shared interaction priors are learned across datasets and adapted through arbitrary context sets of observed people and joints. The model represents each scene through a group-level latent state that captures shared interaction dynamics and person-level latent states that capture individual behaviour conditioned on the evolving group context. This modelling choice enables coherent generation under full, sparse, or partial observations while exposing compact social-state vectors that can serve as an interface for downstream embodied-agent systems. We evaluate BRAID under a unified SMPL-based representation on social forecasting, tracking and in-filling, and response generation, using metrics that assess not only reconstruction accuracy but also realism, diversity, temporal alignment, and interpersonal coordination. We further analyse the hierarchical latent space, showing that it captures separable group- and individual-level structure.

</details>


### [207] [Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37725)
*Rulin Shao,Shannon Zejiang Shen,Junjie Oscar Yin,Yuetai Li,Minheng Wang,Hamish Ivison,Radha Poovendran,Nathan Lambert,Teng Xiao,Mike Lewis,Wen-tau Yih,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLM通过把上下文当成可写文件并让模型自主维护，实现了更高效的上下文管理、可学习的策略以及明显的性能与计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有由外部控制器管理上下文的方法在长期运行、多代理或资源受限场景下效率低下且不易扩展，故将上下文管理内置于模型以提升重要信息保持、减少带宽/计算开销并支持模型级的策略学习。

Method: 将上下文抽象为文件并赋予模型对该文件的不受限更新能力；以现有大模型零样本构建CLM；在多代理场景中为各代理维护独立上下文文件；通过自然语言指令与技能优化循环对CLM进行引导；引入在线强化学习以优化行为策略；联合设计后端的Suffix Cache Reuse以减少服务端重复计算。

Result: 在多项基准上优于现有上下文管理策略：在BrowseComp-Plus上准确率提升11.4%且FLOPs降低21.5%；在12小时EdgeBench上得分提升5%且FLOPs降低59%；在24小时多仓库代理群任务上以相同算力获得65%的相对提升；通过技能优化循环在某上下文管理任务上提升最多35.9点；在线RL使Qwen3.5-9B在BrowseComp-Plus上性能提升47.6%且FLOPs减少12%；Suffix Cache Reuse使服务端计算在相同性能下减少35%。

Conclusion: 将上下文视为可编辑的文件，让语言模型自主维护和更新上下文，能显著提升多任务和多代理场景下的性能与计算效率，同时自然支持基于指令的调控与在线强化学习优化，并能通过服务端缓存策略进一步降低推理成本。

Abstract: We introduce Context Language Models (CLMs), language models that natively manage their own context. We implement this by treating the context as a file and allowing the model to make unrestricted updates to this file. This allows the model to learn what is most important to maintain in context, and naturally extends to multi-agent systems where multiple agent contexts coexist as files. Building CLMs zero-shot with existing models outperforms SOTA context management strategies across a variety of tasks: 11.4% higher accuracy with 21.5% fewer FLOPs on BrowseComp-Plus, 5% higher scores with 59% fewer FLOPs on 12-hour EdgeBench, and 65% greater improvement with the same compute on a 24-hour multi-repository agent-swarm task. Moreover, by shifting context management from external harness control to intrinsic model behavior, CLMs naturally enable both in-context and parametric learning of context-management strategies. We show that CLMs can be steered with natural-language instructions evolved through a standard skill-optimization loop, improving held-out accuracy by up to 35.9 points on a context-management task while reducing compute. We also introduce an online reinforcement learning method for CLMs, improving Qwen3.5-9B performance on BrowseComp-Plus by 47.6% while using 12% fewer FLOPs. Finally, we co-design Suffix Cache Reuse for CLM serving, further reducing server-side compute by 35% relative to standard SGLang at matched performance.

</details>


### [208] [Spatiotemporal Hyperedges for EEG Seizure Detection and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37730)
*Hyunju Kim,Sheo Yon Jhin,Noseong Park,Nabil Imam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用少量软超边代替两两边，HyBrain以更紧凑的时空表征提升EEG发作检测与预测性能，同时保持效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作稀少、时空局部且为多通道协同事件，传统逐对通道边建模既遗漏重要的时空耦合又训练成本高。

Method: 每个通道每秒由Mamba骨干网络生成一个token；一个时空超边模块通过软隶属关系将这些token汇聚为E_h个共享组嵌入并回广播；同一编码器用于窗口检测、逐秒检测与发作前预测三项下游任务。

Result: 在TUSZ与CHB‑MIT上对十个基线方法进行对比，在所有报告配置下AUROC最高，长片段发作前预测上优势最大；训练时长与峰值GPU内存与最优高效基线相当；单个超边可定性地覆盖preictal→ictal→postictal轨迹。

Conclusion: 提出的HyBrain通过软超边（soft hyperedges）在时空上汇总EEG证据，显著优于基于时序逐时刻两两通道边的动态图神经网络，在多个数据集和任务上取得最优AUROC并保持训练效率。

Abstract: Seizure detection and prediction from EEG are clinically important but challenging because seizures are rare, temporally localized, and propagate as coordinated events across multiple channels. Recent dynamic graph neural networks model this by running a temporal model over a sequence of per-time-step pairwise channel edges. However, this pairwise construction misses the spatiotemporal coupling that constitutes a seizure, at substantial training cost. We propose HyBrain, which summarizes spatiotemporal EEG evidence through a small set of soft hyperedges rather than pairwise edges. A per-channel Mamba backbone produces one token per (channel, second), and a spatiotemporal hyperedge block pools these tokens into E_h shared group embeddings through soft memberships and broadcasts them back. The same encoder serves three downstream tasks: window-based detection, one-second point-wise detection, and preictal seizure prediction. On TUSZ and CHB-MIT, HyBrain achieves the best AUROC on every reported setting against ten baselines, with the largest gap on long-clip preictal prediction. It also matches the most efficient baselines in training time and peak GPU memory. A qualitative analysis shows that even a single learned hyperedge cleanly captures the preictal -> ictal -> postictal trajectory on a real seizure clip.

</details>


### [209] [ContextRender: From Execution Dependencies to Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37743)
*Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka L. Dantanarayana,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过执行依赖图和“观察到的重用”信号，ContextRender在有限上下文预算下更聪明地选择历史工具结果，既保留未来可能需要的信息又显著降低推理成本，实验证明优于若干基线并接近或超越使用完整历史的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，代理会积累大量工具输出，始终传递全部历史既昂贵又不必要；现有上下文管理方法易忽视早期工具结果在后续执行中的实际使用情况，导致必要信息被遗漏或浪费上下文空间。

Method: 提出ContextRender：维护一个执行依赖图来持久化工具调用的关系；用Tool-Flow Analysis追踪后续操作如何重用早期工具结果，计算“观察到的重用”信号；设计渲染器将该信号与时间近因性和语义相关性结合，在固定的历史预算内选择要加入上下文的结果，并对被省略的结果进行保留以备后用。

Result: 在AppWorld和含8个目标的问答集上、用三种执行模型评估，在6K的历史预算下，ContextRender优于比较的上下文管理基线，性能接近或超过传递完整历史的效果，并将平均推理成本相对于Full history减少10.2%–32.2%；消融实验显示“观察到的重用”提升了任务表现并促成更多被后续重用的结果得以保留。

Conclusion: ContextRender通过维护执行依赖的持久图并基于“观察到的重用”信号选择历史结果，能够在受限的上下文预算下保留未来步骤可能需要的信息，从而在大幅降低推理成本的同时保持或接近使用完整历史的任务性能。

Abstract: LLM agents performing long-horizon tasks accumulate tool results that later steps may need. Passing the full history to every invocation is costly even when it fits within the context window, while reducing it risks omitting needed information. Existing context management methods can overlook how earlier tool results are used in subsequent execution, leaving needed information out of context. We introduce ContextRender, which manages context through a persistent graph of execution dependencies. We develop Tool-Flow Analysis to track how later operations reuse information from earlier tool results, providing a signal called observed reuse. A renderer combines this signal with recency and semantic relevance to select results within a fixed history budget, retaining omitted results for later use. Across AppWorld and 8-objective QA with three execution models, ContextRender outperforms the evaluated context management baselines using a 6K history budget, well below the models' maximum context windows. Within this budget, it achieves task performance close to or above that of passing the full history while reducing mean inference cost by 10.2%-32.2% relative to Full history. Ablations show that observed reuse improves task performance and retention of results reused later.

</details>


### [210] [Cross-Entropy Guided Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37751)
*Yury Nahshan,Nati Daniel,Jacob Goldberger,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在路由层引入token级错误监督（两种实现），能在保持稀疏计算不变的前提下稳定提升多项选择QA任务的准确率，使用Itakura--Saito或指数NLL对齐亲和度与误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的MoE路由器通过负载均衡与语言模型损失学习亲和度，但这些目标未直接把路由亲和度与token级别的预测误差对齐，导致路由并非最优地分配困难样本给合适专家。

Method: 提出两种token-error监督：1) 为每个专家预测误差分数，使用亲和度加权的误差总和与下一个token交叉熵对齐，并在top-K选择前用个体误差分数衰减亲和度；2) 直接将路由器的亲和度与模型目标对齐，无需额外head或推理时修改。两者使用Itakura--Saito散度或指数负对数似然来匹配亲和度与token误差。

Result: 在两种稀疏MoE骨干与四个多项选择QA基准上进行了评估：在Granite上平均提升≈2.3个百分点；在ARC-Challenge上使用更强监督时提升达2.94个百分点。保持原生稀疏预算与聚合策略，代码随附。

Conclusion: 在稀疏MoE中引入基于token误差的路由监督可以显著提升下游任务性能，同时保持原有稀疏执行预算与聚合策略。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) large language models scale model capacity by routing each token to a small subset of experts. Their routers are regularized with load balancing terms and learn affinity scores through the language-model objective. However, these objectives do not provide direct alignment between routing affinities and token-level error. We introduce token-error supervision for sparse routing in two forms. The first form predicts an error score per expert. The affinity-weighted aggregate of these scores is aligned to the next-token cross-entropy loss, while the individual scores attenuate affinity before top-$K$ selection. The second directly aligns the router's affinities to the model's objective without requiring an additional head or inference-time modification. Both formulations use the Itakura--Saito divergence or an exponential negative log-likelihood for aligning affinities and token errors. Across two sparse MoE backbones and four multiple-choice question-answering benchmarks, we evaluate both supervision mechanisms. On Granite, our method improves accuracy by approximately 2.3 percentage points on average over a parameter-matched routing baseline. With stronger supervision, the gain on ARC-Challenge reaches 2.94 points. Both mechanisms preserve the native sparse execution budget and aggregation policy. Our code is available in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [211] [OmniVCBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning Towards AI Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37773)
*Manyu Li,Xunkai Li,Yongfu Xiong,Yi Liu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniVCBench is a figure-focused benchmark and judge framework for evaluating AIVC interpretation abilities (6k QA pairs, MLLM judge, hard-negative MCQ mining), showing complementary MCQ and open-response evaluation and promising correlation between them.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing AIVC benchmarks focus on simulation outputs; the work targets the understudied interpretation layer—how models read figures, trace evidence to sources, explain mechanisms, and formulate hypotheses.

Method: Curated 6,077 single- and multi-subfigure QA pairs from scientific figures and contextual text; designed three interpretation tasks mapped to Predict–Explain–Discover guided by Bloom’s taxonomy; implemented AIVC-Judge, a task-conditioned MLLM-as-judge with category-specific, reference-aware rubrics; used Model-Derived Hard-Negative Mining to convert model inference errors into MCQ distractors; evaluated heterogeneous model pool with both open responses and MCQs.

Result: Released OmniVCBench dataset and evaluation pipeline; demonstrated feasibility of MLLM-based judging and MDHNM for lower-cost MCQ creation; observed positive correlation between MCQ accuracy and AIVC-Judge scores across models; provided code and data demo.

Conclusion: OmniVCBench provides a novel, figure-centric benchmark and evaluation framework targeting the interpretation component of Artificial Intelligence Virtual Cells (AIVCs), coupling a curated QA dataset with a judge framework (AIVC-Judge) and a hard-negative mining strategy (MDHNM). It shows that MCQ performance correlates with reference-aware open-response judgments, offering complementary evaluation modes.

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence Virtual Cells (AIVCs) are envisioned as scientific agents that simulate cellular responses, explain underlying mechanisms, and support hypothesis-driven discovery. Existing AIVC benchmarks, however, operate primarily at the simulation layer, motivating complementary evaluation of how models interpret experimental evidence and formulate biological hypotheses. We introduce OmniVCBench, a figure-centric, source-traceable benchmark for the interpretation component of an AIVC. It contains 6,077 curated single- and multi-subfigure question--answer pairs derived from figures and experimental contexts in the scientific literature. Guided by Bloom's taxonomy, we instantiate interpretation-layer counterparts of the AIVC Predict--Explain--Discover agenda through three scientific reasoning tasks. We further introduce AIVC-Judge, a task-conditioned MLLM-as-a-judge framework with category-specific, reference-aware rubrics for evaluating open-ended responses. A complementary Model-Derived Hard-Negative Mining (MDHNM) strategy converts plausible errors observed during model inference into MCQ distractors for lower-cost evaluation. Within the evaluated heterogeneous model pool, MCQ accuracy correlates positively with AIVC-Judge scores, providing a complementary view of performance alongside open-response evaluation. Code and data demo are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniVCBench.

</details>
